  const currencyKey =
    product.market === "US" || product.market === "OTHER"
      ? "USD"
      : product.market === "EU"
      ? "EUR"
      : product.market === "UK"
      ? "GBP"
      : "BRL";

  const currentRate = rates[currencyKey] || 1;

  // Conversões para BRL (cálculo centralizado)
  const priceInBRL = convertToBRL(product.price || 0, product.market, rates);
  const grossInBRL = convertToBRL(
    product.commissionGross || 0,
    product.market,
    rates
  );
  const commissionInBRL = convertToBRL(
    product.commissionLiquid || 0,
    product.market,
    rates
  );

  const isInternational = product.market !== "BR";

  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // RENDERIZAÇÃO
  // --------------------------------------------------------------------------

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Informações Básicas */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Info size={18} className="text-blue-600" /> Informações do Produto
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Nome
            </div>
            <div className="font-medium text-gray-900">{product.name}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Nicho
            </div>
            <div className="font-medium text-gray-900">{product.nicho}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Mercado
            </div>
            <div className="font-medium text-gray-900">{product.market}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Tipo do Produto
            </div>
            <div className="font-medium text-gray-900">{product.type}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Plataforma
            </div>
            <div className="font-medium text-gray-900">{product.platform}</div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Funil
            </div>
            <div className="font-medium text-gray-900">
              {product.funnelStage === "fundo"
                ? "🎯 Fundo"
                : product.funnelStage === "meio"
                ? "🤔 Meio"
                : "🥶 Topo"}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Métricas Financeiras */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-green-50 to-emerald-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-green-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-green-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <DollarSign size={18} /> Estrutura de Comissão
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {/* BLOCO 1: PREÇO DO PRODUTO */}
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-green-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Preço do Produto
            </div>
            {isInternational ? (
              <>
                <div className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-600">
                  {originalSymbol} {product.price?.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-gray-900">
                  ≈ R$ {priceInBRL.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-gray-900">
                R$ {product.price?.toFixed(2)}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* BLOCO 2: COMISSÃO BRUTA */}
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-green-200">

              </Tooltip>
            </div>
            {isInternational ? (
              <>
                <div className="text-lg font-bold text-green-600">
                  {originalSymbol} {product.commissionGross?.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-700">
                  ≈ R$ {grossInBRL.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-700">
                R$ {product.commissionGross?.toFixed(2)}

            <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
              {product.commissionPct}% do preço
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* BLOCO 3: MARGEM DE CONTRIBUIÇÃO */}
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-green-200">

              Margem de Contribuição

                <Info size={12} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
              </Tooltip>
            </div>
            {isInternational ? (
              <>
                <div className="text-lg font-bold text-green-700">
                  {originalSymbol} {product.commissionLiquid?.toFixed(2)}

                <Crosshair size={12} /> CPC Bom
              </div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-700">
                R$ {product.cpcBom?.toFixed(2)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-green-600 mt-1">Meta ideal</div>
            </div>
          </Tooltip>

          <Tooltip text="5.56% da comissão bruta - Zona intermediária">
            <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-yellow-300 cursor-help hover:shadow-lg transition-shadow">

                cliques no CPC Bom. Quanto maior, melhor!
              </p>
              {isInternational && (

                  ⚠️ <strong>Atenção:</strong> Valores convertidos para BRL

                  {currentRate.toFixed(2)}
                </p>
              )}
              <p>

                cliques em vendas para empatar. Metas abaixo de 2% são
                excelentes!
              </p>
