==========================================
ARQUIVO: Financeiro.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Setup\Tabs\Financeiro.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import { getCurrencySymbol } from "../../../utils/currency";
import {
  DollarSign,
  TrendingUp,
  AlertCircle,
  Target,
  Calculator,
  Settings,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  calculateBreakEvenSimple,
  calculateSimulatorMetrics,
} from "../../../services/logic";
import { CostsManager } from "../../shared/CostsManager";
import type { OperationalCosts, Product } from "../../../types";

// Função auxiliar para conversão de moeda
const convertToBRL = (
  value: number,
  market: string,
  rates: Record<string, number>
): number => {
  const currency =
    market === "US" || market === "OTHER"
      ? "USD"
      : market === "EU"
      ? "EUR"
      : market === "UK"
      ? "GBP"
      : "BRL";

  const exchangeRate = rates[currency] || 1;
  return value * exchangeRate;
};

interface FinanceiroProps {
  product: Product;
  rates: Record<string, number>;
  globalFixedCosts: number;
  productSpecificCosts: OperationalCosts;
  setProductSpecificCosts: (costs: OperationalCosts) => void;
  activeProductsCount: number;
}

const Tooltip: React.FC<{ text: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({
  text,
  children,
}) => {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <div
        onMouseEnter={() => setShow(true)}
        onMouseLeave={() => setShow(false)}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
      {show && (
        <div className="absolute z-50 bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap shadow-lg">
          {text}
          <div className="absolute top-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 -mt-1">
            <div className="border-4 border-transparent border-t-gray-900"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export const Financeiro: React.FC<FinanceiroProps> = ({
  product,
  rates,
  globalFixedCosts,
  productSpecificCosts,
  setProductSpecificCosts,
  activeProductsCount,
}) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Object.keys(rates).length === 0) {
      console.error("❌ Taxas de câmbio não carregadas!");
    }
  }, [rates]);

  const originalSymbol = getCurrencySymbol(product.market);
  // ✅ SMART DEFAULTS: Calcular budget inicial baseado no produto
  const [customBudget, setCustomBudget] = useState(() => {
    const cpc = product.cpcBom || 1;
    const crRealista = product.requiredCR || 1.5; // % realista

    // Budget para fazer 1 venda com CR realista
    const clicksParaUmaVenda = Math.ceil(100 / crRealista);
    const budgetInteligente = Math.ceil(clicksParaUmaVenda * cpc);

    // Arredondar para múltiplo de 10 (mais legível)
    return Math.ceil(budgetInteligente / 10) * 10;
  });

  const [customCPC, setCustomCPC] = useState(product.cpcBom || 1);
  const [budgetInput, setBudgetInput] = useState(String(customBudget));
  const [cpcInput, setCpcInput] = useState(String(customCPC));
  const [selectedOfferId, setSelectedOfferId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // ✅ Sincronizar inputs quando produto mudar
  useEffect(() => {
    setBudgetInput(String(customBudget));
  }, [customBudget]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCpcInput(String(customCPC));
  }, [customCPC]);

  // ✅ NOVO: Calcular dias do mês atual (28, 30 ou 31)
  const daysInCurrentMonth = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();
  }, []);

  // Calcular comissão da oferta selecionada
  const activeCommission = useMemo(() => {
    let commissionOriginal = product.commissionLiquid || 0;

    if (selectedOfferId && product.offers) {
      const offer = product.offers.find((o) => o.id === selectedOfferId);
      if (offer) {
        commissionOriginal = ((offer.price * offer.commissionPct) / 100) * 0.95;
      }
    }

    if (product.market === "BR") {
      return commissionOriginal;
    }

    return convertToBRL(commissionOriginal, product.market, rates);
  }, [selectedOfferId, product, rates]);

  // Nome da oferta ativa
  const activeOfferName = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedOfferId && product.offers) {
      const offer = product.offers.find((o) => o.id === selectedOfferId);
      if (offer) return offer.name;
    }
    return "Oferta Padrão";
  }, [selectedOfferId, product]);

  // Produto com comissão ativa
  const productWithActiveCommission = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      ...product,
      commissionLiquid: activeCommission,
    };
  }, [product, activeCommission]);

  // Calcular o custo global rateado
  const globalCostRateado = useMemo(() => {
    return activeProductsCount > 0 ? globalFixedCosts / activeProductsCount : 0;
  }, [globalFixedCosts, activeProductsCount]);

  // ✅ Custo fixo total MENSAL = rateado + específico
  const custoFixoTotalMensal = useMemo(() => {
    return globalCostRateado + productSpecificCosts.total;
  }, [globalCostRateado, productSpecificCosts]);

  // ✅ Custo fixo DIÁRIO (apenas para exibição visual)
  const custoFixoDiarioDisplay = useMemo(() => {
    return custoFixoTotalMensal / daysInCurrentMonth;
  }, [custoFixoTotalMensal, daysInCurrentMonth]);

  // ✅ Break-even data (VISÃO DIÁRIA - passa valor MENSAL para logic.ts)
  const breakEvenData = useMemo(() => {
    const result = calculateBreakEvenSimple(
      productWithActiveCommission,
      customBudget,
      customCPC,
      custoFixoTotalMensal, // ✅ Passa valor MENSAL - logic.ts faz o rateio
      rates,
      daysInCurrentMonth // ✅ Logic.ts divide internamente
    );

    // Calcular custo diário para display
    const custoFixoDiario = custoFixoTotalMensal / daysInCurrentMonth;
    const investimentoDiarioTotal = customBudget + custoFixoDiario;

    return {
      ...result,
      investimentoAds: customBudget,
      investimentoCustos: custoFixoDiario,
      investimentoTotal: investimentoDiarioTotal,
    };
  }, [
    productWithActiveCommission,
    customBudget,
    customCPC,
    custoFixoTotalMensal,
    rates,
    daysInCurrentMonth,
  ]);

  // ✅ Cenários dinâmicos baseados no CR de break-even (VISÃO DIÁRIA - Clean Architecture)
  const scenarios = useMemo(() => {
    // Proteção contra CR inválido
    const requiredCR =
      breakEvenData.requiredCR && isFinite(breakEvenData.requiredCR)
        ? breakEvenData.requiredCR
        : 0;

    const step = 0.5;

    // Gerar 5 CRs dinâmicos
    const crValues = [
      Math.max(0.1, requiredCR - 1.5 * step),
      Math.max(0.1, requiredCR - 0.5 * step),
      requiredCR,
      requiredCR + 0.5 * step,
      requiredCR + 1.5 * step,
    ];

    return crValues.map((cr) => {
      // ✅ CORRIGIDO: Passa globalFixedCosts (valor BRUTO MENSAL)
      // Logic.ts faz o rateio de produtos E o rateio diário internamente
      const metrics = calculateSimulatorMetrics(
        productWithActiveCommission,
        customBudget,
        customCPC,
        cr,
        custoFixoTotalMensal, // ✅ CORRETO: Usa o total (Global + Específico)
        1, // ✅ CORRETO: Diz para não dividir, pois já é o valor final
        rates,
        daysInCurrentMonth
      );

      // ✅ SEM CÁLCULOS MANUAIS - metrics.profit e metrics.roi JÁ vêm corretos
      return {
        cr,
        clicks: metrics.clicks,
        sales: metrics.sales,
        revenue: metrics.revenue,
        profit: metrics.profit,
        roi: metrics.roi,
        isBreakEven: Math.abs(cr - requiredCR) < 0.01,
      };
    });
  }, [
    breakEvenData,
    productWithActiveCommission,
    customBudget,
    customCPC,
    custoFixoTotalMensal,
    rates,
    daysInCurrentMonth,
  ]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Gerenciar Custos Específicos do Produto */}
      <CostsManager
        costs={productSpecificCosts}
        setCosts={setProductSpecificCosts}
        title="🎯 Custos Específicos deste Produto"
        subtitle="Custos exclusivos para este produto (ex: designer freelancer, scripts personalizados)"
        emptyMessage="Nenhum custo específico. Adicione custos exclusivos deste produto."
        colorScheme="purple"
      />

      {/* Seletor de Oferta */}
      {product.offers && product.offers.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-50 to-indigo-50 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-purple-200 mb-6">
          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-purple-900 mb-2 block">
            📦 Selecione a Oferta para Análise Financeira:
          </label>
          <select
            value={selectedOfferId || ""}
            onChange={(e) => setSelectedOfferId(e.target.value || null)}
            className="w-full p-3 rounded-lg text-gray-900 font-bold text-sm border-2 border-purple-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none"
          >
            <option value="">
              Oferta Padrão ({originalSymbol}{" "}
              {product.commissionLiquid.toFixed(2)}
              {product.market !== "BR" &&
                ` ≈ R$ ${convertToBRL(
                  product.commissionLiquid,
                  product.market,
                  rates
                ).toFixed(2)}`}
              )
            </option>
            {product.offers.map((offer) => {
              const commissionOriginal =
                ((offer.price * offer.commissionPct) / 100) * 0.95;
              const commissionBRL = convertToBRL(
                commissionOriginal,
                product.market,
                rates
              );
              return (
                <option key={offer.id} value={offer.id}>
                  {offer.name} - {originalSymbol} {offer.price.toFixed(2)}
                  (Comissão:{" "}
                  {product.market !== "BR"
                    ? `${originalSymbol} ${commissionOriginal.toFixed(
                        2
                      )} ≈ R$ ${commissionBRL.toFixed(2)}`
                    : `R$ ${commissionOriginal.toFixed(2)}`}
                  )
                </option>
              );
            })}
          </select>
          <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-purple-700 font-semibold">
            💡 Oferta ativa: <strong>{activeOfferName}</strong> - Comissão em
            BRL: R$ {activeCommission.toFixed(2)}
            {product.market !== "BR" && (
              <span className="text-purple-600 ml-1">
                (Convertido de {originalSymbol})
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ✅ Resumo de Custos - VISÃO DIÁRIA */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-50 to-amber-50 p-4 rounded-xl border-2 border-orange-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <Settings className="text-orange-600" size={18} />
          <h4 className="font-bold text-orange-900">
            💰 Resumo de Custos Fixos ({daysInCurrentMonth} dias no mês)
          </h4>
        </div>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-sm">
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-orange-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Custo Global Rateado (Mensal)
            </div>
            <div className="text-lg font-black text-orange-700">
              R$ {globalCostRateado.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
              R$ {globalFixedCosts.toFixed(2)} ÷ {activeProductsCount}{" "}
              produto(s)
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-orange-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Custos Específicos (Mensal)
            </div>
            <div className="text-lg font-black text-purple-700">
              R$ {productSpecificCosts.total.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
              Exclusivos deste produto
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-orange-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Custo Fixo Total (Mensal)
            </div>
            <div className="text-lg font-black text-red-700">
              R$ {custoFixoTotalMensal.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">Rateado + Específicos</div>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-green-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              ⭐ Custo Fixo DIÁRIO (Display)
            </div>
            <div className="text-lg font-black text-green-700">
              R$ {custoFixoDiarioDisplay.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
              R$ {custoFixoTotalMensal.toFixed(2)} ÷ {daysInCurrentMonth} dias
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Configuração de Investimento DIÁRIO */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <DollarSign size={18} className="text-green-600" /> Configuração de
          Investimento DIÁRIO em Ads
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">
              Orçamento Diário em Ads (R$)
              <Tooltip text="Quanto você pretende investir em anúncios POR DIA">
                <AlertCircle
                  size={14}
                  className="inline ml-1 text-gray-400 cursor-help"
                />
              </Tooltip>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              inputMode="numeric"
              value={budgetInput}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const valor = e.target.value;
                // Permitir vazio, números e decimais
                if (valor === "" || /^\d*\.?\d*$/.test(valor)) {
                  setBudgetInput(valor);
                }
              }}
              onBlur={() => {
                // Ao perder foco, converter e validar
                const numero = parseFloat(budgetInput) || 0;
                const valorFinal = Math.max(0, numero);
                setCustomBudget(valorFinal);
                setBudgetInput(String(valorFinal));
              }}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                // Enter também aplica o valor
                if (e.key === "Enter") {
                  e.currentTarget.blur();
                }
              }}
              className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-lg font-bold"
              placeholder="Ex: 100"
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">
              CPC Estimado (R$)
              <Tooltip text="Custo por clique que você pretende trabalhar">
                <AlertCircle
                  size={14}
                  className="inline ml-1 text-gray-400 cursor-help"
                />
              </Tooltip>
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              inputMode="decimal"
              value={cpcInput}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const valor = e.target.value;
                // Permitir vazio, números e até 2 casas decimais
                if (valor === "" || /^\d*\.?\d{0,2}$/.test(valor)) {
                  setCpcInput(valor);
                }
              }}
              onBlur={() => {
                // Ao perder foco, converter e validar
                const numero = parseFloat(cpcInput) || 0.01;
                const valorFinal = Math.max(0.01, numero);
                setCustomCPC(valorFinal);
                setCpcInput(valorFinal.toFixed(2));
              }}
              onKeyDown={(e) => {
                // Enter também aplica o valor
                if (e.key === "Enter") {
                  e.currentTarget.blur();
                }
              }}
              className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-lg font-bold"
              placeholder="Ex: 2.50"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="md:col-span-2 flex justify-end">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                // Recalcular smart defaults
                const cpc = product.cpcBom || 1;
                const crRealista = product.requiredCR || 1.5;
                const clicksParaUmaVenda = Math.ceil(100 / crRealista);
                const budgetInteligente = Math.ceil(clicksParaUmaVenda * cpc);
                const budgetFinal = Math.ceil(budgetInteligente / 10) * 10;

                setCustomBudget(budgetFinal);
                setBudgetInput(String(budgetFinal));
                setCustomCPC(product.cpcBom || 1);
                setCpcInput((product.cpcBom || 1).toFixed(2));
              }}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
            >
              <Target size={16} />
              Restaurar Valores Inteligentes
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Métricas de Break-Even DIÁRIO */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-50 to-indigo-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-purple-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Target size={18} /> Ponto de Equilíbrio DIÁRIO (Break-Even)
        </h3>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Cliques Totais (Dia)
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-purple-700">
              {breakEvenData.totalClicks}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-purple-600 mt-1">
              R$ {customBudget} ÷ R$ {customCPC.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
              CR Necessária
              <Tooltip text="Taxa de conversão para empatar (incluindo custos fixos diários)">
                <AlertCircle size={12} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
              </Tooltip>
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-purple-700">
              {breakEvenData.requiredCR.toFixed(2)}%
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-purple-600 mt-1">Para break-even</div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Vendas Necessárias (Dia)
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-purple-700">
              {breakEvenData.salesNeeded}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-purple-600 mt-1">Para empatar</div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
              Receita Break-Even (Dia)
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-700">
              R$ {breakEvenData.revenueAtBreakEven.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-green-600 mt-1">
              = Investimento Diário Total
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Interpretação DIÁRIA */}
        <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <Calculator
              className="text-purple-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
              size={20}
            />
            <div className="text-sm text-purple-800">
              <strong>💡 Interpretação DIÁRIA:</strong> Com investimento de{" "}
              <strong>R$ {customBudget}</strong> em ads/dia e{" "}
              <strong>R$ {custoFixoDiarioDisplay.toFixed(2)}</strong> em custos
              fixos diários (total:{" "}
              <strong>R$ {breakEvenData.investimentoTotal.toFixed(2)}</strong>
              /dia), você terá{" "}
              <strong>{breakEvenData.totalClicks} cliques</strong>. Para empatar
              (break-even), precisa converter{" "}
              <strong>{breakEvenData.requiredCR.toFixed(2)}%</strong>
              desses cliques em vendas, gerando{" "}
              <strong>{breakEvenData.salesNeeded} venda(s)</strong> e receita de{" "}
              <strong>R$ {breakEvenData.revenueAtBreakEven.toFixed(2)}</strong>{" "}
              no dia.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabela de Cenários Dinâmicos DIÁRIOS */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <TrendingUp size={18} className="text-blue-600" /> Cenários de
          Conversão DIÁRIOS (Dinâmicos - baseados no Break-Even)
        </h3>

        <div className="overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead>
              <tr className="bg-gray-50 border-b-2 border-gray-200">
                <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                  CR (%)
                </th>
                <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                  Cliques/Dia
                </th>
                <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                  Vendas/Dia
                </th>
                <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                  Receita/Dia
                </th>
                <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                  Resultado/Dia
                </th>
                <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                  ROI/Dia
                </th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {scenarios.map((scenario, idx) => (
                <tr
                  key={idx}
                  className={`border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50 ${
                    scenario.isBreakEven
                      ? "bg-yellow-50 border-2 border-yellow-400"
                      : scenario.profit >= 0
                      ? "bg-green-50"
                      : "bg-red-50"
                  }`}
                >
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold">
                    {scenario.cr.toFixed(2)}%
                    {scenario.isBreakEven && (
                      <span className="ml-2 text-xs bg-yellow-400 text-yellow-900 px-2 py-1 rounded-full font-bold">
                        BREAK-EVEN
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="p-3">{scenario.clicks}</td>
                  <td className="p-3 font-bold">{scenario.sales}</td>
                  <td className="p-3 text-green-700 font-bold">
                    R$ {scenario.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                  </td>
                  <td
                    className={`p-3 font-bold ${
                      scenario.profit >= 0 ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    R$ {scenario.profit.toFixed(2)}
                  </td>
                  <td
                    className={`p-3 font-bold ${
                      scenario.roi >= 0 ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {scenario.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-blue-500">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
            <AlertCircle
              className="text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
              size={20}
            />
            <div className="text-sm text-blue-800">
              <strong>⚠️ Análise de Viabilidade DIÁRIA:</strong> Considerando o
              orçamento diário em ads (<strong>R$ {customBudget}</strong>) e
              custos fixos diários (
              <strong>R$ {custoFixoDiarioDisplay.toFixed(2)}</strong>), sua taxa
              mínima de conversão para não ter prejuízo no dia (Break-Even) é de{" "}
              <strong>{breakEvenData.requiredCR.toFixed(2)}%</strong>. A tabela
              acima mostra cenários dinâmicos ao redor deste ponto de
              equilíbrio. Qualquer conversão acima deste valor gerará{" "}
              <strong>resultado positivo no dia</strong>.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: Simulador.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Setup\Tabs\Simulador.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";

import {
  Calculator,
  TrendingUp,
  Target,
  Zap,
  DollarSign,
  MousePointer,
  ShoppingCart,
  Percent,
  Info,
  ArrowLeftRight,
  AlertTriangle,
} from "lucide-react";
import { calculateSimulatorMetrics } from "../../../services/logic";
import type { Product } from "../../../types";

// Função auxiliar para conversão de moeda
const convertToBRL = (
  value: number,
  market: string,
  rates: Record<string, number>
): number => {
  const currency =
    market === "US" || market === "OTHER"
      ? "USD"
      : market === "EU"
      ? "EUR"
      : market === "UK"
      ? "GBP"
      : "BRL";

  const exchangeRate = rates[currency] || 1;
  return value * exchangeRate;
};

interface SimuladorProps {
  product: Product;
  rates: Record<string, number>;
  fixedCostsTotal?: number;
  activeProductsCount?: number;
  specificCostsTotal?: number;
}

type CalculationMode = "normal" | "reverse";

const Tooltip: React.FC<{ text: string; children: React.ReactNode }> = ({
  text,
  children,
}) => {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div className="relative inline-block">
      <div
        onMouseEnter={() => setShow(true)}
        onMouseLeave={() => setShow(false)}
      >
        {children}
      </div>
      {show && (
        <div className="absolute z-50 bottom-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap shadow-lg">
          {text}
          <div className="absolute top-full left-1/2 transform -translate-x-1/2 -mt-1">
            <div className="border-4 border-transparent border-t-gray-900"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export const Simulador: React.FC<SimuladorProps> = ({
  product,
  rates, // ✅ NOVO: Recebe do componente pai
  fixedCostsTotal = 0,
  activeProductsCount = 1,
  specificCostsTotal = 0,
}) => {
  // ✅ 1. Calcular dias do mês atual (Padronização Contábil)
  const daysInCurrentMonth = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    return new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth() + 1, 0).getDate();
  }, []);

  // ✅ 2. CUSTO FIXO DIÁRIO (Rateio + Específico) / Dias
  const totalFixedCostsDaily = useMemo(() => {
    const monthlyShare =
      activeProductsCount > 0 ? fixedCostsTotal / activeProductsCount : 0;
    const totalMonthly = monthlyShare + specificCostsTotal;
    return totalMonthly / daysInCurrentMonth;
  }, [
    fixedCostsTotal,
    activeProductsCount,
    specificCostsTotal,
    daysInCurrentMonth,
  ]);

  // ✅ Para display nos alertas (mensal)
  const totalFixedCostsMonthly = useMemo(() => {
    const monthlyShare =
      activeProductsCount > 0 ? fixedCostsTotal / activeProductsCount : 0;
    return monthlyShare + specificCostsTotal;
  }, [fixedCostsTotal, activeProductsCount, specificCostsTotal]);

  const feeFactor = useMemo(() => {
    // Se não houver dados de comissão, o fator é 1 (ou seja, taxa zero para não bugar)
    if (!product.commissionGross || product.commissionGross === 0) return 1;

    // Aqui ele pega exatamente a proporção que você definiu nos Dados Principais
    return product.commissionLiquid / product.commissionGross;
  }, [product.commissionGross, product.commissionLiquid]);

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<CalculationMode>("normal");
  const [selectedOfferId, setSelectedOfferId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const activeCommission = useMemo(() => {
    let commissionOriginal = product.commissionLiquid || 0; // Valor líquido original // 1. Calcular a comissão da oferta selecionada (na moeda original)

    if (selectedOfferId && product.offers) {
      const offer = product.offers.find((o) => o.id === selectedOfferId);
      if (offer) {
        const bruta = (offer.price * offer.commissionPct) / 100;
        commissionOriginal = bruta * feeFactor;
      }
    } // 2. Se for BR, retorna o original. Senão, converte usando rates.

    if (product.market === "BR") {
      return commissionOriginal;
    } // Usa a função auxiliar para converter

    return convertToBRL(commissionOriginal, product.market, rates);
  }, [selectedOfferId, product, rates]);

  const activeOfferName = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedOfferId && product.offers) {
      const offer = product.offers.find((o) => o.id === selectedOfferId);
      if (offer) return offer.name;
    }
    return "Oferta Padrão";
  }, [selectedOfferId, product]);

  // 🎯 SMART DEFAULTS: Calcula CR mínima de Break-Even
  const calculateBreakEvenCR = useMemo(() => {
    const cpcToUse = product.cpcBom || 1;
    const commission =
      product.commissionLiquidBRL || product.commissionLiquid || 0;

    if (commission <= 0 || cpcToUse <= 0) return 3; // Fallback seguro

    // CR mínima = (CPC / Comissão) × 100
    const minCR = (cpcToUse / commission) * 100;

    // Adiciona 20% de margem de segurança
    const safeCR = minCR * 1.2;

    // Limita entre 0.5% e 15% (faixa realista)
    return Math.max(0.5, Math.min(15, safeCR));
  }, [product.cpcBom, product.commissionLiquidBRL, product.commissionLiquid]);

  // 1. Define os valores iniciais baseados no produto
  const defaultCpc = product.cpcBom || 1;
  // ✅ MUDANÇA: Usa CR inteligente calculada
  const defaultCr = calculateBreakEvenCR;

  // ✅ Budget para pelo menos 1 venda (arredonda pra cima)
  const smartBudget = Math.ceil((defaultCpc / (defaultCr / 100)) * 1.1);

  const [budget, setBudget] = useState(smartBudget);
  const [cpc, setCpc] = useState(defaultCpc);
  const [cr, setCR] = useState(defaultCr);

  const [targetProfit, setTargetProfit] = useState(500);
  const [reverseCpc, setReverseCpc] = useState(product.cpcBom || 1);
  // ✅ MUDANÇA: Modo reverso também usa CR inteligente
  const [reverseCr, setReverseCr] = useState(calculateBreakEvenCR);
  // 🎯 Estados temporários para permitir string vazia durante digitação
  const [budgetInput, setBudgetInput] = useState(smartBudget.toString());
  const [cpcInput, setCpcInput] = useState(defaultCpc.toString());
  const [crInput, setCrInput] = useState(defaultCr.toFixed(1));
  const [targetProfitInput, setTargetProfitInput] = useState("500");
  const [reverseCpcInput, setReverseCpcInput] = useState(defaultCpc.toString());
  const [reverseCrInput, setReverseCrInput] = useState(defaultCr.toFixed(1));

  const validBudget = Math.max(0, budget);
  const validCpc = Math.max(0.01, cpc);
  const validCr = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, cr));

  const validTargetProfit = Math.max(0, targetProfit);
  const validReverseCpc = Math.max(0.01, reverseCpc);
  const validReverseCr = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, reverseCr));

  const dynamicRequiredCR =
    activeCommission && cpc > 0 ? (cpc / activeCommission) * 100 : 0;

  const dynamicRequiredCRReverse =
    activeCommission && reverseCpc > 0
      ? (reverseCpc / activeCommission) * 100
      : 0;

  const productWithActiveCommission = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      ...product,
      commissionLiquid: activeCommission,
    };
  }, [product, activeCommission]);

  const metrics = useMemo(() => {
    // 1. Calcula apenas a FATIA do Custo Global para este produto
    const globalShare =
      activeProductsCount > 0 ? fixedCostsTotal / activeProductsCount : 0;

    // 2. Soma com o Custo Específico INTEIRO (sem dividir)
    // Este é o valor exato que este produto deve pagar no mês
    const trueMonthlyCost = globalShare + specificCostsTotal;

    const baseMetrics = calculateSimulatorMetrics(
      productWithActiveCommission,
      validBudget,
      validCpc,
      validCr,
      trueMonthlyCost, // ✅ Passamos o custo mensal JÁ ajustado
      1, // ✅ Passamos 1 para a função não dividir o custo específico
      rates,
      daysInCurrentMonth
    );

    return {
      ...baseMetrics,
      investmentAds: validBudget,
      investmentFixed: totalFixedCostsDaily,
      investmentTotal: validBudget + totalFixedCostsDaily,
    };
  }, [
    productWithActiveCommission,
    validBudget,
    validCpc,
    validCr,
    fixedCostsTotal,
    activeProductsCount,
    specificCostsTotal,
    totalFixedCostsDaily,
    daysInCurrentMonth,
    rates,
  ]);
  const reverseMetrics = useMemo(() => {
    if (!activeCommission || validReverseCpc <= 0 || validReverseCr <= 0) {
      return {
        requiredBudget: 0,
        clicks: 0,
        sales: 0,
        revenue: 0,
        profit: 0,
        profitReal: 0, // ⭐ NOVO
        roi: 0,
        isViable: false,
        investmentAds: 0,
        investmentFixed: totalFixedCostsDaily,
        investmentTotal: totalFixedCostsDaily,
      };
    }

    const crDecimal = validReverseCr / 100;

    // 1️⃣ Calcular LUCRO POR VENDA (margem unitária)
    const cpaReal = validReverseCpc / crDecimal; // Custo para fazer 1 venda
    const profitPerSale = activeCommission - cpaReal; // Lucro líquido por venda

    // 2️⃣ Se margem negativa = inviável
    if (profitPerSale <= 0) {
      return {
        requiredBudget: 0,
        clicks: 0,
        sales: 0,
        revenue: 0,
        profit: 0,
        profitReal: 0,
        roi: -100,
        isViable: false,
        investmentAds: 0,
        investmentFixed: totalFixedCostsDaily,
        investmentTotal: totalFixedCostsDaily,
      };
    }

    // 3️⃣ META DE FATURAMENTO = Lucro Desejado + Custos Fixos
    const targetTotalProfit = validTargetProfit + totalFixedCostsDaily;

    // 4️⃣ VENDAS NECESSÁRIAS (sempre arredondar para cima!)
    const requiredSales = Math.ceil(targetTotalProfit / profitPerSale);

    // 5️⃣ Calcular cliques e budget baseados nas vendas inteiras
    const requiredClicks = Math.ceil(requiredSales / crDecimal);
    const requiredBudget = requiredClicks * validReverseCpc;

    // 6️⃣ RECALCULAR MÉTRICAS FINAIS com os valores inteiros
    const sales = requiredSales; // Já é inteiro
    const clicks = requiredClicks; // Já é inteiro
    const revenue = sales * activeCommission; // Receita = Comissão Líquida Total

    // 7️⃣ LUCRO REAL PROJETADO (pode ser maior que a meta!)
    const totalInvestment = requiredBudget + totalFixedCostsDaily;
    const profitReal = revenue - totalInvestment; // ⭐ Lucro que realmente vai entrar

    // 8️⃣ ROI baseado no lucro real
    const roi = totalInvestment > 0 ? (profitReal / totalInvestment) * 100 : 0;

    return {
      requiredBudget,
      clicks,
      sales,
      revenue,
      profit: validTargetProfit, // Meta mínima solicitada
      profitReal, // ⭐ NOVO: Lucro real projetado (normalmente maior)
      roi,
      isViable: true,
      investmentAds: requiredBudget,
      investmentFixed: totalFixedCostsDaily,
      investmentTotal: totalInvestment,
    };
  }, [
    activeCommission,
    validTargetProfit,
    validReverseCpc,
    validReverseCr,
    totalFixedCostsDaily,
  ]);
  const status = useMemo(() => {
    if (metrics.profit > 0) return "profit";
    if (metrics.profit < 0) return "loss";
    return "breakeven";
  }, [metrics.profit]);

  const statusConfig = {
    profit: {
      bg: "from-green-50 to-emerald-50",
      border: "border-green-300",
      text: "text-green-700",
      icon: "🎉",
      message: "Cenário lucrativo!",
    },
    loss: {
      bg: "from-red-50 to-rose-50",
      border: "border-red-300",
      text: "text-red-700",
      icon: "⚠️",
      message: "Cenário de prejuízo",
    },
    breakeven: {
      bg: "from-yellow-50 to-amber-50",
      border: "border-yellow-300",
      text: "text-yellow-700",
      icon: "⚖️",
      message: "Break-even (empate)",
    },
  };

  const currentStatus = statusConfig[status];

  const resetToDefaults = () => {
    const resetBudget = smartBudget;
    const resetCpc = defaultCpc;
    const resetCr = calculateBreakEvenCR;

    setBudget(resetBudget);
    setCpc(resetCpc);
    setCR(resetCr);
    setTargetProfit(500);
    setReverseCpc(resetCpc);
    setReverseCr(resetCr);

    // ✅ Atualiza também os inputs temporários
    setBudgetInput(resetBudget.toString());
    setCpcInput(resetCpc.toFixed(2));
    setCrInput(resetCr.toFixed(1));
    setTargetProfitInput("500");
    setReverseCpcInput(resetCpc.toFixed(2));
    setReverseCrInput(resetCr.toFixed(1));
  };

  const toggleMode = () => {
    setMode(mode === "normal" ? "reverse" : "normal");
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 text-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <Calculator size={28} />
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Simulador de Break-Even</h2>
              <p className="text-blue-100 text-sm">
                {mode === "normal"
                  ? "Calcule o lucro baseado no investimento"
                  : "Calcule o investimento necessário para atingir sua meta"}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={toggleMode}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-3 bg-white text-blue-600 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-blue-50 transition-all shadow-md hover:shadow-lg"
          >
            <ArrowLeftRight size={20} />
            {mode === "normal" ? "Modo Reverso" : "Modo Normal"}
          </button>
        </div>

        {product.offers && product.offers.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-4 bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-lg p-3">
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-white/90 mb-2 block">
              📦 Selecione a Oferta para Simular:
            </label>
            <select
              value={selectedOfferId || ""}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedOfferId(e.target.value || null)}
              className="w-full p-2 rounded-lg text-gray-900 font-bold text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-300 outline-none"
            >
              <option value="">
                Oferta Padrão (R$ {activeCommission.toFixed(2)})
              </option>
              {product.offers.map((offer) => {
                const bruta = (offer.price * offer.commissionPct) / 100;
                const rawCommission = bruta * feeFactor;

                // 2. Converte para BRL usando a cotação do dia
                const priceInBRL = convertToBRL(
                  offer.price,
                  product.market,
                  rates
                );
                const commissionInBRL = convertToBRL(
                  rawCommission,
                  product.market,
                  rates
                );

                return (
                  <option key={offer.id} value={offer.id}>
                    {offer.name} - R$ {priceInBRL.toFixed(2)} (Comissão: R${" "}
                    {commissionInBRL.toFixed(2)})
                  </option>
                );
              })}
            </select>
            <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-white/80">
              💡 Oferta ativa: <strong>{activeOfferName}</strong> - Comissão: R${" "}
              {activeCommission.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {mode === "normal" && (
        <>
          {/* ✅ ALERTA DE CUSTOS FIXOS TOTAL */}
          {totalFixedCostsMonthly > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-orange-50 border-l-4 border-orange-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg mb-6">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <AlertTriangle
                  className="text-orange-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                  size={20}
                />
                <div className="text-sm text-orange-900">
                  <strong>💰 Custo Fixo Total:</strong> Considerando{" "}
                  <strong>R$ {totalFixedCostsMonthly.toFixed(2)}/mês</strong>{" "}
                  (R$ {totalFixedCostsDaily.toFixed(2)}/dia)
                  <div className="text-xs mt-1 opacity-90">
                    (Rateio diário considerado nos cálculos)
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap size={18} className="text-yellow-600" /> Parâmetros da
              Simulação
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <DollarSign size={16} className="text-green-600" />
                  Investimento ADS diário (R$)
                  <Tooltip text="Quanto você vai investir em anúncios por dia">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={budgetInput}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const val = e.target.value;
                    setBudgetInput(val);
                    const num = parseFloat(val);
                    if (!isNaN(num) && num >= 0) {
                      setBudget(num);
                    } else if (val === "") {
                      setBudget(0);
                    }
                  }}
                  onBlur={() => {
                    if (budgetInput === "" || isNaN(parseFloat(budgetInput))) {
                      setBudgetInput(smartBudget.toString());
                      setBudget(smartBudget);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-xl font-bold transition-colors"
                  placeholder="0"
                />
                <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  Exemplo: R$ 100, R$ 500, R$ 1000
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <MousePointer size={16} className="text-blue-600" />
                  CPC (R$)
                  <Tooltip text="Custo por clique que você espera pagar">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={cpcInput}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const val = e.target.value;
                    setCpcInput(val);
                    const num = parseFloat(val);
                    if (!isNaN(num) && num > 0) {
                      setCpc(num);
                    }
                  }}
                  onBlur={() => {
                    if (cpcInput === "" || parseFloat(cpcInput) <= 0) {
                      setCpcInput(defaultCpc.toFixed(2));
                      setCpc(defaultCpc);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-xl font-bold transition-colors"
                  placeholder="0.00"
                />
                <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                  <span
                    className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${
                      cpc <= (product.cpcBom || 0)
                        ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                        : cpc <= (product.cpcInter || 0)
                        ? "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700"
                        : cpc <= (product.cpcApert || 0)
                        ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700"
                        : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {cpc <= (product.cpcBom || 0)
                      ? "✓ Bom"
                      : cpc <= (product.cpcInter || 0)
                      ? "⚠ Intermediário"
                      : cpc <= (product.cpcApert || 0)
                      ? "⚠ Apertado"
                      : "✗ Ruim"}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-gray-500">
                    Ref: R$ {(product.cpcBom || 0).toFixed(2)}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Percent size={16} className="text-purple-600" />
                  Taxa de Conversão (%)
                  <Tooltip text="Percentual de cliques que viram vendas">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={crInput}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const val = e.target.value;
                    setCrInput(val);
                    const num = parseFloat(val);
                    if (!isNaN(num) && num >= 0 && num <= 100) {
                      setCR(num);
                    }
                  }}
                  onBlur={() => {
                    if (crInput === "" || parseFloat(crInput) < 0) {
                      setCrInput(defaultCr.toFixed(1));
                      setCR(defaultCr);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-xl font-bold transition-colors"
                  placeholder="0.0"
                />

                <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                  <span
                    className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-bold ${
                      cr < dynamicRequiredCR
                        ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                        : metrics.profit < 0
                        ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-800"
                        : cr < dynamicRequiredCR * 1.2
                        ? "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700"
                        : "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {cr < dynamicRequiredCR
                      ? "⚠️ Taxa Insuficiente"
                      : metrics.profit < 0
                      ? "📉 Falta Orçamento"
                      : cr < dynamicRequiredCR * 1.2
                      ? "⚖️ Margem Apertada"
                      : "🔥 Lucro Real"}
                  </span>

                  <span className="text-gray-500">
                    Min Teórico: {dynamicRequiredCR.toFixed(2)}%
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
              <button
                onClick={resetToDefaults}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <Target size={16} />
                Restaurar Valores Padrão
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${currentStatus.bg} p-6 rounded-xl border-2 ${currentStatus.border} shadow-lg`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
              <div className="text-3xl">{currentStatus.icon}</div>
              <div>
                <h3 className={`text-xl font-black ${currentStatus.text}`}>
                  {currentStatus.message}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                  Resultado baseado nos parâmetros informados
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
              <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                  <MousePointer size={14} />
                  Cliques
                </div>
                <div className="text-3xl font-black text-gray-900">
                  {metrics.clicks}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                  R$ {validBudget} ÷ R$ {validCpc.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                  <ShoppingCart size={14} />
                  Vendas
                </div>
                <div className="text-3xl font-black text-blue-700">
                  {metrics.sales}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                  {metrics.clicks} × {validCr.toFixed(1)}%
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                  <DollarSign size={14} />
                  Comissões Líquidas
                  <Tooltip text="Valor recebido da plataforma após taxas, antes de descontar anúncios e custos fixos.">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </div>
                <div className="text-3xl font-black text-green-700">
                  R$ {metrics.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                  {metrics.sales} × R$ {activeCommission.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                  <TrendingUp size={14} />
                  ROI
                </div>
                <div
                  className={`text-3xl font-black ${
                    metrics.roi >= 0 ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  {metrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                  Lucro ÷ Investimento
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <Calculator
                  className={`flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 ${currentStatus.text}`}
                  size={20}
                />
                <div className="text-sm text-gray-800">
                  <strong>Resumo Financeiro Completo:</strong> Com investimento
                  de <strong>R$ {validBudget.toFixed(2)}</strong> em anúncios
                  por dia
                  {metrics.investmentFixed > 0 && (
                    <>
                      {" "}
                      + <strong>
                        R$ {metrics.investmentFixed.toFixed(2)}
                      </strong>{" "}
                      de custos fixos (total:{" "}
                      <strong>R$ {metrics.investmentTotal.toFixed(2)}</strong>)
                    </>
                  )}
                  , CPC de <strong>R$ {validCpc.toFixed(2)}</strong> e CR de{" "}
                  <strong>{validCr.toFixed(1)}%</strong>, você terá{" "}
                  <strong>{metrics.clicks} cliques</strong>, gerando{" "}
                  <strong>{metrics.sales} venda(s)</strong> da oferta{" "}
                  <strong>{activeOfferName}</strong> e comissões líquidas totais
                  de{" "}
                  <strong className="text-green-700">
                    R$ {metrics.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                  </strong>
                  . Seu lucro líquido será de{" "}
                  <strong
                    className={
                      metrics.profit >= 0 ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700"
                    }
                  >
                    R$ {metrics.profit.toFixed(2)}
                  </strong>{" "}
                  com ROI de{" "}
                  <strong
                    className={
                      metrics.roi >= 0 ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700"
                    }
                  >
                    {metrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                  </strong>
                  .
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            className={`border-l-4 p-5 rounded-r-lg mt-6 transition-colors ${
              metrics.profit >= 0
                ? "bg-green-50 border-green-500"
                : "bg-red-50 border-red-500"
            }`}
          >
            <h4
              className={`font-bold mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 ${
                metrics.profit >= 0 ? "text-green-900" : "text-red-900"
              }`}
            >
              {metrics.profit >= 0
                ? "💡 Cenário Lucrativo"
                : "🚨 Cenário de Prejuízo"}
            </h4>

            <ul
              className={`text-sm space-y-2 ${
                metrics.profit >= 0 ? "text-green-800" : "text-red-800"
              }`}
            >
              <li>
                <strong>• Realidade do CPC:</strong> Com CPC de{" "}
                <b>R$ {validCpc.toFixed(2)}</b>, você precisa converter acima de{" "}
                <b>{dynamicRequiredCR.toFixed(2)}%</b> para empatar.
              </li>

              {dynamicRequiredCR > 2.5 ? (
                <li>
                  <strong>• Alerta de Risco:</strong> Sua meta de conversão (
                  {dynamicRequiredCR.toFixed(2)}%) está muito alta para
                  e-commerce. Foque em baixar o CPC urgente!
                </li>
              ) : (
                <li>
                  <strong>• Viabilidade:</strong> A meta de conversão (
                  {dynamicRequiredCR.toFixed(2)}%) está dentro da média de
                  mercado (1% a 2.5%). Sinal verde para testar.
                </li>
              )}

              <li>
                <strong>• Retorno:</strong> Cada R$ 1,00 investido está voltando
                <strong>
                  {" "}
                  R${" "}
                  {(metrics.revenue / (metrics.investmentTotal || 1)).toFixed(
                    2
                  )}
                </strong>
                .
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {mode === "reverse" && (
        <>
          {/* ✅ ALERTA DE CUSTOS FIXOS - MODO REVERSO CORRIGIDO */}
          {totalFixedCostsMonthly > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-purple-50 border-l-4 border-purple-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg mb-6">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <Info
                  className="text-purple-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                  size={20}
                />
                <div className="text-sm text-purple-900">
                  <strong>💡 Atenção:</strong> Para atingir o lucro desejado, o
                  cálculo já considera o custo fixo total de{" "}
                  <strong>R$ {totalFixedCostsMonthly.toFixed(2)}/mês</strong>{" "}
                  (R$ {totalFixedCostsDaily.toFixed(2)}/dia) investimento
                  mostrado cobre: Anúncios + Custos Operacionais + Lucro Meta.
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Target size={18} className="text-purple-600" /> Calculadora de
              Meta Reversa
            </h3>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <DollarSign size={16} className="text-green-600" />
                  Lucro Desejado Diário (R$)
                  <Tooltip text="Quanto de lucro você quer ter">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  inputMode="decimal"
                  value={targetProfitInput}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const val = e.target.value;
                    setTargetProfitInput(val);
                    const num = parseFloat(val);
                    if (!isNaN(num) && num >= 0) {
                      setTargetProfit(num);
                    }
                  }}
                  onBlur={() => {
                    if (
                      targetProfitInput === "" ||
                      parseFloat(targetProfitInput) < 0
                    ) {
                      setTargetProfitInput("500");
                      setTargetProfit(500);
                    }
                  }}
                  className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-purple-300 rounded-lg focus:border-purple-500 focus:outline-none text-xl font-bold transition-colors"
                  min="0"
                  step="50"
                />
                <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  Exemplo: R$ 500, R$ 1000, R$ 5000
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <MousePointer size={16} className="text-blue-600" />
                  CPC Esperado (R$)
                  <Tooltip text="Custo por clique que você espera pagar">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={reverseCpc}
                  onChange={(e) => setReverseCpc(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-purple-300 rounded-lg focus:border-purple-500 focus:outline-none text-xl font-bold transition-colors"
                  min="0.01"
                  step="0.01"
                />
                <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  Ref: R$ {(product.cpcBom || 0).toFixed(2)}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Percent size={16} className="text-purple-600" />
                  CR Esperado (%)
                  <Tooltip text="Taxa de conversão que você espera conseguir">
                    <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                  </Tooltip>
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={reverseCr}
                  onChange={(e) => setReverseCr(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-purple-300 rounded-lg focus:border-purple-500 focus:outline-none text-xl font-bold transition-colors"
                  min="0"
                  max="100"
                  step="0.1"
                />
                <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                  Min Teórico: {dynamicRequiredCRReverse.toFixed(2)}%
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end">
              <button
                onClick={resetToDefaults}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 font-bold rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <Target size={16} />
                Restaurar Valores Padrão
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            className={`bg-gradient-to-br p-6 rounded-xl border-2 shadow-lg ${
              reverseMetrics.isViable
                ? "from-purple-50 to-indigo-50 border-purple-300"
                : "from-red-50 to-rose-50 border-red-300"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
              <div className="text-3xl">
                {reverseMetrics.isViable ? "🎯" : "⚠️"}
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3
                  className={`text-xl font-black ${
                    reverseMetrics.isViable ? "text-purple-700" : "text-red-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  {reverseMetrics.isViable
                    ? "Investimento Calculado!"
                    : "Cenário Inviável"}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                  {reverseMetrics.isViable
                    ? "Veja quanto você precisa investir para atingir sua meta"
                    : "Com esses parâmetros, não é possível ter lucro"}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            {reverseMetrics.isViable ? (
              <>
                {/* 1. CARD DE INVESTIMENTO TOTAL CORRIGIDO */}
                <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 p-6 rounded-xl mb-4 shadow-lg">
                  <div className="text-center text-white">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase mb-2 opacity-90">
                      💰 Investimento Total Necessário (Diário)
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-5xl font-black mb-2">
                      R$ {reverseMetrics.investmentTotal.toFixed(2)}
                    </p>
                    <div className="text-sm opacity-90 space-y-1">
                      <p>
                        R$ {reverseMetrics.requiredBudget.toFixed(2)} em
                        anúncios
                        {reverseMetrics.investmentFixed > 0 && (
                          <>
                            {" "}
                            + R$ {reverseMetrics.investmentFixed.toFixed(
                              2
                            )}{" "}
                            custos fixos
                          </>
                        )}
                      </p>
                      <p>
                        Para lucro líquido de{" "}
                        <strong>R$ {validTargetProfit.toFixed(2)}</strong>
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* ⭐ NOVO: Card de Lucro Real Projetado */}
                {reverseMetrics.profitReal > reverseMetrics.profit && (
                  <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-emerald-600 p-6 rounded-xl mb-4 shadow-lg">
                    <div className="text-center text-white">
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase mb-2 opacity-90">
                        💰 Lucro Real Projetado
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-5xl font-black mb-2">
                        R$ {reverseMetrics.profitReal.toFixed(2)}
                      </p>
                      <div className="text-sm opacity-90 space-y-1">
                        <p>
                          Meta mínima:{" "}
                          <strong>R$ {reverseMetrics.profit.toFixed(2)}</strong>
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-green-200 font-bold">
                          ✨ Você vai lucrar R${" "}
                          {(
                            reverseMetrics.profitReal - reverseMetrics.profit
                          ).toFixed(2)}{" "}
                          a mais!
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs opacity-75 mt-2">
                          (Devido ao arredondamento para vendas inteiras)
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* 2. GRADE DE MÉTRICAS (MANTIDA) */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-4">
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                      <MousePointer size={14} /> Cliques
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-3xl font-black text-gray-900">
                      {reverseMetrics.clicks}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                      Necessários
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                      <ShoppingCart size={14} /> Vendas
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-3xl font-black text-blue-700">
                      {reverseMetrics.sales}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                      Com CR de {validReverseCr.toFixed(1)}%
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                      <DollarSign size={14} />
                      Comissões Líquidas
                      <Tooltip text="Soma das comissões projetadas para atingir sua meta de lucro.">
                        <Info size={14} className="text-gray-400 cursor-help" />
                      </Tooltip>
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-3xl font-black text-green-700">
                      R$ {reverseMetrics.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                      Total Esperada
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                      <TrendingUp size={14} /> ROI
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-3xl font-black text-purple-700">
                      {reverseMetrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                      Retorno Projetado
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* 3. RESUMO DA META REVERSA CORRIGIDO */}
                <div className="p-4 bg-white rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                    <Calculator
                      className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 text-purple-700"
                      size={20}
                    />
                    <div className="text-sm text-gray-800">
                      <strong>Resumo da Meta Reversa:</strong> Para atingir um
                      lucro
                      <strong className="text-purple-700">
                        {" "}
                        mínimo
                      </strong> de{" "}
                      <strong className="text-purple-700">
                        R$ {reverseMetrics.profit.toFixed(2)}
                      </strong>
                      {reverseMetrics.investmentFixed > 0 && (
                        <>
                          {" "}
                          (considerando{" "}
                          <strong>
                            R$ {reverseMetrics.investmentFixed.toFixed(2)}
                          </strong>{" "}
                          de custos fixos)
                        </>
                      )}
                      , você precisa investir{" "}
                      <strong className="text-purple-700">
                        R$ {reverseMetrics.requiredBudget.toFixed(2)}
                      </strong>{" "}
                      em anúncios
                      {reverseMetrics.investmentFixed > 0 && (
                        <>
                          {" "}
                          +{" "}
                          <strong>
                            R$ {reverseMetrics.investmentFixed.toFixed(2)}
                          </strong>{" "}
                          de custos fixos (total:{" "}
                          <strong>
                            R$ {reverseMetrics.investmentTotal.toFixed(2)}
                          </strong>
                          )
                        </>
                      )}
                      . Com CPC de{" "}
                      <strong>R$ {validReverseCpc.toFixed(2)}</strong> e CR de{" "}
                      <strong>{validReverseCr.toFixed(1)}%</strong>, você terá{" "}
                      <strong>{reverseMetrics.clicks} cliques</strong>, gerando{" "}
                      <strong>{reverseMetrics.sales} venda(s)</strong> da oferta{" "}
                      <strong>{activeOfferName}</strong> e comissões líquidas
                      totais de{" "}
                      <strong className="text-green-700">
                        R$ {reverseMetrics.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                      </strong>
                      .
                      {reverseMetrics.profitReal > reverseMetrics.profit && (
                        <>
                          {" "}
                          <strong className="text-green-700">
                            Seu lucro real será de R${" "}
                            {reverseMetrics.profitReal.toFixed(2)}
                          </strong>{" "}
                          (R${" "}
                          {(
                            reverseMetrics.profitReal - reverseMetrics.profit
                          ).toFixed(2)}{" "}
                          acima da meta),
                        </>
                      )}{" "}
                      resultando em ROI de{" "}
                      <strong className="text-purple-700">
                        {reverseMetrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                      </strong>
                      .
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              /* Cenário Inviável */
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-lg border-2 border-red-300">
                <div className="text-center">
                  <AlertTriangle
                    className="mx-auto mb-4 text-red-600"
                    size={48}
                  />
                  <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-red-900 mb-2">
                    Cenário Impossível
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-sm text-red-800 mb-4">
                    Com CPC de <strong>R$ {validReverseCpc.toFixed(2)}</strong>{" "}
                    e CR de <strong>{validReverseCr.toFixed(1)}%</strong>, você{" "}
                    <strong>sempre</strong> terá prejuízo, independente do
                    investimento.
                  </p>
                  <div className="bg-red-50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-red-200">
                    <p className="text-sm text-red-900 font-bold mb-2">
                      💡 Para tornar este cenário viável:
                    </p>
                    <ul className="text-sm text-red-800 space-y-2 text-left">
                      <li>
                        <strong>• Opção 1:</strong> Reduza o CPC para{" "}
                        <strong>
                          no máximo R$ {activeCommission.toFixed(2)}
                        </strong>{" "}
                        (valor da comissão da oferta ativa)
                      </li>
                      <li>
                        <strong>• Opção 2:</strong> Aumente o CR para{" "}
                        <strong>
                          acima de {dynamicRequiredCRReverse.toFixed(2)}%
                        </strong>
                      </li>
                      <li>
                        <strong>• Opção 3:</strong> Escolha um produto com
                        comissão maior
                      </li>
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Dicas - Modo Reverso */}
          {reverseMetrics.isViable && (
            <div className="border-l-4 p-5 rounded-r-lg bg-purple-50 border-purple-500">
              <h4 className="font-bold mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-purple-900">
                💡 Dicas para Atingir Sua Meta
              </h4>

              <ul className="text-sm space-y-2 text-purple-800">
                <li>
                  <strong>• Planejamento:</strong> Com investimento de{" "}
                  <b>R$ {reverseMetrics.requiredBudget.toFixed(2)}</b>, você
                  precisa de <b>{reverseMetrics.sales} vendas</b> para bater sua
                  meta de lucro.
                </li>

                <li>
                  <strong>• Viabilidade:</strong> O CR de{" "}
                  {validReverseCr.toFixed(2)}% está{" "}
                  {validReverseCr >= dynamicRequiredCRReverse * 1.2
                    ? "muito acima do mínimo necessário. Excelente margem de segurança!"
                    : validReverseCr >= dynamicRequiredCRReverse
                    ? "dentro do esperado, mas com pouca margem. Cuidado com variações!"
                    : "ABAIXO do mínimo necessário. Você terá prejuízo!"}
                </li>

                <li>
                  <strong>• Escalabilidade:</strong> Cada R$ 1,00 investido está
                  retornando{" "}
                  <strong>
                    R${" "}
                    {(
                      reverseMetrics.revenue /
                      (reverseMetrics.investmentTotal || 1)
                    ).toFixed(2)}
                  </strong>
                  .{" "}
                  {reverseMetrics.roi >= 50
                    ? "Ótimo potencial de escala!"
                    : reverseMetrics.roi >= 20
                    ? "Viável para testar."
                    : "ROI baixo, cuidado ao escalar."}
                </li>

                {reverseMetrics.requiredBudget > 5000 && (
                  <li>
                    <strong>• Risco:</strong> Investimento alto (R${" "}
                    {reverseMetrics.requiredBudget.toFixed(2)}). Considere
                    testar com 20-30% desse valor primeiro para validar as
                    métricas.
                  </li>
                )}
              </ul>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

ARQUIVO: index.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Setup\index.tsx
