import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  Save,
  DollarSign,
  TrendingUp,
  Target,
  MousePointer2,
  Globe,
} from "lucide-react";
import type { Product, Keyword, Ad, OperationalCosts } from "../../types";
import { useCurrency } from "../../contexts/CurrencyContext";
import { calculateProductMetrics } from "../../services/logic";

// ✅ ABAS
import { FichaTecnica } from "./Tabs/FichaTecnica";
import { Financeiro } from "./Tabs/Financeiro";
import { Simulador } from "./Tabs/Simulador";
import { Estrategia } from "./Tabs/Estrategia";

interface SetupProps {
  product: Product;
  onUpdate: (product: Product) => void;
  fixedCosts: OperationalCosts;
  setFixedCosts: (costs: OperationalCosts) => void;
  globalFixedCostsTotal?: number;
  activeProductsCount?: number;
}

type TabType = "ficha" | "financeiro" | "simulador" | "estrategia";

interface TabConfig {
  id: TabType;
  label: string;
  icon: string;
}

const TABS: TabConfig[] = [
  { id: "ficha", label: "Ficha Técnica", icon: "📋" },
  { id: "financeiro", label: "Financeiro", icon: "💰" },
  { id: "simulador", label: "Simulador", icon: "🧮" },
  { id: "estrategia", label: "Estratégia", icon: "⚡" },
];

const Setup: React.FC<SetupProps> = ({
  product,
  onUpdate,
  globalFixedCostsTotal = 0,
  activeProductsCount = 1,
}) => {
  const { rates } = useCurrency();
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<TabType>("ficha");
  const [keywords, setKeywords] = useState<Keyword[]>(product.keywords || []);
  const [negativeKeywords, setNegativeKeywords] = useState<string>(
    product.negativeKeywords || ""
  );
  const [ads, setAds] = useState<Ad[]>(product.ads || []);

  // ============================================================================
  // CÁLCULOS FINANCEIROS PARA O HEADER
  // ============================================================================
  const financialMetrics = useMemo(() => {
    const currency =
      product.market === "US" || product.market === "OTHER"
        ? "USD"
        : product.market === "EU"
        ? "EUR"
        : product.market === "UK"
        ? "GBP"
        : "BRL";

    const exchangeRate = rates[currency] || 1;

    // ✅ Cálculo direto (sem depender de propriedades inexistentes)
    const commissionInBRL = (product.commissionLiquid || 0) * exchangeRate;
    const commissionGrossInBRL = (product.commissionGross || 0) * exchangeRate;
    const priceInBRL = (product.price || 0) * exchangeRate;

    return {
      currency,
      exchangeRate,
      commissionInBRL,
      commissionGrossInBRL,
      priceInBRL,
    };
  }, [product, rates]);

  // ============================================================================
  // HANDLERS
  // ============================================================================
  const handleSave = () => {
    // ✅ RECALCULA MÉTRICAS COM COTAÇÃO ATUAL ANTES DE SALVAR
    const updatedMetrics = calculateProductMetrics(product, rates);

    const updatedProduct: Product = {
      ...product,
      // ✅ Estados locais das abas
      keywords,
      negativeKeywords,
      ads,

      // ✅ Mantém valores originais em moeda estrangeira
      commissionGross: product.commissionGross,
      commissionLiquid: product.commissionLiquid,
      price: product.price,

      // ✅ Métricas calculadas pelo motor central
      cpcSuggested: updatedMetrics.cpcSuggested,
      clicksPurchasable: updatedMetrics.clicksPurchasable,
      requiredCR: updatedMetrics.requiredCR,
      breakEvenCPC: updatedMetrics.breakEvenCPC,
    };

    onUpdate(updatedProduct);
    alert(
      `✅ Configuração salva! Comissão atual: R$ ${financialMetrics.commissionInBRL.toFixed(
        2
      )} (Taxa: ${financialMetrics.exchangeRate.toFixed(4)})`
    );
  };

  const handleProductUpdate = (field: keyof Product, value: unknown) => {
    onUpdate({
      ...product,
      [field]: value,
    });
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // RENDERIZAÇÃO
  // ============================================================================
  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500 pb-20">
      {/* HEADER STATS */}
      <div className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2 text-lg">
            <Globe size={20} className="text-blue-600" /> Setup da Campanha:{" "}
            {product.name}
          </h3>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <span className="text-xs font-bold bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider">
              {product.funnelStage} de Funil
            </span>
            <span className="text-xs font-bold bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider">
              {product.type}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div className="p-3 bg-green-50 rounded-lg border border-green-100 relative overflow-hidden group">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-green-600 uppercase font-bold flex gap-1 mb-1">
              <DollarSign size={12} /> Comissão
            </div>
            <div className="font-bold text-green-700 text-xl">
              R$ {financialMetrics.commissionInBRL.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            <Target
              className="absolute -right-4 -bottom-4 text-green-100 opacity-50"
              size={60}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg border border-blue-100 relative overflow-hidden">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-blue-600 uppercase font-bold flex gap-1 mb-1">
              <TrendingUp size={12} /> CPC Médio de Mercado
            </div>
            <div className="font-bold text-blue-700 text-xl">
              R${" "}
              {(
                product.manualBenchmarkCPC ||
                product.cpcSuggested ||
                0
              ).toFixed(2)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 bg-cyan-50 rounded-lg border border-cyan-100 relative overflow-hidden">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-cyan-600 uppercase font-bold flex gap-1 mb-1">
              <MousePointer2 size={12} /> Poder Fogo
            </div>
            <div className="font-bold text-cyan-700 text-xl">
              {product.clicksPurchasable || 0}{" "}
              <span className="text-sm">cliques</span>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="p-3 bg-purple-50 rounded-lg border border-purple-100 relative overflow-hidden">
            <div className="text-[10px] text-purple-600 uppercase font-bold flex gap-1 mb-1">
              <Target size={12} /> Conv. Nec.
            </div>
            <div className="font-bold text-purple-700 text-xl">
              {(product.requiredCR || 0).toFixed(2)}%
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* NAVEGAÇÃO POR TABS */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
        <div className="border-b border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
          <div className="flex overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
            {TABS.map((tab) => (
              <button
                key={tab.id}
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)}
                className={`flex-shrink-0 px-6 py-4 font-bold text-base transition-all flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 ${
                  activeTab === tab.id
                    ? "border-blue-600 text-blue-600 bg-white"
                    : "border-transparent text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100"
                }`}
              >
                {tab.icon} {tab.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CONTEÚDO DA ABA */}
        <div className="p-6">
          {activeTab === "ficha" && (
            <FichaTecnica product={product} rates={rates} />
          )}

          {activeTab === "financeiro" && (
            <Financeiro
              product={product}
              rates={rates}
              globalFixedCosts={globalFixedCostsTotal}
              activeProductsCount={activeProductsCount}
              productSpecificCosts={
                product.specificCosts || { items: [], total: 0 }
              }
              setProductSpecificCosts={(costs) =>
                handleProductUpdate("specificCosts", costs)
              }
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === "simulador" && (
            <Simulador
              product={product}
              rates={rates}
              fixedCostsTotal={globalFixedCostsTotal}
              activeProductsCount={activeProductsCount}
              specificCostsTotal={product.specificCosts?.total || 0}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === "estrategia" && (
            <Estrategia
              product={product}
              keywords={keywords}
              setKeywords={setKeywords}
              negativeKeywords={negativeKeywords}
              setNegativeKeywords={setNegativeKeywords}
              ads={ads}
              setAds={setAds}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BOTÃO SALVAR */}
      <div className="flex justify-center mt-8">
        <button
          onClick={handleSave}
          className="bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-green-700 hover:from-green-700 hover:to-green-800 text-white px-8 py-4 rounded-xl font-bold flex items-center gap-3 shadow-2xl transition-all hover:scale-105 active:scale-95 ring-4 ring-green-100"
        >
          <Save size={24} />
          Salvar Configuração Completa
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export { Setup };
export default Setup;

ARQUIVO: CostsManager.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\shared\CostsManager.tsx

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Plus, Trash2, Edit2, Check, X, DollarSign } from 'lucide-react';
import type { OperationalCosts, CostItem } from '../../types';

interface CostsManagerProps {
  costs: OperationalCosts;
  setCosts: (costs: OperationalCosts) => void;
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  emptyMessage?: string;
  colorScheme?: 'blue' | 'green' | 'purple' | 'orange';
}

export const CostsManager: React.FC<CostsManagerProps> = ({
  costs,
  setCosts,
  title,
  subtitle,
  emptyMessage = "Nenhum custo adicionado ainda.",
  colorScheme = 'blue'
}) => {
  const [isAdding, setIsAdding] = useState(false);
  const [newCostName, setNewCostName] = useState('');
  const [newCostValue, setNewCostValue] = useState('');
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [editName, setEditName] = useState('');
  const [editValue, setEditValue] = useState('');

  // Cores por esquema
  const colors = {
    blue: {
      bg: 'bg-blue-50',
      border: 'border-blue-200',
      text: 'text-blue-700',
      button: 'bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700',
      badge: 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700'
    },
    green: {
      bg: 'bg-green-50',
      border: 'border-green-200',
      text: 'text-green-700',
      button: 'bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700',
      badge: 'bg-green-100 text-green-700'
    },
    purple: {
      bg: 'bg-purple-50',
      border: 'border-purple-200',
      text: 'text-purple-700',
      button: 'bg-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-700',
      badge: 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700'
    },
    orange: {
      bg: 'bg-orange-50',
      border: 'border-orange-200',
      text: 'text-orange-700',
      button: 'bg-orange-600 hover:bg-orange-700',
      badge: 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700'
    }
  };

  const color = colors[colorScheme];

  // Calcular total automaticamente
  const calculateTotal = (items: CostItem[]): number => {
    return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (item.value || 0), 0);
  };

  // Adicionar novo custo
  const handleAddCost = () => {
    if (!newCostName.trim() || !newCostValue || parseFloat(newCostValue) <= 0) {
      alert('⚠️ Preencha o nome e um valor válido!');
      return;
    }

    const newCost: CostItem = {
      id: `cost_${Date.now()}`,
      name: newCostName.trim(),
      value: parseFloat(newCostValue)
    };

    const updatedItems = [...costs.items, newCost];
    
    setCosts({
      items: updatedItems,
      total: calculateTotal(updatedItems)
    });

    // Limpar formulário
    setNewCostName('');
    setNewCostValue('');
    setIsAdding(false);
  };

  // Remover custo
  const handleRemoveCost = (id: string) => {
    const updatedItems = costs.items.filter(item => item.id !== id);
    
    setCosts({
      items: updatedItems,
      total: calculateTotal(updatedItems)
    });
  };

  // Iniciar edição
  const handleStartEdit = (item: CostItem) => {
    setEditingId(item.id);
    setEditName(item.name);
    setEditValue(item.value.toString());
  };

  // Salvar edição
  const handleSaveEdit = (id: string) => {
    if (!editName.trim() || !editValue || parseFloat(editValue) <= 0) {
      alert('⚠️ Nome e valor são obrigatórios!');
      return;
    }

    const updatedItems = costs.items.map(item =>
      item.id === id
        ? { ...item, name: editName.trim(), value: parseFloat(editValue) }
        : item
    );

    setCosts({
      items: updatedItems,
      total: calculateTotal(updatedItems)
    });

    setEditingId(null);
    setEditName('');
    setEditValue('');
  };

  // Cancelar edição
  const handleCancelEdit = () => {
    setEditingId(null);
    setEditName('');
    setEditValue('');
  };

  return (
    <div className={`${color.bg} border-2 ${color.border} rounded-xl p-6 shadow-sm`}>
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
        <div>
          <h3 className={`font-bold ${color.text} text-lg flex items-center gap-2`}>
            <DollarSign size={20} />
            {title}
          </h3>
          {subtitle && (
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mt-1">{subtitle}</p>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className={`${color.badge} px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-lg shadow-sm`}>
          R$ {costs.total.toFixed(2)}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Lista de Custos */}
      <div className="space-y-2 mb-4">
        {costs.items.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400 text-sm border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white">
            {emptyMessage}
          </div>
        ) : (
          costs.items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 flex items-center justify-between group hover:shadow-md transition-all"
            >
              {editingId === item.id ? (
                // Modo Edição
                <div className="flex-1 flex items-center gap-3">
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={editName}
                    onChange={(e) => setEditName(e.target.value)}
                    className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                    placeholder="Nome do custo"
                    autoFocus
                  />
                  <div className="relative w-32">
                    <span className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 text-xs font-bold">R$</span>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      step="0.01"
                      value={editValue}
                      onChange={(e) => setEditValue(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                      placeholder="0,00"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => handleSaveEdit(item.id)}
                    className="p-2 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-green-700 transition-colors"
                    title="Salvar"
                  >
                    <Check size={16} />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={handleCancelEdit}
                    className="p-2 bg-gray-600 text-white rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors"
                    title="Cancelar"
                  >
                    <X size={16} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                // Modo Visualização
                <>
                  <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="font-medium text-gray-700">{item.name}</span>
                    <span className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg">
                      R$ {item.value.toFixed(2)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 ml-4 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleStartEdit(item)}
                      className="p-2 text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                      title="Editar"
                    >
                      <Edit2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveCost(item.id)}
                      className="p-2 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                      title="Remover"
                    >
                      <Trash2 size={16} />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Formulário de Adicionar */}
      {isAdding ? (
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-gray-300 space-y-3">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={newCostName}
            onChange={(e) => setNewCostName(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
            placeholder="Nome do custo (ex: ChatGPT Plus)"
            autoFocus
          />
          <div className="relative">
            <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 text-sm font-bold">R$</span>
            <input
              type="number"
              step="0.01"
              value={newCostValue}
              onChange={(e) => setNewCostValue(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
              placeholder="0,00"
              onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Enter' && handleAddCost()}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button
              onClick={handleAddCost}
              className="flex-1 bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-green-700 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <Check size={18} />
              Adicionar
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setIsAdding(false);
                setNewCostName('');
                setNewCostValue('');
              }}
              className="flex-1 bg-gray-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-gray-700 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
            >
              <X size={18} />
              Cancelar
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <button
          onClick={() => setIsAdding(true)}
          className={`w-full ${color.button} text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md`}
        >
          <Plus size={20} />
          Adicionar Novo Custo
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: CurrencyDisplay.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\shared\CurrencyDisplay.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import type { Market } from '../../types';
import { useCurrency } from '../../contexts/CurrencyContext';
import { 
  getCurrencySymbol, 
  convertToBRL, 
  formatCurrency 
} from '../../utils/currency';
import { AnimatedNumber } from '../AnimatedNumber';

// ========================================
// TIPOS
// ========================================

interface CurrencyDisplayProps {
  /** Valor na moeda original */
  value: number;
  
  /** Mercado/moeda do produto */
  market: Market;
  
  /** Animar mudanças de valor */
  animate?: boolean;
  
  /** Mostrar conversão em BRL ao lado */
  showConverted?: boolean;
  
  /** Número de casas decimais */
  decimals?: number;
  
  /** Classes CSS adicionais */
  className?: string;
  
  /** Mostrar código da moeda (USD, EUR, etc) */
  showCode?: boolean;
  
  /** Inverter (mostrar BRL como principal e original como secundário) */
  invertDisplay?: boolean;
}

// ========================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// ========================================

export const CurrencyDisplay: React.FC<CurrencyDisplayProps> = ({
  value,
  market,
  animate = false,
  showConverted = false,
  decimals = 2,
  className = '',
  showCode = false,
  invertDisplay = false,
}) => {
  const { rates } = useCurrency();
  const symbol = getCurrencySymbol(market);
  
  // Calcular valor convertido em BRL
  const convertedValue = useMemo(() => {
    if (market === 'BR') return value;
    return convertToBRL(value, market, rates);
  }, [value, market, rates]);
  
  // ===== MODO 1: Apenas valor original (padrão) =====
  if (!showConverted && !invertDisplay) {
    if (animate) {
      return (
        <span className={className}>
          <AnimatedNumber
            value={value}
            prefix={`${symbol} `}
            decimals={decimals}
            duration={800}
          />
          {showCode && (
            <span className="text-xs ml-1 opacity-60">
              {market}
            </span>
          )}
        </span>
      );
    }
    
    return (
      <span className={className}>
        {formatCurrency(value, market, decimals)}
        {showCode && (
          <span className="text-xs ml-1 opacity-60">
            {market}
          </span>
        )}
      </span>
    );
  }
  
  // ===== MODO 2: Mostrar conversão ao lado (original + BRL) =====
  if (showConverted && !invertDisplay && market !== 'BR') {
    return (
      <span className={className}>
        {animate ? (
          <AnimatedNumber
            value={value}
            prefix={`${symbol} `}
            decimals={decimals}
            duration={800}
          />
        ) : (
          formatCurrency(value, market, decimals)
        )}
        
        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 ml-2 font-medium">
          (R$ {convertedValue.toFixed(2)})
        </span>
      </span>
    );
  }
  
  // ===== MODO 3: Invertido (BRL principal, original secundário) =====
  if (invertDisplay && market !== 'BR') {
    return (
      <span className={className}>
        {animate ? (
          <AnimatedNumber
            value={convertedValue}
            prefix="R$ "
            decimals={decimals}
            duration={800}
          />
        ) : (
          formatCurrency(convertedValue, 'BR', decimals)
        )}
        
        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 ml-2 font-medium">
          ({symbol} {value.toFixed(decimals)})
        </span>
      </span>
    );
  }
  
  // ===== MODO 4: BRL puro (sem conversão) =====
  return (
    <span className={className}>
      {animate ? (
        <AnimatedNumber
          value={value}
          prefix="R$ "
          decimals={decimals}
          duration={800}
        />
      ) : (
        formatCurrency(value, 'BR', decimals)
      )}
    </span>
  );
};

// ========================================
// VARIANTES PRÉ-CONFIGURADAS
// ========================================

/** Exibe apenas o valor na moeda original */
export const CurrencyOriginal: React.FC<
  Omit<CurrencyDisplayProps, 'showConverted' | 'invertDisplay'>
> = (props) => (
  <CurrencyDisplay {...props} showConverted={false} invertDisplay={false} />
);

/** Exibe valor original com conversão em BRL entre parênteses */
export const CurrencyWithBRL: React.FC<
  Omit<CurrencyDisplayProps, 'showConverted' | 'invertDisplay'>
> = (props) => (
  <CurrencyDisplay {...props} showConverted={true} invertDisplay={false} />
);

/** Exibe valor em BRL com original entre parênteses */
export const CurrencyBRLFirst: React.FC<
  Omit<CurrencyDisplayProps, 'showConverted' | 'invertDisplay'>
> = (props) => (
  <CurrencyDisplay {...props} showConverted={false} invertDisplay={true} />
);

import React from 'react';

interface TooltipProps {
  text: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}

export const Tooltip: React.FC<TooltipProps> = ({ text, children }) => {
  return (
    <div className="group relative inline-flex items-center">
      {children}
      <div className="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 px-3 py-2 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded-lg opacity-0 invisible group-hover:opacity-100 group-hover:visible transition-all duration-200 pointer-events-none z-50 w-64 shadow-xl whitespace-normal">
        <p className="leading-relaxed">{text}</p>
        <div className="absolute top-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -mt-1 border-4 border-transparent border-t-gray-900"></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";
import { Bot, X, Send, Sparkles, Search, Eye, EyeOff } from "lucide-react";
import { generateAIResponse } from "../services/gemini";
import type { AppContext } from "../hooks/useAppContext";
import { usePageVision, formatPageVisionForAI } from "../hooks/usePageVision";

// Hook para detectar mobile
const useIsMobile = () => {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkMobile = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < 768);
    };

    checkMobile();
    window.addEventListener("resize", checkMobile);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("resize", checkMobile);
  }, []);

  return isMobile;
};

/**
 * Gera um System Prompt inteligente baseado no contexto
 */
function gerarSystemPrompt(
  contexto?: AppContext | null,
  pageVision?: string
): string {
  let basePrompt = `Você é um assistente inteligente especializado em gestão de marketing de afiliados.`;

  // Adiciona contexto da página atual
  if (contexto) {
    const {
      paginaAtual,
      totalProdutos,
      totalCampanhas,
      produtoAtual,
      campanhaAtual,
      capacidades,
    } = contexto;

    let contextInfo = "";

    if (produtoAtual) {
      contextInfo = `O usuário está visualizando o produto "${produtoAtual.nome}".`;
    } else if (campanhaAtual) {
      contextInfo = `O usuário está visualizando a campanha "${campanhaAtual.nome}".`;
    } else if (paginaAtual && paginaAtual !== "Página Desconhecida") {
      contextInfo = `O usuário está na página "${paginaAtual}".`;
    }

    const stats: string[] = [];
    if (totalProdutos !== undefined) stats.push(`${totalProdutos} produtos`);
    if (totalCampanhas !== undefined) stats.push(`${totalCampanhas} campanhas`);
    const statsInfo =
      stats.length > 0 ? `O sistema possui ${stats.join(" e ")}.` : "";

    basePrompt += `

CONTEXTO DA SESSÃO:
${contextInfo}
${statsInfo}`;

    if (capacidades && capacidades.length > 0) {
      basePrompt += `

SUAS CAPACIDADES:
${capacidades.map((c) => `- ${c}`).join("\n")}`;
    }
  }

  // Adiciona visão da página (se ativada)
  if (pageVision) {
    basePrompt += `

${pageVision}`;
  }

  basePrompt += `

INSTRUÇÕES DE COMPORTAMENTO:

1. PRIMEIRA INTERAÇÃO (saudações como "oi", "olá", "help"):
   - Apresente-se de forma natural e amigável
   - Mencione SUTILMENTE onde o usuário está (ex: "Vejo que você está no Dashboard")
   - Ofereça ajuda sem ser verboso

2. PERGUNTAS ESPECÍFICAS:
   - Responda DIRETAMENTE ao que foi perguntado
   - Use o contexto/visão da página APENAS se for relevante para a resposta
   - Seja objetivo e prático
   - NÃO mencione elementos da página que não sejam relevantes

3. PERGUNTAS SOBRE A PÁGINA ATUAL (ex: "o que tem aqui?", "o que posso fazer?"):
   - Use as informações de estrutura da página
   - Explique os elementos e funcionalidades disponíveis
   - Seja descritivo mas organizado

4. ANÁLISE DE DADOS:
   - Se houver métricas/dados visíveis, use-os para dar insights
   - Ofereça interpretações e sugestões baseadas nos dados

IMPORTANTE:
- NUNCA invente ou alucine informações que não estão no contexto
- Se não souber algo, seja honesto
- Mantenha respostas concisas mas completas
- Sempre responda em Português (Brasil)`;

  return basePrompt;
}

export const AIAssistant: React.FC<{
  contextoApp?: AppContext | null;
}> = ({ contextoApp }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState<
    { role: "user" | "ai"; text: string; urls?: string[] }[]
  >([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [useSearch, setUseSearch] = useState(false);
  const [useVision, setUseVision] = useState(true); // Visão ativada por padrão
  const isMobile = useIsMobile();

  // 👁️ Hook de visão da página
  const pageVisionData = usePageVision();
  const pageVisionText = useVision
    ? formatPageVisionForAI(pageVisionData)
    : undefined;

  // 🔹 Rolagem automática sempre que chegam novas mensagens
  useEffect(() => {
    const box = document.getElementById("ai-messages");
    if (box) box.scrollTop = box.scrollHeight;
  }, [messages]);

  const handleSend = async () => {
    if (!input.trim() || loading) return;

    const userMsg = input.trim();
    setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { role: "user", text: userMsg }]);
    setInput("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      // 🧠 Gera o system prompt inteligente COM visão da página
      const systemPrompt = gerarSystemPrompt(contextoApp, pageVisionText);

      // 📝 Monta o prompt completo
      const fullPrompt = `${systemPrompt}

PERGUNTA DO USUÁRIO:
${userMsg}`;

      // 🤖 Chama a API com o modelo correto
      const model = "gemini-2.5-flash";

      const res = await generateAIResponse(fullPrompt, model, useSearch);

      const text =
        typeof res.text === "string" && res.text.trim() !== ""
          ? res.text
          : "Desculpe, não consegui gerar uma resposta.";

      setMessages((prev) => [...prev, { role: "ai", text, urls: res.urls }]);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Erro ao enviar mensagem:", error);
      setMessages((prev) => [
        ...prev,
        { role: "ai", text: "Erro ao conectar com a IA. Tente novamente." },
      ]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // ✅ POSIÇÃO DINÂMICA: Mobile = bottom-20, Desktop = bottom-6
  const containerStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    position: "fixed",
    bottom: isMobile ? "80px" : "24px",
    right: "24px",
    zIndex: isMobile ? 25 : 50,
  };

  return (
    <div style={containerStyle}>
      {!isOpen && (
        <button
          onClick={() => setIsOpen(true)}
          className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-purple-600 text-white p-4 rounded-full shadow-lg hover:scale-105 transition flex items-center gap-2"
        >
          <Bot size={24} />
          <span className="font-semibold hidden md:inline">Assistente IA</span>
        </button>
      )}

      {isOpen && (
        <div
          className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl flex flex-col border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom"
          style={{
            width: isMobile ? "90vw" : "450px",
            maxWidth: "450px",
            height: isMobile ? "70vh" : "500px",
          }}
        >
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-purple-600 p-4 flex justify-between items-center text-white">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Sparkles size={20} />
              <h3 className="font-bold">Assistente Inteligente</h3>
            </div>
            <button
              className="hover:bg-white/20 rounded-full p-1"
              onClick={() => setIsOpen(false)}
            >
              <X size={20} />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Chat container */}
          <div
            id="ai-messages"
            className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4 bg-gray-50"
          >
            {messages.length === 0 && (
              <div className="text-center text-gray-400 mt-10">
                <Bot size={48} className="mx-auto mb-2 opacity-50" />
                <p className="text-sm px-4 mb-2">
                  Olá! Sou seu assistente de gestão de afiliados.
                </p>
                <p className="text-xs px-4 text-gray-500">
                  {useVision
                    ? "Estou analisando esta página e posso ajudar com o que vê aqui!"
                    : "Pergunte sobre estratégias, análises ou funcionalidades!"}
                </p>
              </div>
            )}

            {messages.map((m, i) => (
              <div
                key={i}
                className={`flex ${
                  m.role === "user" ? "justify-end" : "justify-start"
                }`}
              >
                <div
                  className={`max-w-[85%] rounded-2xl p-3 text-sm
                  ${
                    m.role === "user"
                      ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                      : "bg-white text-gray-800 border border-gray-200"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">
                    {m.text}
                  </div>

                  {m.urls && m.urls.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100">
                      <p className="text-xs font-bold mb-1">Fontes:</p>
                      {m.urls.map((u, idx) => (
                        <a
                          key={idx}
                          href={u}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="block text-xs text-blue-500 truncate hover:underline"
                        >
                          {new URL(u).hostname}
                        </a>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}

            {loading && (
              <div className="flex justify-start">
                <div className="bg-gray-200 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs text-gray-500 animate-pulse">
                  Pensando...
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Input */}
          <div className="p-3 bg-white border-t border-gray-100">
            <div className="flex gap-3 mb-2">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-600 cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={useVision}
                  onChange={(e) => setUseVision(e.target.checked)}
                />
                {useVision ? <Eye size={12} /> : <EyeOff size={12} />}
                Visão da Página
              </label>

              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-600 cursor-pointer">
                <input
                  type="checkbox"
                  checked={useSearch}
                  onChange={(e) => setUseSearch(e.target.checked)}
                />
                <Search size={12} />
                Buscar Web
              </label>
            </div>

            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={input}
                onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && !loading && handleSend()}
                placeholder="Digite sua pergunta..."
                className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none"
              />

              <button
                onClick={handleSend}
                disabled={loading || !input.trim()}
                className="bg-blue-600 text-white p-2 rounded-full disabled:opacity-50 hover:bg-blue-700 transition-colors flex-shrink-0"
              >
                <Send size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: AlertsPanel.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\AlertsPanel.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  AlertTriangle, 
  CheckCircle, 
  Info, 
  AlertCircle,
  X,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Bell,
  BellOff
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { Alert } from '../../services/logic';

interface AlertsPanelProps {
  alerts: Alert[];
  onDismiss?: (alertId: string) => void;
}

export const AlertsPanel: React.FC<AlertsPanelProps> = ({ alerts, onDismiss }) => {
  const [dismissedAlerts, setDismissedAlerts] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [expandedAlerts, setExpandedAlerts] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false);

  // Filtrar alertas não dispensados
  const visibleAlerts = alerts.filter(alert => !dismissedAlerts.has(alert.id));

  // Mostrar apenas os 3 primeiros ou todos
  const displayedAlerts = showAll ? visibleAlerts : visibleAlerts.slice(0, 3);

  const handleDismiss = (alertId: string) => {
    setDismissedAlerts(prev => new Set([...prev, alertId]));
    if (onDismiss) {
      onDismiss(alertId);
    }
  };

  const toggleExpand = (alertId: string) => {
    setExpandedAlerts(prev => {
      const newSet = new Set(prev);
      if (newSet.has(alertId)) {
        newSet.delete(alertId);
      } else {
        newSet.add(alertId);
      }
      return newSet;
    });
  };

  const getAlertStyles = (type: Alert['type']) => {
    switch (type) {
      case 'danger':
        return {
          bg: 'bg-red-50',
          border: 'border-red-200',
          text: 'text-red-800',
          icon: <AlertTriangle className="text-red-600" size={20} />,
          iconBg: 'bg-red-100'
        };
      case 'warning':
        return {
          bg: 'bg-yellow-50',
          border: 'border-yellow-200',
          text: 'text-yellow-800',
          icon: <AlertCircle className="text-yellow-600" size={20} />,
          iconBg: 'bg-yellow-100'
        };
      case 'success':
        return {
          bg: 'bg-green-50',
          border: 'border-green-200',
          text: 'text-green-800',
          icon: <CheckCircle className="text-green-600" size={20} />,
          iconBg: 'bg-green-100'
        };
      case 'info':
        return {
          bg: 'bg-blue-50',
          border: 'border-blue-200',
          text: 'text-blue-800',
          icon: <Info className="text-blue-600" size={20} />,
          iconBg: 'bg-blue-100'
        };
    }
  };

  const getCategoryLabel = (category: Alert['category']) => {
    switch (category) {
      case 'campaign': return '📢 Campanha';
      case 'product': return '📦 Produto';
      case 'budget': return '💰 Orçamento';
      case 'goal': return '🎯 Meta';
      case 'opportunity': return '🚀 Oportunidade';
    }
  };

  if (visibleAlerts.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-green-50 to-emerald-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-green-200 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-3 bg-green-100 rounded-lg">
            <CheckCircle className="text-green-600" size={24} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-green-900 text-lg">Tudo certo! 🎉</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-green-700">
              Nenhum alerta crítico no momento. Suas campanhas estão rodando bem!
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 bg-orange-100 rounded-lg">
            <Bell className="text-orange-600" size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg">Alertas Inteligentes</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
              {visibleAlerts.length} alerta{visibleAlerts.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} detectado{visibleAlerts.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Botão Limpar Todos */}
        {visibleAlerts.length > 0 && (
          <button
            onClick={() => setDismissedAlerts(new Set(visibleAlerts.map(a => a.id)))}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <BellOff size={16} />
            Limpar todos
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Lista de Alertas */}
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {displayedAlerts.map((alert) => {
          const styles = getAlertStyles(alert.type);
          const isExpanded = expandedAlerts.has(alert.id);

          return (
            <div
              key={alert.id}
              className={`${styles.bg} border ${styles.border} rounded-lg p-4 transition-all duration-200 hover:shadow-md`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                {/* Ícone */}
                <div className={`${styles.iconBg} p-2 rounded-lg flex-shrink-0`}>
                  {styles.icon}
                </div>

                {/* Conteúdo */}
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  {/* Categoria */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
                      {getCategoryLabel(alert.category)}
                    </span>
                    {alert.productName && (
                      <span className="text-xs bg-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-medium text-gray-700">
                        {alert.productName}
                      </span>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  {/* Título */}
                  <h4 className={`font-bold ${styles.text} mb-1`}>
                    {alert.title}
                  </h4>

                  {/* Mensagem */}
                  <p className={`text-sm ${styles.text} opacity-90`}>
                    {alert.message}
                  </p>

                  {/* Detalhes expandíveis */}
                  {(alert.details || alert.actionSuggestion) && (
                    <div className="mt-2">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => toggleExpand(alert.id)}
                        className={`flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold ${styles.text} hover:underline`}
                      >
                        {isExpanded ? (
                          <>
                            <ChevronUp size={14} />
                            Menos detalhes
                          </>
                        ) : (
                          <>
                            <ChevronDown size={14} />
                            Ver detalhes
                          </>
                        )}
                      </button>

                      {isExpanded && (
                        <div className="mt-3 space-y-2 pl-2 border-l-2 border-current opacity-75">
                          {alert.details && (
                            <p className="text-xs">
                              <strong>Detalhes:</strong> {alert.details}
                            </p>
                          )}
                          {alert.actionSuggestion && (
                            <p className="text-xs">
                              <strong>💡 Sugestão:</strong> {alert.actionSuggestion}
                            </p>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Botão Fechar */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => handleDismiss(alert.id)}
                  className="flex-shrink-0 p-1 hover:bg-white rounded-lg transition-colors opacity-50 hover:opacity-100"
                  title="Dispensar alerta"
                >
                  <X size={16} />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Botão Ver Mais/Menos */}
      {visibleAlerts.length > 3 && (
        <div className="mt-4 text-center">
          <button
            onClick={() => setShowAll(!showAll)}
            className="px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            {showAll ? (
              <>Mostrar menos</>
            ) : (
              <>Ver todos ({visibleAlerts.length - 3} mais)</>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: Analysis.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Analysis.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";
import type {
  Product,
  Campaign,
  CampaignStatus,
  AlertConfig,
  AIAnalysisResponse,
  VerdictResult,
} from "../types";
import { analyzeCampaignVerdict } from "../services/logic";
import { useCurrency } from "../contexts/CurrencyContext";
import {
  getCurrencySymbol,
  getExchangeRate,
  convertToBRL,
} from "../utils/currency";
import {
  DollarSign,
  TrendingUp,
  Crosshair,
  Flame,
  Target,
  AlertTriangle,
  Save,
  BrainCircuit,
  Filter,
  ArrowRight,
  Zap,
  Bell,
  X,
  Copy,
  Play,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
  BarChart3,
  MousePointer2,
  Wallet,
  TrendingDown,
  AlertCircle,
  Droplets,
  Activity,
  Info,
  Award,
  ArrowDown,
} from "lucide-react";

interface ConversionBreakdown {
  offerId: string;
  offerName: string;
  quantity: number;
  commission: number;
  revenue: number;
}

interface AnalysisProps {
  product: Product;
  products: Product[];
  history: Campaign[];
  onAddToHistory: (campaign: Campaign) => void;
  fixedCostsTotal?: number;
  activeProductsCount?: number;
}

export const Analysis: React.FC<AnalysisProps> = ({
  product,
  products,
  history,
  onAddToHistory,
  fixedCostsTotal = 0,
  activeProductsCount = 1,
}) => {
  // ========================================
  // 🌍 SISTEMA MULTI-MOEDAS
  // ========================================
  const { rates } = useCurrency();
  const currencySymbol = getCurrencySymbol(product.market);
  const currentExchangeRate = getExchangeRate(product.market, rates);

  // ✅ BUSCAR ÚLTIMA CAMPANHA DO PRODUTO NO HISTÓRICO
  const lastCampaign = useMemo(() => {
    const productCampaigns = history.filter((h) => h.productId === product.id);
    if (productCampaigns.length === 0) return null;

    return productCampaigns.sort(
      (a, b) => new Date(b.date).getTime() - new Date(a.date).getTime()
    )[0];
  }, [history, product.id]);

  // ✅ CARREGAR DADOS DO ÚLTIMO REGISTRO OU USAR PADRÕES
  const [realData, setRealData] = useState({
    impressions: lastCampaign?.impressions || 1000,
    clicks: lastCampaign?.clicks || 0,
    spend: lastCampaign?.spend || 0,
    conversions: lastCampaign?.conversions || 0,
  });

  // ✅ SISTEMA DE BREAKDOWN POR OFERTA
  const [useBreakdown, setUseBreakdown] = useState(false);
  const [conversionBreakdown, setConversionBreakdown] = useState<
    ConversionBreakdown[]
  >([]);

  // ✅ CALCULAR RECEITA COM BASE NO BREAKDOWN OU OFERTA PADRÃO
  const revenue = useMemo(() => {
    if (useBreakdown && conversionBreakdown.length > 0) {
      // Soma todas as receitas (já em moeda original)
      const totalOriginal = conversionBreakdown.reduce(
        (sum, item) => sum + item.revenue,
        0
      );
      // Converte para BRL
      return convertToBRL(totalOriginal, product.market, rates);
    } else {
      // Receita simples: conversões * comissão (usar valor já salvo em BRL)
      return realData.conversions * (product.commissionLiquidBRL || 0);
    }
  }, [useBreakdown, conversionBreakdown, realData.conversions, product, rates]);

  const [campaignStatus, setCampaignStatus] =
    useState<CampaignStatus>("aprendizado");
  const [selectedPeriod, setSelectedPeriod] = useState(1);
  const [customPeriod, setCustomPeriod] = useState("");
  const [alertConfig, setAlertConfig] = useState<AlertConfig>({
    cpcMax: product.cpcApert,
    roasMin: 1.5,
    roiMin: 10,
    crMin: product.requiredCR || 1.0,
    ctrMin: 1.0,
  });

  const [aiReport, setAiReport] = useState<AIAnalysisResponse | null>(null);
  const [showAIModal, setShowAIModal] = useState(false);
  const [aiJsonInput, setAiJsonInput] = useState("");

  // ✅ SINCRONIZAR BREAKDOWN QUANDO CONVERSÕES MUDAM
  useEffect(() => {
    if (useBreakdown && product.offers && product.offers.length > 0) {
      if (
        lastCampaign?.conversionBreakdown &&
        lastCampaign.conversionBreakdown.length > 0
      ) {
        setConversionBreakdown(lastCampaign.conversionBreakdown);
      } else if (conversionBreakdown.length === 0) {
        const initialBreakdown = product.offers.map((offer) => ({
          offerId: offer.id,
          offerName: offer.name,
          quantity: 0,
          commission: ((offer.price * offer.commissionPct) / 100) * 0.95,
          revenue: 0,
        }));
        setConversionBreakdown(initialBreakdown);
      }
    }
  }, [useBreakdown, product.offers, lastCampaign]);

  // ✅ HANDLER: Atualizar quantidade de conversão por oferta
  const updateBreakdownQuantity = (offerId: string, quantity: number) => {
    setConversionBreakdown((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) =>
        item.offerId === offerId
          ? { ...item, quantity, revenue: quantity * item.commission }
          : item
      )
    );
  };

  // ✅ CALCULAR TOTAL DE CONVERSÕES DO BREAKDOWN
  const totalBreakdownConversions = useMemo(() => {
    return conversionBreakdown.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.quantity, 0);
  }, [conversionBreakdown]);

  // ✅ SINCRONIZAR CONVERSÕES TOTAIS COM BREAKDOWN
  useEffect(() => {
    if (useBreakdown && totalBreakdownConversions !== realData.conversions) {
      setRealData((prev) => ({
        ...prev,
        conversions: totalBreakdownConversions,
      }));
    }
  }, [totalBreakdownConversions, useBreakdown]);

  const [particles, setParticles] = useState<
    Array<{ id: number; x: number; delay: number }>
  >([]);
  const [hoveredSection, setHoveredSection] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // --- CÁLCULOS MEMOIZADOS PARA PERFORMANCE ---
  const metrics = useMemo(() => {
    const dailyGlobalFixed =
      fixedCostsTotal / 30 / Math.max(activeProductsCount, 1);
    const dailySpecificFixed = (product.specificCosts?.total || 0) / 30;
    const totalDailyFixed = dailyGlobalFixed + dailySpecificFixed;

    const cpc = realData.clicks > 0 ? realData.spend / realData.clicks : 0;
    const ctr =
      realData.impressions > 0
        ? (realData.clicks / realData.impressions) * 100
        : 0;
    const cr =
      realData.clicks > 0 ? (realData.conversions / realData.clicks) * 100 : 0;
    const roas = realData.spend > 0 ? revenue / realData.spend : 0;
    const cpa =
      realData.conversions > 0 ? realData.spend / realData.conversions : 0;

    const profit = revenue - realData.spend - totalDailyFixed * selectedPeriod;
    const totalInvested = realData.spend + totalDailyFixed * selectedPeriod;
    const roi = totalInvested > 0 ? (profit / totalInvested) * 100 : 0;

    return { cpc, ctr, cr, roas, roi, cpa, profit };
  }, [
    realData,
    revenue,
    fixedCostsTotal,
    product.specificCosts,
    selectedPeriod,
    activeProductsCount,
  ]);

  // --- LÓGICA DO FUNIL VERTICAL ---
  const funnelData = useMemo(() => {
    const { ctr, cr } = metrics;

    const topWidth = 100;
    const middleWidth = Math.max(20, Math.min(100, (ctr / 10) * 100));
    const bottomWidth = Math.max(
      15,
      Math.min(middleWidth, (cr / product.requiredCR) * middleWidth * 0.6)
    );

    const ctrColor =
      ctr >= 5
        ? "from-green-400 to-green-600"
        : ctr >= 2
        ? "from-yellow-400 to-yellow-600"
        : "from-red-400 to-red-600";

    const crColor =
      cr >= product.requiredCR
        ? "from-green-400 to-green-600"
        : cr >= product.requiredCR * 0.5
        ? "from-yellow-400 to-yellow-600"
        : "from-red-400 to-red-600";

    const ctrBottleneck = ctr < 2;
    const crBottleneck = cr < product.requiredCR * 0.5;

    const lostClicks = realData.impressions - realData.clicks;
    const lostConversions = realData.clicks - realData.conversions;
    const ctrLossPercent =
      realData.impressions > 0 ? (lostClicks / realData.impressions) * 100 : 0;
    const crLossPercent =
      realData.clicks > 0 ? (lostConversions / realData.clicks) * 100 : 0;

    return {
      topWidth,
      middleWidth,
      bottomWidth,
      ctrColor,
      crColor,
      ctrBottleneck,
      crBottleneck,
      lostClicks,
      lostConversions,
      ctrLossPercent,
      crLossPercent,
    };
  }, [metrics, realData, product.requiredCR]);

  // --- ANIMAÇÃO DE PARTÍCULAS NO FUNIL ---
  useEffect(() => {
    if (realData.clicks > 0 && realData.conversions > 0) {
      const interval = setInterval(() => {
        const newParticle = {
          id: Date.now(),
          x: Math.random() * 80 + 10,
          delay: Math.random() * 0.5,
        };
        setParticles((prev) => [...prev.slice(-15), newParticle]);
      }, 800);
      return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }
  }, [realData.clicks, realData.conversions]);

  // --- LÓGICA DA BARRA DE CPC HORIZONTAL (Gauge) ---
  const gaugeData = useMemo(() => {
    const maxVisualCPC = product.cpcApert * 1.5;
    const needlePosition = Math.min((metrics.cpc / maxVisualCPC) * 100, 100);

    const cpcStatus =
      metrics.cpc <= product.cpcBom
        ? "excellent"
        : metrics.cpc <= product.cpcInter
        ? "good"
        : metrics.cpc <= product.cpcApert
        ? "warning"
        : "danger";

    return { maxVisualCPC, needlePosition, cpcStatus };
  }, [metrics.cpc, product]);

  // --- ALERTAS INTELIGENTES ---
  const activeAlerts: string[] = useMemo(() => {
    const alerts: string[] = [];

    if (metrics.cpc > alertConfig.cpcMax && realData.clicks > 0) {
      alerts.push(
        `🔴 CPC R$ ${metrics.cpc.toFixed(
          2
        )} acima do limite (R$ ${alertConfig.cpcMax.toFixed(2)})`
      );
    }

    if (
      realData.spend > 0 &&
      metrics.roas < alertConfig.roasMin &&
      metrics.roas > 0
    ) {
      alerts.push(
        `📉 ROAS ${metrics.roas.toFixed(2)}x abaixo da meta (${
          alertConfig.roasMin
        }x)`
      );
    }

    if (realData.spend > 0 && metrics.roi < 0) {
      alerts.push(
        `💸 ROI negativo: prejuízo de ${Math.abs(metrics.roi).toFixed(1)}%`
      );
    }

    if (
      realData.spend > 0 &&
      metrics.roi > 0 &&
      metrics.roi < alertConfig.roiMin
    ) {
      alerts.push(
        `⚠️ ROI ${metrics.roi.toFixed(1)}% abaixo da meta (${
          alertConfig.roiMin
        }%)`
      );
    }

    if (realData.clicks > 20 && metrics.cr < alertConfig.crMin) {
      alerts.push(
        `🎯 Conversão ${metrics.cr.toFixed(
          2
        )}% abaixo da meta (${alertConfig.crMin.toFixed(2)}%)`
      );
    }

    if (realData.impressions > 100 && metrics.ctr < alertConfig.ctrMin) {
      alerts.push(
        `👁️ CTR ${metrics.ctr.toFixed(2)}% muito baixo (mínimo ${
          alertConfig.ctrMin
        }%)`
      );
    }

    return alerts;
  }, [metrics, realData, alertConfig, currencySymbol]);

  const verdict: VerdictResult = useMemo(
    () =>
      analyzeCampaignVerdict(
        realData.spend,
        realData.conversions,
        product.commissionLiquidBRL || 0, // ✅ BRL
        campaignStatus
      ),
    [
      realData.spend,
      realData.conversions,
      product.commissionLiquidBRL, // ✅ Adicionar dependência
      campaignStatus,
    ]
  );

  const excellentPerformance =
    metrics.cr >= product.requiredCR && metrics.ctr >= 3 && metrics.roas >= 2;

  // --- HANDLERS ---

  // 🌍 HANDLER ATUALIZADO: Salvar com Snapshot de Moeda
  const handleSaveToHistory = () => {
    if (realData.spend === 0) {
      alert("⚠️ Adicione dados de gasto antes de salvar!");
      return;
    }

    // 🌍 Calcular valores na moeda original
    const revenueInBRL = revenue; // Já está convertido pelo useMemo
    const originalRevenue =
      product.market !== "BR" ? revenue / currentExchangeRate : revenue;

    const campaignData: Campaign = {
      id: Date.now(),
      date: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
      productId: product.id,
      productName: product.name,
      nicho: product.nicho,
      spend: realData.spend, // Sempre em BRL
      clicks: realData.clicks,
      conversions: realData.conversions,
      revenue: revenueInBRL, // Sempre em BRL para cálculos
      cpc: metrics.cpc,
      cr: metrics.cr,
      roas: metrics.roas,
      roi: metrics.roi,
      profit: metrics.profit,

      // 🌍 SNAPSHOT DE MOEDA (NOVO)
      originalCurrency: product.market,
      exchangeRate: currentExchangeRate,
      originalRevenue: originalRevenue,
    };

    // ✅ Adicionar breakdown se estiver ativo
    if (useBreakdown && conversionBreakdown.length > 0) {
      campaignData.conversionBreakdown = conversionBreakdown.filter(
        (item) => item.quantity > 0
      );
    }

    onAddToHistory(campaignData);

    const btn = document.getElementById("save-history-btn");
    if (btn) {
      btn.classList.add("scale-95", "bg-green-600");
      setTimeout(() => {
        btn.classList.remove("scale-95", "bg-green-600");
        alert("✅ Campanha registrada no histórico!");
      }, 200);
    }
  };

  const copyPrompt = () => {
    const prompt = `VOCÊ DEVE RESPONDER EXCLUSIVAMENTE EM PORTUGUÊS BRASILEIRO. NUNCA USE INGLÊS.

Analise estes resultados de campanha Google Ads e gere sugestões. Retorne APENAS JSON EM PORTUGUÊS:
CPC Real: R$ ${metrics.cpc.toFixed(2)} | CR Real: ${metrics.cr.toFixed(
      2
    )}% | ROAS: ${metrics.roas.toFixed(2)}
Meta CPC: R$ ${product.cpcSuggested.toFixed(
      2
    )} | Meta CR: ${product.requiredCR.toFixed(2)}%
Produto: ${product.name} (${product.nicho})

IMPORTANTE: Todos os textos devem estar em PORTUGUÊS BRASILEIRO.
RETORNE JSON: { "diagnostico": "texto em português", "sugestoes": [{ "priority": "ALTA", "area": "CPC", "action": "texto em português", "details": "texto em português", "resultado": "texto em português" }] }`;
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(prompt);

    const copyBtn = document.getElementById("copy-prompt-btn");
    if (copyBtn) {
      const originalText = copyBtn.innerHTML;
      copyBtn.innerHTML =
        '<span class="flex items-center gap-1"><svg class="w-3 h-3" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 20 20"><path d="M16.707 5.293a1 1 0 010 1.414l-8 8a1 1 0 01-1.414 0l-4-4a1 1 0 011.414-1.414L8 12.586l7.293-7.293a1 1 0 011.414 0z"/></svg> Copiado!</span>';
      setTimeout(() => {
        copyBtn.innerHTML = originalText;
      }, 2000);
    }
  };

  const processAIResponse = () => {
    try {
      const json = JSON.parse(aiJsonInput);
      if (json.diagnostico) {
        setAiReport(json);
        setShowAIModal(false);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      alert("❌ Erro ao processar JSON. Verifique o formato.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500 pb-10">
      {/* --- 1. BARRA DE DADOS DO PRODUTO (HEADER) --- */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm grid grid-cols-5 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <DollarSign size={10} /> Comis. Líq.
          </div>
          <div className="font-bold text-green-600 text-lg">
            R$ {(product.commissionLiquidBRL || 0).toFixed(2)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-slate-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-100 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <TrendingUp size={10} /> CPC Médio
          </div>
          <div className="font-bold text-slate-900 text-lg">
            R$ {product.cpcSuggested.toFixed(2)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-green-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-green-100 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-green-700 uppercase font-bold mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Crosshair size={10} /> Meta CPC Bom
          </div>
          <div className="font-bold text-green-700 text-lg">
            R$ {product.cpcBom.toFixed(2)}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-cyan-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-cyan-100 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-cyan-700 uppercase font-bold mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Flame size={10} /> Poder Fogo
          </div>
          <div className="font-bold text-cyan-700 text-lg">
            {product.clicksPurchasable}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-purple-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-purple-100 hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
          <div className="text-[10px] text-purple-700 uppercase font-bold mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Target size={10} /> Meta Conv.
          </div>
          <div className="font-bold text-purple-700 text-lg">
            {product.requiredCR.toFixed(2)}%
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* --- 2. ÁREA DE INPUTS ORGANIZADA --- */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {/* COLUNA ESQUERDA: INPUTS DE DADOS */}
        <div className="col-span-2 bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
              <BarChart3 className="text-blue-600" size={20} /> Dados da
              Campanha
              {lastCampaign && (
                <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-500 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-blue-200">
                  📅 Últimos dados:{" "}
                  {new Date(lastCampaign.date).toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}
                </span>
              )}
            </h3>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              {/* NOVO: Seletor de Período com Customização */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">
                  Período:
                </span>
                <div className="flex gap-1 items-center">
                  {[1, 7, 15, 30].map((days) => (
                    <button
                      key={days}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setSelectedPeriod(days);
                        setCustomPeriod(""); // Limpa input customizado
                      }}
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                        selectedPeriod === days && customPeriod === ""
                          ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md"
                          : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-200"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {days}d
                    </button>
                  ))}

                  {/* Divisor */}
                  <div className="h-6 w-px bg-gray-300 mx-1"></div>

                  {/* Input Customizado */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      min="1"
                      max="365"
                      placeholder="X"
                      value={customPeriod}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const val = e.target.value;
                        setCustomPeriod(val);
                        if (val && parseInt(val) > 0) {
                          setSelectedPeriod(parseInt(val));
                        }
                      }}
                      className={`w-12 px-2 py-1.5 border rounded text-xs font-bold text-center transition-all ${
                        customPeriod !== ""
                          ? "border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700"
                          : "border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-600"
                      } focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none`}
                    />
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">
                      dias
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Status (já existia) */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">
                  Status:
                </span>
                <select
                  className="p-1.5 border border-gray-200 rounded text-xs font-bold bg-white cursor-pointer outline-none focus:border-blue-500 transition-colors text-gray-700"
                  value={campaignStatus}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setCampaignStatus(e.target.value as CampaignStatus)
                  }
                >
                  <option value="aprendizado">🔵 Aprendizado</option>
                  <option value="qualificada">🟢 Qualificada</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-8">
            {/* Grupo Tráfego */}
            <div className="space-y-4 border-r border-gray-100 pr-6">
              <h4 className="text-xs font-black text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                <MousePointer2 size={12} /> Tráfego (Funil)
              </h4>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-1 block">
                  Impressões
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none transition-all hover:border-blue-300 bg-white text-gray-900"
                  placeholder="0"
                  value={realData.impressions || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setRealData({
                      ...realData,
                      impressions: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-1 block">
                  Cliques
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  className="w-full p-2.5 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none transition-all hover:border-blue-300 bg-white text-gray-900"
                  placeholder="0"
                  value={realData.clicks || ""}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setRealData({
                      ...realData,
                      clicks: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                    })
                  }
                />
              </div>
              <div className="pt-2">
                <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">
                  CTR Calculado
                </div>
                <div
                  className={`text-xl font-bold transition-colors ${
                    metrics.ctr >= 5
                      ? "text-green-600"
                      : metrics.ctr >= 2
                      ? "text-yellow-600"
                      : "text-red-600"
                  }`}
                >
                  {metrics.ctr.toFixed(2)}%
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Grupo Financeiro */}
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <h4 className="text-xs font-black text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                <Wallet size={12} /> Financeiro & Conversão
              </h4>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 mb-1 block">
                    Gasto (R$)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    className="w-full p-2.5 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500 outline-none transition-all hover:border-red-300 bg-white text-gray-900"
                    placeholder="0.00"
                    value={realData.spend || ""}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setRealData({
                        ...realData,
                        spend: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Conversões com breakdown opcional */}
              <div>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                  <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600">
                    Conversões (Vendas)
                  </label>
                  {product.offers && product.offers.length > 1 && (
                    <button
                      onClick={() => setUseBreakdown(!useBreakdown)}
                      className={`text-[10px] px-2 py-1 rounded font-bold transition-all ${
                        useBreakdown
                          ? "bg-purple-600 text-white"
                          : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-300"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {useBreakdown ? "📊 Detalhado" : "📝 Simples"}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>

                {!useBreakdown ? (
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    className="w-full p-2.5 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none transition-all hover:border-purple-300 bg-white text-gray-900"
                    placeholder="0"
                    value={realData.conversions || ""}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setRealData({
                        ...realData,
                        conversions: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                      })
                    }
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2 bg-purple-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-purple-700 font-bold uppercase mb-2">
                      Distribuição por Oferta
                    </div>
                    {conversionBreakdown.map((item) => (
                      <div
                        key={item.offerId}
                        className="flex items-center gap-2"
                      >
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-700 font-medium">
                          {item.offerName}
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 ml-1">
                            (R${" "}
                            {convertToBRL(
                              item.commission,
                              product.market,
                              rates
                            ).toFixed(2)}
                            )
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          min="0"
                          className="w-20 p-1.5 border border-purple-300 rounded text-sm font-bold text-center focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none"
                          value={item.quantity || ""}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            updateBreakdownQuantity(
                              item.offerId,
                              parseInt(e.target.value) || 0
                            )
                          }
                        />
                      </div>
                    ))}
                    <div className="pt-2 mt-2 border-t border-purple-300 flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-purple-900">
                      <span>TOTAL:</span>
                      <span>{totalBreakdownConversions} vendas</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="pt-2 bg-green-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-green-200 mt-3">
                <div className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-600 uppercase">
                  Receita Calculada
                </div>
                <div className="text-xl font-black text-green-700">
                  R$ {revenue.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
                {useBreakdown && conversionBreakdown.length > 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-[9px] text-green-600 mt-1 space-y-0.5">
                    {conversionBreakdown
                      .filter((item) => item.quantity > 0)
                      .map((item) => (
                        <div key={item.offerId}>
                          {item.quantity}× {item.offerName} = R${" "}
                          {convertToBRL(
                            item.revenue,
                            product.market,
                            rates
                          ).toFixed(2)}
                        </div>
                      ))}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="text-[9px] text-green-600 mt-1">
                    {realData.conversions} × R${" "}
                    {(product.commissionLiquidBRL || 0).toFixed(2)}
                  </div>
                )}

                {product.market !== "BR" && (
                  <div className="text-[9px] text-green-600 mt-1 italic border-t border-green-200 pt-1">
                    Taxa: {getCurrencySymbol(product.market)}1 = R${" "}
                    {currentExchangeRate.toFixed(2)}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-gray-100 flex justify-end">
            <button
              id="save-history-btn"
              onClick={handleSaveToHistory}
              className="bg-slate-800 text-white px-5 py-2.5 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-slate-900 transition-all flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transform active:scale-95 duration-200"
            >
              <Save size={18} /> Registrar no Histórico
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* COLUNA DIREITA: VEREDITO & ALERTAS */}
        <div className="space-y-6">
          {/* Badge de Performance Destacada */}
          {excellentPerformance && realData.conversions > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-orange-400 p-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg animate-pulse">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-white font-bold">
                <Award size={20} />
                <span>🎉 Performance Excepcional!</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-white/90 mt-1">
                CR e CTR acima da meta!
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Card de Veredito */}
          {realData.spend > 0 ? (
            <div
              className={`p-6 rounded-xl border-l-8 shadow-md h-fit ${verdict.colorClass} bg-white transition-all duration-500`}
            >
              <h2
                className={`text-xl font-black flex items-center gap-2 ${verdict.textColorClass} mb-2`}
              >
                {verdict.emoji} {verdict.title}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
                {verdict.action}
              </p>

              <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-2">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-gray-400">
                  Custo/Comissão
                </span>
                <span
                  className={`text-2xl font-black ${verdict.textColorClass}`}
                >
                  {verdict.ratioPct.toFixed(0)}%
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className={`h-full ${verdict.barColorClass} transition-all duration-1000 ease-out`}
                  style={{ width: `${Math.min(verdict.ratioPct, 100)}%` }}
                ></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-gray-50 border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200 p-6 rounded-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center h-full min-h-[200px]">
              <BarChart3 className="text-gray-300 mb-2" size={40} />
              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm font-medium">
                Insira os dados de Gasto e Receita para gerar o veredito.
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Alertas */}
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-3">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <Bell size={16} /> Alertas de Performance
              </h4>
              <span className="text-[10px] bg-slate-100 px-2 py-1 rounded text-slate-500 font-mono">
                {activeAlerts.length} ativo
                {activeAlerts.length !== 1 ? "s" : ""}
              </span>
            </div>
            {activeAlerts.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {activeAlerts.map((alert, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={i}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs bg-red-50 p-3 rounded border border-red-200 font-semibold text-red-700 animate-in slide-in-from-left duration-300"
                  >
                    <AlertTriangle size={14} className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                    <span className="leading-relaxed">{alert}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="text-center py-4 text-gray-400 text-xs">
                <CheckCircle2
                  size={20}
