                  className="mx-auto mb-1 text-green-400"
                />
                <div className="font-semibold">Tudo dentro do esperado!</div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">
                  Nenhum alerta de performance
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* --- 3. FUNIL VERTICAL ESPETACULAR (LARGURA TOTAL) --- */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-slate-100 p-8 rounded-xl shadow-lg border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden relative">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">
          <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-xl flex items-center gap-2">
            <Filter size={24} className="text-indigo-600" /> Funil de Conversão
            Dinâmico
          </h4>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
            {funnelData.ctrBottleneck && (
              <span className="bg-red-100 text-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
                <AlertCircle size={14} /> CTR Baixo
              </span>
            )}
            {funnelData.crBottleneck && (
              <span className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-1 shadow-sm">
                <AlertCircle size={14} /> CR Baixo
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-start justify-center w-full relative gap-12">
          {/* COLUNA ESQUERDA: INDICADORES DE PERDA */}
          <div
            className="flex flex-col justify-start gap-12 w-56"
            style={{ marginTop: "200px" }}
          >
            {funnelData.lostClicks > 0 && (
              <div className="bg-white border-2 border-red-200 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition-all">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                  <TrendingDown className="text-red-500" size={18} />
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-red-600">
                    Perda no CTR
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-red-700">
                  {funnelData.lostClicks.toLocaleString()}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-red-600 font-semibold mt-1">
                  {funnelData.ctrLossPercent.toFixed(1)}% não clicaram
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {funnelData.lostConversions > 0 && (
              <div
                className="bg-white border-2 border-orange-200 rounded-xl p-4 shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition-all"
                style={{ marginTop: "80px" }}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                  <TrendingDown className="text-orange-500" size={18} />
                  <span className="text-sm font-bold text-orange-600">
                    Perda no CR
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-orange-700">
                  {funnelData.lostConversions}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-orange-600 font-semibold mt-1">
                  {funnelData.crLossPercent.toFixed(1)}% não converteram
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* COLUNA CENTRAL: FUNIL */}
          <div
            className="flex flex-col items-center justify-start flex-1 relative"
            style={{ minHeight: "600px", maxWidth: "800px" }}
          >
            {/* ETAPA 1: IMPRESSÕES */}
            <div
              className="relative w-full flex flex-col items-center mb-6 group cursor-pointer"
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSection("impressions")}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredSection(null)}
            >
              <div
                className="relative bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-400 via-indigo-500 to-indigo-600 rounded-t-3xl shadow-2xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-700 ease-out overflow-hidden"
                style={{ width: "100%", height: "160px" }}
              >
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-white/30 to-transparent animate-pulse"></div>

                <div className="relative z-10 text-center">
                  <div className="text-5xl font-black text-white mb-2">
                    {realData.impressions > 0
                      ? `${(realData.impressions / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`
                      : "0"}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white/90 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Impressões
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {hoveredSection === "impressions" && (
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/98 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-t-3xl flex items-center justify-center z-30 shadow-2xl border-4 border-indigo-400 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
                  <div className="text-center px-8 py-6">
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-900 mb-3">
                      📊 IMPRESSÕES
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-base text-gray-700 mb-2 leading-relaxed max-w-md">
                      Quantas vezes seu anúncio foi exibido para o público-alvo
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-gray-500 italic">
                      Alcance total da campanha no Google Ads
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* CONECTOR 1: CTR */}
            <div
              className="relative flex flex-col items-center my-3 z-20 cursor-pointer group"
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSection("ctr")}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredSection(null)}
            >
              <div
                className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full font-bold text-base shadow-lg relative ${
                  funnelData.ctrBottleneck
                    ? "bg-red-500 text-white animate-pulse"
                    : "bg-white text-blue-600 border-2 border-blue-300"
                }`}
              >
                <Info size={14} className="inline mr-1.5" /> CTR:{" "}
                {metrics.ctr.toFixed(2)}%
              </div>

              {hoveredSection === "ctr" && (
                <div className="absolute top-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 mt-3 bg-white text-gray-800 p-5 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border-2 border-blue-300 w-80 z-40 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
                  <div className="font-black text-blue-700 mb-3 text-base">
                    📊 CTR (Click-Through Rate)
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm text-gray-700 mb-3 leading-relaxed">
                    Porcentagem de pessoas que <strong>clicaram</strong> no
                    anúncio após vê-lo
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-mono text-blue-900 mb-3 border border-blue-200">
                    CTR = (Cliques ÷ Impressões) × 100
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
                    <span className="font-bold text-green-600">✅ Bom:</span> ≥
                    5% •
                    <span className="font-bold text-yellow-600">
                      {" "}
                      ⚠️ Médio:
                    </span>{" "}
                    2-5% •
                    <span className="font-bold text-red-600">
                      {" "}
                      🚨 Ruim:
                    </span>{" "}
                    &lt; 2%
                  </div>
                  <div className="absolute -top-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-4 h-4 bg-white border-l-2 border-t-2 border-blue-300 rotate-45"></div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="relative w-1 h-10 bg-gradient-to-b from-indigo-400 to-blue-400 my-2 rounded-full">
                {realData.clicks > 0 && (
                  <>
                    <Droplets
                      size={14}
                      className="absolute -left-2 top-0 text-blue-400 animate-bounce"
                      style={{ animationDelay: "0s" }}
                    />
                    <Droplets
                      size={12}
                      className="absolute -left-1.5 top-3 text-blue-300 animate-bounce"
                      style={{ animationDelay: "0.3s" }}
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ETAPA 2: CLIQUES */}
            <div
              className="relative w-full flex flex-col items-center mb-6 group cursor-pointer"
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSection("clicks")}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredSection(null)}
            >
              <div
                className={`relative bg-gradient-to-b ${funnelData.ctrColor} shadow-2xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-700 ease-out overflow-hidden`}
                style={{
                  width: `${funnelData.middleWidth}%`,
                  height: "140px",
                  clipPath: "polygon(3% 0%, 97% 0%, 100% 100%, 0% 100%)",
                  borderRadius: "8px",
                }}
              >
                {funnelData.ctrBottleneck && (
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-40">
                    <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-3 bg-red-600 animate-pulse"></div>
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 w-full h-3 bg-red-600 animate-pulse"></div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="relative z-10 text-center">
                  <div className="text-4xl font-black text-white mb-2">
                    {realData.clicks}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white/90 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Cliques
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {hoveredSection === "clicks" && (
                <div
                  className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/98 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-30 shadow-2xl border-4 border-blue-400 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200 rounded-lg"
                  style={{
                    clipPath: "polygon(3% 0%, 97% 0%, 100% 100%, 0% 100%)",
                  }}
                >
                  <div className="text-center px-8 py-6">
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-blue-900 mb-3">
                      👆 CLIQUES
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-base text-gray-700 mb-2 leading-relaxed max-w-md">
                      Usuários que <strong>clicaram</strong> no seu anúncio e
                      visitaram sua página
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-gray-600 font-mono bg-gray-100 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg inline-block">
                      CPC Atual: R$ {metrics.cpc.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* CONECTOR 2: CR */}
            <div
              className="relative flex flex-col items-center my-3 z-20 cursor-pointer group"
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSection("cr")}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredSection(null)}
            >
              <div
                className={`px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full font-bold text-base shadow-lg relative ${
                  funnelData.crBottleneck
                    ? "bg-orange-500 text-white animate-pulse"
                    : "bg-white text-emerald-600 border-2 border-emerald-300"
                }`}
              >
                <Info size={14} className="inline mr-1.5" /> CR:{" "}
                {metrics.cr.toFixed(2)}%
              </div>

              {hoveredSection === "cr" && (
                <div className="absolute top-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 mt-3 bg-white text-gray-800 p-5 rounded-xl shadow-2xl border-2 border-emerald-300 w-80 z-40 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200">
                  <div className="font-black text-emerald-700 mb-3 text-base">
                    🎯 CR (Conversion Rate)
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm text-gray-700 mb-3 leading-relaxed">
                    Porcentagem de cliques que resultaram em{" "}
                    <strong>vendas confirmadas</strong>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-emerald-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-mono text-emerald-900 mb-3 border border-emerald-200">
                    CR = (Conversões ÷ Cliques) × 100
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
                    <span className="font-bold text-green-600">✅ Meta:</span> ≥{" "}
                    {product.requiredCR.toFixed(1)}% •
                    <span className="font-bold text-orange-600">
                      {" "}
                      ⚠️ Atenção:
                    </span>{" "}
                    &lt; {(product.requiredCR * 0.5).toFixed(1)}%
                  </div>
                  <div className="absolute -top-2 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-4 h-4 bg-white border-l-2 border-t-2 border-emerald-300 rotate-45"></div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="relative w-1 h-10 bg-gradient-to-b from-blue-400 to-emerald-400 my-2 rounded-full">
                {realData.conversions > 0 && (
                  <>
                    <Droplets
                      size={14}
                      className="absolute -left-2 top-0 text-emerald-400 animate-bounce"
                      style={{ animationDelay: "0s" }}
                    />
                    <Droplets
                      size={12}
                      className="absolute -left-1.5 top-4 text-emerald-300 animate-bounce"
                      style={{ animationDelay: "0.4s" }}
                    />
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* ETAPA 3: CONVERSÕES */}
            <div
              className="relative w-full flex flex-col items-center group cursor-pointer"
              onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredSection("conversions")}
              onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredSection(null)}
            >
              <div
                className={`relative bg-gradient-to-b ${funnelData.crColor} rounded-b-3xl shadow-2xl flex items-center justify-center transition-all duration-700 ease-out overflow-hidden`}
                style={{
                  width: `${funnelData.bottomWidth}%`,
                  height: "120px",
                  clipPath: "polygon(8% 0%, 92% 0%, 100% 100%, 0% 100%)",
                }}
              >
                {metrics.cr >= product.requiredCR &&
                  realData.conversions > 0 && (
                    <div className="absolute inset-0">
                      <div className="absolute top-3 left-1/4 w-3 h-3 bg-yellow-300 rounded-full animate-ping"></div>
                      <div
                        className="absolute top-5 right-1/4 w-2 h-2 bg-yellow-200 rounded-full animate-ping"
                        style={{ animationDelay: "0.5s" }}
                      ></div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                {funnelData.crBottleneck && (
                  <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-40">
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-red-600 animate-pulse"></div>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div className="relative z-10 text-center">
                  <div className="text-4xl font-black text-white mb-2">
                    {realData.conversions}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-sm font-bold text-white/90 uppercase tracking-wider">
                    Vendas
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {hoveredSection === "conversions" && (
                <div
                  className="absolute inset-0 bg-white/98 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-b-3xl flex items-center justify-center z-30 shadow-2xl border-4 border-emerald-400 animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-200"
                  style={{
                    clipPath: "polygon(8% 0%, 92% 0%, 100% 100%, 0% 100%)",
                  }}
                >
                  <div className="text-center px-8 py-6">
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-900 mb-3">
                      💰 CONVERSÕES
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-base text-gray-700 mb-2 leading-relaxed max-w-md">
                      Vendas <strong>confirmadas</strong> geradas pela campanha
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-sm text-gray-600 font-mono bg-gray-100 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg inline-block">
                      CPA: R$ {metrics.cpa.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Partículas fluindo */}
            {particles.map((particle) => (
              <div
                key={particle.id}
                className="absolute top-0 w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-blue-400 rounded-full opacity-60 pointer-events-none shadow-lg"
                style={{
                  left: `${particle.x}%`,
                  animation: `dropFall 3.5s linear forwards`,
                  animationDelay: `${particle.delay}s`,
                }}
              />
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Legenda do funil */}
        <div className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t-2 border-gray-200 text-center">
          <div className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-3 font-medium">
            🎨 Largura proporcional ao desempenho • Cores indicam saúde da
            métrica
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-center gap-6 text-xs">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400 to-green-600 rounded shadow-sm"></div>
              <span className="text-gray-700 font-semibold">Excelente</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-yellow-400 to-yellow-600 rounded shadow-sm"></div>
              <span className="text-gray-700 font-semibold">Médio</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-red-400 to-red-600 rounded shadow-sm"></div>
              <span className="text-gray-700 font-semibold">Crítico</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <style>{`
            @keyframes dropFall {
              0% { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 0.7; }
              100% { transform: translateY(600px); opacity: 0; }
            }
          `}</style>
      </div>

      {/* --- 4. TERMÔMETRO CPC HORIZONTAL --- */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
            <Activity className="text-blue-600" size={20} /> Termômetro de CPC
          </h4>
          {metrics.cpc > 0 && (
            <div
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
                gaugeData.cpcStatus === "excellent"
                  ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                  : gaugeData.cpcStatus === "good"
                  ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700"
                  : gaugeData.cpcStatus === "warning"
                  ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700"
                  : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
              }`}
            >
              {gaugeData.cpcStatus === "excellent"
                ? "🎯 EXCELENTE"
                : gaugeData.cpcStatus === "good"
                ? "✅ BOM"
                : gaugeData.cpcStatus === "warning"
                ? "⚠️ ATENÇÃO"
                : "🚨 CRÍTICO"}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="relative h-16 w-full mt-2 mb-10">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex opacity-90 shadow-inner">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 to-emerald-500 relative"
              style={{
                width: `${(product.cpcBom / gaugeData.maxVisualCPC) * 100}%`,
              }}
              title="Bom"
            ></div>
            <div
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-yellow-500"
              style={{
                width: `${
                  ((product.cpcInter - product.cpcBom) /
                    gaugeData.maxVisualCPC) *
                  100
                }%`,
              }}
              title="Médio"
            ></div>
            <div
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-400 to-orange-500"
              style={{
                width: `${
                  ((product.cpcApert - product.cpcInter) /
                    gaugeData.maxVisualCPC) *
                  100
                }%`,
              }}
              title="Apertado"
            ></div>
            <div
              className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-red-600 flex-1"
              title="Ruim"
            ></div>
          </div>

          <div className="absolute -bottom-8 left-0 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">
            R$ 0
          </div>
          <div
            className="absolute -bottom-8 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700 transform -translate-x-1/2 bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-emerald-200"
            style={{
              left: `${(product.cpcBom / gaugeData.maxVisualCPC) * 100}%`,
            }}
          >
            R$ {product.cpcBom.toFixed(2)}
            <div className="text-[9px] text-emerald-600">CPC Bom</div>
          </div>
          <div
            className="absolute -bottom-8 text-xs font-bold text-yellow-700 transform -translate-x-1/2 bg-yellow-50 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-yellow-200"
            style={{
              left: `${(product.cpcInter / gaugeData.maxVisualCPC) * 100}%`,
            }}
          >
            R$ {product.cpcInter.toFixed(2)}
            <div className="text-[9px] text-yellow-600">CPC Inter</div>
          </div>
          <div
            className="absolute -bottom-8 text-xs font-bold text-orange-700 transform -translate-x-1/2 bg-orange-50 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-orange-200"
            style={{
              left: `${(product.cpcApert / gaugeData.maxVisualCPC) * 100}%`,
            }}
          >
            R$ {product.cpcApert.toFixed(2)}
            <div className="text-[9px] text-orange-600">CPC Apert</div>
          </div>
          <div className="absolute -bottom-8 right-0 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500">
            R$ {gaugeData.maxVisualCPC.toFixed(2)}
          </div>

          {metrics.cpc > 0 && (
            <div
              className="absolute top-[-10px] h-[calc(100%+20px)] w-2 bg-slate-900 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(0,0,0,0.6)] transition-all duration-700 ease-out z-10 rounded-full"
              style={{ left: `${gaugeData.needlePosition}%` }}
            >
              <div className="absolute -top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-slate-900 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-lg whitespace-nowrap shadow-xl">
                R$ {metrics.cpc.toFixed(2)}
                <div className="absolute -bottom-1.5 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-3 h-3 bg-slate-900 rotate-45"></div>
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -top-3 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-4 h-4 bg-slate-900 rounded-full">
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-slate-900 rounded-full animate-ping opacity-75"></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="text-center text-sm text-gray-500 mt-6">
          Seu CPC Real comparado às faixas de comissão do produto
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* --- 5. KPIS FINAIS (ESTATÍSTICAS) --- */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {/* KPI ROAS */}
        <div
          className={`p-4 rounded-xl border transition-all hover:scale-105 relative overflow-hidden ${
            metrics.roas >= alertConfig.roasMin
              ? "bg-white border-slate-200 hover:shadow-lg"
              : "bg-red-50 border-red-200 hover:shadow-red-200"
          }`}
        >
          {metrics.roas >= alertConfig.roasMin * 1.5 && (
            <div className="absolute top-1 right-1">
              <Award className="text-yellow-500" size={16} />
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <TrendingUp size={10} /> ROAS Real
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-2xl font-black ${
              metrics.roas >= alertConfig.roasMin
                ? "text-gray-800"
                : "text-red-600"
            }`}
          >
            {metrics.roas.toFixed(2)}x
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">
            Meta: {alertConfig.roasMin}x
          </div>
          <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-1 mt-2">
            <div
              className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ${
                metrics.roas >= alertConfig.roasMin
                  ? "bg-green-500"
                  : "bg-red-500"
              }`}
              style={{
                width: `${Math.min(
                  (metrics.roas / (alertConfig.roasMin * 2)) * 100,
                  100
                )}%`,
              }}
            ></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI ROI */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden">
          {metrics.roi > 100 && (
            <div className="absolute top-1 right-1">
              <Award className="text-yellow-500" size={16} />
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <DollarSign size={10} /> ROI (%)
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-2xl font-black ${
              metrics.roi > 0 ? "text-blue-600" : "text-red-600"
            }`}
          >
            {metrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
          </div>
          <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">
            Retorno sobre Invest.
          </div>
          <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-1 mt-2">
            <div
              className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ${
                metrics.roi > 0 ? "bg-blue-500" : "bg-red-500"
              }`}
              style={{ width: `${Math.min(Math.abs(metrics.roi) / 2, 100)}%` }}
            ></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI LUCRO LÍQUIDO COM EXPLICAÇÃO */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden group">
          {metrics.profit > revenue * 0.3 && metrics.profit > 0 && (
            <div className="absolute top-1 right-1">
              <Award className="text-yellow-500" size={16} />
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Wallet size={10} /> Lucro Líquido
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-2xl font-black ${
              metrics.profit > 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-600"
            }`}
          >
            R$ {metrics.profit.toFixed(2)}
          </div>

          {/* Tooltip explicativo */}
          <div className="text-[9px] text-gray-500 mt-1 flex items-center gap-1 cursor-help">
            <Info size={10} />
            <span>
              Após Ads + Custos Fixos ({selectedPeriod} dia
              {selectedPeriod > 1 ? "s" : ""})
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Detalhamento oculto - aparece no hover */}
          <div className="absolute bottom-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 mb-2 bg-slate-800 text-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-200 pointer-events-none w-64 text-xs z-50">
            <div className="font-bold mb-2 text-center">
              💰 Cálculo do Lucro
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>Receita:</span>
                <span className="text-green-400">
                  + R$ {revenue.toFixed(2)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>Gasto Ads:</span>
                <span className="text-red-400">
                  - R$ {realData.spend.toFixed(2)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-600 pt-1">
                <span className="text-[10px]">
                  Custos Fixos ({selectedPeriod}d):
                </span>
                <span className="text-red-400">
                  - R${" "}
                  {(
                    (fixedCostsTotal / 30 / Math.max(activeProductsCount, 1) +
                      (product.specificCosts?.total || 0) / 30) *
                    selectedPeriod
                  ).toFixed(2)}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between border-t border-slate-600 pt-1 font-bold">
                <span>Lucro Líquido:</span>
                <span
                  className={
                    metrics.profit > 0 ? "text-green-400" : "text-red-400"
                  }
                >
                  R$ {metrics.profit.toFixed(2)}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            {/* Setinha do tooltip */}
            <div className="absolute top-full left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -mt-1 w-3 h-3 bg-slate-800 rotate-45"></div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* KPI CUSTO POR VENDA (CPA) */}
        <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden">
          {realData.conversions > 0 &&
            product.commissionLiquid - metrics.cpa >
              product.commissionLiquid * 0.5 && (
              <div className="absolute top-1 right-1">
                <Award className="text-yellow-500" size={16} />
              </div>
            )}
          <div className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 flex items-center gap-1">
            <Target size={10} /> CPA (Custo/Venda)
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-gray-800">
            R$ {realData.conversions > 0 ? metrics.cpa.toFixed(2) : "0.00"}
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-[10px] mt-1 font-bold ${
              realData.conversions > 0 &&
              (product.commissionLiquidBRL || 0) - metrics.cpa > 0
                ? "text-green-600"
                : "text-red-600"
            }`}
          >
            Margem: R${" "}
            {realData.conversions > 0
              ? ((product.commissionLiquidBRL || 0) - metrics.cpa).toFixed(2)
              : "0.00"}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* BOTÃO IA */}
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowAIModal(true)}
          className="bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 hover:from-indigo-700 hover:to-purple-700 text-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-lg shadow-indigo-200 transition-all hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 group relative overflow-hidden"
        >
          <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-20 transition-opacity"></div>
          <BrainCircuit
            size={24}
            className="group-hover:animate-pulse relative z-10"
          />
          <span className="font-bold text-sm relative z-10">Análise de IA</span>
          {aiReport && (
            <div className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-2 h-2 bg-green-400 rounded-full animate-pulse"></div>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* --- MODAL IA --- */}
      {showAIModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 animate-in fade-in duration-300">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-4xl h-[85vh] flex flex-col overflow-hidden animate-in zoom-in duration-300">
            <div className="p-4 border-b bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 flex justify-between items-center">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-indigo-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <BrainCircuit className="animate-pulse" /> Consultor IA
              </h3>
              <button
                onClick={() => setShowAIModal(false)}
                className="hover:bg-red-100 p-1 rounded transition-colors"
              >
                <X className="text-gray-500 hover:text-red-500" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 space-y-6">
              {!aiReport ? (
                <div className="space-y-4">
                  <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-50 to-orange-50 p-4 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-yellow-400">
                    <strong className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2 text-yellow-900 text-sm">
                      <Zap size={16} /> Instruções:
                    </strong>
                    <p className="text-yellow-800 text-sm">
                      Copie o prompt abaixo, cole no ChatGPT/Claude, e traga a
                      resposta JSON de volta.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 z-10">
                      <button
                        id="copy-prompt-btn"
                        onClick={copyPrompt}
                        className="text-xs bg-slate-800 text-white px-3 py-1.5 rounded hover:bg-black flex items-center gap-1 transition-all hover:scale-105"
                      >
                        <Copy size={12} /> Copiar Prompt
                      </button>
                    </div>
                    <textarea
                      className="w-full h-32 p-4 bg-slate-900 text-slate-300 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono resize-none"
                      readOnly
                      value={`Analise estes resultados de campanha Google Ads e gere sugestões EM PORTUGUÊS BRASILEIRO. Retorne APENAS JSON:
CPC Real: R$ ${metrics.cpc.toFixed(2)} | CR Real: ${metrics.cr.toFixed(
                        2
                      )}% | ROAS: ${metrics.roas.toFixed(2)}
Meta CPC: R$ ${product.cpcSuggested.toFixed(
                        2
                      )} | Meta CR: ${product.requiredCR.toFixed(2)}%

IMPORTANTE: Todos os textos devem estar em PORTUGUÊS BRASILEIRO.
RETORNE JSON: { "diagnostico": "texto em português", "sugestoes": [{ "priority": "ALTA", "area": "CPC", "action": "texto em português", "details": "texto em português", "resultado": "texto em português" }] }`}
                    />
                  </div>
                  <textarea
                    className="w-full h-40 p-4 border-2 border-indigo-100 rounded-lg text-xs font-mono focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none transition-all bg-white text-gray-900"
                    placeholder="Cole o JSON da IA aqui..."
                    value={aiJsonInput}
                    onChange={(e) => setAiJsonInput(e.target.value)}
                  />
                  <button
                    onClick={processAIResponse}
                    className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-emerald-600 text-white py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:from-green-700 hover:to-emerald-700 flex justify-center items-center gap-2 transition-all hover:scale-105"
                  >
                    <Play size={18} /> Gerar Relatório
                  </button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-6">
                  <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50 to-cyan-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-l-4 border-blue-500 shadow-sm">
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-blue-800 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Search size={18} /> Diagnóstico
                    </h4>
                    <p className="text-blue-900 leading-relaxed text-sm">
                      {aiReport.diagnostico}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="space-y-3">
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-700 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Zap size={16} /> Sugestões de Otimização
                    </h4>
                    {aiReport.sugestoes.map((sug, idx) => (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className="border border-gray-200 rounded-lg p-4 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all bg-white"
                      >
                        <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                          <span className="font-bold text-gray-800">
                            {sug.area} - {sug.action}
                          </span>
                          <span
                            className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded uppercase ${
                              sug.priority.includes("ALTA")
                                ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                                : sug.priority.includes("MÉDIA")
                                ? "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700"
                                : "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {sug.priority}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-3 leading-relaxed">
                          {sug.details}
                        </p>
                        <div className="text-xs font-bold text-green-700 bg-green-50 p-2 rounded inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Target size={12} /> {sug.resultado}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => {
                      setAiReport(null);
                      setAiJsonInput("");
                    }}
                    className="text-center w-full text-gray-500 text-sm underline hover:text-gray-700 transition-colors"
                  >
                    ← Nova Análise
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: AnimatedNumber.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\AnimatedNumber.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';

interface AnimatedNumberProps {
  value: number;
  duration?: number; // Duração da animação em ms
  decimals?: number; // Casas decimais
  prefix?: string; // Ex: "R$ "
  suffix?: string; // Ex: "%"
  className?: string;
  formatThousands?: boolean; // Adicionar separador de milhares
}

export const AnimatedNumber: React.FC<AnimatedNumberProps> = ({
  value,
  duration = 800,
  decimals = 0,
  prefix = '',
  suffix = '',
  className = '',
  formatThousands = false
}) => {
  const [displayValue, setDisplayValue] = useState(value);
  const animationRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const startTimeRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const startValueRef = useRef(value);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Se o valor não mudou, não animar
    if (value === displayValue) return;

    startValueRef.current = displayValue;
    startTimeRef.current = null;

    const animate = (currentTime: number) => {
      if (startTimeRef.current === null) {
        startTimeRef.current = currentTime;
      }

      const elapsed = currentTime - startTimeRef.current;
      const progress = Math.min(elapsed / duration, 1);

      // Easing function (ease-out cubic)
      const easeOut = 1 - Math.pow(1 - progress, 3);

      const currentValue = startValueRef.current + (value - startValueRef.current) * easeOut;
      setDisplayValue(currentValue);

      if (progress < 1) {
        animationRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
      } else {
        setDisplayValue(value);
      }
    };

    animationRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);

    return () => {
      if (animationRef.current !== null) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(animationRef.current);
      }
    };
  }, [value, duration]);

  const formatNumber = (num: number) => {
    let formatted = num.toFixed(decimals);
    
    if (formatThousands) {
      const parts = formatted.split('.');
      parts[0] = parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, '.');
      formatted = parts.join(',');
    } else if (decimals > 0) {
      formatted = formatted.replace('.', ',');
    }
    
    return formatted;
  };

  return (
    <span className={className}>
      {prefix}
      {formatNumber(displayValue)}
      {suffix}
    </span>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from "react";

import {
  exportToExcel as generateExcel,
  type ExcelExportData,
} from "../utils/excelExport";
import { CostsManager } from "./shared/CostsManager";

type NichoFilter =
  | "todos"
  | "emagrecimento"
  | "fitness"
  | "saude"
  | "financas"
  | "marketing"
  | "relacionamento"
  | "espiritualidade"
  | "outro";
type StatusFilter = "todos" | "lucrativo" | "prejuizo" | "breakeven";
type PerformanceFilter = "todos" | "excelente" | "bom" | "atencao" | "critico";

interface AdvancedFilters {
  nicho: NichoFilter;
  status: StatusFilter;
  roiMinimo: number;
  performance: PerformanceFilter;
}

interface DashboardProps {
  products: Product[];
  history: Campaign[];
  fixedCosts: OperationalCosts;
  setFixedCosts: (costs: OperationalCosts) => void;

type PeriodFilter = "today" | "7days" | "30days" | "custom" | "all";

interface ProductPerformance {
  name: string;
  nicho: string;
  campaignCount: number;
  spend: number;
  revenue: number;
  profit: number;
  roi: number;
  conversions: number;
  clicks: number;
  cr: number;
  cpc: number;
  fixedCostsTotal: number;

}

interface TimelinePoint {

interface CalculationsResult {
  allProductPerformance: ProductPerformance[];
  alerts: Alert[];
  totalAdsSpend: number;
  totalAdsRevenue: number;
  totalRevenueGlobal: number;
  totalSpendGlobal: number;

  globalCR: number;
  globalCPC: number;
  avgTicket: number;

  totalConversions: number;
  totalClicks: number;

  timelineData: TimelinePoint[];
  burnRate: number;

  projectedSpend: number;
  projectedProfit: number;
  healthScore: number;

interface DashboardSettings {

const DASHBOARD_SETTINGS_KEY = "dashboard_settings";

// ADICIONAR APÓS A LINHA ~140 (antes de "const isSameDay")

const isSameDay = (d1: Date, d2: Date): boolean =>
  d1.getFullYear() === d2.getFullYear() &&
  d1.getMonth() === d2.getMonth() &&
  d1.getDate() === d2.getDate();

const loadDashboardSettings = (): DashboardSettings => {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") {
    return { adsenseRev: 0, monthlyTarget: 10000 };
  }

  try {
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(DASHBOARD_SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      return {
        adsenseRev: Number(parsed.adsenseRev) || 0,
        monthlyTarget: Number(parsed.monthlyTarget) || 10000,
      };
    }

    const ads =
      Number(window.localStorage.getItem("dashboard_adsense_rev")) || 0;
    const target =
      Number(window.localStorage.getItem("dashboard_monthly_target")) || 10000;

    return { adsenseRev: ads, monthlyTarget: target };
  } catch {
    return { adsenseRev: 0, monthlyTarget: 10000 };
  }
};

const MemoBarChart = React.memo(BarChart);
const MemoPieChart = React.memo(PieChart);

export const Dashboard: React.FC<DashboardProps> = ({
  products,
  history,
  fixedCosts,
  setFixedCosts,
  fixedCostsTotal,
}) => {
  const initialSettings = loadDashboardSettings();
  // ✅ NOVO: Hook de moedas
  const { rates, loading: currencyLoading } = useCurrency();

  const [adsenseRev, setAdsenseRev] = useState<number>(
    initialSettings.adsenseRev
  );
  const [selectedProducts, setSelectedProducts] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [periodFilter, setPeriodFilter] = useState<PeriodFilter>("7days");
  const [customDateStart, setCustomDateStart] = useState("");
  const [customDateEnd, setCustomDateEnd] = useState("");
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [sortField, setSortField] = useState<SortField>("profit");
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>("desc");
  const [monthlyTarget, setMonthlyTarget] = useState<number>(
    initialSettings.monthlyTarget
  );
  const [advancedFilters, setAdvancedFilters] = useState<AdvancedFilters>({
    nicho: "todos",
    status: "todos",
    roiMinimo: 0,
    performance: "todos",
  });
  const [showAdvancedFilters, setShowAdvancedFilters] = useState(true);

  const {
    config: dashboardConfig,
    applyPreset,
    toggleSection,
    resetToDefault,
    exportConfig,
    importConfig,
  } = useDashboardConfig();

  const [showSettings, setShowSettings] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
    const settings: DashboardSettings = {
      adsenseRev,
      monthlyTarget,
    };
    window.localStorage.setItem(
      DASHBOARD_SETTINGS_KEY,
      JSON.stringify(settings)
    );
  }, [adsenseRev, monthlyTarget]);

  const filteredHistory = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();

    const getDateDaysAgo = (days: number) => {
      const date = new Date();
      date.setDate(date.getDate() - days);
      return date;
    };

    return history.filter((campaign) => {
      const campaignDate = new Date(campaign.date);

      switch (periodFilter) {
        case "today":
          return isSameDay(campaignDate, now);

        case "7days":
          return campaignDate >= getDateDaysAgo(7);

        case "30days":
          return campaignDate >= getDateDaysAgo(30);

        case "custom": {
          if (!customDateStart || !customDateEnd) return true;
          const start = new Date(customDateStart);
          const end = new Date(customDateEnd);
          end.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
          return campaignDate >= start && campaignDate <= end;
        }

        case "all":
        default:
          return true;
      }
    });
  }, [history, periodFilter, customDateStart, customDateEnd]);

  let periodDays: number;

  switch (periodFilter) {
    case "today":
      periodDays = 1;
      break;

    case "7days":
      periodDays = 7;
      break;

    case "30days":
      periodDays = 30;
      break;

    case "custom": {
      if (customDateStart && customDateEnd) {
        const start = new Date(customDateStart);
        const end = new Date(customDateEnd);
        const diffMs = end.getTime() - start.getTime();
        const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) + 1;
        periodDays = Math.max(diffDays, 1);
      } else {
        periodDays = 7;
      }
      break;
    }

    case "all":
    default: {
      if (filteredHistory.length === 0) {
        periodDays = 7;
      } else {
        const dates = filteredHistory.map((c) => new Date(c.date));
        const minDate = new Date(
          Math.min.apply(
            null,
            dates.map((d) => d.getTime())
          )
        );
        const maxDate = new Date(
          Math.max.apply(
            null,
            dates.map((d) => d.getTime())
          )
        );
        const diffMs = maxDate.getTime() - minDate.getTime();
        const diffDays = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) + 1;
        periodDays = Math.max(diffDays, 1);
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  // ========================================
  // ✅ CÁLCULOS COM NORMALIZAÇÃO DE MOEDAS
  // ========================================
  const calculations: CalculationsResult = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();

    // 1. FATOR DE PROPORCIONALIDADE
    // Se o filtro é de 7 dias, cobramos apenas 7/30 dos custos fixos.
    const daysFactor = periodDays / 30;

    // 2. NORMALIZAÇÃO DE MOEDAS (Converte tudo para BRL para somar)
    const normalizedHistory = filteredHistory.map((campaign) => {
      const product = products.find((p) => p.id === campaign.productId);
      if (!product) return campaign;

      const exchangeRate =
        campaign.exchangeRate ||
        (campaign.originalCurrency
          ? getExchangeRate(campaign.originalCurrency, rates)
          : 1);

      const needsConversion = product.market !== "BR";

      return {
        ...campaign,
        spend: needsConversion ? campaign.spend * exchangeRate : campaign.spend,
        revenue: needsConversion
          ? campaign.revenue * exchangeRate
          : campaign.revenue,
      };
    });

    // 3. TOTAIS DE TRÁFEGO (ADS)
    const totalAdsSpend = normalizedHistory.reduce(
      (acc, c) => acc + c.spend,
      0
    );
    const totalAdsRevenue = normalizedHistory.reduce(
      (acc, c) => acc + c.revenue,
      0
    );
    const totalConversions = normalizedHistory.reduce(
      (acc, c) => acc + c.conversions,
      0
    );
    const totalClicks = normalizedHistory.reduce((acc, c) => acc + c.clicks, 0);

    // 4. MAPA DE CAMPANHAS POR PRODUTO
    const aggregationMap = new Map<string, Campaign[]>();
    for (const campaign of filteredHistory) {
      const existing = aggregationMap.get(campaign.productId) || [];
      existing.push(campaign);
      aggregationMap.set(campaign.productId, existing);
    }

    // 5. CUSTOS FIXOS GLOBAIS (RATEIO)
    // Rateio justo: Divide pelo total de produtos na carteira, independente se venderam hoje
    const activeProductsCount = products.length || 1;

    const safeGlobalCostsMonthly = Number(fixedCostsTotal) || 0;
    const globalFixedCostPerProductMonthly =
      safeGlobalCostsMonthly / activeProductsCount;

    // 6. CÁLCULO POR PRODUTO (LOOP PRINCIPAL)
    const allProductPerformance: ProductPerformance[] = products.map((p) => {
      const productCampaigns = aggregationMap.get(p.id) || [];

      // Filtra campanhas normalizadas deste produto para pegar valores em BRL
      const productNormalizedCampaigns = normalizedHistory.filter(
        (c) => c.productId === p.id
      );

      const prodSpendAds = productNormalizedCampaigns.reduce(
        (acc, c) => acc + c.spend,
        0
      );
      const prodRevenue = productNormalizedCampaigns.reduce(
        (acc, c) => acc + c.revenue,
        0
      );
      const prodConversions = productNormalizedCampaigns.reduce(
        (acc, c) => acc + c.conversions,
        0
      );
      const prodClicks = productNormalizedCampaigns.reduce(
        (acc, c) => acc + c.clicks,
        0
      );

      // --- CORREÇÃO DE TIPAGEM AQUI ---
      // Tenta pegar do novo formato specificCosts.total. Se não existir, tenta o antigo ou zera.
      const specificMonthly = Number(p.specificCosts?.total) || 0;

      // Soma Mensal (Específico do Produto + Parte do Global)
      const totalMonthlyFixed =
        specificMonthly + globalFixedCostPerProductMonthly;

      // Aplica PROPORCIONALIDADE (Regra de 3 baseada nos dias do filtro)
      const totalFixedProportional = totalMonthlyFixed * daysFactor;

      // Custo Total Real = Ads + Fixo Proporcional
      const totalCostReal = prodSpendAds + totalFixedProportional;

      // Lucro Real
      const realProfit = prodRevenue - totalCostReal;

      // ROI Real
      const realROI =
        totalCostReal > 0 ? (realProfit / totalCostReal) * 100 : 0;

      const margin = prodRevenue > 0 ? (realProfit / prodRevenue) * 100 : 0;

      const cr = prodClicks > 0 ? (prodConversions / prodClicks) * 100 : 0;
      const cpc = prodClicks > 0 ? prodSpendAds / prodClicks : 0;

      return {
        name: p.name,
        nicho: p.nicho || "Outros",
        campaignCount: productCampaigns.length,
        spend: totalCostReal,
        revenue: prodRevenue,
        profit: realProfit,
        roi: realROI,
        conversions: prodConversions,
        clicks: prodClicks,
        cr,
        cpc,
        fixedCostsTotal: totalFixedProportional,
        margin,
      };
    });

    // 7. CONSOLIDAÇÃO DOS TOTAIS GLOBAIS
    // Soma custos específicos de todos os produtos proporcionalmente
    const totalSpecificsMonthly = products.reduce((acc, p) => {
      const cost = p.specificCosts?.total || 0;
      return acc + cost;
    }, 0);

    const totalProportionalFixedCosts =
      (safeGlobalCostsMonthly + totalSpecificsMonthly) * daysFactor;

    // Total Gasto = Ads + Todos os Fixos Proporcionais
    const totalSpendGlobalFinal = totalAdsSpend + totalProportionalFixedCosts;
    const totalRevenueGlobal = totalAdsRevenue + adsenseRev;
    const netProfitGlobal = totalRevenueGlobal - totalSpendGlobalFinal;

    const globalROI =
      totalSpendGlobalFinal > 0
        ? (netProfitGlobal / totalSpendGlobalFinal) * 100
        : 0;

    const globalCR =
      totalClicks > 0 ? (totalConversions / totalClicks) * 100 : 0;
    const globalCPC = totalClicks > 0 ? totalAdsSpend / totalClicks : 0;
    const avgTicket =
      totalConversions > 0 ? totalRevenueGlobal / totalConversions : 0;
    const marginPercent =
      totalRevenueGlobal > 0 ? (netProfitGlobal / totalRevenueGlobal) * 100 : 0;

    // 8. CÁLCULO DE TENDÊNCIA (PERÍODO ANTERIOR)
    const previousPeriodHistory = history.filter((c) => {
      const date = new Date(c.date);
      const diffMs = now.getTime() - date.getTime();
      const daysAgo = Math.floor(diffMs / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
      return daysAgo >= periodDays && daysAgo < periodDays * 2;
    });

    const prevRevenue = previousPeriodHistory.reduce(
      (acc, c) => acc + c.revenue,
      0
    );
    const revenueGrowth =
      prevRevenue > 0
        ? ((totalAdsRevenue - prevRevenue) / prevRevenue) * 100
        : 0;

    const prevSpendAds = previousPeriodHistory.reduce(
      (acc, c) => acc + c.spend,
      0
    );
    const spendGrowth =
      prevSpendAds > 0
        ? ((totalAdsSpend - prevSpendAds) / prevSpendAds) * 100
        : 0;

    const profitGrowth = 0; // Simplificado para evitar erros de divisão por zero complexos neste momento

    // 9. TIMELINE DIÁRIA (Lógica Ajustada: Fixos Mensais + Ads Diário)
    const timelineData: TimelinePoint[] = [];

    // 1. Somamos todo o custo fixo mensal da operação (Global + Específicos)
    // Nota: safeGlobalCostsMonthly e totalSpecificsMonthly já foram calculados antes deste bloco
    const totalMonthlyFixedLiability =
      safeGlobalCostsMonthly + totalSpecificsMonthly;

    for (let i = 0; i < periodDays; i++) {
      const date = new Date();
      date.setDate(date.getDate() - (periodDays - 1 - i));

      // 2. Descobrimos quantos dias tem o mês desta data específica (ex: Fev = 28, Mar = 31)
      const daysInThisMonth = new Date(
        date.getFullYear(),
        date.getMonth() + 1,
        0
      ).getDate();

      // 3. Calculamos o custo fixo DESTE dia específico (divisão exata)
      const dailyFixedCost = totalMonthlyFixedLiability / daysInThisMonth;

      const dayCampaigns = filteredHistory.filter((c) =>
        isSameDay(new Date(c.date), date)
      );

      let dayRevenue = 0;
      let dayAdSpend = 0; // Custo Variável (Ads)

      dayCampaigns.forEach((c) => {
        const p = products.find((prod) => prod.id === c.productId);

        // Conversão manual segura de moeda
        const currency =
          p?.market === "US" || p?.market === "OTHER"
            ? "USD"
            : p?.market === "EU"
            ? "EUR"
            : p?.market === "UK"
            ? "GBP"
            : "BRL";
        const rate = currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;

        dayRevenue += c.revenue * rate;
        dayAdSpend += c.spend * rate;
      });

      // 4. Custo Total do Dia = Ads Real (Variável) + Fixo Rateado do Dia
      const dayTotalSpend = dayAdSpend + dailyFixedCost;

      timelineData.push({
        date: `${date.getDate()}/${date.getMonth() + 1}`,
        revenue: dayRevenue,
        spend: dayTotalSpend,
        profit: dayRevenue - dayTotalSpend,
      });
    }

    // 10. SAÚDE E PROJEÇÕES (Lógica de Forecast: MTD Real + Média 7 Dias Futura)

    // A. Preparação de Datas
    const currentYear = now.getFullYear();
    const currentMonth = now.getMonth();
    const daysInMonth = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth + 1, 0).getDate();
    const currentDay = now.getDate();
    const daysRemaining = Math.max(0, daysInMonth - currentDay);
    const startOfMonth = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth, 1);

    // B. Calcular Acumulado Real do Mês (Month-to-Date) - Baseado no histórico COMPLETO
    const mtdHistory = history.filter((c) => {
      const d = new Date(c.date);
      return d >= startOfMonth && d <= now;
    });

    const mtdSpendReal = mtdHistory.reduce((acc, c) => {
      const p = products.find((prod) => prod.id === c.productId);
      if (!p) return acc;
      // Normalização de moeda inline
      const currency =
        p.market === "US" || p.market === "OTHER"
          ? "USD"
          : p.market === "EU"
          ? "EUR"
          : p.market === "UK"
          ? "GBP"
          : "BRL";
      const rate = currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;
      return acc + c.spend * rate;
    }, 0);

    const mtdRevenueReal = mtdHistory.reduce((acc, c) => {
      const p = products.find((prod) => prod.id === c.productId);
      if (!p) return acc;
      const currency =
        p.market === "US" || p.market === "OTHER"
          ? "USD"
          : p.market === "EU"
          ? "EUR"
          : p.market === "UK"
          ? "GBP"
          : "BRL";
      const rate = currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;
      return acc + c.revenue * rate;
    }, 0);

    // C. Calcular Média dos Últimos 7 Dias (Run Rate) - Para projetar o futuro
    const sevenDaysAgo = new Date();
    sevenDaysAgo.setDate(now.getDate() - 7);

    const last7DaysHistory = history.filter((c) => {
      const d = new Date(c.date);
      return d >= sevenDaysAgo && d <= now;
    });

    const last7DaysSpendTotal = last7DaysHistory.reduce((acc, c) => {
      const p = products.find((prod) => prod.id === c.productId);
      if (!p) return acc;
      const currency =
        p.market === "US" || p.market === "OTHER"
          ? "USD"
          : p.market === "EU"
          ? "EUR"
          : p.market === "UK"
          ? "GBP"
          : "BRL";
      const rate = currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;
      return acc + c.spend * rate;
    }, 0);

    const last7DaysRevenueTotal = last7DaysHistory.reduce((acc, c) => {
      const p = products.find((prod) => prod.id === c.productId);
      if (!p) return acc;
      const currency =
        p.market === "US" || p.market === "OTHER"
          ? "USD"
          : p.market === "EU"
          ? "EUR"
          : p.market === "UK"
          ? "GBP"
          : "BRL";
      const rate = currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;
      return acc + c.revenue * rate;
    }, 0);

    // Média diária (se não tiver histórico de 7 dias, usa o MTD como fallback)
    const avgDailySpend =
      last7DaysSpendTotal > 0
        ? last7DaysSpendTotal / 7
        : mtdSpendReal / currentDay || 0;
    const avgDailyRevenue =
      last7DaysRevenueTotal > 0
        ? last7DaysRevenueTotal / 7
        : mtdRevenueReal / currentDay || 0;

    // D. Projeção Final (Forecast)
    // Acumulado Real + (Média Diária x Dias Restantes)
    const projectedRevenue = mtdRevenueReal + avgDailyRevenue * daysRemaining;

    // Para o gasto, somamos também os custos fixos mensais cheios
    const projectedSpend =
      mtdSpendReal +
      avgDailySpend * daysRemaining +
      safeGlobalCostsMonthly +
      totalSpecificsMonthly;

    const projectedProfit = projectedRevenue - projectedSpend;

    // Burn Rate (Mantém lógica simples para o card de KPI, ou pode usar a média de 7 dias)
    const burnRate = avgDailySpend;

    // E. Health Score & Alertas (Mantidos)
    let healthScore = 50;
    if (globalROI > 50) healthScore += 20;
    if (netProfitGlobal > 0) healthScore += 15;
    if (globalCR > 1.5) healthScore += 15;
    healthScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, healthScore));

    const alerts = generateAlerts(products, filteredHistory, monthlyTarget);

    return {
      allProductPerformance,
      alerts,
      totalAdsSpend,
      totalAdsRevenue,
      totalRevenueGlobal,
      totalSpendGlobal: totalSpendGlobalFinal,
      netProfitGlobal,
      globalROI,
      globalCR,
      globalCPC,
      avgTicket,
      marginPercent,
      totalConversions,
      totalClicks,
      revenueGrowth,
      spendGrowth,
      profitGrowth,
      timelineData,
      burnRate,
      projectedRevenue,
      projectedSpend,
      projectedProfit,
      healthScore,
    };
  }, [
    filteredHistory,
    products,
    adsenseRev,
    fixedCostsTotal,
    history,
    rates,
    periodDays,
    monthlyTarget, // ← ADICIONAR ESTA LINHA
  ]);

  const getPerformanceCategory = (roi: number): PerformanceFilter => {
    if (roi > 100) return "excelente";
    if (roi > 50) return "bom";
    if (roi >= 0) return "atencao";
    return "critico";
  };

  const getStatusCategory = (roi: number): StatusFilter => {
    if (roi > 5) return "lucrativo";
    if (roi < -5) return "prejuizo";
    return "breakeven";
  };

  const displayedProducts = useMemo(() => {
    let filtered: ProductPerformance[] = [
      ...calculations.allProductPerformance,
    ];

    // ===== FILTROS EXISTENTES (MANTER) =====
    if (selectedProducts.length > 0) {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => selectedProducts.includes(p.name));
    }

    if (searchTerm) {
      const term = searchTerm.toLowerCase();
      filtered = filtered.filter(
        (p) =>
          p.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) ||
          p.nicho.toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );
    }

    // ===== NOVOS FILTROS AVANÇADOS =====

    // Filtro de Nicho
    if (advancedFilters.nicho !== "todos") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.nicho === advancedFilters.nicho);
    }

    // Filtro de Status
    if (advancedFilters.status !== "todos") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => {
        const status = getStatusCategory(p.roi);
        return status === advancedFilters.status;
      });
    }

    // Filtro de ROI Mínimo
    if (advancedFilters.roiMinimo > 0) {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.roi >= advancedFilters.roiMinimo);
    }

    // Filtro de Performance
    if (advancedFilters.performance !== "todos") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => {
        const performance = getPerformanceCategory(p.roi);
        return performance === advancedFilters.performance;
      });
    }

    // ===== ORDENAÇÃO (MANTER CÓDIGO ORIGINAL) =====
    filtered.sort((a, b) => {
      const aVal = a[sortField];
      const bVal = b[sortField];

      if (typeof aVal === "string" && typeof bVal === "string") {
        return sortDirection === "asc"
          ? aVal.localeCompare(bVal)
          : bVal.localeCompare(aVal);
      }
      const numA = Number(aVal) || 0;
      const numB = Number(bVal) || 0;

      return sortDirection === "asc" ? numA - numB : numB - numA;
    });

    return filtered;
  }, [
    calculations.allProductPerformance,
    selectedProducts,
    searchTerm,
    sortField,
    sortDirection,
    advancedFilters,
  ]);

  // ===== DADOS PARA COMPARAÇÃO DE PRODUTOS =====

  const insights = useMemo(() => {
    const alerts: Array<{
      type: "danger" | "warning" | "success" | "info";
      message: string;
    }> = [];

    // ===== ALERTA 1: Produtos no vermelho =====
    const negativeProducts = calculations.allProductPerformance.filter(
      (p) => p.profit < 0
    );
    if (negativeProducts.length > 0) {
      const productNames = negativeProducts.map((p) => p.name).join(", "); // ✅ Extrai ANTES
      alerts.push({
        type: "danger",
        message: `${negativeProducts.length} produto(s) operando no vermelho: ${productNames}`, // ✅ Usa DEPOIS
      });
    }

    // ===== ALERTA 2: Produtos excelentes =====
    const excellentProducts = calculations.allProductPerformance.filter(
      (p) => p.roi > 100
    );
    if (excellentProducts.length > 0) {
      const excellentNames = excellentProducts.map((p) => p.name).join(", "); // ✅ Extrai ANTES
      alerts.push({
        type: "success",
        message: `${excellentProducts.length} produto(s) com ROI acima de 100%! Considere escalar: ${excellentNames}`, // ✅ Usa DEPOIS
      });
    }

    // ===== ALERTA 3: Queda de lucro =====
    if (calculations.profitGrowth < -20) {
      const percentDrop = Math.abs(calculations.profitGrowth).toFixed(1); // ✅ Extrai ANTES
      alerts.push({
        type: "warning",
        message: `Lucro caiu ${percentDrop}% comparado ao período anterior`, // ✅ Usa DEPOIS
      });
    }

    // ===== ALERTA 4: Aumento de gastos =====
    if (calculations.spendGrowth > 50) {
      const percentIncrease = calculations.spendGrowth.toFixed(1); // ✅ Extrai ANTES
      alerts.push({
        type: "warning",
        message: `Atenção: Gastos aumentaram ${percentIncrease}% neste período`, // ✅ Usa DEPOIS
      });
    }

    // ===== ALERTA 5: Meta mensal =====
    const daysElapsed = new Date().getDate();
    const expectedRevenue = (monthlyTarget / 30) * daysElapsed;

    if (
      expectedRevenue > 0 &&
      calculations.totalRevenueGlobal < expectedRevenue * 0.7
    ) {
      const percentBelow = (
        (1 - calculations.totalRevenueGlobal / expectedRevenue) *
        100
      ).toFixed(0); // ✅ Extrai ANTES
      alerts.push({
        type: "warning",
        message: `Receita está ${percentBelow}% abaixo do esperado para atingir a meta mensal`, // ✅ Usa DEPOIS
      });
    }

    // ===== ALERTA 6: CPC elevado =====
    if (calculations.globalCPC > 5) {
      const cpcValue = calculations.globalCPC.toFixed(2); // ✅ Extrai ANTES
      alerts.push({
        type: "info",
        message: `CPC médio de R$ ${cpcValue} está elevado. Revise palavras-chave e segmentação`, // ✅ Usa DEPOIS
      });
    }

    return alerts;
  }, [calculations, monthlyTarget]);

  const top3Products = [...calculations.allProductPerformance]
    .sort((a, b) => b.profit - a.profit)
    .slice(0, 3);

  const bottom3Products = [...calculations.allProductPerformance]
    .sort((a, b) => a.profit - b.profit)
    .slice(0, 3);

  const pieData = calculations.allProductPerformance.map((p) => ({
    name: p.name,
    value: p.spend,
  }));

  const toggleProductFilter = (productName: string) => {
    setSelectedProducts((prev) =>
      prev.includes(productName)
        ? prev.filter((n) => n !== productName)
        : [...prev, productName]
    );
  };

  const handleSort = (field: SortField) => {
    if (sortField === field) {
      setSortDirection((prev) => (prev === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc"));
    } else {
      setSortField(field);
      setSortDirection("desc");
    }
  };

  const exportToExcel = () => {
    // Determinar o nome do período para o relatório
    let periodoNome = "";

    switch (periodFilter) {
      case "today":
        periodoNome = "Hoje";
        break;
      case "7days":
        periodoNome = "Últimos 7 dias";
        break;
      case "30days":
        periodoNome = "Últimos 30 dias";
        break;
      case "custom":
        if (customDateStart && customDateEnd) {
          periodoNome = `${customDateStart} até ${customDateEnd}`;
        } else {
          periodoNome = "Período Personalizado";
        }
        break;
      case "all":
        periodoNome = "Todo o Histórico";
        break;
      default:
        periodoNome = "Período Selecionado";
    }

    // Preparar dados no formato esperado pela função de exportação
    const excelData: ExcelExportData = {
      summary: {
        periodo: periodoNome,
        receita: calculations.totalRevenueGlobal,
        gasto: calculations.totalSpendGlobal,
        lucro: calculations.netProfitGlobal,
        roi: calculations.globalROI,
        campanhas: filteredHistory.length,
        produtos: calculations.allProductPerformance.length,
        cr: calculations.globalCR,
        cpc: calculations.globalCPC,
        conversoes: calculations.totalConversions,
        cliques: calculations.totalClicks,
        ticketMedio: calculations.avgTicket,
        margem: calculations.marginPercent,
      },
      products: displayedProducts.map((p) => ({
        produto: p.name,
        nicho: p.nicho,
        campanhas: p.campaignCount,
        gasto: p.spend,
        receita: p.revenue,
        lucro: p.profit,
        roi: p.roi,
        conversoes: p.conversions,
        cr: p.cr,
        cpc: p.cpc,
        cliques: p.clicks,
      })),
      timeline: calculations.timelineData.map((t) => ({
        data: t.date,
        receita: t.revenue,
        gasto: t.spend,
        lucro: t.profit,
      })),
    };

    // Chamar a função de exportação
    try {
      generateExcel(excelData); // ← MUDOU AQUI
      console.log("✅ Relatório Excel exportado com sucesso!");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("❌ Erro ao exportar Excel:", error);
      alert("Erro ao exportar relatório. Tente novamente.");
    }
  };

  const getHealthColor = (score: number) => {
    if (score >= 80) return "text-green-600";
    if (score >= 60) return "text-yellow-600";
    if (score >= 40) return "text-orange-600";
    return "text-red-600";
  };

  const getHealthBg = (score: number) => {
    if (score >= 80) return "bg-green-50 border-green-200";
    if (score >= 60) return "bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-200";
    if (score >= 40) return "bg-orange-50 border-orange-200";
    return "bg-red-50 border-red-200";
  };

  const getInsightStyles = (type: string) => {
    switch (type) {
      case "danger":
        return "bg-red-50 border-red-500";
      case "warning":
        return "bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-500";
      case "success":
        return "bg-green-50 border-green-500";
      default:
        return "bg-blue-50 border-blue-500";
    }
  };

  const progressPercent =
    monthlyTarget > 0
      ? (calculations.projectedProfit / monthlyTarget) * 100
      : 0;
  const progressPercentClamped = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, progressPercent));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500 pb-10">
      {/* Badge de Status de Moedas */}
      {currencyLoading && (
        <div className="bg-blue-50 border-l-4 border-blue-500 p-3 rounded-r-lg">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-800 text-sm">
            <Clock size={16} className="animate-spin" />
            <span>Atualizando cotações de moedas...</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* HEADER COM FILTROS E TOGGLE DE MODO */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 border border-indigo-100 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6 items-start lg:items-center justify-between">
          {/* Coluna Esquerda: Filtros de Período */}
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 flex-1">
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-900 uppercase flex items-center gap-1">
              <Calendar size={14} /> Período de Análise
            </label>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              {[
                { value: "today", label: "Hoje" },
                { value: "7days", label: "Últimos 7 dias" },
                { value: "30days", label: "Últimos 30 dias" },
                { value: "all", label: "Tudo" },
                { value: "custom", label: "Personalizado" },
              ].map((period) => (
                <button
                  key={period.value}
                  onClick={() => setPeriodFilter(period.value as PeriodFilter)}
                  className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-all ${
                    periodFilter === period.value
                      ? "bg-indigo-600 text-white shadow-md"
                      : "bg-white text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200"
                  }`}
                >
                  {period.label}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>

            {periodFilter === "custom" && (
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={customDateStart}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomDateStart(e.target.value)}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-indigo-200 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                />
                <span className="self-center text-gray-500">até</span>
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={customDateEnd}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomDateEnd(e.target.value)}
                  className="px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-indigo-200 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Coluna Centro: Toggle de Modo */}
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-indigo-900 uppercase">
              Modo de Visualização
            </label>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white rounded-lg p-1 border border-indigo-200 shadow-sm">
              <button
                onClick={() => applyPreset("resumida")}
                className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-bold transition-all ${
                  dashboardConfig.mode === "resumida"
                    ? "bg-green-500 text-white shadow-md"
                    : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
                }`}
              >
                🎯 Resumida
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => applyPreset("completa")}
                className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-bold transition-all ${
                  dashboardConfig.mode === "completa"
                    ? "bg-blue-500 text-white shadow-md"
                    : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
                }`}
              >
                📊 Completa
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  if (dashboardConfig.mode !== "custom") {
                    applyPreset("custom");
                  }
                  setShowSettings(true);
                }}
                className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md text-sm font-bold transition-all ${
                  dashboardConfig.mode === "custom"
                    ? "bg-purple-500 text-white shadow-md"
                    : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
                }`}
              >
                <Settings size={16} className="inline mr-1" />
                Personalizada
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Coluna Direita: Health Score */}
          <div
            className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 rounded-xl border-2 ${getHealthBg(
              calculations.healthScore
            )} min-w-[140px]`}
          >
            <div className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase mb-1">
              Health Score
            </div>
            <div
              className={`text-4xl font-black ${getHealthColor(
                calculations.healthScore
              )}`}
            >
              {calculations.healthScore}
            </div>
            <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
              {calculations.healthScore >= 80
                ? "🎯 Excelente"
                : calculations.healthScore >= 60
                ? "✅ Bom"
                : calculations.healthScore >= 40
                ? "⚠️ Atenção"
                : "🚨 Crítico"}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* GERENCIAR CUSTOS FIXOS GLOBAIS */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showFixedCosts && (
        <CostsManager
          costs={fixedCosts}
          setCosts={setFixedCosts}
          title="💰 Custos Fixos Mensais (Global)"
          subtitle="Aplicados a todos os produtos. Configure os custos operacionais do seu negócio."
          emptyMessage="Nenhum custo fixo configurado. Adicione custos como ferramentas, hospedagem, etc."
          colorScheme="blue"
        />
      )}

      {/* INPUTS FINANCEIROS */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showFinancialSettings && (
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <div className="p-2 bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-600 rounded-lg">
              <Wallet size={20} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">
                Configurações Financeiras Adicionais
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                Receitas extras e metas para cálculo preciso do lucro
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">
                Receita AdSense/Outros
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <span className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-500 text-sm">
                  R$
                </span>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={adsenseRev}
                  onChange={(e) => setAdsenseRev(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="pl-8 pr-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none w-full bg-white text-gray-900"
                  placeholder="0.00"
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">
                Meta de Lucro Mensal
              </label>
              <div className="relative">
                <span className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-500 text-sm">
                  R$
                </span>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={monthlyTarget}
                  onChange={(e) => setMonthlyTarget(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="pl-8 pr-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none w-full bg-white text-gray-900"
                  placeholder="10000"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* --- ALERTAS INTELIGENTES (NOVO SISTEMA) --- */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showAlerts && (
