        <AlertsPanel alerts={calculations.alerts} />
      )}

      {/* --- INSIGHTS ANTIGOS (MANTER POR ENQUANTO) --- */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showInsights && insights.length > 0 && (
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Zap className="text-yellow-600" size={20} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">Insights Complementares</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {insights.map((insight, idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className={`flex items-start gap-3 p-4 rounded-lg border-l-4 ${getInsightStyles(
                  insight.type
                )}`}
              >
                {insight.type === "danger" && (
                  <AlertTriangle
                    className="text-red-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                    size={18}
                  />
                )}
                {insight.type === "warning" && (
                  <AlertCircle
                    className="text-yellow-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                    size={18}
                  />
                )}
                {insight.type === "success" && (
                  <CheckCircle
                    className="text-green-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                    size={18}
                  />
                )}
                {insight.type === "info" && (
                  <Activity
                    className="text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
                    size={18}
                  />
                )}
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-800 font-medium">
                  {insight.message}
                </p>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KPI CARDS PRINCIPAIS */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showMainKPIs && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-green-50 to-emerald-50 p-5 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-green-100 card-hover group animate-fade-in-up">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-green-700 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
                  Receita Global
                </p>
                <h3 className="text-3xl font-black text-green-900">
                  <AnimatedNumber
                    value={calculations.totalRevenueGlobal}
                    decimals={2}
                    prefix="R$ "
                    duration={1000}
                  />
                </h3>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-green-100 rounded-lg text-green-600 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <DollarSign size={24} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              {calculations.revenueGrowth >= 0 ? (
                <TrendingUp className="text-green-600" size={14} />
              ) : (
                <TrendingDown className="text-red-600" size={14} />
              )}
              <span
                className={`font-bold ${
                  calculations.revenueGrowth >= 0
                    ? "text-green-600"
                    : "text-red-600"
                }`}
              >
                {Math.abs(calculations.revenueGrowth).toFixed(1)}%
              </span>
              <span className="text-gray-600">vs período anterior</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-green-200 text-[10px] text-green-700">
              Ads: R$ {calculations.totalAdsRevenue.toFixed(2)} + Extra: R${" "}
              {adsenseRev.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-red-50 to-orange-50 p-5 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-red-100 hover:shadow-md transition-all group card-hover animate-fade-in-up animate-delay-2">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-red-700 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
                  Custo Total
                </p>
                <h3 className="text-3xl font-black text-red-900">
                  <AnimatedNumber
                    value={calculations.totalSpendGlobal}
                    decimals={2}
                    prefix="R$ "
                    duration={1000}
                  />
                </h3>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-red-100 rounded-lg text-red-600 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <Activity size={24} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              {calculations.spendGrowth >= 0 ? (
                <TrendingUp className="text-red-600" size={14} />
              ) : (
                <TrendingDown className="text-green-600" size={14} />
              )}
              <span
                className={`font-bold ${
                  calculations.spendGrowth >= 0
                    ? "text-red-600"
                    : "text-green-600"
                }`}
              >
                {Math.abs(calculations.spendGrowth).toFixed(1)}%
              </span>
              <span className="text-gray-600">vs período anterior</span>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-2 pt-2 border-t border-red-200 text-[10px] text-red-700">
              Ads: R$ {calculations.totalAdsSpend.toFixed(2)} + Fixos: R${" "}
              {fixedCostsTotal.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div
            className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${
              calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                ? "from-blue-50 to-cyan-50"
                : "from-red-50 to-pink-50"
            } p-5 rounded-xl shadow-sm border ${
              calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                ? "border-blue-100"
                : "border-red-100"
            } hover:shadow-md transition-all group card-hover animate-fade-in-up animate-delay-3`}
          >
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p
                  className={`text-xs font-bold uppercase mb-1 ${
                    calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                      ? "text-blue-700"
                      : "text-red-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  Lucro Líquido Real
                </p>
                <h3
                  className={`text-3xl font-black ${
                    calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                      ? "text-blue-900"
                      : "text-red-900"
                  }`}
                >
                  <AnimatedNumber
                    value={calculations.netProfitGlobal}
                    decimals={2}
                    prefix="R$ "
                    duration={1000}
                  />
                </h3>
              </div>
              <div
                className={`p-2 rounded-lg group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform ${
                  calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                    ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-600"
                    : "bg-red-100 text-red-600"
                }`}
              >
                <Calculator size={24} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
              {calculations.profitGrowth >= 0 ? (
                <TrendingUp className="text-green-600" size={14} />
              ) : (
                <TrendingDown className="text-red-600" size={14} />
              )}
              <span
                className={`font-bold ${
                  calculations.profitGrowth >= 0
                    ? "text-green-600"
                    : "text-red-600"
                }`}
              >
                {Math.abs(calculations.profitGrowth).toFixed(1)}%
              </span>
              <span className="text-gray-600">vs período anterior</span>
            </div>
            <div
              className={`mt-2 pt-2 border-t text-[10px] font-semibold ${
                calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                  ? "border-blue-200 text-blue-700"
                  : "border-red-200 text-red-700"
              }`}
            >
              Margem: {calculations.marginPercent.toFixed(1)}% •{" "}
              {calculations.netProfitGlobal >= 0
                ? "🟢 No Azul"
                : "🔴 No Vermelho"}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-50 to-indigo-50 p-5 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-purple-100 hover:shadow-md transition-all group card-hover animate-fade-in-up animate-delay-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className="text-xs text-purple-700 font-bold uppercase mb-1">
                  ROI Global
                </p>
                <h3
                  className={`text-3xl font-black ${
                    calculations.globalROI >= 0
                      ? "text-purple-900"
                      : "text-red-900"
                  }`}
                >
                  <AnimatedNumber
                    value={calculations.globalROI}
                    decimals={1}
                    suffix="%"
                    duration={1000}
                  />
                </h3>
              </div>
              <div className="p-2 bg-purple-100 rounded-lg text-purple-600 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
                <Target size={24} />
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-3 pt-2 border-t border-purple-200 text-[10px] text-purple-700 space-y-1">
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <span>Retorno sobre Investimento</span>
                <span className="font-bold">
                  {calculations.globalROI > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                  {calculations.globalROI.toFixed(0)}%
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* KPI CARDS SECUNDÁRIOS */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showSecondaryKPIs && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-8 gap-3">
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Percent size={14} className="text-indigo-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                CR Global
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.globalCR}
                decimals={2}
                suffix="%"
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <MousePointer2 size={14} className="text-blue-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                CPC Médio
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.globalCPC}
                decimals={2}
                prefix="R$ "
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <ShoppingCart size={14} className="text-green-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                Ticket Médio
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.avgTicket}
                decimals={2}
                prefix="R$ "
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Target size={14} className="text-purple-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                Conversões
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.totalConversions}
                decimals={0}
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <MousePointer2 size={14} className="text-cyan-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                Cliques
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.totalClicks}
                decimals={0}
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Clock size={14} className="text-orange-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                Burn/Dia
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.burnRate}
                decimals={0}
                prefix="R$ "
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <BarChart3 size={14} className="text-pink-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                Campanhas
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={filteredHistory.length}
                decimals={0}
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Users size={14} className="text-teal-600" />
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase">
                Produtos
              </p>
            </div>
            <h4 className="text-xl font-black text-gray-900">
              <AnimatedNumber
                value={calculations.allProductPerformance.length}
                decimals={0}
                duration={800}
              />
            </h4>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* PROJEÇÃO MENSAL */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showProjection && (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg text-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
            <RefreshCw className="animate-spin" size={24} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">Projeção para Fim do Mês</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mb-1">Receita Projetada</p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black">
                R$ {calculations.projectedRevenue.toFixed(2)}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mb-1">Custo Projetado</p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black">
                R$ {calculations.projectedSpend.toFixed(2)}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mb-1">Lucro Projetado</p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black">
                R$ {calculations.projectedProfit.toFixed(2)}
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mb-1">Meta de Lucro</p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-black">
                R$ {monthlyTarget.toFixed(2)}
              </p>
              <div className="w-full bg-indigo-400 rounded-full h-2 mt-2">
                <div
                  className="bg-white h-2 rounded-full transition-all duration-700"
                  style={{ width: `${progressPercentClamped}%` }}
                />
              </div>
              <p className="text-xs text-indigo-200 mt-1">
                {progressPercent.toFixed(0)}% da meta
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* TOP 3 & BOTTOM 3 */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showHighlights && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
              <Crown className="text-yellow-600" size={20} />
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-900">
                🏆 Top 3 Produtos (Lucro)
              </h4>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {top3Products.map((p, idx) => (
                <div
                  key={p.name}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-green-50 to-emerald-50 rounded-lg border border-green-200"
                >
                  <div
                    className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm ${
                      idx === 0
                        ? "bg-yellow-400 text-yellow-900"
                        : idx === 1
                        ? "bg-gray-300 text-gray-700"
                        : "bg-orange-300 text-orange-800"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {idx + 1}
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{p.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">{p.nicho}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="font-black text-green-600">
                      R$ {p.profit.toFixed(2)}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                      ROI: {p.roi.toFixed(0)}%
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
              <AlertTriangle className="text-red-600" size={20} />
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-900">
                ⚠️ Produtos que Precisam de Atenção
              </h4>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {bottom3Products.map((p) => (
                <div
                  key={p.name}
                  className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-50 to-orange-50 rounded-lg border border-red-200"
                >
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-red-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm">
                    !
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{p.name}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">{p.nicho}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="font-black text-red-600">
                      R$ {p.profit.toFixed(2)}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                      ROI: {p.roi.toFixed(0)}%
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* 1️⃣ GRÁFICO DE TENDÊNCIA COMPLETO */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showTrendChart && (
        <TrendChart
          timelineData={calculations.timelineData.map((t) => ({
            date: t.date,
            revenue: t.revenue,
            spend: t.spend, // ← Já inclui custos fixos agora!
            profit: t.profit, // ← Já está correto!
            roi: t.spend > 0 ? ((t.revenue - t.spend) / t.spend) * 100 : 0,
          }))}
          onPeriodChange={(days) => {
            console.log(`Período alterado para: ${days} dias`);
          }}
        />
      )}

      {/* 2️⃣ FUNIL DE CONVERSÃO - NOVO (Formato de funil real) */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showFunnelChart && (
        <FunnelChart
          clicks={calculations.totalClicks}
          conversions={calculations.totalConversions}
          revenue={calculations.totalAdsRevenue}
        />
      )}

      {/* ===== GRÁFICOS ANTIGOS MANTIDOS (4) ===== */}
      {(dashboardConfig.sections.showPieChart ||
        dashboardConfig.sections.showBarCharts) && (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          {/* PIECHART - Distribuição de Gastos */}
          {dashboardConfig.sections.showPieChart && (
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
              <h4 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4 text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                <PieIcon size={20} className="text-purple-600" />
                Distribuição de Gastos
              </h4>
              <div className="h-[300px]">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <MemoPieChart>
                    <Pie
                      data={pieData}
                      cx="50%"
                      cy="50%"
                      labelLine={false}
                      label={({ percent }) => {
                        const safePercent = percent || 0;
                        if (safePercent < 0.05) return null;
                        return `${(safePercent * 100).toFixed(0)}%`;
                      }}
                      outerRadius={90}
                      fill="#8884d8"
                      dataKey="value"
                    >
                      {pieData.map((entry, index) => (
                        <Cell
                          key={`cell-${index}`}
                          fill={
                            [
                              "#6366f1",
                              "#8b5cf6",
                              "#ec4899",
                              "#f59e0b",
                              "#10b981",
                              "#3b82f6",
                            ][index % 6]
                          }
                        />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>

                    <Tooltip
                      formatter={(value: number) => `R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`}
                    />
                  </MemoPieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* BARCHART - Receita vs Gasto */}
          {dashboardConfig.sections.showBarCharts && (
            <>
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <h4 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4 text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <BarChart3 size={20} className="text-green-600" />
                  Receita vs Gasto
                </h4>
                <div className="h-[300px]">
                  <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    <MemoBarChart data={displayedProducts.slice(0, 10)}>
                      <CartesianGrid
                        strokeDasharray="3 3"
                        vertical={false}
                        stroke="#f0f0f0"
                      />
                      <XAxis
                        dataKey="name"
                        stroke="#9ca3af"
                        fontSize={11}
                        angle={-45}
                        textAnchor="end"
                        height={80}
                      />
                      <YAxis stroke="#9ca3af" fontSize={11} />
                      <Tooltip
                        formatter={(value: number) => [
                          `R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`,
                          "",
                        ]}
                        contentStyle={{
                          borderRadius: "8px",
                          border: "none",
                          boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)",
                        }}
                      />
                      <Legend />
                      <Bar
                        dataKey="spend"
                        name="Gasto"
                        fill="#f87171"
                        radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                      />
                      <Bar
                        dataKey="revenue"
                        name="Receita"
                        fill="#34d399"
                        radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                      />
                    </MemoBarChart>
                  </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 5️⃣ BARCHART HORIZONTAL - Lucratividade */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <h4 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4 text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Target size={20} className="text-indigo-600" />
                  Lucratividade
                </h4>
                <div className="h-[300px]">
                  <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    <MemoBarChart
                      data={displayedProducts.slice(0, 10)}
                      layout="vertical"
                    >
                      <CartesianGrid
                        strokeDasharray="3 3"
                        horizontal={false}
                        stroke="#f0f0f0"
                      />
                      <XAxis type="number" stroke="#9ca3af" fontSize={11} />
                      <YAxis
                        dataKey="name"
                        type="category"
                        stroke="#9ca3af"
                        fontSize={11}
                        width={120}
                      />
                      <Tooltip
                        formatter={(value: number) => [
                          `R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`,
                          "Lucro",
                        ]}
                        cursor={{ fill: "transparent" }}
                        contentStyle={{
                          borderRadius: "8px",
                          border: "none",
                          boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)",
                        }}
                      />
                      <Bar
                        dataKey="profit"
                        name="Lucro Líquido"
                        radius={[0, 4, 4, 0]}
                      >
                        {displayedProducts.slice(0, 10).map((entry, index) => (
                          <Cell
                            key={`cell-${index}`}
                            fill={entry.profit >= 0 ? "#34d399" : "#f87171"}
                          />
                        ))}
                      </Bar>
                    </MemoBarChart>
                  </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* 7️⃣ GRÁFICO DE MARGEM POR PRODUTO - NOVO */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <h4 className="text-lg font-bold mb-4 text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Percent size={20} className="text-emerald-600" />
                  Margem de Lucro (Top 3)
                </h4>
                <div className="h-[300px]">
                  <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    <MemoBarChart
                      data={displayedProducts
                        .sort((a, b) => b.margin - a.margin)
                        .slice(0, 3)
                        .map((p) => ({
                          name: p.name,
                          margin: p.margin,
                          revenue: p.revenue,
                          profit: p.profit,
                        }))}
                    >
                      <CartesianGrid
                        strokeDasharray="3 3"
                        vertical={false}
                        stroke="#f0f0f0"
                      />
                      <XAxis
                        dataKey="name"
                        stroke="#9ca3af"
                        fontSize={11}
                        angle={-15}
                        textAnchor="end"
                        height={60}
                      />
                      <YAxis
                        stroke="#9ca3af"
                        fontSize={11}
                        tickFormatter={(value) => `${value.toFixed(0)}%`}
                      />
                      <Tooltip
                        formatter={(value: number, name: string) => {
                          if (name === "Margem")
                            return [`${value.toFixed(2)}%`, name];
                          return [`R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`, name];
                        }}
                        cursor={{ fill: "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)" }}
                        contentStyle={{
                          borderRadius: "8px",
                          border: "none",
                          boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)",
                        }}
                      />
                      <Bar dataKey="margin" name="Margem" radius={[8, 8, 0, 0]}>
                        {displayedProducts
                          .sort((a, b) => b.margin - a.margin)
                          .slice(0, 3)
                          .map((entry, index) => (
                            <Cell
                              key={`cell-${index}`}
                              fill={
                                entry.margin >= 30
                                  ? "#10b981" // Verde
                                  : entry.margin >= 15
                                  ? "#f59e0b" // Amarelo
                                  : "#ef4444" // Vermelho
                              }
                            />
                          ))}
                      </Bar>
                    </MemoBarChart>
                  </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>

                {/* Legenda de Cores */}
                <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3 justify-center text-xs">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-green-500"></div>
                    <span className="text-gray-600">≥ 30% (Excelente)</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-yellow-500"></div>
                    <span className="text-gray-600">15-30% (Bom)</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-red-500"></div>
                    <span className="text-gray-600">{"<"} 15% (Crítico)</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ===== FILTROS AVANÇADOS (NOVO) ===== */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showAdvancedFilters && (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-50 to-indigo-50 border border-purple-200 p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Filter className="text-purple-600" size={20} />
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">🔍 Filtros Avançados</h3>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={() => setShowAdvancedFilters(!showAdvancedFilters)}
              className="px-3 py-1 bg-white rounded-lg text-sm font-medium text-purple-600 hover:bg-purple-100 transition-colors border border-purple-200"
            >
              {showAdvancedFilters ? "▲ Ocultar" : "▼ Mostrar"}
            </button>
          </div>

          {showAdvancedFilters && (
            <>
              {/* Linha 1: Nicho e Status */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    📂 Nicho
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={advancedFilters.nicho}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setAdvancedFilters((prev) => ({
                        ...prev,
                        nicho: e.target.value as NichoFilter,
                      }))
                    }
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                  >
                    <option value="todos">Todos os Nichos</option>
                    <option value="emagrecimento">🏃 Emagrecimento</option>
                    <option value="fitness">💪 Fitness</option>
                    <option value="saude">🏥 Saúde</option>
                    <option value="financas">💰 Finanças</option>
                    <option value="marketing">📈 Marketing</option>
                    <option value="relacionamento">❤️ Relacionamento</option>
                    <option value="espiritualidade">🙏 Espiritualidade</option>
                    <option value="outro">📦 Outro</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    📊 Status
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={advancedFilters.status}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setAdvancedFilters((prev) => ({
                        ...prev,
                        status: e.target.value as StatusFilter,
                      }))
                    }
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                  >
                    <option value="todos">Todos os Status</option>
                    <option value="lucrativo">
                      ✅ Lucrativo (ROI {">"} 5%)
                    </option>
                    <option value="prejuizo">
                      🚨 Prejuízo (ROI {"<"} -5%)
                    </option>
                    <option value="breakeven">⚖️ Break-even (ROI ≈ 0%)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Linha 2: ROI Mínimo e Performance */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    📈 ROI Mínimo (%)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    value={advancedFilters.roiMinimo}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setAdvancedFilters((prev) => ({
                        ...prev,
                        roiMinimo: Number(e.target.value),
                      }))
                    }
                    placeholder="Ex: 50"
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-semibold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                    🎯 Performance
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={advancedFilters.performance}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setAdvancedFilters((prev) => ({
                        ...prev,
                        performance: e.target.value as PerformanceFilter,
                      }))
                    }
                    className="w-full px-4 py-2 rounded-lg border border-purple-200 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                  >
                    <option value="todos">Todas as Performances</option>
                    <option value="excelente">
                      🏆 Excelente (ROI {">"} 100%)
                    </option>
                    <option value="bom">✅ Bom (ROI 50-100%)</option>
                    <option value="atencao">⚠️ Atenção (ROI 0-50%)</option>
                    <option value="critico">🚨 Crítico (ROI {"<"} 0%)</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Botão Limpar Filtros */}
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-3 border-t border-purple-200">
                <button
                  onClick={() =>
                    setAdvancedFilters({
                      nicho: "todos",
                      status: "todos",
                      roiMinimo: 0,
                      performance: "todos",
                    })
                  }
                  disabled={
                    advancedFilters.nicho === "todos" &&
                    advancedFilters.status === "todos" &&
                    advancedFilters.roiMinimo === 0 &&
                    advancedFilters.performance === "todos"
                  }
                  className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
                    advancedFilters.nicho === "todos" &&
                    advancedFilters.status === "todos" &&
                    advancedFilters.roiMinimo === 0 &&
                    advancedFilters.performance === "todos"
                      ? "bg-gray-200 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed"
                      : "bg-white text-purple-600 border border-purple-200 hover:bg-purple-50"
                  }`}
                >
                  🗑️ Limpar Filtros
                </button>

                <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-purple-700">
                  ✅ {displayedProducts.length} produtos filtrados
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ===== BARRA DE BUSCA E AÇÕES + TABELA ===== */}
      {dashboardConfig.sections.showProductTable && (
        <>
          {/* Barra de Busca */}
          <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 items-stretch md:items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-700 min-w-fit">
                <Filter size={18} />
                <h4 className="font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Filtros & Comparador
                </h4>
              </div>

              <div className="relative flex-1 max-w-md">
                <Search
                  className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400"
                  size={18}
                />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  placeholder="Buscar produto ou nicho..."
                  value={searchTerm}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
                  className="pl-10 pr-4 py-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={exportToExcel}
                className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-indigo-700 transition-colors shadow-md hover:shadow-lg whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <Download size={18} />
                📊 Exportar Excel
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tabela */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
            <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center">
              <h4 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                <FolderOpen size={20} />
                Detalhamento por Produto
                {displayedProducts.length !==
                  calculations.allProductPerformance.length && (
                  <span className="text-sm font-normal text-gray-600">
                    ({displayedProducts.length} de{" "}
                    {calculations.allProductPerformance.length})
                  </span>
                )}
              </h4>
            </div>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm text-left">
                <thead className="bg-gray-50 text-gray-700 font-semibold">
                  <tr>
                    <th
                      className="px-6 py-4 cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSort("name")}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                        Produto{" "}
                        {sortField === "name" &&
                          (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Nicho</th>
                    <th
                      className="px-6 py-4 text-center cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSort("campaignCount")}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                        Campanhas{" "}
                        {sortField === "campaignCount" &&
                          (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th
                      className="px-6 py-4 text-right cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSort("spend")}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        Gasto Total{" "}
                        {sortField === "spend" &&
                          (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                          (Fixos Totais)
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th
                      className="px-6 py-4 text-right cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSort("revenue")}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        Receita{" "}
                        {sortField === "revenue" &&
                          (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th
                      className="px-6 py-4 text-right cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSort("profit")}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1">
                        Lucro Líquido{" "}
                        {sortField === "profit" &&
                          (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th
                      className="px-6 py-4 text-center cursor-pointer hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => handleSort("roi")}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1">
                        ROI{" "}
                        {sortField === "roi" &&
                          (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
                      </div>
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Margem %</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">CR %</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">CPC</th>
                    <th className="px-6 py-4 text-center">Conv.</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
                  {displayedProducts.map((p, idx) => (
                    <tr
                      key={idx}
                      className="bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"
                    >
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 font-bold text-gray-900">
                        {p.name}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center text-gray-600">
                        {p.nicho}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center text-gray-900">
                        {p.campaignCount}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right text-gray-900">
                        R$ {p.spend.toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                          R$ {(Number(p.fixedCostsTotal) || 0).toFixed(2)}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right text-gray-900">
                        R$ {p.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                      <td
                        className={`px-6 py-4 text-right font-black ${
                          p.profit >= 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-600"
                        }`}
                      >
                        R$ {p.profit.toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                        <span
                          className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${
                            p.roi >= 50
                              ? "bg-green-100 text-green-800"
                              : p.roi >= 0
                              ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800"
                              : "bg-red-100 text-red-800"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {p.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center">
                        <span
                          className={`px-2 py-1 rounded text-xs font-semibold ${
                            p.margin >= 30
                              ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                              : p.margin >= 15
                              ? "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700"
                              : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {p.margin.toFixed(1)}%
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center text-gray-900">
                        {p.cr.toFixed(2)}%
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center text-gray-900">
                        R$ {p.cpc.toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-6 py-4 text-center text-gray-900">
                        {p.conversions}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                  {displayedProducts.length === 0 && (
                    <tr>
                      <td
                        colSpan={12}
                        className="px-6 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                      >
                        Nenhum produto corresponde aos filtros aplicados.
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* Modal de Configurações */}
      <DashboardSettings
        isOpen={showSettings}
        onClose={() => setShowSettings(false)}
        currentMode={dashboardConfig.mode}
        sections={dashboardConfig.sections}
        onApplyPreset={applyPreset}
        onToggleSection={toggleSection}
        onReset={resetToDefault}
        onExport={exportConfig}
        onImport={importConfig}
      />
      {/* Fecha a div principal (space-y-6) */}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: DashboardSettings.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\DashboardSettings.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import { X, Download, Upload, RotateCcw, Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import type { DashboardMode, DashboardSections } from '../types/dashboardConfig';
import { SECTIONS_METADATA } from '../types/dashboardConfig';

interface DashboardSettingsProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  currentMode: DashboardMode;
  sections: DashboardSections;
  onApplyPreset: (mode: DashboardMode) => void;
  onToggleSection: (sectionId: keyof DashboardSections) => void;
  onReset: () => void;
  onExport: () => void;
  onImport: (file: File) => void;
}

export const DashboardSettings: React.FC<DashboardSettingsProps> = ({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  currentMode,
  sections,
  onApplyPreset,
  onToggleSection,
  onReset,
  onExport,
  onImport,
}) => {
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  // Agrupar seções por categoria
  const sectionsByCategory = SECTIONS_METADATA.reduce((acc, section) => {
    if (!acc[section.category]) {
      acc[section.category] = [];
    }
    acc[section.category].push(section);
    return acc;
  }, {} as Record<string, typeof SECTIONS_METADATA>);

  const categoryLabels = {
    config: '⚙️ Configurações',
    alert: '🔔 Alertas',
    kpi: '📊 Indicadores (KPIs)',
    chart: '📈 Gráficos',
    table: '📋 Dados Detalhados',
  };

  const handleImportClick = () => {
    fileInputRef.current?.click();
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (file) {
      onImport(file);
      // Resetar input
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  };

  return (
    <>
      {/* Overlay escuro */}
      <div 
        className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-40 animate-in fade-in duration-200"
        onClick={onClose}
      />

      {/* Modal */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 pointer-events-none">
        <div 
          className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-4xl max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden pointer-events-auto animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200"
          onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        >
          
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white p-6 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <Layers size={28} />
              <div>
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Personalizar Dashboard</h2>
                <p className="text-indigo-100 text-sm">Escolha o que você quer ver</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-2 hover:bg-white hover:bg-opacity-20 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            >
              <X size={24} />
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Body */}
          <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto max-h-[calc(90vh-180px)]">
            
            {/* Modo Rápido - Presets */}
            <div className="mb-8">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                🚀 Layouts Rápidos
              </h3>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                
                {/* Resumida */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => onApplyPreset('resumida')}
                  className={`p-5 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all text-left ${
                    currentMode === 'resumida'
                      ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-50 shadow-lg scale-105'
                      : 'border-gray-200 bg-white hover:border-green-300 hover:shadow-md'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="text-3xl mb-2">🎯</div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">Resumida</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                    Só o essencial: KPIs principais, alertas críticos e top 3 produtos
                  </div>
                  {currentMode === 'resumida' && (
                    <div className="mt-3 text-xs font-bold text-green-600">
                      ✓ Ativo
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>

                {/* Completa */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => onApplyPreset('completa')}
                  className={`p-5 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all text-left ${
                    currentMode === 'completa'
                      ? 'border-blue-500 bg-blue-50 shadow-lg scale-105'
                      : 'border-gray-200 bg-white hover:border-blue-300 hover:shadow-md'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="text-3xl mb-2">📊</div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">Completa</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                    Tudo disponível: gráficos, tabelas e análises detalhadas
                  </div>
                  {currentMode === 'completa' && (
                    <div className="mt-3 text-xs font-bold text-blue-600">
                      ✓ Ativo
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>

                {/* Custom */}
                <button
                  onClick={() => onApplyPreset('custom')}
                  className={`p-5 rounded-xl border-2 transition-all text-left ${
                    currentMode === 'custom'
                      ? 'border-purple-500 bg-purple-50 shadow-lg scale-105'
                      : 'border-gray-200 bg-white hover:border-purple-300 hover:shadow-md'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="text-3xl mb-2">⚙️</div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">Personalizado</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                    Escolha cada seção individualmente do seu jeito
                  </div>
                  {currentMode === 'custom' && (
                    <div className="mt-3 text-xs font-bold text-purple-600">
                      ✓ Ativo
                    </div>
                  )}
                </button>

              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Seções Individuais */}
            <div className="mb-8">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                  🎨 Seções Visíveis
                </h3>
                {currentMode !== 'custom' && (
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 px-3 py-1 rounded-full">
                    💡 Altere qualquer item para ativar o modo Personalizado
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Agrupar por categoria */}
              <div className="space-y-6">
                {Object.entries(sectionsByCategory).map(([category, categorySection]) => (
                  <div key={category}>
                    <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3 uppercase tracking-wide">
                      {categoryLabels[category as keyof typeof categoryLabels]}
                    </h4>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      {categorySection.map((section) => (
                        <label
                          key={section.id}
                          className="flex items-start gap-3 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-100 cursor-pointer transition-colors border border-gray-200"
                        >
                          <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={sections[section.id]}
                            onChange={() => onToggleSection(section.id)}
                            className="mt-1 w-5 h-5 text-indigo-600 rounded focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500"
                          />
                          <div className="flex-1">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                              <span className="text-lg">{section.icon}</span>
                              <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">
                                {section.label}
                              </span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                              {section.description}
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        </label>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

          </div>

          {/* Footer */}
          <div className="bg-gray-50 p-6 border-t border-gray-200 flex flex-wrap gap-3 items-center justify-between">
            
            {/* Botões Esquerda */}
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button
                onClick={onReset}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"
              >
                <RotateCcw size={18} />
                Restaurar Padrão
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={onExport}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"
              >
                <Download size={18} />
                Exportar
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={handleImportClick}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"
              >
                <Upload size={18} />
                Importar
              </button>
              
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                accept=".json"
                onChange={handleFileChange}
                className="hidden"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Botão Direita */}
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-6 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-indigo-700 transition-colors shadow-md"
            >
              ✓ Aplicar e Fechar
            </button>

          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: GoogleAdsImporter.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\GoogleAdsImporter.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Upload, CheckCircle, AlertCircle, X, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from 'lucide-react';
import type { Campaign, Product } from '../types';

interface GoogleAdsCampaign {
  originalName: string;
  date: string;
  impressions: number;
  clicks: number;
  spend: number;
  conversions: number;
  revenue: number;
  cpc: number;
  cr: number;
  roas: number;
  roi: number;
  profit: number;
  productId: string | null;
  productName: string | null;
  nicho: string | null;
  suggestedProduct: string;
  suggestedNicho: string;
}

interface GoogleAdsImportData {
  source: string;
  extractedAt: string;
  dateRange: {
    start: string;
    end: string;
  };
  campaigns: GoogleAdsCampaign[];
}

interface Props {
  products: Product[];
  onImport: (campaigns: Campaign[]) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

export const GoogleAdsImporter: React.FC<Props> = ({ products, onImport, onClose }) => {
  const [step, setStep] = useState<'upload' | 'mapping' | 'preview'>('upload');
  const [importData, setImportData] = useState<GoogleAdsImportData | null>(null);
  const [mappings, setMappings] = useState<Map<number, string>>(new Map());
  const [expandedCampaigns, setExpandedCampaigns] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [error, setError] = useState<string>('');

  // Função para calcular similaridade entre strings
  const calculateSimilarity = (str1: string, str2: string): number => {
    const s1 = str1.toLowerCase();
    const s2 = str2.toLowerCase();
    
    // Similaridade exata
    if (s1 === s2) return 1;
    
    // Contém
    if (s1.includes(s2) || s2.includes(s1)) return 0.8;
    
    // Palavras em comum
    const words1 = s1.split(/\s+/);
    const words2 = s2.split(/\s+/);
    const commonWords = words1.filter(w => words2.includes(w));
    
    if (commonWords.length > 0) {
      return 0.5 + (commonWords.length / Math.max(words1.length, words2.length)) * 0.3;
    }
    
    return 0;
  };

  // Função para tentar mapear automaticamente
  const autoMapCampaigns = (data: GoogleAdsImportData) => {
    const newMappings = new Map<number, string>();
    
    data.campaigns.forEach((campaign, index) => {
      let bestMatch: { productId: string; score: number } | null = null;
      
      products.forEach(product => {
        const nameSimilarity = calculateSimilarity(campaign.suggestedProduct, product.name);
        const nichoMatch = campaign.suggestedNicho === product.nicho ? 0.2 : 0;
        const totalScore = nameSimilarity + nichoMatch;
        
        if (totalScore > 0.7 && (!bestMatch || totalScore > bestMatch.score)) {
          bestMatch = { productId: product.id, score: totalScore };
        }
      });
      
      if (bestMatch) {
        newMappings.set(index, bestMatch.productId);
      }
    });
    
    setMappings(newMappings);
  };

  // Handler de upload do arquivo
  const handleFileUpload = (event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = event.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const json = JSON.parse(e.target?.result as string);
        
        // Validar estrutura
        if (!json.source || json.source !== 'google-ads') {
          throw new Error('Arquivo inválido. Use o arquivo exportado pela extensão.');
        }
        
        if (!json.campaigns || !Array.isArray(json.campaigns)) {
          throw new Error('Formato de dados inválido.');
        }
        
        setImportData(json);
        autoMapCampaigns(json);
        setStep('mapping');
        setError('');
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Erro ao ler arquivo');
      }
    };
    
    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  // Handler de mudança de mapeamento
  const handleMappingChange = (campaignIndex: number, productId: string) => {
    const newMappings = new Map(mappings);
    if (productId === '') {
      newMappings.delete(campaignIndex);
    } else {
      newMappings.set(campaignIndex, productId);
    }
    setMappings(newMappings);
  };

  // Toggle expansão de campanha
  const toggleCampaignExpand = (index: number) => {
    const newExpanded = new Set(expandedCampaigns);
    if (newExpanded.has(index)) {
      newExpanded.delete(index);
    } else {
      newExpanded.add(index);
    }
    setExpandedCampaigns(newExpanded);
  };

  // Finalizar importação
  const handleFinishImport = () => {
    if (!importData) return;

    const newCampaigns: Campaign[] = importData.campaigns
      .filter((_, index) => mappings.has(index))
      .map((campaign, index) => {
        const productId = mappings.get(index)!;
        const product = products.find(p => p.id === productId)!;
        
        return {
          id: Date.now() + index,
          date: campaign.date,
          productId: product.id,
          productName: product.name,
          nicho: product.nicho,
          spend: campaign.spend,
          clicks: campaign.clicks,
          conversions: campaign.conversions,
          revenue: campaign.revenue,
          cpc: campaign.cpc,
          cr: campaign.cr,
          roas: campaign.roas,
          roi: campaign.roi,
          profit: campaign.profit
        };
      });

    onImport(newCampaigns);
  };

  // Estatísticas
  const stats = importData ? {
    total: importData.campaigns.length,
    mapped: mappings.size,
    unmapped: importData.campaigns.length - mappings.size,
    autoMapped: Array.from(mappings.keys()).filter(index => {
      const campaign = importData.campaigns[index];
      const product = products.find(p => p.id === mappings.get(index));
      return product && calculateSimilarity(campaign.suggestedProduct, product.name) > 0.7;
    }).length
  } : null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[90vh] overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 text-white p-6 flex justify-between items-center">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
              <Upload size={28} />
              Importar do Google Ads
            </h2>
            <p className="text-purple-100 text-sm mt-1">
              {step === 'upload' && 'Faça upload do arquivo JSON exportado'}
              {step === 'mapping' && 'Associe as campanhas aos seus produtos'}
              {step === 'preview' && 'Revise antes de confirmar'}
            </p>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-2 hover:bg-white hover:bg-opacity-20 rounded-lg transition-colors"
          >
            <X size={24} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Content */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* STEP 1: Upload */}
          {step === 'upload' && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="border-2 border-dashed border-gray-300 rounded-xl p-12 text-center hover:border-purple-500 transition-colors">
                <Upload size={48} className="mx-auto text-gray-400 mb-4" />
                <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800 mb-2">
                  Selecione o arquivo JSON
                </h3>
                <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm mb-4">
                  Arquivo exportado pela extensão Chrome
                </p>
                <label className="inline-block">
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    accept=".json"
                    onChange={handleFileUpload}
                    className="hidden"
                  />
                  <span className="px-6 py-3 bg-purple-600 text-white rounded-lg font-medium cursor-pointer hover:bg-purple-700 transition-colors inline-block">
                    📁 Escolher Arquivo
                  </span>
                </label>
              </div>

              {error && (
                <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-lg p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
                  <AlertCircle className="text-red-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={20} />
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-semibold text-red-800">Erro ao importar</p>
                    <p className="text-red-600 text-sm">{error}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                <h4 className="font-semibold text-blue-900 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                  💡 Como usar:
                </h4>
                <ol className="text-blue-800 text-sm space-y-1 list-decimal list-inside">
                  <li>Instale a extensão Chrome</li>
                  <li>Acesse sua conta Google Ads</li>
                  <li>Clique na extensão e escolha o período</li>
                  <li>Clique em "Extrair Dados"</li>
                  <li>Faça upload do arquivo JSON aqui</li>
                </ol>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 2: Mapping */}
          {step === 'mapping' && importData && stats && (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              {/* Estatísticas */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                  <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-purple-900">{stats.total}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-purple-600">Total de Campanhas</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                  <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-900">{stats.autoMapped}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-green-600">Mapeadas Auto</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                  <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">{stats.mapped}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-blue-600">Prontas p/ Importar</div>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-orange-50 border border-orange-200 rounded-lg p-4">
                  <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-orange-900">{stats.unmapped}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-orange-600">Sem Produto</div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Lista de Campanhas */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {importData.campaigns.map((campaign, index) => {
                  const isExpanded = expandedCampaigns.has(index);
                  const isMapped = mappings.has(index);
                  const selectedProduct = isMapped ? products.find(p => p.id === mappings.get(index)) : null;
                  
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={index}
                      className={`border-2 rounded-lg overflow-hidden transition-all ${
                        isMapped ? 'border-green-200 bg-green-50' : 'border-orange-200 bg-orange-50'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="p-4">
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                          <div className="flex-1">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
                              {isMapped ? (
                                <CheckCircle className="text-green-600 flex-shrink-0" size={20} />
                              ) : (
                                <AlertCircle className="text-orange-600 flex-shrink-0" size={20} />
                              )}
                              <h4 className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{campaign.originalName}</h4>
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-3 mb-3 text-sm">
                              <div>
                                <span className="text-gray-600">Data:</span>{' '}
                                <span className="font-medium">{campaign.date}</span>
                              </div>
                              <div>
                                <span className="text-gray-600">Gasto:</span>{' '}
                                <span className="font-medium">R$ {campaign.spend.toFixed(2)}</span>
                              </div>
                              <div>
                                <span className="text-gray-600">Conversões:</span>{' '}
                                <span className="font-medium">{campaign.conversions}</span>
                              </div>
                              <div>
                                <span className="text-gray-600">ROI:</span>{' '}
                                <span className={`font-medium ${campaign.roi >= 0 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-600'}`}>
                                  {campaign.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 flex-shrink-0">
                                Produto:
                              </label>
                              <select
                                value={mappings.get(index) || ''}
                                onChange={(e) => handleMappingChange(index, e.target.value)}
                                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none text-sm"
                              >
                                <option value="">Selecione um produto...</option>
                                {products.map(product => (
                                  <option key={product.id} value={product.id}>
                                    {product.name} ({product.nicho})
                                  </option>
                                ))}
                              </select>
                              
                              <button
                                onClick={() => toggleCampaignExpand(index)}
                                className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                              >
                                {isExpanded ? <ChevronUp size={18} /> : <ChevronDown size={18} />}
                              </button>
                            </div>

                            {campaign.suggestedProduct && !isMapped && (
                              <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
                                💡 Sugestão: <span className="font-medium">{campaign.suggestedProduct}</span>
                                {campaign.suggestedNicho && ` • Nicho: ${campaign.suggestedNicho}`}
                              </div>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {isExpanded && (
                          <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 text-sm">
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">Impressões</div>
                              <div className="font-semibold">{campaign.impressions.toLocaleString()}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">Cliques</div>
                              <div className="font-semibold">{campaign.clicks.toLocaleString()}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">CPC</div>
                              <div className="font-semibold">R$ {campaign.cpc.toFixed(2)}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">CR</div>
                              <div className="font-semibold">{campaign.cr.toFixed(2)}%</div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">Receita</div>
                              <div className="font-semibold text-green-600">R$ {campaign.revenue.toFixed(2)}</div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">Lucro</div>
                              <div className={`font-semibold ${campaign.profit >= 0 ? 'text-green-600' : 'text-red-600'}`}>
                                R$ {campaign.profit.toFixed(2)}
                              </div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                              <div className="text-gray-600 text-xs">ROAS</div>
                              <div className="font-semibold">{campaign.roas.toFixed(2)}x</div>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="border-t border-gray-200 p-6 bg-gray-50 flex justify-between items-center">
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-6 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-gray-100 transition-colors"
          >
            Cancelar
          </button>

          {step === 'mapping' && stats && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                {stats.mapped} de {stats.total} campanhas prontas
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={handleFinishImport}
                disabled={stats.mapped === 0}
                className={`px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-colors ${
                  stats.mapped > 0
                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 text-white hover:from-purple-700 hover:to-indigo-700'
                    : 'bg-gray-300 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed'
                }`}
              >
                ✅ Importar {stats.mapped} Campanha{stats.mapped !== 1 ? 's' : ''}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
