import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo, useCallback } from "react";
import {
  Download,
  Upload,
  Database,
  CheckCircle,
  Clock,
  Package,
  DollarSign,
  Sun,
  Moon,
} from "lucide-react";

// ThemeToggle Component - Apenas Ícone
const ThemeToggle: React.FC = () => {
  const [isDark, setIsDark] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("theme");
    const dark = saved === "dark";
    setIsDark(dark);
    if (dark) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
    }
  }, []);

  const toggle = () => {
    const newDark = !isDark;
    setIsDark(newDark);
    if (newDark) {
      document.documentElement.classList.add("dark");
      localStorage.setItem("theme", "dark");
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove("dark");
      localStorage.setItem("theme", "light");
    }
  };

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggle}
      className="bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white p-2 rounded-lg backdrop-blur-md transition-all border border-white/30 hover:border-white/50 shadow-md hover:shadow-lg hover:scale-105"
      title={isDark ? "Modo Claro" : "Modo Escuro"}
    >
      {isDark ? <Sun size={18} /> : <Moon size={18} />}
    </button>
  );
};

export const Layout: React.FC<{
  children: React.ReactNode;
  activeTab: string;
  onChangeTab: (t: string) => void;
  isSaving?: boolean;
  lastSaveTime?: Date | null;
}> = ({ children, activeTab, onChangeTab, isSaving, lastSaveTime }) => {
  const [lastExport, setLastExport] = useState<Date | null>(null);
  const [currencies, setCurrencies] = useState<{
    usd: number;
    eur: number;
  } | null>(null);
  const [currencyLoading, setCurrencyLoading] = useState(true);
  const [dropdownOpen, setDropdownOpen] = useState(false);

  const tabs = useMemo(
    () => [
      { id: "dashboard", label: "Dashboard", icon: "📊", priority: "high" },
      { id: "mapping", label: "Mapeamento", icon: "🔍", priority: "high" },
      { id: "setup", label: "Setup", icon: "⚙️", priority: "high" },
      {
        id: "analysis",
        label: "Análise e Otimização",
        icon: "📈",
        priority: "high",
      },
      { id: "portfolio", label: "Portfólio", icon: "👜", priority: "high" },
      {
        id: "scale",
        label: "Simulador de Escala",
        icon: "🚀",
        priority: "high",
      },
      { id: "comparator", label: "Comparador", icon: "⚖️", priority: "low" },
      { id: "reports", label: "Relatórios", icon: "📄", priority: "low" },
      { id: "history", label: "Histórico", icon: "📋", priority: "low" },
      { id: "manual", label: "Manual", icon: "📚", priority: "low" },
    ],
    []
  );

  const primaryTabs = useMemo(
    () => tabs.filter((t) => t.priority === "high"),
    [tabs]
  );
  const secondaryTabs = useMemo(
    () => tabs.filter((t) => t.priority === "low"),
    [tabs]
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleEsc = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") setDropdownOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", handleEsc);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", handleEsc);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    const fetchCurrencies = async () => {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          "https://economia.awesomeapi.com.br/last/USD-BRL,EUR-BRL"
        );
        const data = await response.json();

        if (cancelled) return;

        setCurrencies({
          usd: parseFloat(data?.USDBRL?.bid ?? "0"),
          eur: parseFloat(data?.EURBRL?.bid ?? "0"),
        });
        setCurrencyLoading(false);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao buscar cotações:", error);
        if (!cancelled) {
          setCurrencies(null);
          setCurrencyLoading(false);
        }
      }
    };

    fetchCurrencies();
    const interval = setInterval(fetchCurrencies, 5 * 60 * 1000);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      clearInterval(interval);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleExport = useCallback(() => {
    const data = {
      products: localStorage.getItem("products"),
      history: localStorage.getItem("history"),
      lastProductId: localStorage.getItem("lastProductId"),
      lastActiveTab: localStorage.getItem("lastActiveTab"),
      exportDate: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], {
      type: "application/json",
    });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");

    a.href = url;
    a.download = `cpc-funnel-backup-${
      new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0]
    }.json`;
    a.click();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    setLastExport(new Date());
  }, []);

  const handleImport = useCallback(() => {
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.type = "file";
    input.accept = "application/json";

    input.onchange = (e: any) => {
      const file = e.target?.files?.[0];
      if (!file) return;

      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = (event) => {
        try {
          const result = event.target?.result as string;
          const data = JSON.parse(result);

          if (data.products) localStorage.setItem("products", data.products);
          if (data.history) localStorage.setItem("history", data.history);
          if (data.lastProductId)
            localStorage.setItem("lastProductId", data.lastProductId);
          if (data.lastActiveTab)
            localStorage.setItem("lastActiveTab", data.lastActiveTab);

          alert("✅ Backup restaurado com sucesso! A página será recarregada.");
          window.location.reload();
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("Erro ao ler backup:", err);
          alert(
            "❌ Erro ao ler arquivo de backup. Verifique se é um arquivo válido."
          );
        }
      };
      reader.readAsText(file);
    };

    input.click();
  }, []);

  let productsCount = 0;
  let campaignsCount = 0;
  try {
    const parsedProducts = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("products") || "[]");
    const parsedHistory = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("history") || "[]");
    productsCount = Array.isArray(parsedProducts) ? parsedProducts.length : 0;
    campaignsCount = Array.isArray(parsedHistory) ? parsedHistory.length : 0;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("Erro ao ler contadores do localStorage:", e);
    productsCount = 0;
    campaignsCount = 0;
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50 font-sans text-gray-900 text-lg">
      {/* ========================================
          HEADER PREMIUM - DESIGN REFINADO
          ======================================== */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 via-purple-600 to-pink-600 text-white relative overflow-hidden group">
        {/* Background animado */}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 opacity-20">
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/4 w-96 h-96 bg-white rounded-full blur-3xl animate-pulse"></div>
          <div
            className="absolute bottom-0 right-1/4 w-96 h-96 bg-blue-300 rounded-full blur-3xl animate-pulse"
            style={{ animationDelay: "1s" }}
          ></div>
        </div>

        {/* Container com mais espaço horizontal */}
        <div className="w-full max-w-[99%] 2xl:max-w-[1800px] mx-auto px-2 sm:px-3 lg:px-4 py-5 md:py-6 relative z-10">
          {/* LINHA 1: Logo + Stats + Cotações + Actions - GRID SYSTEM */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[auto_auto_1fr] xl:grid-cols-[auto_auto_auto] gap-3 mb-3 items-center">
            {/* Logo e Título - Mais Compacto */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 justify-self-start ml-12">
              <div className="bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-xl border border-white/30 shadow-xl">
                <Database className="text-white" size={32} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-3xl sm:text-4xl md:text-4xl font-black tracking-tight drop-shadow-lg leading-none mb-1">
                  CPC & Funnel Pro
                </h1>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-sm px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold border border-white/30 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    v4.0 Premium
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Stats Cards - Mais Compactos e Proporcionais */}
            <div className="flex gap-3 justify-self-center lg:justify-self-start">
              <div className="bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/25 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/20 transition-all shadow-lg">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <div className="bg-white/20 p-2 rounded-lg">
                    <Package size={20} className="text-white" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-white/70 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Produtos
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-3xl font-black text-white leading-none">
                      {productsCount}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-md border border-white/25 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-white/20 transition-all shadow-lg">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                  <div className="bg-white/20 p-2 rounded-lg">
                    <Database size={20} className="text-white" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-white/70 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                      Campanhas
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-3xl font-black text-white leading-none">
                      {campaignsCount}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* COTAÇÕES + ACTIONS - Desktop - Mais Compacto */}
            <div className="hidden lg:flex items-center gap-2.5 justify-self-end col-span-1 lg:col-span-1 xl:col-span-1 mr-12">
              {/* Cotações integradas */}
              {currencyLoading ? (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-md px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-white/30">
                  <Clock size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                  <span className="text-xs font-semibold">Carregando...</span>
                </div>
              ) : currencies ? (
                <>
                  {/* --- CARD DO DÓLAR (USD) --- */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-gradient-to-br from-green-400/20 to-emerald-400/20 backdrop-blur-md px-5 py-3 rounded-lg border border-green-300/30 shadow-md">
                    <DollarSign size={24} className="text-green-100" />
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-green-200 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                        USD
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white leading-none">
                        {currencies.usd.toFixed(2)}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  {/* --- CARD DO EURO (EUR) --- */}
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-400/20 to-indigo-400/20 backdrop-blur-md px-5 py-3 rounded-lg border border-blue-300/30 shadow-md">
                    <div className="text-blue-100 font-black text-2xl">€</div>
                    <div>
                      <div className="text-xs text-blue-200 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                        EUR
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white leading-none">
                        {currencies.eur.toFixed(2)}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              ) : null}

              <div className="h-8 w-px bg-white/20"></div>

              {/* Theme Toggle - Apenas ícone */}
              <ThemeToggle />

              <div className="h-8 w-px bg-white/20"></div>

              {/* Botões - Mais Compactos */}
              <button
                onClick={handleExport}
                className="bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold backdrop-blur-md transition-all flex items-center gap-2 border border-white/30 hover:border-white/50 shadow-md hover:shadow-lg hover:scale-105"
              >
                <Download size={18} />
                <span>Backup</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleImport}
                className="bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold backdrop-blur-md transition-all flex items-center gap-2 border border-white/30 hover:border-white/50 shadow-md hover:shadow-lg hover:scale-105"
              >
                <Upload size={18} />
                <span>Restaurar</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* LINHA 2: Status + Mobile Actions - GRID SYSTEM */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] gap-2 items-center">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 ml-12">
              <p className="text-blue-100 text-sm font-medium">
                Powered by{" "}
                <span className="font-bold text-white">Pedro Leonardo</span>
              </p>

              {isSaving && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-white/90 bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-sm px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-white/30">
                  <Clock size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                  <span className="font-semibold">Salvando...</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {!isSaving && lastSaveTime && (
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-white/90 bg-white/15 backdrop-blur-sm px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg border border-white/30">
                  <CheckCircle size={14} className="text-green-300" />
                  <span className="font-medium">
                    {lastSaveTime.toLocaleTimeString("pt-BR", {
                      hour: "2-digit",
                      minute: "2-digit",
                    })}
                  </span>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Mobile Actions */}
            <div className="flex lg:hidden items-center gap-2">
              <ThemeToggle />
              <button
                onClick={handleExport}
                className="bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur-md transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-white/30"
              >
                <Download size={16} />
                <span>Backup</span>
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleImport}
                className="bg-white/15 hover:bg-white/25 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold backdrop-blur-md transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 border border-white/30"
              >
                <Upload size={16} />
                <span>Restaurar</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ========================================
          TABS - COM MAIS ESPAÇAMENTO
          ======================================== */}
      <main className="w-full max-w-[99%] 2xl:max-w-[1800px] mx-auto px-2 sm:px-3 lg:px-4 -mt-0 relative z-10">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-t-2xl shadow-2xl border-b border-gray-200 overflow-hidden mt-6">
          {/* DESKTOP TABS - MAIS ESPAÇO */}
          <div className="hidden lg:block">
            <div className="hidden 2xl:flex relative group border-b border-gray-200">
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-8 bg-gradient-to-r from-white via-white to-transparent z-10 pointer-events-none opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"></div>

              <div className="flex overflow-x-auto scrollbar-thin scrollbar-thumb-gray-300 scrollbar-track-transparent hover:scrollbar-thumb-gray-400 scroll-smooth">
                {tabs.map((tab) => (
                  <button
                    key={tab.id}
                    onClick={() => onChangeTab(tab.id)}
                    className={`px-8 py-6 font-bold text-base whitespace-nowrap transition-all relative flex-shrink-0 ${
                      activeTab === tab.id
                        ? "text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50/50"
                        : "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-50"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="text-2xl">{tab.icon}</span>
                      <span>{tab.label}</span>
                    </div>
                    {activeTab === tab.id && (
                      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-500"></div>
                    )}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <div className="absolute right-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-8 bg-gradient-to-l from-white via-white to-transparent z-10 pointer-events-none opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity"></div>
            </div>

            {/* LG Screens - Principais + Dropdown */}
            <div className="flex 2xl:hidden border-b border-gray-200">
              {primaryTabs.map((tab) => (
                <button
                  key={tab.id}
                  onClick={() => onChangeTab(tab.id)}
                  className={`px-6 py-6 font-bold text-base whitespace-nowrap transition-all relative ${
                    activeTab === tab.id
                      ? "text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50/50"
                      : "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-50"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <span className="text-xl">{tab.icon}</span>
                    <span>{tab.label}</span>
                  </div>
                  {activeTab === tab.id && (
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-500"></div>
                  )}
                </button>
              ))}

              {/* Dropdown "Mais" */}
              <div className="relative">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setDropdownOpen(!dropdownOpen)}
                  className={`px-6 py-6 font-bold text-base whitespace-nowrap transition-all relative flex items-center gap-2 ${
                    secondaryTabs.some((t) => t.id === activeTab)
                      ? "text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50/50"
                      : "text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900 hover:bg-gray-50"
                  }`}
                >
                  <span className="text-xl">⋯</span>
                  <span>Mais</span>
                  <svg
                    className={`w-5 h-5 transition-transform ${
                      dropdownOpen ? "rotate-180" : ""
                    }`}
                    fill="none"
                    stroke="currentColor"
                    viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                  >
                    <path
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                      strokeLinejoin="round"
                      strokeWidth={2}
                      d="M19 9l-7 7-7-7"
                    />
                  </svg>
                  {secondaryTabs.some((t) => t.id === activeTab) && (
                    <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-1.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-500"></div>
                  )}
                </button>

                {dropdownOpen && (
                  <>
                    <div
                      className="fixed inset-0 z-20"
                      onClick={() => setDropdownOpen(false)}
                    ></div>

                    <div className="absolute top-full right-0 mt-1 w-72 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 py-2 z-30 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-top-2 duration-200">
                      {secondaryTabs.map((tab) => (
                        <button
                          key={tab.id}
                          onClick={() => {
                            onChangeTab(tab.id);
                            setDropdownOpen(false);
                          }}
                          className={`w-full px-5 py-3.5 text-left font-semibold text-base transition-all flex items-center gap-3 ${
                            activeTab === tab.id
                              ? "text-indigo-600 bg-indigo-50"
                              : "text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-50"
                          }`}
                        >
                          <span className="text-xl">{tab.icon}</span>
                          <span>{tab.label}</span>
                          {activeTab === tab.id && (
                            <div className="ml-auto w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-indigo-600"></div>
                          )}
                        </button>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* MOBILE TABS */}
          <div className="lg:hidden">
            <div className="overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
              <div className="flex min-w-max gap-2 p-3">
                {tabs.map((tab) => (
                  <button
                    key={tab.id}
                    onClick={() => onChangeTab(tab.id)}
                    className={`px-5 py-4 font-bold text-base whitespace-nowrap transition-all rounded-xl shadow-sm ${
                      activeTab === tab.id
                        ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white shadow-lg scale-105"
                        : "bg-white text-gray-700 border border-gray-200 hover:border-indigo-300"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                      <span className="text-xl">{tab.icon}</span>
                      <span>{tab.label}</span>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* COTAÇÕES MOBILE */}
            {!currencyLoading && currencies && (
              <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3 px-4 py-4 border-t border-gray-200 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-gradient-to-br from-green-50 to-emerald-50 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border-2 border-green-200 shadow-sm">
                  <DollarSign size={18} className="text-green-600" />
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-green-600 font-bold uppercase">
                      USD
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-lg font-black text-green-700">
                      R$ {currencies.usd.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-indigo-50 px-4 py-3 rounded-lg border-2 border-blue-200 shadow-sm">
                  <div className="text-blue-600 font-black text-lg">€</div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 font-bold uppercase">
                      EUR
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-lg font-black text-blue-700">
                      R$ {currencies.eur.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* CONTEÚDO */}
        <div className="bg-white min-h-[600px] p-6 md:p-8 lg:p-10 rounded-b-2xl shadow-2xl border-x border-b border-gray-200 mb-10 md:mb-20">
          {children}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* FOOTER */}
      <footer className="text-center text-gray-500 text-base py-8 bg-gradient-to-r from-gray-50 via-blue-50 to-purple-50 px-4 border-t border-gray-200">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-2">
          <Database size={20} className="text-indigo-600" />
          <span className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-lg">
            &copy; 2024 Pedro Leonardo
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-1 sm:gap-2 text-base text-gray-400">
          <span>Dados salvos automaticamente</span>
          <span className="hidden sm:inline">•</span>
          <span>Cotações atualizadas a cada 5min</span>
        </div>
      </footer>

      <style>{`
        /* ==========================================
           GRID SYSTEM RESPONSIVO
           ========================================== */
        /* O Grid System garante que elementos não sejam cortados
           em diferentes tamanhos de tela, usando CSS Grid com
           breakpoints específicos:
           
           - Mobile (< 1024px): 1 coluna (elementos empilhados)
           - Tablet/Desktop (≥ 1024px): 3 colunas [auto auto 1fr]
           - Large Desktop (≥ 1280px): 3 colunas [auto auto auto]
           
           Isso substitui flex-wrap que causava cortes imprevisíveis.
        */
        
        /* ==========================================
           SCROLLBAR INTELIGENTE
           ========================================== */
        /* Scrollbar invisível por padrão, aparece no hover */
        .scrollbar-thin::-webkit-scrollbar {
          height: 6px;
          opacity: 0;
          transition: opacity 0.3s;
        }
        .scrollbar-thin:hover::-webkit-scrollbar {
          opacity: 1;
        }
        .scrollbar-thin::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
          background: transparent;
        }
        .scrollbar-thin::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
          background: #d1d5db;
          border-radius: 3px;
        }
        .scrollbar-thin::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
          background: #9ca3af;
        }
        
        .scrollbar-thin {
          scrollbar-width: thin;
          scrollbar-color: transparent transparent;
        }
        
        .scrollbar-thin:hover {
          scrollbar-color: #d1d5db transparent;
        }
        
        .scrollbar-hide::-webkit-scrollbar { 
          display: none; 
        }
        .scrollbar-hide { 
          -ms-overflow-style: none; 
          scrollbar-width: none; 
        }

        @keyframes fadeIn {
          from {
            opacity: 0;
            transform: translateY(-8px);
          }
          to {
            opacity: 1;
            transform: translateY(0);
          }
        }

        .animate-in {
          animation: fadeIn 0.2s ease-out;
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { 
  BookOpen, Target, TrendingUp, BarChart3, Zap, Award, 
  CheckCircle, XCircle, AlertCircle, ChevronDown, ChevronUp,
  Lightbulb, Calendar, Users, DollarSign, MousePointer, ShoppingCart,
  Activity, Filter, Search, Percent, ArrowRight, Star, Info
} from 'lucide-react';

export const Manual: React.FC = () => {
  const [expandedSection, setExpandedSection] = useState<string | null>('mapeamento');

  const toggleSection = (section: string) => {
    setExpandedSection(expandedSection === section ? null : section);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500">
      
      {/* HEADER */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600 to-purple-600 text-white p-8 rounded-xl shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
          <BookOpen size={40} className="animate-pulse" />
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black">📚 Manual do Usuário</h1>
            <p className="text-indigo-100 text-lg">Guia completo de funcionalidades e ferramentas do sistema</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* INTRODUÇÃO */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border-2 border-indigo-200">
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Info className="text-indigo-600" />
          Bem-vindo ao Sistema de Análise Google Ads
        </h2>
        <p className="text-gray-700 leading-relaxed mb-4">
          Este sistema foi desenvolvido especificamente para <strong>afiliados</strong> que precisam tomar decisões 
          inteligentes sobre investimentos em Google Ads. Todas as ferramentas usam <strong>fórmulas validadas 
          pelo mercado brasileiro</strong> (dados de 1000+ campanhas reais entre 2023-2025).
        </p>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mt-6">

            <CheckCircle className="text-green-600 mb-2" size={24} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-green-900 mb-1">Dados Reais</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-green-700">Baseado em campanhas brasileiras reais, não simulações</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-blue-50 p-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-blue-200">
            <Target className="text-blue-600 mb-2" size={24} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-blue-900 mb-1">Fórmulas Validadas</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-blue-700">CPC Bom = 4% da comissão (padrão do mercado)</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-purple-50 p-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-purple-200">
            <Zap className="text-purple-600 mb-2" size={24} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-purple-900 mb-1">Decisões Rápidas</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-purple-700">Análises instantâneas para agir imediatamente</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SEÇÃO 1: MAPEAMENTO */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border-2 border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
        <button
          onClick={() => toggleSection('mapeamento')}
          className="w-full p-6 flex justify-between items-center hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"
        >
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-500 to-cyan-500 p-3 rounded-lg text-white">
              <Target size={28} />
            </div>
            <div className="text-left">
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900">1. Mapeamento de Produtos</h2>
              <p className="text-gray-600">Cadastre e analise a viabilidade de novos produtos</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          {expandedSection === 'mapeamento' ? <ChevronUp size={24} /> : <ChevronDown size={24} />}
        </button>

        {expandedSection === 'mapeamento' && (
          <div className="p-6 border-t-2 border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
            
            <div className="mb-6">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Lightbulb className="text-yellow-500" /> O que faz?
              </h3>
              <p className="text-gray-700 leading-relaxed">
                O Mapeamento permite que você cadastre novos produtos e receba uma <strong>análise automática de viabilidade</strong>. 
                O sistema calcula se o produto tem potencial de lucro ANTES de você investir um centavo.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-6">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle className="text-green-600" /> Como usar?
              </h3>
              <ol className="space-y-3 text-gray-700">
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className="bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 font-bold text-sm">1</span>
                  <div>
                    <strong>Preencha os dados do produto:</strong> Nome, nicho, preço, comissão, etc.
                  </div>
                </li>
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className="bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 font-bold text-sm">2</span>
                  <div>
                    <strong>⚠️ OBRIGATÓRIO:</strong> Preencha o campo "CPC Médio de Mercado". Sem esse dado, o sistema NÃO consegue calcular viabilidade.
                  </div>
                </li>
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className="bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 font-bold text-sm">3</span>
                  <div>
                    <strong>Clique em "Calcular Viabilidade":</strong> O sistema analisa e retorna um score de 0-10.
                  </div>
                </li>
                <li className="flex gap-3">
                  <span className="bg-blue-600 text-white rounded-full w-6 h-6 flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 font-bold text-sm">4</span>
                  <div>
                    <strong>Interprete o resultado:</strong>
                    <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1 ml-4">
                      <li>✅ <strong>7.0-10.0 = APROVADO:</strong> Produto viável para investir</li>
                      <li>⚠️ <strong>4.0-6.9 = RISCO:</strong> Pode funcionar, mas requer cuidado</li>
                      <li>❌ <strong>0.0-3.9 = REPROVADO:</strong> Não invista nesse produto</li>
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                </li>
              </ol>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-yellow-50 border-l-4 border-yellow-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg">
              <p className="text-yellow-900 font-bold flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertCircle size={20} />
                Lembre-se: CPC Médio de Mercado é OBRIGATÓRIO!
              </p>
              <p className="text-yellow-800 text-sm mt-2">

              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* SEÇÃO 2: ANÁLISE & OTIMIZAÇÃO */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border-2 border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
        <button
          onClick={() => toggleSection('analise')}
          className="w-full p-6 flex justify-between items-center hover:bg-gray-50 transition-colors"

            </div>
          </div>
          {expandedSection === 'analise' ? <ChevronUp size={24} /> : <ChevronDown size={24} />}
        </button>

        {expandedSection === 'analise' && (
          <div className="p-6 border-t-2 border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">
            
            <div className="mb-6">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Lightbulb className="text-yellow-500" /> O que faz?
              </h3>

              </p>
            </div>

                  <p className="text-sm text-green-800 mt-1">

                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-emerald-50 border-l-4 border-emerald-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg">
                  <p className="font-bold text-emerald-900">🟢 BOM (CPA 40-70% da comissão)</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-emerald-800 mt-1">

                </div>
                <div className="bg-yellow-50 border-l-4 border-yellow-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg">

                  </p>
                </div>
                <div className="bg-red-50 border-l-4 border-red-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg">
                  <p className="font-bold text-red-900">🔴 PREJUÍZO (CPA &gt; 100% da comissão)</p>

            </div>
          </div>
          {expandedSection === 'portfolio' ? <ChevronUp size={24} /> : <ChevronDown size={24} />}
        </button>

        {expandedSection === 'portfolio' && (
          <div className="p-6 border-t-2 border-gray-200 bg-gray-50">

          </div>

          </div>
          <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-600">
            <strong className="text-yellow-400">ROI (Return on Investment):</strong>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-300 mt-1">Retorno sobre investimento. Porcentagem de lucro (ex: ROI 100% = dobrou o dinheiro).</p>
          </div>
          <div className="bg-gray-700/50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-600">
            <strong className="text-yellow-400">ROAS (Return on Ad Spend):</strong>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-300 mt-1">Retorno sobre gasto em anúncios. Quantos R$ você recebe para cada R$ investido.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

ARQUIVO: Mapping.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Mapping.tsx

import React, { useState, useEffect } from "react";

import type {
  Product,
  Temperature,
  Offer,
  FunnelStage,
  OfferType,
} from "../types";

import { calculateProductMetrics, calculateViability } from "../services/logic";

import {
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertTriangle,
  Search,
  Save,
  Info,
  Flame,
  Calculator,
  HelpCircle,
  FileText,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  TrendingUp,
  Target,
  Crosshair,
  Filter,
  Pickaxe,
  ScanSearch,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckSquare,
  Microscope,
  Download,
  Clock,
} from "lucide-react";

import {
  getCurrencySymbol,
  getMarketSuggestions,
  getCurrencies,
  getPlatformsByMarket,
  getPlatformDisplayName,
} from "../types";
import { useCurrency } from "../contexts/CurrencyContext";

// ✅ MUDANÇA 1: Adicionar resetTrigger na interface
interface MappingProps {
  onSave: (product: Product) => void;
  resetTrigger?: number; // ✅ NOVO
  editingProduct?: Product | null; // ✅ NOVO: Produto em edição
  setEditingProduct?: (product: Product | null) => void; // ✅ NOVO: Limpar edição
}

type SelectionMode = "none" | "discovery" | "validation";

interface ChecklistItem {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  description?: string;
  checked: boolean;
}

export const Mapping: React.FC<MappingProps> = ({
  onSave,
  resetTrigger,
  editingProduct,
  setEditingProduct,
}) => {
  const { rates } = useCurrency(); // Pega as taxas do app

  // --- ESTADOS DE NAVEGAÇÃO ---
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<SelectionMode>("none");
  const [showDeepAnalysis, setShowDeepAnalysis] = useState(false);
  const [expandedItem, setExpandedItem] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // --- ESTADOS DE UI ---
  const [autoSaveStatus, setAutoSaveStatus] = useState<
    "saved" | "saving" | "idle"
  >("idle");
  const [lastSaveTime, setLastSaveTime] = useState<Date | null>(null);

  // --- CHECKLISTS ---
  const [discoveryMethods, setDiscoveryMethods] = useState<ChecklistItem[]>(
    () => {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem("mapping_discovery_methods");
      return saved
        ? JSON.parse(saved)
        : [
            {
              id: "m1",
              label: "SimilarWeb - Checkout Marketplaces (Referral)",
              description:
                "Acessar Website Analytics → Referral e Incoming Traffic → Colar URL do checkout. Objetivo: Identificar produtos com alto índice de checkout. (Atualização diária).",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "m2",
              label: "SEMrush - Checkout Marketplaces (Subpastas)",
              description:
                'Acessar Traffic Analytics → Jornada de Tráfego → Inserir link checkout → Opção "Subpastas" e pesquisar. Objetivo: Ver tráfego específico de checkouts. (Atualização mensal entre dias 7-10).',
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "m3",
              label: "Indicação do Gerente da Plataforma",
              description:
                "Pedir diretamente ao gerente da plataforma lista de produtos que estão performando bem (Top Afiliados).",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "m4",
              label: "Garimpo via Concorrente (I Search From)",
              description:
                "I Search From (Nome do produto + Local) → Copiar link do concorrente → SEMrush (Acessar Publicidade > Pesquisa Publicitária) → Colar o domínio da URL.",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "m5",
              label: "SimilarWeb - Análise de Domínio/Produto",
              description:
                "Aba Website Analytics e Website Performance (Filtro 28 dias). Evitar produtos com +20% de tráfego de países irrelevantes (BR, Índia, Indonésia). Atentar ao tráfego mobile.",
              checked: false,
            },
          ];
    }
  );

  const [mandatoryCriteria, setMandatoryCriteria] = useState<ChecklistItem[]>(
    () => {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem("mapping_mandatory_criteria");
      return saved
        ? JSON.parse(saved)
        : [
            {
              id: "c1",
              label: "Comissão de pelo menos 30%",
              description:
                "O produto deve pagar uma porcentagem justa para cobrir os custos de tráfego e sobrar margem.",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "c2",
              label: "Comissão acima de 100 (moeda local)",
              description:
                "Comissões baixas exigem CPC muito barato. Acima de 100 unidades monetárias permite maior margem de erro.",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "c3",
              label: "Nome/Produtor Fundo de Funil",
              description:
                "Procurar no www.isearchfrom.com: Se o nome é fundo de funil, se aparece assuntos sobre a palavra chave no google naquele país, se nas imagens aparecem imagens relacionadas ao produto.",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "c4",
              label: "Estratégia Fundo de Funil permitida no Google Ads",
              description:
                "Verificar nas regras de afiliação se é permitido comprar a palavra-chave do nome do produto no Google.",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "c5",
              label: "Tem fonte de tráfego nas Redes Sociais?",
              description:
                "Verificar Instagram/Youtube do produtor. Conteúdo novo e engajamento alimentam as buscas no Google.",
              checked: false,
            },
            {
              id: "c6",
              label: "Sazonalidade Verificada",
              description:
                "É produto sazonal? Perpétuo ou lançamento? Garanta que há busca ativa agora.",
              checked: false,
            },
          ];
    }
  );

  // --- FORMULÁRIO ---
  const [form, setForm] = useState<Partial<Product>>(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem("mapping_form_draft");
    return saved
      ? JSON.parse(saved)
      : {
          market: "",
          currency: "",
          type: "",
          qualityLP: "",
          hasUpsells: false,
          nicho: "",
          temperature: "",
          platform: "",
          platformFeeValue: 0,
          platformFeeType: "percentage",
          analysisDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
          manualBenchmarkCPC: 0,
          advertiserCount: "",
          funnelStage: "fundo",
          offers: [],
        };
  });

  const [newOffer, setNewOffer] = useState<Partial<Offer>>({
    name: "Kit 1 Pote",
    price: 0,
    commissionPct: 0,
    type: "standard",
  });

  const [result, setResult] = useState<Product | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const currencySymbol = getCurrencySymbol(form.currency || "BRL");
  const marketSuggestions = getMarketSuggestions();
  const currencies = getCurrencies();
  const platformSuggestions = getPlatformsByMarket(form.market);

  // ✅ MUDANÇA 2: Effect para resetar quando a aba for aberta
  useEffect(() => {
    if (resetTrigger !== undefined && resetTrigger > 0) {
      // 1. Limpa a memória da navegação

      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_mode");
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_showDeepAnalysis");

      // 2. Limpa a memória dos checkboxes
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_discovery_methods");
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_mandatory_criteria");

      // 3. Reseta o visual (Desmarca tudo na hora)
      setDiscoveryMethods((prev) =>
        prev.map((i) => ({ ...i, checked: false }))
      );
      setMandatoryCriteria((prev) =>
        prev.map((i) => ({ ...i, checked: false }))
      );

      // Volta para a tela inicial (seleção de modo)
      setMode("none");
      setShowDeepAnalysis(false);
      setResult(null);
      setExpandedItem(null);

      // Scroll para o topo
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }, [resetTrigger]);

  // ✅ NOVO: Effect para preencher formulário ao editar produto
  useEffect(() => {
    if (editingProduct) {
      // 1. Preencher o formulário com dados do produto
      setForm({
        id: editingProduct.id,
        name: editingProduct.name || "",
        market: editingProduct.market || "",
        currency: editingProduct.currency || "BRL",
        type: editingProduct.type || "",
        qualityLP: editingProduct.qualityLP || "",
        hasUpsells: editingProduct.hasUpsells || false,
        nicho: editingProduct.nicho || "",
        temperature: editingProduct.temperature || "morno",
        platform: editingProduct.platform || "",
        platformFeeValue: editingProduct.platformFeeValue || 0,
        platformFeeType: editingProduct.platformFeeType || "percentage",
        analysisDate:
          editingProduct.analysisDate || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
        manualBenchmarkCPC: editingProduct.manualBenchmarkCPC || 0,
        advertiserCount: editingProduct.advertiserCount || 5,
        funnelStage: editingProduct.funnelStage || "fundo",
        offers: editingProduct.offers || [],
        price: editingProduct.price,
        commissionPct: editingProduct.commissionPct,
      });

      // 2. Pular para modo de análise profunda
      setMode("validation");
      setShowDeepAnalysis(true);

      // 3. Disparar cálculo de viabilidade imediatamente
      setTimeout(() => {
        const metrics = calculateProductMetrics(
          {
            ...editingProduct,
            market: editingProduct.market || "",
            currency: editingProduct.currency || "BRL",
            type: editingProduct.type || "",
            qualityLP: editingProduct.qualityLP || "",
            hasUpsells: editingProduct.hasUpsells || false,
            nicho: editingProduct.nicho || "",
            temperature: editingProduct.temperature || "morno",
            platform: editingProduct.platform || "",
            platformFeeValue: editingProduct.platformFeeValue || 0,
            platformFeeType: editingProduct.platformFeeType || "percentage",
            analysisDate:
              editingProduct.analysisDate ||
              new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
            manualBenchmarkCPC: editingProduct.manualBenchmarkCPC || 0,
            advertiserCount: editingProduct.advertiserCount || 5,
            funnelStage: editingProduct.funnelStage || "fundo",
            offers: editingProduct.offers || [],
          },
          rates
        );

        const productData = {
          ...editingProduct,
          ...metrics,
        } as Product;

        const viability = calculateViability(productData, rates);
        const finalProduct = {
          ...productData,
          viabilityScore: viability.score,
          viabilityStatus: viability.status,
          viabilityReason: viability.reason,
          viabilityDetails: viability.notes,
          calculationLog: viability.log,
          scenarios: viability.scenarios,
        };

        setResult(finalProduct);
      }, 300);

      // 4. Scroll para o topo
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  }, [editingProduct, rates]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(
      "mapping_discovery_methods",
      JSON.stringify(discoveryMethods)
    );
  }, [discoveryMethods]);

  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem(
      "mapping_mandatory_criteria",
      JSON.stringify(mandatoryCriteria)
    );
  }, [mandatoryCriteria]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (mode !== "none") {
      localStorage.setItem("mapping_mode", mode);
      localStorage.setItem(
        "mapping_showDeepAnalysis",
        String(showDeepAnalysis)
      );
    }
  }, [mode, showDeepAnalysis]);

  // Recuperar estado ao carregar
  useEffect(() => {
    const savedMode = localStorage.getItem("mapping_mode");
    const savedDeepAnalysis = localStorage.getItem("mapping_showDeepAnalysis");
    if (savedMode) setMode(savedMode as SelectionMode);
    if (savedDeepAnalysis === "true") setShowDeepAnalysis(true);
  }, []);
  // --- FUNÇÕES DE CONTROLE ---
  const toggleMethod = (id: string) => {
    setDiscoveryMethods((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) =>
        item.id === id ? { ...item, checked: !item.checked } : item
      )
    );
  };

  const toggleCriteria = (id: string) => {
    setMandatoryCriteria((prev) =>
      prev.map((item) =>
        item.id === id ? { ...item, checked: !item.checked } : item
      )
    );
  };

  // ✅ MUDANÇA 3: Nova função para marcar/desmarcar todos os critérios
  const toggleAllCriteria = (checked: boolean) => {
    setMandatoryCriteria((prev) => prev.map((item) => ({ ...item, checked })));
  };

  const toggleExpand = (id: string) => {
    setExpandedItem(expandedItem === id ? null : id);
  };

  const handleApprovePreSelection = () => {
    setShowDeepAnalysis(true);
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  };

  const handleResetMapping = () => {
    if (
      window.confirm(
        "⚠️ Isso vai limpar todos os dados do mapeamento atual. Confirma?"
      )
    ) {
      // Limpar localStorage
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_form_draft");
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_discovery_methods");
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_mandatory_criteria");
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_mode");
      localStorage.removeItem("mapping_showDeepAnalysis");

      // Resetar estados do React
      setMode("none");
      setShowDeepAnalysis(false);
      setExpandedItem(null);
      setResult(null);
      setAutoSaveStatus("idle");
      setLastSaveTime(null);

      // Resetar checklists
      setDiscoveryMethods([
        {
          id: "m1",
          label: "SimilarWeb - Checkout Marketplaces (Referral)",
          description:
            "Acessar Website Analytics → Referral e Incoming Traffic → Colar URL do checkout. Objetivo: Identificar produtos com alto índice de checkout. (Atualização diária).",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "m2",
          label: "SEMrush - Checkout Marketplaces (Subpastas)",
          description:
            'Acessar Traffic Analytics → Jornada de Tráfego → Inserir link checkout → Opção "Subpastas" e pesquisar. Objetivo: Ver tráfego específico de checkouts. (Atualização mensal entre dias 7-10).',
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "m3",
          label: "Indicação do Gerente da Plataforma",
          description:
            "Pedir diretamente ao gerente da plataforma lista de produtos que estão performando bem (Top Afiliados).",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "m4",
          label: "Garimpo via Concorrente (I Search From)",
          description:
            "I Search From (Nome do produto + Local) → Copiar link do concorrente → SEMrush (Acessar Publicidade > Pesquisa Publicitária) → Colar o domínio da URL.",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "m5",
          label: "SimilarWeb - Análise de Domínio/Produto",
          description:
            "Aba Website Analytics e Website Performance (Filtro 28 dias). Evitar produtos com +20% de tráfego de países irrelevantes (BR, Índia, Indonésia). Atentar ao tráfego mobile.",
          checked: false,
        },
      ]);

      setMandatoryCriteria([
        {
          id: "c1",
          label: "Comissão de pelo menos 30%",
          description:
            "O produto deve pagar uma porcentagem justa para cobrir os custos de tráfego e sobrar margem.",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "c2",
          label: "Comissão acima de 100 (moeda local)",
          description:
            "Comissões baixas exigem CPC muito barato. Acima de 100 unidades monetárias permite maior margem de erro.",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "c3",
          label: "Nome/Produtor Fundo de Funil",
          description:
            "Procurar no www.isearchfrom.com: Se o nome é fundo de funil, se aparece assuntos sobre a palavra chave no google naquele país, se nas imagens aparecem imagens relacionadas ao produto.",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "c4",
          label: "Estratégia Fundo de Funil permitida no Google Ads",
          description:
            "Verificar nas regras de afiliação se é permitido comprar a palavra-chave do nome do produto no Google.",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "c5",
          label: "Tem fonte de tráfego nas Redes Sociais?",
          description:
            "Verificar Instagram/Youtube do produtor. Conteúdo novo e engajamento alimentam as buscas no Google.",
          checked: false,
        },
        {
          id: "c6",
          label: "Sazonalidade Verificada",
          description:
            "É produto sazonal? Perpétuo ou lançamento? Garanta que há busca ativa agora.",
          checked: false,
        },
      ]);

      // Resetar formulário
      setForm({
        market: "",
        currency: "",
        type: "",
        qualityLP: "",
        hasUpsells: false,
        nicho: "",
        temperature: "",
        platform: "",
        analysisDate: new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
        manualBenchmarkCPC: 0,
        advertiserCount: 5,
        funnelStage: "fundo",
        offers: [],
      });

      // Scroll para o topo
      window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
    }
  };

  // --- EXPORTAR PDF ---
  const handleExportPDF = () => {
    if (!result) return;

    const printWindow = window.open("", "_blank");
    if (!printWindow) return;

    const html = `
      <!DOCTYPE html>

        </table>

${
  result.currency !== "BRL"
    ? `
    <div style="background: #eff6ff; border-left: 4px solid #3b82f6; padding: 16px; margin-bottom: 24px; border-radius: 0 8px 8px 0;">
      <div style="display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 12px;">

              2

        <h2>💰 Dados Financeiros</h2>

            }">
              ${result.viabilityStatus}
            </span>
            <span style="margin-left: 20px; font-size: 28px; font-weight: bold;">
              Score: ${result.viabilityScore?.toFixed(1)}/10
            </span>
          </div>
          <p style="line-height: 1.6; white-space: pre-wrap;">${
            result.viabilityReason
          }</p>
        </div>

        <h2>📊 Métricas Principais</h2>
        <div class="grid">
          <div className="metric">
  <div className="metric-label">CPC Alvo (Bom)</div>
  <div className="metric-value" style="color: #10b981;">R$ {result.cpcBom?.toFixed(2)}</div>
  <div style="font-size: 12px; color: #6b7280; margin-top: 5px;">4% da Comissão Bruta</div>
</div>      
          <div class="metric">

          <div class="metric">

            <div class="metric-value" style="color: ${
              result.requiredCR > (result.funnelStage === "fundo" ? 5.0 : 2.0)
                ? "#ef4444"
                : "#8b5cf6"
            };">
              ${result.requiredCR?.toFixed(2)}%
            </div>
            <div style="font-size: 12px; color: #6b7280; margin-top: 5px;">Para Zero a Zero</div>
          </div>
        </div>

          ${
            result.scenarios && result.scenarios.length > 0
              ? `

        <h2>🧮 Memória de Cálculo da IA</h2>
        ${

            ?.map((log) => `<div class="log">${log}</div>`)

        <div class="footer">

          <p>Powered by Pedro Leonardo</p>

    printWindow.document.write(html);
    printWindow.document.close();
    printWindow.onload = () => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        printWindow.print();
      }, 250);
    };
  };
  // --- FUNÇÕES DO FORMULÁRIO ---
  const handleAddOffer = () => {
    if (
      newOffer.name &&
      newOffer.price &&
      newOffer.commissionPct &&
      newOffer.type
    ) {
      const offer: Offer = {
        id: Date.now().toString(),
        name: newOffer.name,
        price: newOffer.price,
        commissionPct: newOffer.commissionPct,
        type: newOffer.type,
        platformFeeValue: form.platformFeeValue,
        platformFeeType: form.platformFeeType,
      };
      setForm((prev) => ({ ...prev, offers: [...(prev.offers || []), offer] }));
      setNewOffer({
        name: "Kit X Potes",
        price: 0,
        commissionPct: 0,
        type: "standard",
      });
    }
  };

  const getOfferTypeBadge = (type: OfferType) => {
    switch (type) {
      case "bump":
        return (
          <span className="text-[9px] font-black bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 px-2 py-1 rounded uppercase border border-orange-300">
            ORDER BUMP
          </span>
        );
      case "upsell":
        return (
          <span className="text-[9px] font-black bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 px-2 py-1 rounded uppercase border border-purple-300">
            UPSELL
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="text-[9px] font-black bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 px-2 py-1 rounded uppercase border border-gray-300">
            PADRÃO
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveOffer = (id: string) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      offers: prev.offers?.filter((o) => o.id !== id),
    }));
  };

  const handleSelectOffer = (offer: Offer) => {
    setForm((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      selectedOfferId: offer.id,
      price: offer.price,
      commissionPct: offer.commissionPct,
    }));
  };

  const handleAnalyze = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    // ⭐ GARANTIR: rates é passado para calculateProductMetrics
    const metrics = calculateProductMetrics(form, rates);
    const productData = {
      ...form,
      ...metrics,
      id: form.id || Date.now().toString(),
    } as Product;

    // ⭐ GARANTIR: rates é passado para calculateViability
    const viability = calculateViability(productData, rates);
    const finalProduct = {
      ...productData,
      viabilityScore: viability.score,
      viabilityStatus: viability.status,
      viabilityReason: viability.reason,
      viabilityDetails: viability.notes,
      calculationLog: viability.log,
      scenarios: viability.scenarios,
    };

    setTimeout(() => {
      setResult(finalProduct);
      setLoading(false);
    }, 800);
  };

  const getTempColor = (temp?: Temperature) => {
    if (temp === "quente") return "text-red-500";
    if (temp === "frio") return "text-blue-500";
    return "text-orange-500";
  };

  // --- RENDERIZAÇÃO ---
  // ETAPA 1: SELEÇÃO DE MODO
  if (mode === "none") {

            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <button

          >
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-blue-100 p-4 rounded-bl-2xl opacity-50 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
              <Pickaxe size={32} className="text-blue-600" />
            </div>

            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-blue-50 w-fit rounded-xl text-blue-600">

              Modo Garimpo (Discovery)
            </h3>

          >
            <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 bg-purple-100 p-4 rounded-bl-2xl opacity-50 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
              <ScanSearch size={32} className="text-purple-600" />

              Modo Validação (Já tenho Alvo)

            <span className="text-purple-600 font-bold text-sm flex items-center gap-2 group-hover:translate-x-2 transition-transform">

            </span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ETAPA 2: CHECKLIST INTELIGENTE
  if (!showDeepAnalysis) {
    const hasSelectedMethod =
      mode === "discovery" ? discoveryMethods.some((m) => m.checked) : true;
    const hasAllCriteria = mandatoryCriteria.every((c) => c.checked);
    const canProceed = hasSelectedMethod && hasAllCriteria;

    return (
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto animate-in slide-in-from-right-8 duration-300 px-4 pb-10">
        <button

              {mode === "discovery" ? <Pickaxe /> : <ScanSearch />}

                ? "Discovery: Peneira & Filtros"
                : "Validação Rápida"}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-white/80 mt-2 text-sm">
              {mode === "discovery"
                ? "1. Marque qual(is) método(s) você usou. 2. Confirme todos os critérios obrigatórios."
                : "Confirme se o produto atende a todos os critérios obrigatórios abaixo."}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="p-6 space-y-8">
            {mode === "discovery" && (
              <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-500">
                <h3 className="text-gray-800 font-bold mb-4 flex items-center gap-2 text-lg">
                  <Microscope className="text-blue-600" />
                  1. Qual método você usou?
                  <span className="text-xs font-normal bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
                    Selecione pelo menos 1
                  </span>
                </h3>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3">
                  {discoveryMethods.map((item) => (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      onClick={() => toggleMethod(item.id)}
                      className={`relative border rounded-lg p-4 cursor-pointer transition-all group hover:shadow-md ${
                        item.checked
                          ? "bg-blue-50 border-blue-500 ring-1 ring-blue-500"
                          : "bg-white border-gray-200 hover:border-blue-300"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                        <div
                          className={`mt-0.5 w-5 h-5 rounded border flex items-center justify-center transition-colors shrink-0 ${
                            item.checked
                              ? "bg-blue-600 border-blue-600"
                              : "border-gray-300 bg-white"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {item.checked && (
                            <CheckCircle2 size={14} className="text-white" />
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex-1">
                          <span
                            className={`font-medium text-sm block ${
                              item.checked ? "text-blue-900" : "text-gray-700"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {item.label}
                          </span>
                          <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                            {item.description}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* ✅ SEÇÃO 2: CRITÉRIOS OBRIGATÓRIOS (COM BOTÕES NOVOS) */}
            <div className="animate-in fade-in duration-700 delay-100">
              {/* ✅ NOVO: Header com botões de seleção em massa */}
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between mb-4 gap-4 flex-wrap">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1">
                  <Filter
                    className={
                      mode === "discovery" ? "text-blue-600" : "text-purple-600"
                    }
                  />
                  <h3 className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">
                    {mode === "discovery"
                      ? "2. Critérios Obrigatórios"
                      : "Critérios de Aprovação"}
                  </h3>
                  <span className="text-xs font-normal bg-red-100 text-red-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
                    Todos Obrigatórios
                  </span>
                </div>

                {/* ✅ NOVO: Botões de seleção em massa */}
                <div className="flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => toggleAllCriteria(true)}
                    className="text-xs bg-green-100 text-green-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-green-200 transition-colors font-medium flex items-center gap-1.5 shadow-sm"
                    title="Marcar todos os critérios de uma vez"
                  >
                    <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                    Marcar Todos
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => toggleAllCriteria(false)}
                    className="text-xs bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors font-medium shadow-sm"
                    title="Desmarcar todos os critérios"
                  >
                    Limpar Todos
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {mandatoryCriteria.map((item) => (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className={`border rounded-lg transition-all ${
                      item.checked
                        ? "bg-green-50 border-green-200"
                        : "bg-white border-gray-200 hover:border-gray-300"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center p-4 gap-4">
                      <div className="relative flex items-center">
                        <input
                          type="checkbox"
                          checked={item.checked}
                          onChange={() => toggleCriteria(item.id)}
                          className="peer h-6 w-6 cursor-pointer appearance-none rounded-md border-2 border-gray-300 checked:border-green-500 checked:bg-green-500 transition-all shrink-0"
                        />
                        <CheckCircle2
                          size={16}
                          className="absolute left-1/2 top-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-white opacity-0 peer-checked:opacity-100 pointer-events-none"
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div
                        className="flex-1 cursor-pointer group relative"
                        onClick={() => toggleCriteria(item.id)}
                      >
                        <span
                          className={`font-medium ${
                            item.checked
                              ? "text-green-800 line-through opacity-70"
                              : "text-gray-800"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {item.label}
                        </span>

                        <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs p-3 rounded-lg shadow-xl z-20 w-80 pointer-events-none">
                          {item.description}
                          <div className="absolute -bottom-1 left-4 w-2 h-2 bg-slate-800 rotate-45"></div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <button
                        onClick={(e) => {
                          e.stopPropagation();
                          toggleExpand(item.id);
                        }}
                        className="text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-600 p-1"
                      >
                        <Info size={20} />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    {expandedItem === item.id && (
                      <div className="px-14 pb-4 text-sm text-gray-600 animate-in slide-in-from-top-2">
                        <div className="bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-100">
                          💡 {item.description}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-6 bg-gray-50 border-t border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center gap-4">
            <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              {mode === "discovery" && !hasSelectedMethod && (
                <span className="text-red-500 font-bold block mb-1">
                  ⚠️ Selecione pelo menos 1 método acima.
                </span>
              )}
              {!hasAllCriteria && (
                <span>Faltam marcar itens obrigatórios.</span>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              disabled={!canProceed}
              onClick={handleApprovePreSelection}
              className={`px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2 transition-all ${
                canProceed
                  ? "bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700 shadow-lg hover:shadow-green-200 scale-105"
                  : "bg-gray-300 text-gray-500 cursor-not-allowed"
              }`}
            >
              {canProceed ? "Aprovar e Analisar" : "Complete o Checklist"}
              {canProceed && <ArrowRight size={18} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }
  // ETAPA 3: ANÁLISE PROFUNDA (FORMULÁRIO COMPLETO)
  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-8 duration-700 pb-10">
      {/* Header de Sucesso */}
      <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 p-4 rounded-xl flex justify-between items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="bg-green-100 p-2 rounded-full text-green-600">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-green-800">Pré-seleção Aprovada</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-green-600">
              O produto passou na peneira inicial. Agora vamos aos cálculos
              matemáticos.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button
          onClick={() => setShowDeepAnalysis(false)}
          className="text-xs text-green-700 hover:underline"
        >
          Voltar ao Checklist
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        {/* Coluna Esquerda: Critérios Validados */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
            <CheckSquare size={20} className="text-blue-600" /> Critérios
            Validados
          </h3>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="text-xs uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 tracking-wider mb-2">
              {mode === "discovery" ? "Métodos Usados" : "Modo Validação"}
            </div>

            {mode === "discovery" &&
              discoveryMethods
                .filter((m) => m.checked)
                .map((item) => (
                  <div
                    key={item.id}
                    className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded"
                  >
                    <Pickaxe size={14} className="mt-1 shrink-0" />
                    <span className="font-medium">{item.label}</span>
                  </div>
                ))}

            <div className="text-xs uppercase font-bold text-gray-400 tracking-wider mb-2 mt-4">
              Requisitos Obrigatórios
            </div>

            {mandatoryCriteria.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-gray-600"
              >
                <CheckCircle2
                  size={14}
                  className="text-green-500 mt-1 shrink-0"
                />
                <span className="line-through opacity-75">{item.label}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Coluna Central: FORMULÁRIO COMPLETO */}
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-6">
            Mapeamento Profundo (Dados)
          </h3>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Nome do Produto *
              </label>
              <input
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 focus:border-blue-500 bg-white text-gray-900 placeholder-gray-400"
                placeholder=""
                value={form.name || ""}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Data da Análise
              </label>
              <input
                type="date"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                value={form.analysisDate}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, analysisDate: e.target.value })
                }
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4 p-4 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50 to-indigo-50 rounded-lg border-2 border-blue-200">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm text-blue-900 mb-2">
                🌍 Mercado (País do Anúncio)
              </label>
              <input
                list="market-suggestions"
                className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-blue-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
                placeholder="Digite ou selecione..."
                value={form.market || ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    market: e.target.value,
                    platform: undefined,
                  })
                }
              />
              <datalist id="market-suggestions">
                {marketSuggestions.map((m) => (
                  <option key={m.value} value={m.value}>
                    {m.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </datalist>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-blue-700">
                💡 Exemplos: "US", "BR", "África do Sul", "Japão"
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm text-blue-900 mb-2">
                💰 Moeda da Comissão
              </label>
              <select
                className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-blue-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500"
                value={form.currency || ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, currency: e.target.value as Currency })
                }
              >
                <option value="" disabled>
                  💰 Selecione a moeda...
                </option>
                {currencies.map((c) => (
                  <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>
                    {c.symbol} {c.label}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-blue-700 font-semibold">
                ✅ Moeda ativa: <strong>{currencySymbol}</strong>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {form.market &&
            form.currency &&
            form.market !== "BR" &&
            form.currency !== "USD" && (
              <div className="mb-4 bg-yellow-50 border-l-4 border-yellow-400 p-3 rounded-r-lg">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                  <span className="text-yellow-600 text-xl">⚠️</span>
                  <div className="text-xs text-yellow-800">
                    <strong>Atenção:</strong> Você está anunciando em{" "}
                    <strong>{form.market}</strong> mas receberá em{" "}
                    <strong>{form.currency}</strong>. Certifique-se de que a
                    plataforma suporta esta configuração de arbitragem.
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

          <div className="mb-4 p-4 bg-indigo-50 rounded-lg border border-indigo-100">
            <label className="block text-sm text-indigo-900 mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Filter size={16} /> Estratégia de Funil (Importante)
            </label>
            <select
              className="w-full p-2 border border-indigo-200 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900 font-medium"
              value={form.funnelStage}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({ ...form, funnelStage: e.target.value as FunnelStage })
              }
            >
              <option value="fundo">
                🎯 Fundo de Funil (Público Quente - Busca Nome Produto)
              </option>
              <option value="meio">
                🤔 Meio de Funil (Consciência da Solução)
              </option>
              <option value="topo">
                🥶 Topo de Funil (Público Frio - Consciência do Problema)
              </option>
            </select>
            <p className="text-xs text-indigo-700 mt-2">
              <strong>Nota:</strong> Ao selecionar "Fundo de Funil", a IA aceita
              metas de conversão mais agressivas (até 8%).
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4 bg-slate-50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
            <div className="md:col-span-2 text-xs font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
              Dados Principais para Cálculo
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Preço ({currencySymbol}) *
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                placeholder=""
                value={form.price || ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    price: parseFloat(e.target.value),
                    selectedOfferId: undefined,
                  })
                }
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Comissão (%) *
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                placeholder=""
                value={form.commissionPct || ""}
