                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    commissionPct: parseFloat(e.target.value),
                    selectedOfferId: undefined,
                  })
                }
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Taxa da Plataforma
              </label>

              {/* Toggle entre % e Valor Fixo */}
              <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() =>
                    setForm({ ...form, platformFeeType: "percentage" })
                  }
                  className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded ${
                    form.platformFeeType === "percentage"
                      ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                      : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  Porcentagem (%)
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, platformFeeType: "fixed" })}
                  className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded ${
                    form.platformFeeType === "fixed"
                      ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                      : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  Valor Fixo ({currencySymbol})
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Input do valor */}
              <input
                type="number"
                step="0.01"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                placeholder={
                  form.platformFeeType === "percentage"
                    ? "Ex: 5"
                    : `Ex: ${currencySymbol} 10`
                }
                value={form.platformFeeValue || ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    platformFeeValue: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                  })
                }
              />

              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                {form.platformFeeType === "percentage"
                  ? "% descontado sobre a comissão bruta"
                  : `Valor fixo em ${
                      form.currency || "moeda local"
                    } descontado da comissão`}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-6 border border-dashed border-gray-300 rounded-lg p-4">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
              <div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                  📦 Estrutura de Ofertas / Kits
                  <div className="group relative">
                    <HelpCircle
                      size={14}
                      className="text-slate-400 cursor-help"
                    />
                    <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 w-80 bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs p-3 rounded-lg z-10 shadow-xl">
                      <strong>💡 Como usar:</strong>
                      <br />• <strong>Padrão/Kit:</strong> Ofertas normais (1
                      pote, 3 potes, etc)
                      <br />• <strong>Order Bump:</strong> Oferta adicional na
                      página de checkout
                      <br />• <strong>Upsell:</strong> Oferta pós-compra (página
                      de obrigado)
                      <br />
                      <br />O sistema detecta automaticamente a presença de
                      bumps/upsells e ajusta o score de viabilidade!
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                  ✨ Cadastre todas as ofertas (kits, bumps, upsells). Selecione
                  a mais vendida para análise.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 mb-4">
              {form.offers?.map((offer) => (
                <div
                  key={offer.id}
                  onClick={() => handleSelectOffer(offer)}
                  className={`p-3 rounded-lg border cursor-pointer transition-all hover:shadow-md flex flex-col min-w-[140px] relative ${
                    form.selectedOfferId === offer.id
                      ? "bg-blue-50 border-blue-500 ring-2 ring-blue-500"
                      : "bg-white border-gray-200 hover:border-blue-300"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 z-10">
                    {getOfferTypeBadge(offer.type)}
                  </div>

                  <div className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-900 mt-2">
                    {offer.name}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                    {currencySymbol} {offer.price.toFixed(2)}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-medium text-green-600">
                    {offer.commissionPct}% Comis.
                  </div>

                  {form.selectedOfferId === offer.id && (
                    <div className="mt-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-600 uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                      <CheckCircle2 size={12} /> Selecionado
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      handleRemoveOffer(offer.id);
                    }}
                    className="mt-2 self-end text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 transition-colors"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={14} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-3 bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
              <div className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-600 uppercase tracking-wide mb-2">
                ➕ Adicionar Nova Oferta
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-medium block mb-1">
                    Nome da Oferta
                  </label>
                  <input
                    className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded text-sm bg-white text-gray-900 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                    placeholder="Ex: Kit 3 Potes, E-book Bônus"
                    value={newOffer.name}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setNewOffer({ ...newOffer, name: e.target.value })
                    }
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-medium block mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                    🏷️ Tipo de Oferta
                    <div className="group relative inline-block">
                      <HelpCircle
                        size={12}
                        className="text-gray-400 cursor-help"
                      />
                      <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 w-64 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs p-2 rounded z-50 shadow-xl whitespace-normal">
                        Tipo da oferta: Padrão (kit normal), Order Bump
                        (checkout) ou Upsell (pós-venda)
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </label>
                  <select
                    className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded text-sm bg-white text-gray-900 font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                    value={newOffer.type}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setNewOffer({
                        ...newOffer,
                        type: e.target.value as OfferType,
                      })
                    }
                  >
                    <option value="standard">📦 Padrão / Kit</option>
                    <option value="bump">🎁 Order Bump</option>
                    <option value="upsell">🚀 Upsell</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-medium block mb-1">
                    Preço ({currencySymbol})
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded text-sm bg-white text-gray-900"
                    placeholder=""
                    value={newOffer.price || ""}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setNewOffer({
                        ...newOffer,
                        price: parseFloat(e.target.value),
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-medium block mb-1">
                    Comissão (%)
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded text-sm bg-white text-gray-900 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                    placeholder=""
                    value={newOffer.commissionPct || ""}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setNewOffer({
                        ...newOffer,
                        commissionPct: parseFloat(e.target.value),
                      })
                    }
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-end">
                  <button
                    onClick={handleAddOffer}
                    className="w-full bg-gray-800 text-white p-2 rounded hover:bg-black transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-bold text-sm"
                  >
                    <Plus size={16} /> Adicionar
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-3 p-3 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-blue-400 text-xs text-blue-800">
                <strong>💡 DICA:</strong> Order Bumps e Upsells aumentam
                automaticamente o <strong>Score de Viabilidade</strong> do
                produto em <strong>+1.5 pontos</strong> quando cadastrados!
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Nicho
              </label>
              <select
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                value={form.nicho}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, nicho: e.target.value })}
              >
                <option value="" disabled>
                  -- Selecione o Nicho --
                </option>
                <option value="emagrecimento">Emagrecimento</option>
                <option value="fitness">Fitness</option>
                <option value="saude">Saúde</option>
                <option value="financas">Finanças</option>
                <option value="marketing">Marketing</option>
                <option value="relacionamento">Relacionamento</option>
                <option value="espiritualidade">Espiritualidade</option>
                <option value="outro">Outro</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                Plataforma
                {form.market !== "BR" && (
                  <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-2 py-1 rounded font-bold">
                    INTERNACIONAL
                  </span>
                )}
              </label>
              <input
                list="platform-suggestions"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                placeholder="Digite ou selecione..."
                value={form.platform || ""}
                onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, platform: e.target.value })}
              />
              <datalist id="platform-suggestions">
                {platformSuggestions.map((p) => (
                  <option key={p} value={p}>
                    {getPlatformDisplayName(p)}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </datalist>
              <div className="mt-1 text-xs text-gray-500">
                💡 Sugestões baseadas no mercado. Pode digitar manualmente.
              </div>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Temperatura
              </label>
              <select
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                value={form.temperature}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, temperature: e.target.value as any })
                }
              >
                <option value="quente">🔥 Quente</option>
                <option value="morno">☀️ Morno</option>
                <option value="frio">❄️ Frio</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1 flex justify-between">
                Volume de Busca
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">Keyword Planner</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                placeholder="Ex: 5000"
                value={form.searchVolume || ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, searchVolume: parseFloat(e.target.value) })
                }
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1 flex justify-between">
                Qtd. Anunciantes
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">No Topo</span>
              </label>
              <input
                type="number"
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                placeholder="Ex: 3"
                value={form.advertiserCount || ""}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({
                    ...form,
                    advertiserCount: parseFloat(e.target.value),
                  })
                }
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mb-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Tipo de Produto
              </label>
              <select
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                value={form.type}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, type: e.target.value as any })
                }
              >
                <option value="" disabled>
                  -- Selecione o Tipo --
                </option>
                <option value="fisico">Produto Físico</option>
                <option value="digital">Digital</option>
                <option value="curso">Curso Online</option>
                <option value="software">SaaS / Software</option>
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">
                Qualidade da LP
              </label>
              <select
                className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900"
                value={form.qualityLP}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setForm({ ...form, qualityLP: e.target.value as any })
                }
              >
                <option value="excelente">Excelente</option>
                <option value="boa">Boa</option>
                <option value="media">Média</option>
                <option value="fraca">Fraca</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-4 p-4 bg-amber-50 rounded-lg border-2 border-amber-300">
            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-amber-900 mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
              💰 CPC Médio de Mercado (R$) *
              <div className="group relative">
                <HelpCircle size={14} className="text-amber-600 cursor-help" />
                <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 w-80 bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs p-3 rounded-lg z-50 shadow-xl">
                  <strong>💡 Por que em Reais?</strong>
                  <br />
                  Independente do país do produto, insira o valor que você
                  pretende pagar por clique convertido para Reais (BRL). O
                  Google Ads cobra em Reais, e precisamos desse valor para
                  calcular se a comissão cobre o custo de tráfego.
                  <div className="absolute -bottom-1 left-4 w-2 h-2 bg-slate-900 rotate-45"></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </label>
            <input
              type="number"
              step="0.01"
              className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-amber-400 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900 font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 focus:border-amber-500 outline-none"
              placeholder="Ex: 2.50 (em Reais)"
              value={form.manualBenchmarkCPC || ""}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  manualBenchmarkCPC: parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0,
                })
              }
            />
            <div className="mt-2 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs text-amber-800 bg-amber-100 p-2 rounded border border-amber-200">
              <span className="text-amber-600 font-bold shrink-0">⚠️</span>
              <p className="leading-relaxed">
                <strong>Obrigatório:</strong> Insira o CPC em Reais (BRL). Sem
                este valor, a análise de viabilidade não pode ser calculada.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-6">
            <button
              onClick={handleAnalyze}
              disabled={loading || !form.name || !form.price}
              className="w-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-purple-600 text-white p-3 rounded-lg font-bold shadow-md hover:shadow-lg transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            >
              {loading ? "Analisando..." : "Analisar Viabilidade do Produto"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {result && (
        <div
          className={`bg-white p-8 rounded-xl shadow-xl border-t-4 ${
            result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
              ? "border-green-500"
              : "border-red-500"
          } animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-8`}
        >
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-8 border-b border-gray-100 pb-6 gap-4">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                {result.name}
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-normal bg-gray-100 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-gray-200">
                  <Flame
                    size={14}
                    className={getTempColor(result.temperature)}
                  />
                  {result.temperature?.toUpperCase()}
                </span>
                {result.funnelStage === "fundo" && (
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-bold bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-indigo-200">
                    <Target size={14} /> FUNDO DE FUNIL
                  </span>
                )}
              </h2>
              <p className="text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                {result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO" ? (
                  <CheckCircle2 className="text-green-500" />
                ) : (
                  <AlertTriangle className="text-red-500" />
                )}
                Status:{" "}
                <span
                  className={`font-bold ${
                    result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                      ? "text-green-600"
                      : "text-red-600"
                  }`}
                >
                  {result.viabilityStatus}
                </span>
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-3">
              <div className="text-right">
                <div
                  className={`text-4xl font-black ${
                    result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                      ? "text-green-600"
                      : "text-red-600"
                  }`}
                >
                  {result.viabilityScore?.toFixed(1)}
                  <span className="text-lg text-gray-400 font-normal">/10</span>
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide">
                  Score de Viabilidade
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={handleExportPDF}
                className="bg-indigo-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-indigo-700 transition-colors shadow-md hover:shadow-lg text-sm"
              >
                <Download size={16} /> Exportar Relatório PDF
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {result.currency !== "BRL" && (
            <div className="mb-6 bg-blue-50 border-l-4 border-blue-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="text-blue-600 text-2xl">💱</div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-blue-800 mb-2">
                    Conversão Automática de Moeda
                  </h4>
                  <div className="text-sm text-blue-900 space-y-1">
                    <p>
                      • <strong>Comissão:</strong>{" "}
                      {result.commissionLiquid?.toFixed(2)} {result.currency}
                    </p>
                    <p>
                      • <strong>Em Reais:</strong> R${" "}
                      {result.commissionLiquidBRL?.toFixed(2)}
                    </p>
                    <p>
                      • <strong>Taxa:</strong> 1 {result.currency} = R${" "}
                      {(rates[result.currency] || 1).toFixed(2)}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs mt-2 opacity-75">
                      ℹ️ Cálculos feitos em BRL pois o tráfego é pago em Reais.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {result.viabilityReason && (
            <div
              className={`mb-8 border-l-4 p-5 rounded-r-lg ${
                result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                  ? "bg-green-50 border-green-500"
                  : "bg-red-50 border-red-500"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <FileText
                  className={`${
                    result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                      ? "text-green-600"
                      : "text-red-600"
                  } shrink-0 mt-1`}
                  size={24}
                />
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <h4
                    className={`font-bold ${
                      result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                        ? "text-green-800"
                        : "text-red-800"
                    } mb-2`}
                  >
                    {result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                      ? "Por que foi Aprovado?"
                      : "Entenda a Reprovação"}
                  </h4>
                  <div
                    className={`text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap font-medium leading-relaxed ${
                      result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO"
                        ? "text-green-900"
                        : "text-red-900"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {result.viabilityReason}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {result.scenarios && result.scenarios.length > 0 && (
            <div className="mb-8 bg-slate-50 p-6 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                📊 Comparativo de Estratégia (Qual Kit Anunciar?)
              </h4>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                {result.scenarios.map((scenario, i) => (
                  <div
                    key={i}
                    className={`p-4 rounded-lg border-l-4 shadow-sm ${
                      scenario.status === "LUCRO"
                        ? "bg-white border-green-500"
                        : scenario.status === "RISCO"
                        ? "bg-white border-yellow-500"
                        : "bg-white border-red-500"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                      <div className="font-bold text-gray-800">
                        {scenario.offerName}
                      </div>
                      <div
                        className={`text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded ${
                          scenario.status === "LUCRO"
                            ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                            : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {scenario.status === "LUCRO"
                          ? "MARGEM SEGURA"
                          : "RISCO ALTO"}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2 text-sm mt-3">
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-gray-600 border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                        <span>Comissão Paga:</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-blue-600">
                          {currencySymbol} {scenario.commission.toFixed(2)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {result.currency !== "BRL" && (
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-500 pb-1">
                          <span>Equiv. em BRL:</span>
                          <span className="font-medium">
                            R$ {scenario.commissionBRL?.toFixed(2)}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      )}
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-gray-600 border-b border-gray-100 pb-1">
                        <span>Cliques Necessários:</span>
                        <span className="font-bold text-blue-600">
                          {scenario.clicksNeeded}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex justify-between text-gray-600 pt-1">
                        <span>Meta CR (Zero a Zero):</span>
                        <span
                          className={`${
                            scenario.maxCpcBreakEven >
                            (result.funnelStage === "fundo" ? 5.0 : 2.0)
                              ? "text-red-600"
                              : "text-green-600"
                          } font-bold`}
                        >
                          {scenario.maxCpcBreakEven.toFixed(2)}%
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    {scenario.status === "LUCRO" && (
                      <div className="mt-3 text-xs text-green-700 bg-green-50 p-2 rounded flex gap-1 items-center">
                        <TrendingUp size={12} /> Kit Recomendado
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="mb-8 bg-slate-50 p-5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-slate-700 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Calculator size={16} /> 🧮 Memória de Cálculo da IA
            </h4>
            <div className="space-y-2 max-h-64 overflow-y-auto pr-2 scrollbar-thin">
              {result.calculationLog?.map((log, i) => (
                <div
                  key={i}
                  className="text-sm text-slate-600 border-l-2 border-slate-300 pl-3 py-1 font-mono"
                >
                  {log}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-6 mb-8">
            <div className="bg-green-50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-green-100 relative group cursor-help">
              <div className="text-xs text-green-600 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                CPC Alvo (Bom) <HelpCircle size={12} />
              </div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-700">
                R$ {result.cpcBom?.toFixed(2)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-green-600">4% da Comissão Bruta</div>
              <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 w-48 bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs p-2 rounded z-10 shadow-lg">
                Meta de mercado segura para ROI saudável.
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-cyan-50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-cyan-100 relative group cursor-help">
              <div className="text-xs text-cyan-600 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                Poder de Fogo <Crosshair size={12} />
              </div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-cyan-700">
                {result.clicksPurchasable} Cliques
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-cyan-600">Comissão ÷ CPC Ótimo</div>
              <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 w-48 bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs p-2 rounded z-10 shadow-lg">
                Quantos cliques você consegue comprar antes de ter prejuízo.
              </div>
            </div>

            <div
              className={`p-4 rounded-lg border relative group cursor-help ${
                result.requiredCR > (result.funnelStage === "fundo" ? 5.0 : 2.0)
                  ? "bg-red-50 border-red-100 text-red-700"
                  : "bg-purple-50 border-purple-100 text-purple-700"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider flex items-center gap-1">
                Meta de Conversão <Target size={12} />
              </div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold">
                {result.requiredCR?.toFixed(2)}%
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs opacity-80">Zero a Zero</div>
              <div className="hidden group-hover:block absolute bottom-full left-0 mb-2 w-48 bg-slate-800 text-white text-xs p-2 rounded z-10 shadow-lg">
                Você precisa converter 1 venda a cada {result.clicksPurchasable}{" "}
                cliques para empatar.
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-100">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">
                Comissão Líquida
              </div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-700">
                R$ {result.commissionLiquidBRL?.toFixed(2)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-600">Dinheiro no Bolso</div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {result.viabilityStatus === "APROVADO" ? (
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3">
              <button
                onClick={handleExportPDF}
                className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
              >
                <Download size={18} /> Baixar Relatório
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  onSave(result);
                  // Limpar modo de edição após salvar
                  if (editingProduct && setEditingProduct) {
                    setTimeout(() => setEditingProduct(null), 500);
                  }
                }}
                className="bg-green-600 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold flex items-center gap-2 hover:bg-green-700 transition-colors shadow-lg hover:shadow-green-200"
              >
                <Save size={18} />{" "}
                {editingProduct
                  ? "Atualizar Produto"
                  : "Salvar Produto e Ir para Setup"}
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-4">
              <button
                className="text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 font-medium px-4 py-2"
                onClick={() => {
                  handleResetMapping();
                  // Limpar modo de edição ao cancelar
                  if (editingProduct && setEditingProduct) {
                    setEditingProduct(null);
                  }
                }}
              >
                {editingProduct
                  ? "Cancelar Edição"
                  : "Descartar e Novo Mapeamento"}
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  onSave(result);
                  if (editingProduct && setEditingProduct) {
                    setTimeout(() => setEditingProduct(null), 500);
                  }
                }}
                className="bg-gray-200 text-gray-700 px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-bold hover:bg-gray-300 transition-colors"
              >
                Salvar Mesmo Assim (Risco Alto)
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: Onboarding.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Onboarding.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, ChevronRight, ChevronLeft, Check } from 'lucide-react';

interface OnboardingStep {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  icon: string;
  targetTab?: string;
}

interface OnboardingProps {
  onComplete: () => void;
  onSkip: () => void;
}

const ONBOARDING_STEPS: OnboardingStep[] = [
  {
    title: 'Bem-vindo ao Sistema de Gestão de Afiliados! 🎉',
    description: 'Vou te mostrar as principais funcionalidades em 5 passos rápidos. Você pode pular este tutorial a qualquer momento.',
    icon: '👋'
  },
  {
    title: 'Dashboard - Visão Geral',
    description: 'Aqui você vê todos os seus números: receita, gastos, lucro e ROI. É o coração do sistema!',
    icon: '📊',
    targetTab: 'dashboard'
  },
  {
    title: 'Comparador de Produtos',
    description: 'Compare até 3 produtos lado a lado para ver qual está performando melhor e onde investir.',
    icon: '⚖️',
    targetTab: 'comparator'
  },
  {
    title: 'Simulador de Escala',
    description: 'Simule diferentes cenários de aumento de budget e veja o impacto no lucro antes de investir!',
    icon: '🚀',
    targetTab: 'scale'
  },
  {
    title: 'Pronto para começar! 🎯',
    description: 'Agora você conhece o básico. Explore as outras abas (Mapeamento, Setup, Análise) e qualquer dúvida, consulte o Manual!',
    icon: '✅'
  }
];

const ONBOARDING_KEY = 'onboarding_completed';

export const Onboarding: React.FC<OnboardingProps> = ({ onComplete, onSkip }) => {
  const [currentStep, setCurrentStep] = useState(0);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Verificar se já completou onboarding
    const completed = localStorage.getItem(ONBOARDING_KEY);
    if (!completed) {
      setIsVisible(true);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentStep < ONBOARDING_STEPS.length - 1) {
      setCurrentStep(prev => prev + 1);
    } else {
      handleComplete();
    }
  };

  const handlePrevious = () => {
    if (currentStep > 0) {
      setCurrentStep(prev => prev - 1);
    }
  };

  const handleComplete = () => {
    localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_KEY, 'true');
    setIsVisible(false);
    onComplete();
  };

  const handleSkipNow = () => {
    localStorage.setItem(ONBOARDING_KEY, 'true');
    setIsVisible(false);
    onSkip();
  };

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  const step = ONBOARDING_STEPS[currentStep];
  const progress = ((currentStep + 1) / ONBOARDING_STEPS.length) * 100;

  return (
    <>
      {/* Overlay */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black bg-opacity-50 z-50 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-300" />

      {/* Modal */}
      <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 pointer-events-none">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full p-8 pointer-events-auto animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-300">
          {/* Close button */}
          <button
            onClick={handleSkipNow}
            className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-2 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg transition-colors"
            title="Pular tutorial"
          >
            <X size={20} className="text-gray-400" />
          </button>

          {/* Progress bar */}
          <div className="mb-6">
            <div className="h-2 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-600 transition-all duration-500"
                style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2 text-center">
              Passo {currentStep + 1} de {ONBOARDING_STEPS.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Icon */}
          <div className="text-6xl text-center mb-4 animate-in zoom-in duration-500">
            {step.icon}
          </div>

          {/* Content */}
          <div className="text-center mb-8">
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-black text-gray-900 mb-3">
              {step.title}
            </h2>
            <p className="text-gray-600 leading-relaxed">
              {step.description}
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Navigation */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
            <button
              onClick={handlePrevious}
              disabled={currentStep === 0}
              className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium transition-all ${
                currentStep === 0
                  ? 'text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed'
                  : 'text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100'
              }`}
            >
              <ChevronLeft size={20} />
              Anterior
            </button>

            {currentStep < ONBOARDING_STEPS.length - 1 ? (
              <button
                onClick={handleNext}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-600 to-purple-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:shadow-lg transition-all hover:scale-105"
              >
                Próximo
                <ChevronRight size={20} />
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                onClick={handleComplete}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 px-6 py-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-emerald-600 text-white rounded-lg font-bold hover:shadow-lg transition-all hover:scale-105"
              >
                Começar!
                <Check size={20} />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Skip button */}
          {currentStep < ONBOARDING_STEPS.length - 1 && (
            <button
              onClick={handleSkipNow}
              className="w-full mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700 transition-colors"
            >
              Pular tutorial
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
};

// Hook para resetar onboarding (útil para testes)
export const useResetOnboarding = () => {
  return () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(ONBOARDING_KEY);
    window.location.reload();
  };
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: Portfolio.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\Portfolio.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useMemo, useEffect } from "react";
import type {
  PortfolioProps,
  EnrichedProduct,
  OptimizationResult,
  AllocationItem,
  Currency,
} from "../types";
import { optimizePortfolio, optimizePortfolioSimplex } from "../services/logic";
import { useCurrency } from "../contexts/CurrencyContext";
import { getCurrencySymbol } from "../utils/currency";
import {
  Zap,
  TrendingUp,
  DollarSign,
  FolderOpen,
  Search,
  Trash2,
  BarChart2,
  Archive,
  Filter,
  Award,
  AlertTriangle,
  TrendingDown,
  Target,
  CheckCircle,
  XCircle,
  AlertCircle,
  Activity,
  Package,
  Info,
  HelpCircle,
} from "lucide-react";

// ============================================================
// 🎯 TYPE DEFINITIONS
// ============================================================
type SortField = "name" | "profit" | "roi" | "spend" | "revenue" | "campaigns";
type SortDirection = "asc" | "desc";
type FilterStatus = "all" | "profitable" | "negative" | "neutral";
type OptimizationMethod = "simple" | "simplex";

// ============================================================
// 🔧 UTILITY: Mapeamento Market → Currency
// ============================================================
const getCurrencyFromMarket = (market: string): string => {
  switch (market) {
    case "US":
    case "OTHER":
      return "USD";
    case "EU":
      return "EUR";
    case "UK":
      return "GBP";
    case "BR":
    default:
      return "BRL";
  }
};

// ============================================================
// 🔧 UTILITY: Conversão para BRL (apenas valor numérico)
// ============================================================
const convertToBRL = (
  value: number,
  market: string,
  rates: Record<string, number>,
  currencyParam?: string
): number => {
  // Proteção: se o produto estiver marcado como BR ou a moeda for BRL, usar rate = 1
  const currency = currencyParam || getCurrencyFromMarket(market);
  const rate = market === "BR" || currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;
  return (value || 0) * rate;
};

// ============================================================
// 🎨 COMPONENT: Badge de Moeda (Indicador visual)
// ============================================================
interface CurrencyBadgeProps {
  market: string;
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
}

const CurrencyBadge: React.FC<CurrencyBadgeProps> = ({
  market,
  size = "md",
}) => {
  if (market === "BR") return null;

  const sizeClasses = {
    sm: "text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5",
    md: "text-xs px-3 py-1",
    lg: "text-sm px-4 py-1.5",
  };

  const currencyInfo: Record<string, { flag: string; label: string }> = {
    US: { flag: "🇺🇸", label: "USD" },
    OTHER: { flag: "🇺🇸", label: "USD" },
    EU: { flag: "🇪🇺", label: "EUR" },
    UK: { flag: "🇬🇧", label: "GBP" },
  };

  const info = currencyInfo[market] || {
    flag: "🌎",
    label: market,
  };

  return (
    <span
      className={`bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-500 to-purple-500 text-white rounded-full font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${sizeClasses[size]}`}
    >
      {info.flag} {info.label}
    </span>
  );
};

// ============================================================
// 🎨 COMPONENT: Tooltip de Taxa de Câmbio
// ============================================================
interface ExchangeRateTooltipProps {
  market: string;
  rates: Record<string, number>;
}

const ExchangeRateTooltip: React.FC<ExchangeRateTooltipProps> = ({
  market,
  rates,
}) => {
  if (market === "BR") return null;

  const currency = getCurrencyFromMarket(market);
  const rate = rates[currency] || 1;

  return (
    <div className="group relative inline-block ml-1">
      <HelpCircle size={12} className="text-blue-400 cursor-help inline" />
      <div className="invisible group-hover:visible absolute z-[9999] w-52 p-3 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded-lg shadow-xl -top-2 left-5 border border-gray-700">
        <strong className="block mb-1">💱 Taxa de Câmbio</strong>
        <p className="text-gray-300">
          1 {currency} = R$ {rate.toFixed(2)}
        </p>
        <p className="text-gray-400 text-[10px] mt-1">
          Valores convertidos automaticamente
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

// ============================================================
// 🎨 COMPONENT: Exibição de Valor com Conversão Dual
// ============================================================
interface DualCurrencyDisplayProps {
  value: number;
  market: string;
  rates: Record<string, number>;
  currency?: string;
  className?: string;
  showOriginal?: boolean;
}

const DualCurrencyDisplay: React.FC<DualCurrencyDisplayProps> = ({
  value,
  market,
  rates,
  currency,
  className = "",
  showOriginal = true,
}) => {
  const isInternational =
    market !== "BR" && (currency || getCurrencyFromMarket(market)) !== "BRL";

  // Nacional or product explicitly in BRL: apenas BRL
  const currencyFromMarket = currency || getCurrencyFromMarket(market);
  const rate =
    market === "BR" || currencyFromMarket === "BRL"
      ? 1
      : rates[currencyFromMarket] || 1;
  const valueInBRL = (value || 0) * rate;

  if (!isInternational) {
    return (
      <span className={className || "font-bold text-gray-900"}>
        R$ {(valueInBRL || 0).toFixed(2)}
      </span>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      {showOriginal && (
        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-medium">
          {getCurrencySymbol(currencyFromMarket as Currency)}{" "}
          {(value || 0).toFixed(2)}
        </span>
      )}
      <span className={className || "font-bold text-gray-900 text-base"}>
        ≈ R$ {(valueInBRL || 0).toFixed(2)}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
};

export const Portfolio: React.FC<
  PortfolioProps & {
    fixedCostsTotal?: number;
    editingProduct?: Product | null; // ✅ NOVO
    setEditingProduct?: (product: Product | null) => void; // ✅ NOVO
    onChangeTab?: (tab: string) => void; // ✅ NOVO
  }
> = ({
  products,
  history,
  onDeleteProduct,
  onSelectProductForAnalysis,
  fixedCostsTotal = 0,
  editingProduct,
  setEditingProduct,
  onChangeTab,
}) => {
  // ============================================================
  // 🎯 HOOKS & ESTADOS
  // ============================================================
  const { rates } = useCurrency();
  const [simplexBudget, setSimplexBudget] = useState(1000);
  const [allocation, setAllocation] = useState<OptimizationResult | null>(null);
  const [selectedProductId, setSelectedProductId] = useState<string>("");
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState("");
  const [sortField, setSortField] = useState<SortField>("profit");
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<SortDirection>("desc");
  const [filterStatus, setFilterStatus] = useState<FilterStatus>("all");
  const [filterNicho, setFilterNicho] = useState<string>("all");
  const [optimizationMethod, setOptimizationMethod] =
    useState<OptimizationMethod>("simple");

  // Reset allocation quando mudar método
  useEffect(() => {
    // Deferir para evitar setState síncrono dentro do effect (evita cascata de renders)
    const t = setTimeout(() => setAllocation(null), 0);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [optimizationMethod]);

  // ============================================================
  // 🔄 HANDLER: Otimização
  // ============================================================
  const handleOptimize = () => {
    try {
      if (!simplexBudget || simplexBudget <= 0) {
        alert("Por favor, insira um orçamento válido maior que zero.");
        return;
      }

      if (!products || products.length === 0) {
        alert("Não há produtos disponíveis para otimização.");
        return;
      }
      // ✅ NOVO: Calcular produtos ativos para rateio de custos fixos
      const activeProducts = products.filter((p) =>
        history.some((h) => h.productId === p.id)
      );
      const activeProductsCount =
        activeProducts.length > 0 ? activeProducts.length : 1;

      let result: OptimizationResult | null = null;

      try {
        if (optimizationMethod === "simple") {
          result = optimizePortfolio(
            simplexBudget,
            products,
            history || [],
            rates,
            fixedCostsTotal,
            activeProductsCount
          );
        } else {
          result = optimizePortfolioSimplex(
            simplexBudget,
            products,
            history || [],
            rates,
            fixedCostsTotal, // ✅ NOVO: Custos fixos globais
            activeProductsCount
          );
        }
      } catch (calcError) {
        console.error("Erro no cálculo:", calcError);
        alert("Erro no cálculo. Tente novamente.");
        return;
      }

      if (!result || typeof result !== "object") {
        console.error("Resultado inválido:", result);
        alert("Erro ao calcular otimização. Tente novamente.");
        return;
      }

      setAllocation(result);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("❌ Erro geral:", error);
      alert("Ocorreu um erro. Verifique o console (F12) para detalhes.");
      setAllocation(null);
    }
  };

  // ============================================================
  // 📊 ENRIQUECIMENTO DE PRODUTOS (COM CONVERSÃO)
  // ============================================================
  const enrichedProducts: EnrichedProduct[] = useMemo(() => {
    // --- NOVO CÓDIGO (Rateio Global) ---
    // Conta produtos com campanhas para dividir a conta
    const activeProducts = products.filter((p) =>
      history.some((h) => h.productId === p.id)
    );
    const activeCount = activeProducts.length > 0 ? activeProducts.length : 1;
    const globalCostShare = fixedCostsTotal / activeCount;
    // ------------------------------------

    return products.map((p) => {
      const prodHistory = history.filter((h) => h.productId === p.id);

      // Contar campanhas únicas
      const uniqueCampaignIds = new Set(
        prodHistory.map((h) => h.campaignId).filter(Boolean)
      );

      // Pegar último snapshot
      let spend = 0;
      let revenue = 0;
      let conversions = 0;
      let clicks = 0;

      if (prodHistory.length > 0) {
        const lastSnapshot = prodHistory.sort(
          (a, b) => new Date(b.date).getTime() - new Date(a.date).getTime()
        )[0];

        spend = lastSnapshot.spend;
        revenue = lastSnapshot.revenue;
        conversions = lastSnapshot.conversions;
        clicks = lastSnapshot.clicks;
      }

      // ✅ CORREÇÃO: Usar p.costs?.total (não specificCosts)
      const fixedCosts = p.specificCosts?.total || 0;

      // ✅ CONVERSÃO: Converter valores para BRL se internacional
      // Detectar moeda do produto e aplicar exchangeRate seguro
      const spendBRL = convertToBRL(spend || 0, p.market, rates, p.currency);
      const revenueBRL = convertToBRL(
        revenue || 0,
        p.market,
        rates,
        p.currency
      );
      const fixedCostsBRL = convertToBRL(
        fixedCosts || 0,
        p.market,
        rates,
        p.currency
      );

      // Cálculos sempre em BRL (moeda base) com fallbacks || 0
      const profitBRL =
        (revenueBRL || 0) -
        (spendBRL || 0) -
        (fixedCostsBRL || 0) -
        (globalCostShare || 0);
      const totalInvestmentBRL =
        (spendBRL || 0) + (fixedCostsBRL || 0) + (globalCostShare || 0);
      const roi =
        totalInvestmentBRL > 0
          ? ((profitBRL || 0) / (totalInvestmentBRL || 1)) * 100
          : 0;

      const roas = spendBRL > 0 ? revenueBRL / spendBRL : 0;
      const cr = clicks > 0 ? (conversions / clicks) * 100 : 0;
      const cpcBRL = clicks > 0 ? spendBRL / clicks : 0;
      const avgTicketBRL = conversions > 0 ? revenueBRL / conversions : 0;

      // Status baseado em ROI
      let status: "excellent" | "good" | "warning" | "danger" = "good";
      if (roi > 100) status = "excellent";
      else if (roi > 50) status = "good";
      else if (roi > 0) status = "warning";
      else status = "danger";

      // Health Score
      let healthScore = 50;
      if (roi > 100) healthScore += 30;
      else if (roi > 50) healthScore += 20;
      else if (roi > 0) healthScore += 10;
      else healthScore -= 20;

      if (cr > 2) healthScore += 20;
      else if (cr > 1) healthScore += 10;

      healthScore = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, healthScore));

      return {
        ...p,
        campaignCount:
          uniqueCampaignIds.size > 0
            ? uniqueCampaignIds.size
            : prodHistory.length > 0
            ? 1
            : 0,
        // Valores originais (moeda do produto)
        spend,
        revenue,
        fixedCosts,
        // Valores convertidos em BRL
        spendBRL,
        revenueBRL,
        fixedCostsBRL,
        profit: profitBRL,
        roi,
        roas,
        conversions,
        clicks,
        cr,
        cpc: cpcBRL,
        avgTicket: avgTicketBRL,
        status,
        healthScore,
      } as EnrichedProduct;
    });
  }, [products, history, rates, fixedCostsTotal]); // ✅ Corrigido

  // ============================================================
  // 🔍 FILTRAGEM E ORDENAÇÃO
  // ============================================================
  const filteredAndSortedProducts: EnrichedProduct[] = useMemo(() => {
    let filtered = enrichedProducts;

    if (searchTerm) {
      filtered = filtered.filter(
        (p) =>
          p.name.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
          p.nicho.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
          p.market.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
      );
    }

    if (filterStatus !== "all") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => {
        if (filterStatus === "profitable") return p.profit > 0;
        if (filterStatus === "negative") return p.profit < 0;
        if (filterStatus === "neutral") return p.profit === 0;
        return true;
      });
    }

    if (filterNicho !== "all") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.nicho === filterNicho);
    }

    filtered.sort((a, b) => {
      const aVal = a[sortField as keyof EnrichedProduct];
      const bVal = b[sortField as keyof EnrichedProduct];

      if (typeof aVal === "string" && typeof bVal === "string") {
        return sortDirection === "asc"
          ? aVal.localeCompare(bVal)
          : bVal.localeCompare(aVal);
      }

      return sortDirection === "asc"
        ? (aVal as number) - (bVal as number)
        : (bVal as number) - (aVal as number);
    });

    return filtered;
  }, [
    enrichedProducts,
    searchTerm,
    filterStatus,
    filterNicho,
    sortField,
    sortDirection,
  ]);

  // ============================================================
  // 📊 TOTAIS GLOBAIS (CONVERTIDOS PARA BRL)
  // ============================================================
  const totalSpendBRL = enrichedProducts.reduce(
    (acc, p) => acc + (p.spendBRL || 0),
    0
  );
  const totalRevenueBRL = enrichedProducts.reduce(
    (acc, p) => acc + (p.revenueBRL || 0),
    0
  );
  const totalFixedCostsBRL = enrichedProducts.reduce((acc, p) => {
    return acc + (p.campaignCount > 0 ? p.fixedCostsBRL || 0 : 0);
  }, 0);

  const totalProfitBRL = totalRevenueBRL - totalSpendBRL - totalFixedCostsBRL;

  const uniqueGlobalCampaigns = new Set(
    history.map((h) => h.campaignId).filter(Boolean)
  );
  const totalCampaigns =
    uniqueGlobalCampaigns.size > 0
      ? uniqueGlobalCampaigns.size
      : history.length > 0
      ? products.length
      : 0;

  const totalInvestmentBRL = totalSpendBRL + totalFixedCostsBRL;
  const avgROI =
    totalInvestmentBRL > 0 ? (totalProfitBRL / totalInvestmentBRL) * 100 : 0;

  const uniqueNichos = Array.from(new Set(products.map((p) => p.nicho)));
  const top3 = [...enrichedProducts]
    .sort((a, b) => (b.profit || 0) - (a.profit || 0))
    .slice(0, 3);
  const bottom3 = [...enrichedProducts]
    .sort((a, b) => (a.profit || 0) - (b.profit || 0))
    .slice(0, 3);

  const selectedProduct: EnrichedProduct | undefined = enrichedProducts.find(
    (p) => p.id === selectedProductId
  );

  // ============================================================
  // 🎨 FUNÇÕES AUXILIARES
  // ============================================================
  const handleSort = (field: SortField) => {
    if (sortField === field) {
      setSortDirection((prev) => (prev === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc"));
    } else {
      setSortField(field);
      setSortDirection("desc");
    }
  };

  const getStatusColor = (status: EnrichedProduct["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "excellent":
        return "from-green-400 to-emerald-500";
      case "good":
        return "from-blue-400 to-cyan-500";
      case "warning":
        return "from-yellow-400 to-orange-400";
      case "danger":
        return "from-red-400 to-pink-500";
      default:
        return "from-gray-400 to-gray-500";
    }
  };

  const getStatusBadge = (status: EnrichedProduct["status"]) => {
    switch (status) {
      case "excellent":
        return {
          icon: <Award size={14} />,
          text: "🏆 Excelente",
          class: "bg-green-100 text-green-700",
        };
      case "good":
        return {
          icon: <CheckCircle size={14} />,
          text: "✅ Bom",
          class: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700",
        };
      case "warning":
        return {
          icon: <AlertCircle size={14} />,
          text: "⚠️ Atenção",
          class: "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700",
        };
      case "danger":
        return {
          icon: <XCircle size={14} />,
          text: "🚨 Crítico",
          class: "bg-red-100 text-red-700",
        };
      default:
        return {
          icon: null,
          text: "Neutro",
          class: "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700",
        };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-500">
      {/* ============================================ */}
      {/* 📊 HEADER - MÉTRICAS GLOBAIS               */}
      {/* ============================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {/* Card 1: Produtos Ativos */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-cyan-50 p-5 rounded-xl border border-blue-100 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <Package className="text-blue-600" size={20} />
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-700 uppercase">
              Produtos Ativos
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-3xl font-black text-blue-900">
            {products.length}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-blue-600 mt-1">
            {totalCampaigns} campanhas rodadas
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 2: Lucro Líquido (em BRL) */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-green-50 to-emerald-50 p-5 rounded-xl border border-green-100 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <DollarSign className="text-green-600" size={20} />
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-green-700 uppercase">
              Lucro Líquido
            </span>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-3xl font-black ${
              totalProfitBRL >= 0 ? "text-green-900" : "text-red-900"
            }`}
          >
            R$ {totalProfitBRL.toFixed(2)}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-green-600 mt-1">
            Receita: R$ {totalRevenueBRL.toFixed(2)} | Ads: -R${" "}
            {totalSpendBRL.toFixed(2)}
            {totalFixedCostsBRL > 0 &&
              ` | Fixos: -R$ ${totalFixedCostsBRL.toFixed(2)}`}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 3: ROI Médio */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-50 to-indigo-50 p-5 rounded-xl border border-purple-100 shadow-sm group relative">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <Target className="text-purple-600" size={20} />
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-purple-700 uppercase">
              ROI Médio
            </span>
            <div className="relative inline-block">
              <HelpCircle size={14} className="text-purple-400 cursor-help" />
              <div className="invisible group-hover:visible absolute z-[9999] w-64 p-3 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded-lg shadow-xl -top-2 left-6 border border-gray-700">
                <strong className="block mb-1">
                  📊 ROI Médio do Portfolio
                </strong>
                <p className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
                  Fórmula:{" "}
                  <code className="bg-gray-800 px-1 rounded">
                    (Lucro Líquido / Investimento Total) × 100
                  </code>
                </p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-yellow-300 text-[10px]">
                  💡 Valores internacionais convertidos para BRL
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div
            className={`text-3xl font-black ${
              avgROI >= 0 ? "text-purple-900" : "text-red-900"
            }`}
          >
            {avgROI.toFixed(1)}%
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-purple-600 mt-1">
            Retorno sobre investimento total
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Card 4: Investimento Total */}
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-orange-50 to-amber-50 p-5 rounded-xl border border-orange-100 shadow-sm group relative">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
            <Activity className="text-orange-600" size={20} />
            <span className="text-xs font-bold text-orange-700 uppercase">
              Investimento Total
            </span>
            <div className="relative inline-block">
              <HelpCircle size={14} className="text-orange-400 cursor-help" />
              <div className="invisible group-hover:visible absolute z-[9999] w-64 p-3 bg-gray-900 text-white text-xs rounded-lg shadow-xl -top-2 left-6 border border-gray-700">
                <strong className="block mb-1">💰 Investimento Total</strong>
                <p className="text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
                  Soma de <strong>todos os custos</strong> em BRL
                </p>
                <p className="mt-2 text-yellow-300 text-[10px]">
                  💡 Produtos internacionais convertidos automaticamente
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="text-3xl font-black text-orange-900">
            R$ {totalInvestmentBRL.toFixed(2)}
          </div>
          <div className="text-xs text-orange-600 mt-1">
            Ads (R$ {totalSpendBRL.toFixed(2)}) + Fixos (R${" "}
            {totalFixedCostsBRL.toFixed(2)})
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================ */}
      {/* 🏆 TOP 3 & BOTTOM 3                        */}
      {/* ============================================ */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {/* Top 3 Melhores */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <Award className="text-yellow-600" size={20} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">
              🏆 Top 3 Melhores Produtos
            </h3>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {top3.map((p, idx) => (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-green-50 to-emerald-50 rounded-lg border border-green-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all"
              >
                <div
                  className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-black text-lg ${
                    idx === 0
                      ? "bg-yellow-400 text-yellow-900"
                      : idx === 1
                      ? "bg-gray-300 text-gray-700"
                      : "bg-orange-300 text-orange-800"
                  }`}
                >
                  {idx + 1}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{p.name}</p>
                    <CurrencyBadge market={p.market} size="sm" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">{p.nicho}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <DualCurrencyDisplay
                    value={p.profit || 0}
                    market={p.market}
                    currency={p.currency}
                    rates={rates}
                    className="font-black text-green-600"
                  />
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                    ROI: {p.roi.toFixed(0)}%
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Bottom 3 */}
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
            <AlertTriangle className="text-red-600" size={20} />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">
              ⚠️ Produtos que Precisam Atenção
            </h3>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {bottom3.map((p) => (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-red-50 to-orange-50 rounded-lg border border-red-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all"
              >
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-red-500 text-white flex items-center justify-center font-black text-lg">
                  !
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">{p.name}</p>
                    <CurrencyBadge market={p.market} size="sm" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">{p.nicho}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="text-right">
                  <DualCurrencyDisplay
                    value={p.profit || 0}
                    market={p.market}
                    currency={p.currency}
                    rates={rates}
                    className="font-black text-red-600"
                  />
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                    ROI: {p.roi.toFixed(0)}%
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================ */}
      {/* 🔍 ANÁLISE DETALHADA DO PRODUTO            */}
      {/* ============================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 overflow-visible">
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between mb-6 gap-4">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Search size={20} className="text-blue-600" /> Análise Detalhada do
            Produto
          </h3>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <select
              className="p-2.5 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg bg-white text-gray-900 min-w-[250px] font-medium focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
              value={selectedProductId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedProductId(e.target.value)}
            >
              <option value="">Selecione um produto...</option>
              {enrichedProducts.map((p) => (
                <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                  {p.name} - {p.nicho}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
            {selectedProduct && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSelectedProductId("")}
                className="text-sm text-red-500 hover:text-red-700 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                <XCircle size={14} /> Fechar
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {selectedProduct ? (
          <div
            className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${getStatusColor(
              selectedProduct.status
            )} bg-opacity-10 rounded-xl p-6 border-2 overflow-visible ${
              selectedProduct.status === "excellent"
                ? "border-green-300"
                : selectedProduct.status === "good"
                ? "border-blue-300"
                : selectedProduct.status === "warning"
                ? "border-yellow-300"
                : "border-red-300"
            } animate-in fade-in zoom-in duration-300`}
          >
            {/* Header do Produto Selecionado */}
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div
                  className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${getStatusColor(
                    selectedProduct.status
                  )} p-3 rounded-lg text-white shadow-md`}
                >
                  <Archive size={28} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-black text-2xl text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-3 mb-2 flex-wrap">
                    {selectedProduct.name}
                    <span
                      className={`text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full ${
                        getStatusBadge(selectedProduct.status).class
                      } flex items-center gap-1`}
                    >
                      {getStatusBadge(selectedProduct.status).icon}
                      {getStatusBadge(selectedProduct.status).text}
                    </span>
                    <CurrencyBadge market={selectedProduct.market} size="md" />
                    <ExchangeRateTooltip
                      market={selectedProduct.market}
                      rates={rates}
                    />
                  </h4>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm text-gray-600">
                    <span className="font-bold">{selectedProduct.nicho}</span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span>{selectedProduct.market}</span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span
                      className={`px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${
                        selectedProduct.funnelStage === "fundo"
                          ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700"
                          : "bg-purple-100 text-purple-700"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {selectedProduct.funnelStage === "fundo"
                        ? "🎯 Fundo"
                        : "📣 Topo"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => onSelectProductForAnalysis(selectedProduct)}
                className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 flex items-center gap-2 shadow-md transition-all hover:scale-105"
              >
                <BarChart2 size={16} /> Abrir Análise Completa
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Grid de Métricas - COM CONVERSÃO DUAL */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-5 gap-3 mb-4">
              {/* Investimento */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  💰 Investimento
                </div>
                <DualCurrencyDisplay
                  value={
                    selectedProduct.market !== "BR"
                      ? selectedProduct.spend + selectedProduct.fixedCosts
                      : selectedProduct.spendBRL + selectedProduct.fixedCostsBRL
                  }
                  market={selectedProduct.market}
                  rates={rates}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Receita */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  📈 Receita
                </div>
                <DualCurrencyDisplay
                  value={
                    selectedProduct.market !== "BR"
                      ? selectedProduct.revenue
                      : selectedProduct.revenueBRL
                  }
                  market={selectedProduct.market}
                  rates={rates}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Lucro */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  💵 Lucro
                </div>
                <DualCurrencyDisplay
                  value={
                    selectedProduct.market !== "BR"
                      ? selectedProduct.profit /
                        rates[getCurrencyFromMarket(selectedProduct.market)]
                      : selectedProduct.profit
                  }
                  market={selectedProduct.market}
                  rates={rates}
                  className={
                    selectedProduct.profit >= 0
                      ? "font-bold text-green-600"
                      : "font-bold text-red-600"
                  }
                />
              </div>

              {/* ROI */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  📊 ROI
                </div>
                <div
                  className={`font-bold text-lg ${
                    selectedProduct.roi >= 0 ? "text-blue-600" : "text-red-600"
                  }`}
                >
                  {selectedProduct.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* CR */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  🎯 CR
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg">
                  {selectedProduct.cr.toFixed(2)}%
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* CPC */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  👆 CPC
                </div>
                <DualCurrencyDisplay
                  value={
                    selectedProduct.market !== "BR"
                      ? selectedProduct.cpc /
                        rates[getCurrencyFromMarket(selectedProduct.market)]
                      : selectedProduct.cpc
                  }
                  market={selectedProduct.market}
                  rates={rates}
                />
              </div>

              {/* Conversões */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  🛒 Conversões
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg">
                  {selectedProduct.conversions}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* ROAS */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  💹 ROAS
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg">
                  {selectedProduct.roas.toFixed(2)}x
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Campanhas */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  📊 Campanhas
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-gray-900 text-lg">
                  {selectedProduct.campaignCount}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Ticket Médio */}
              <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-3 rounded-lg shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
                <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold mb-1">
                  🎫 Ticket Médio
                </div>
                <DualCurrencyDisplay
                  value={
                    selectedProduct.market !== "BR"
                      ? selectedProduct.avgTicket /
                        rates[getCurrencyFromMarket(selectedProduct.market)]
                      : selectedProduct.avgTicket
                  }
                  market={selectedProduct.market}
                  rates={rates}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Health Score */}
            <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-4 rounded-lg border border-gray-200">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">
                  Health Score
                </span>
                <span
                  className={`text-2xl font-black ${
                    selectedProduct.healthScore >= 80
                      ? "text-green-600"
                      : selectedProduct.healthScore >= 60
                      ? "text-yellow-600"
                      : selectedProduct.healthScore >= 40
                      ? "text-orange-600"
                      : "text-red-600"
                  }`}
                >
