                  {selectedProduct.healthScore}
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-3 overflow-hidden">
                <div
                  className={`h-full transition-all duration-700 rounded-full ${
                    selectedProduct.healthScore >= 80
                      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-emerald-600"
                      : selectedProduct.healthScore >= 60
                      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-500 to-orange-500"
                      : selectedProduct.healthScore >= 40
                      ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-500 to-red-500"
                      : "bg-gradient-to-r from-red-500 to-pink-600"
                  }`}
                  style={{ width: `${selectedProduct.healthScore}%` }}
                ></div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="text-center py-12 text-gray-400 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200">
            <Search className="mx-auto mb-3 text-gray-300" size={40} />
            <p className="font-medium">
              Selecione um produto para ver análise detalhada
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ============================================ */}
      {/* 🔍 FILTROS & BUSCA                         */}
      {/* ============================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-4">
          <Filter className="text-indigo-600" size={20} />
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900">Filtros & Busca</h3>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-4">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search
              className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400"
              size={18}
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Buscar produto, nicho..."
              value={searchTerm}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
              className="pl-10 pr-4 py-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900"
            />
          </div>

          <select
            value={filterStatus}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value as FilterStatus)}
            className="p-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900 font-medium"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os Status</option>
            <option value="profitable">✅ Lucrativos</option>
            <option value="negative">🚨 No Prejuízo</option>
            <option value="neutral">⚪ Neutros</option>
          </select>

          <select
            value={filterNicho}
            onChange={(e) => setFilterNicho(e.target.value)}
            className="p-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-300 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none bg-white text-gray-900 font-medium"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os Nichos</option>
            {uniqueNichos.map((nicho) => (
              <option key={nicho} value={nicho}>
                {nicho}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setSearchTerm("");
              setFilterStatus("all");
              setFilterNicho("all");
            }}
            className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-medium hover:bg-gray-200 transition-colors"
          >
            Limpar Filtros
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="text-sm text-gray-600">
          Mostrando <strong>{filteredAndSortedProducts.length}</strong> de{" "}
          <strong>{products.length}</strong> produtos
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================ */}
      {/* 📁 LISTA DE PRODUTOS                       */}
      {/* ============================================ */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2">
            <FolderOpen size={20} className="text-yellow-500" /> Minha Pasta de
            Produtos
          </h3>
          <div className="flex gap-2 text-xs">
            <button
              onClick={() => handleSort("profit")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded ${
                sortField === "profit"
                  ? "bg-indigo-600 text-white"
                  : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200"
              }`}
            >
              Ordenar por Lucro{" "}
              {sortField === "profit" && (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => handleSort("roi")}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded ${
                sortField === "roi"
                  ? "bg-indigo-600 text-white"
                  : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200"
              }`}
            >
              Ordenar por ROI{" "}
              {sortField === "roi" && (sortDirection === "asc" ? "↑" : "↓")}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-4 max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto pr-2">
          {filteredAndSortedProducts.map((p) => {
            const badge = getStatusBadge(p.status);
            return (
              <div
                key={p.id}
                className={`group relative bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-gray-50 border-2 rounded-xl p-5 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-indigo-300 transition-all duration-300 cursor-pointer ${
                  selectedProductId === p.id
                    ? "border-indigo-500 shadow-lg ring-2 ring-indigo-200"
                    : "border-gray-200"
                }`}
                onClick={() => setSelectedProductId(p.id)}
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3 flex-1">
                    <div
                      className={`bg-gradient-to-br ${getStatusColor(
                        p.status
                      )} p-3 rounded-lg text-white shadow-md`}
                    >
                      <Archive size={24} />
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1">
                      <h4 className="font-black text-gray-900 text-lg mb-1 flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        {p.name}
                        <CurrencyBadge market={p.market} size="sm" />
                      </h4>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-600">
                        <span className="font-semibold">{p.nicho}</span>
                        <span>•</span>
                        <span>{p.market}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <span
                    className={`px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold ${badge.class} flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap`}
                  >
                    {badge.icon}
                    {badge.text}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 mb-4">
                  {/* Lucro com Conversão Dual */}
                  <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-center">
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">
                      Lucro
                    </div>
                    <DualCurrencyDisplay
                      value={
                        p.market !== "BR"
                          ? p.profit / rates[getCurrencyFromMarket(p.market)]
                          : p.profit
                      }
                      market={p.market}
                      rates={rates}
                      className={`font-black text-sm ${
                        p.profit >= 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-600"
                      }`}
                      showOriginal={false}
                    />
                  </div>

                  {/* ROI */}
                  <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-center">
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">
                      ROI
                    </div>
                    <div
                      className={`font-black text-base ${
                        p.roi >= 0 ? "text-blue-600" : "text-red-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.roi.toFixed(0)}%
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Campanhas */}
                  <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-center">
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">
                      Campanhas
                    </div>
                    <div className="font-black text-base text-gray-900">
                      {p.campaignCount}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Health Score */}
                <div className="mb-4">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
                    <span className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">
                      Health Score
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`text-sm font-black ${
                        p.healthScore >= 80
                          ? "text-green-600"
                          : p.healthScore >= 60
                          ? "text-yellow-600"
                          : p.healthScore >= 40
                          ? "text-orange-600"
                          : "text-red-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.healthScore}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2 overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className={`h-full transition-all duration-700 ${
                        p.healthScore >= 80
                          ? "bg-green-500"
                          : p.healthScore >= 60
                          ? "bg-yellow-500"
                          : p.healthScore >= 40
                          ? "bg-orange-500"
                          : "bg-red-500"
                      }`}
                      style={{ width: `${p.healthScore}%` }}
                    ></div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Botões de Ação */}
                <div className="flex gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-gray-200">
                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      onSelectProductForAnalysis(p);
                    }}
                    className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-blue-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all hover:scale-105"
                  >
                    <BarChart2 size={14} /> Análise
                  </button>
                  {setEditingProduct && onChangeTab && (
                    <button
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        setEditingProduct(p);
                        onChangeTab("mapping");
                      }}
                      className="flex-1 bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-purple-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all hover:scale-105"
                      title="Editar este produto no Mapping"
                    >
                      ✏️ Editar
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      onDeleteProduct(p.id);
                    }}
                    className="bg-red-50 text-red-700 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-red-100 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition-all"
                    title="Excluir Produto"
                  >
                    <Trash2 size={14} /> Excluir
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}

          {/* Estado Vazio */}
          {filteredAndSortedProducts.length === 0 && (
            <div className="col-span-2 text-center py-16 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200">
              <FolderOpen size={48} className="mx-auto text-gray-300 mb-3" />
              <p className="text-gray-500 font-medium text-lg mb-2">
                {products.length === 0
                  ? "Sua pasta está vazia"
                  : "Nenhum produto encontrado"}
              </p>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                {products.length === 0
                  ? 'Vá em "Mapeamento" e salve seu primeiro produto'
                  : "Tente ajustar os filtros"}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ============================================ */}
      {/* ⚡ OTIMIZADORES DE PORTFÓLIO               */}
      {/* ============================================ */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-900 to-slate-800 p-8 rounded-xl text-white shadow-xl border border-slate-700">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
          <Zap className="text-yellow-400 animate-pulse" size={28} />
          <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold">Otimizadores de Portfólio</h3>
        </div>

        {/* Seletor de Método */}
        <div className="mb-6">
          <label className="text-sm text-gray-300 mb-3 block font-semibold">
            Escolha o Método de Otimização:
          </label>
          <select
            value={optimizationMethod}
            onChange={(e) =>
              setOptimizationMethod(e.target.value as OptimizationMethod)
            }
            className="w-full p-3 border-2 border-slate-600 rounded-lg bg-slate-700 text-white font-bold focus:ring-2 focus:ring-yellow-500 focus:border-yellow-500 outline-none transition-all"
          >
            <option value="simple">
              📊 Distribuição Equilibrada (Iniciante)
            </option>
            <option value="simplex">🎯 Simplex Otimizado (Avançado)</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        {/* Info Box do Método */}
        {optimizationMethod === "simple" ? (
          <div className="bg-blue-900/30 border-l-4 border-blue-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg mb-6 text-sm text-blue-100">
            <strong className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Info size={16} /> 📊 Distribuição Equilibrada
            </strong>
            <p className="leading-relaxed">
              Distribui o orçamento proporcionalmente ao ROI de cada produto.
              Simples e eficaz!
              <strong className="block mt-2">✅ Ideal para:</strong> Quem está
              começando ou quer uma distribuição rápida e balanceada.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-blue-200 text-xs">
              💡 Produtos internacionais são convertidos automaticamente para
              BRL nos cálculos
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="bg-purple-900/30 border-l-4 border-purple-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg mb-6 text-sm text-purple-100">
            <strong className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-2">
              <Info size={16} /> 🎯 Simplex Otimizado
            </strong>
            <p className="leading-relaxed">
              Algoritmo matemático que maximiza o lucro total considerando
              histórico, confiança nos dados e capacidade de cada produto.
              <strong className="block mt-2">✅ Ideal para:</strong> Quem tem
              histórico (5+ campanhas) e quer maximizar lucro com recomendações
              específicas.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-purple-200 text-xs">
              💡 Todos os cálculos são feitos em BRL (conversão automática
              aplicada)
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Input de Budget + Botões */}
        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 items-end mb-6">
          <div className="flex-1 w-full">
            <label className="text-sm text-gray-300 mb-2 block font-semibold">
              Orçamento Mensal Disponível (R$)
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <span className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400 font-bold">
                R$
              </span>
              <input
                type="number"
                value={simplexBudget}
                onChange={(e) => setSimplexBudget(parseFloat(e.target.value))}
                className="bg-slate-700 border-2 border-slate-600 rounded-lg p-3 pl-10 w-full text-white placeholder-gray-500 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-yellow-500 focus:border-yellow-500 outline-none transition-all font-bold"
                placeholder="1000.00"
              />
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
              💡 Insira seu orçamento em Reais (BRL)
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={handleOptimize}
            className="w-full md:w-auto bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-500 to-orange-500 text-slate-900 px-8 py-3 rounded-lg font-black hover:from-yellow-400 hover:to-orange-400 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg hover:scale-105"
          >
            <TrendingUp size={20} /> Calcular Alocação Ótima
          </button>

          <button
            onClick={() => {
              setAllocation(null);
              setOptimizationMethod("simple");
              setSimplexBudget(1000);
            }}
            className="w-full md:w-auto bg-red-600 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-lg font-black hover:bg-red-700 transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg"
          >
            🔄 Limpar Resultados
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Resultados da Otimização */}
        {allocation && allocation.allocation && (
          <div className="bg-slate-700/50 rounded-xl p-6 animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 border-2 border-slate-600 backdrop-blur-sm">
            {/* Aviso Simplex */}
            {optimizationMethod === "simplex" && (
              <div className="mb-6 bg-purple-900/30 border-l-4 border-purple-500 p-4 rounded-r-lg">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <Info className="text-purple-400 mt-0.5" size={20} />
                  <div>
                    <strong className="text-purple-300 block mb-1">
                      🎯 Modo Conservador Ativo
                    </strong>
                    <p className="text-sm text-purple-200 leading-relaxed">
                      O Simplex só investe onde tem{" "}
                      <strong>
                        confiança nos dados e ROI positivo consistente
                      </strong>
                      .
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-3 bg-purple-950/50 p-2 rounded border border-purple-800">
                      <p className="text-xs text-purple-200">
                        <strong>Por que pode sobrar budget?</strong>
                        <br />
                        • Histórico limitado (menos campanhas = menos confiança)
                        <br />
                        • ROI muito baixo ou inconsistente
                        <br />
                        • Produto já atingiu capacidade máxima recomendada
                        <br />• Risco maior que o retorno esperado
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-purple-300 mt-2">
                      💡 <strong>Dica:</strong> Se quiser distribuir todo o
                      budget, use o modo <strong>Simples</strong>.
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Mensagem customizada */}
            {allocation.message && (
              <div className="mb-6 bg-slate-800 p-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-600">
                <p className="text-white font-medium">{allocation.message}</p>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Métricas Resumo - SIMPLEX */}
            {optimizationMethod === "simplex" &&
              Array.isArray(allocation.allocation) &&
              allocation.allocation.length > 0 && (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-6 bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-600">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold mb-2">
                      Budget Total
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white">
                      R$ {(allocation.totalBudget || 0).toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold mb-2">
                      Lucro Esperado
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-400">
                      R$ {(allocation.totalExpectedProfit || 0).toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold mb-2">
                      ROI Médio
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-yellow-400">
                      {(allocation.efficiency || 0).toFixed(1)}%
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

            {/* Métricas Resumo - SIMPLES */}
            {optimizationMethod === "simple" &&
              Array.isArray(allocation.allocation) &&
              allocation.allocation.length > 0 && (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-6 bg-slate-800 p-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-600">
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold mb-2">
                      Budget Alocado
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white">
                      R${" "}
                      {(Array.isArray(allocation.allocation)
                        ? allocation.allocation.reduce(
                            (total, item) =>
                              total +
                              (item.allocatedBudget || item.amount || 0),
                            0
                          )
                        : 0
                      ).toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold mb-2">
                      Lucro Esperado
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-400">
                      R$ {allocation.totalProfit?.toFixed(2) || "0.00"}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase font-bold mb-2">
                      ROI Médio
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-yellow-400">
                      {allocation.efficiency?.toFixed(1) || "0.0"}%
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

            {/* Header da Lista */}
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-800/50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-600">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Package className="text-blue-400" size={16} />
                <span className="text-sm text-gray-300">
                  Produtos analisados:
                </span>
              </div>
              <span className="font-black text-white text-lg">
                {products.length}
              </span>
            </div>

            <h4 className="font-bold mb-6 text-yellow-400 flex items-center gap-2 text-lg">
              <Target /> Alocação Recomendada
            </h4>

            {/* Lista de Alocações */}
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {(allocation?.allocation || [])
                .filter(
                  (item: AllocationItem | undefined) => item && item.productName
                )
                .map((item: AllocationItem, idx: number) => (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className="p-5 bg-slate-800 rounded-xl border-2 border-slate-600 shadow-lg hover:border-slate-500 transition-all"
                  >
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <div className="font-bold text-white text-xl mb-1">
                          {item.productName}
                        </div>

                        {/* Recomendações Simplex */}
                        {optimizationMethod === "simplex" &&
                          item.recommendation && (
                            <>
                              {item.recommendation === "AUMENTAR" && (
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-blue-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                                  <TrendingUp size={16} />
                                  Aumentar em {item.changePercent?.toFixed(0)}%
                                  (de R$ {item.previousBudget?.toFixed(0)} → R${" "}
                                  {item.allocatedBudget.toFixed(0)})
                                </div>
                              )}

                              {item.recommendation === "REDUZIR" && (
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-orange-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                                  <TrendingDown size={16} />
                                  Reduzir em{" "}
                                  {Math.abs(item.changePercent || 0).toFixed(0)}
                                  % (de R$ {item.previousBudget?.toFixed(0)} →
                                  R$ {item.allocatedBudget.toFixed(0)})
                                </div>
                              )}

                              {item.recommendation === "MANTER" && (
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-green-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                                  <CheckCircle size={16} />
                                  Manter investimento (R${" "}
                                  {item.previousBudget?.toFixed(0)})
                                </div>
                              )}

                              {item.recommendation === "NOVO" && (
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-purple-400 font-semibold mb-1">
                                  ✨ Novo investimento recomendado
                                </div>
                              )}

                              <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">
                                Confiança nos dados:{" "}
                                <span
                                  className={`font-bold ${
                                    item.confidence === "Alta"
                                      ? "text-green-400"
                                      : item.confidence === "Média"
                                      ? "text-yellow-400"
                                      : "text-orange-400"
                                  }`}
                                >
                                  {item.confidence}
                                </span>
                              </div>
                            </>
                          )}

                        {/* Info Simples */}
                        {optimizationMethod === "simple" && (
                          <div className="text-xs text-green-400 font-mono mt-1">
                            🎯 ROI Esperado:{" "}
                            {item.expectedROI?.toFixed(1) || "0.0"}%
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Grid de Valores */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
                      <div className="bg-slate-700 p-3 rounded">
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold">
                          Investir
                        </div>
                        <div className="font-bold text-white text-lg">
                          R${" "}
                          {(item.allocatedBudget || item.amount || 0).toFixed(
                            2
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="bg-slate-700 p-3 rounded">
                        <div className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold">
                          Lucro Esperado
                        </div>
                        <div className="font-bold text-green-400 text-lg">
                          R$ {(item.expectedProfit || 0).toFixed(2)}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}

              {/* Estado Vazio - Nenhuma Alocação */}
              {allocation &&
                Array.isArray(allocation.allocation) &&
                allocation.allocation.length === 0 && (
                  <div className="bg-red-900/20 border-2 border-red-500 rounded-xl p-6">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                      <AlertCircle className="text-red-400 mt-1" size={32} />
                      <div>
                        <strong className="text-red-300 text-lg block mb-2">
                          ⚠️ Nenhuma alocação viável encontrada
                        </strong>
                        <p className="text-red-200 text-sm mb-3 leading-relaxed">
                          {optimizationMethod === "simplex"
                            ? "O Simplex é conservador e não encontrou produtos com confiança suficiente para investir com segurança."
                            : "Nenhum produto apresentou ROI positivo suficiente para recomendar investimento."}
                        </p>
                        <div className="bg-red-950/50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-red-800">
                          <strong className="text-red-300 text-sm block mb-2">
                            💡 O que fazer:
                          </strong>
                          <ul className="text-sm text-red-200 space-y-1.5">
                            {optimizationMethod === "simplex" ? (
                              <>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Use o modo Simples</strong> para
                                  distribuir o budget mesmo com poucos dados
                                </li>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Cadastre mais campanhas</strong>{" "}
                                  para aumentar a confiança nos dados
                                </li>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Melhore o ROI dos produtos</strong>{" "}
                                  testando novas estratégias
                                </li>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Aumente o budget</strong> para dar
                                  mais margem ao algoritmo
                                </li>
                              </>
                            ) : (
                              <>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Revise seus produtos:</strong>{" "}
                                  adicione produtos com ROI positivo
                                </li>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Cadastre mais campanhas</strong>{" "}
                                  para ter dados mais precisos
                                </li>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Otimize suas campanhas</strong> para
                                  melhorar o desempenho
                                </li>
                                <li>
                                  ✓ <strong>Verifique os custos fixos</strong>{" "}
                                  que podem estar impactando o ROI
                                </li>
                              </>
                            )}
                          </ul>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}

              {/* Reserva de Segurança */}
              {allocation && allocation.remainingBudget > 0 && (
                <div className="mt-4 p-5 bg-slate-800/30 rounded-xl border-2 border-slate-600 border-dashed">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-3">
                    <span className="text-gray-300 font-semibold">
                      💰 Reserva de Segurança (Não Alocada)
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-yellow-400 font-black text-xl">
                      R$ {allocation.remainingBudget.toFixed(2)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-slate-900/50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-700">
                    <strong className="text-yellow-300 text-xs block mb-2">
                      🔒 Por que sobrou dinheiro?
                    </strong>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-300 leading-relaxed">
                      {optimizationMethod === "simplex" ? (
                        <>
                          O <strong>Simplex é conservador</strong> e só investe
                          onde tem certeza de retorno positivo.
                          <br />• <strong>Histórico limitado:</strong> Poucos
                          dados = menor confiança
                          <br />• <strong>Capacidade máxima:</strong> Produto já
                          recebeu o máximo recomendado
                          <br />• <strong>ROI insuficiente:</strong> Investir
                          mais não compensaria o risco
                          <span className="block mt-2 text-yellow-200">
                            💡 <strong>Quer usar todo o budget?</strong> Mude
                            para o modo <strong>Simples</strong>
                          </span>
                        </>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          Este valor não foi distribuído porque:
                          <br />• Os produtos já receberam alocação proporcional
                          ao ROI
                          <br />• Investir mais não seria eficiente com os dados
                          atuais
                        </>
                      )}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: ProductComparator.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\ProductComparator.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  RadarChart,
  PolarGrid,
  PolarAngleAxis,
  PolarRadiusAxis,
  Radar,
} from "recharts";
import {
  TrendingUp,
  Award,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle,
  Target,
} from "lucide-react";
import { compareProducts } from "../services/logic";
import { useCurrency } from "../contexts/CurrencyContext";
import type { Product, Campaign } from "../types";

interface ProductComparatorProps {
  products: Product[];
  campaigns: Campaign[];
}

export const ProductComparator: React.FC<ProductComparatorProps> = ({
  products,
  campaigns,
}) => {
  const [selectedProducts, setSelectedProducts] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const { rates } = useCurrency();

  // ============================================================================
  // FUNÇÕES AUXILIARES DE MOEDA
  // ============================================================================

  /**
   * Retorna a taxa de câmbio para conversão em BRL
   * Aceita tanto currency quanto market (BR/BRL/US/USD/UK/GBP)
   */
  const getRate = (currencyOrMarket: string): number => {
    const key = (currencyOrMarket || "BR").toUpperCase();

    if (key === "BR" || key === "BRL") return 1;
    if (key === "US" || key === "USD") return rates.USD || 1;
    if (key === "UK" || key === "GBP") return rates.GBP || 1;

    return 1; // Fallback seguro
  };

  /**
   * Retorna o símbolo da moeda baseado em currency ou market
   */
  const getCurrencySymbol = (currencyOrMarket: string): string => {
    const key = (currencyOrMarket || "BR").toUpperCase();

    if (key === "BR" || key === "BRL") return "R$";
    if (key === "US" || key === "USD") return "$";
    if (key === "UK" || key === "GBP") return "£";

    return "R$"; // Fallback seguro
  };

  /**
   * Verifica se a moeda é internacional (não-BRL)
   */
  const isInternational = (currencyOrMarket: string): boolean => {
    const key = (currencyOrMarket || "BR").toUpperCase();
    return key !== "BR" && key !== "BRL";
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // CÁLCULO DA COMPARAÇÃO
  // ============================================================================

  const comparison = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedProducts.length < 2) return null;
    return compareProducts(selectedProducts, products, campaigns, rates);
  }, [selectedProducts, products, campaigns, rates]);

  // ============================================================================
  // FILTRO DE PRODUTOS (Apenas com histórico)
  // ============================================================================

  const productsWithHistory = useMemo(() => {
    return products.filter((product) =>
      campaigns.some((c) => c.productId === product.id)
    );
  }, [products, campaigns]);

  // ============================================================================
  // HANDLERS
  // ============================================================================

  const toggleProduct = (productId: string) => {
    setSelectedProducts((prev) => {
      if (prev.includes(productId)) {
        return prev.filter((id) => id !== productId);
      }
      if (prev.length >= 3) {
        return [...prev.slice(1), productId]; // Remove primeiro, adiciona novo
      }
      return [...prev, productId];
    });
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // COMPONENTE: CÉLULA MONETÁRIA
  // ============================================================================

  const MoneyCell: React.FC<{
    value: number;
    product: Product;
    showOriginal?: boolean;
  }> = ({ value, product, showOriginal = false }) => {
    const currencyKey = product.currency || product.market || "BR";
    const symbol = getCurrencySymbol(currencyKey);
    const rate = getRate(currencyKey);
    const valueInBRL = value * rate;
    const isIntl = isInternational(currencyKey);

    const colorClass = value >= 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-600";

    // Se for internacional E deve mostrar original
    if (isIntl && showOriginal) {
      return (
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5">
          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">
            {symbol} {value.toFixed(2)}
          </span>
          <span className={`font-bold ${colorClass}`}>
            ≈ R$ {valueInBRL.toFixed(2)}
          </span>
        </div>
      );
    }

    // Caso contrário, sempre exibir em BRL
    return (
      <span className={`font-bold ${colorClass}`}>
        R$ {valueInBRL.toFixed(2)}
      </span>
    );
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // DADOS PARA GRÁFICOS
  // ============================================================================

  const barData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!comparison) return [];

    return [
      {
        name: "ROI (%)",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p) => {
            const product = products.find((prod) => prod.id === p.productId);
            return [product?.name.slice(0, 10) || "N/A", p.roi];
          })
        ),
      },
      {
        name: "CR (%)",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p) => {
            const product = products.find((prod) => prod.id === p.productId);
            return [product?.name.slice(0, 10) || "N/A", p.cr];
          })
        ),
      },
      {
        name: "CPC (R$)",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p) => {
            const product = products.find((prod) => prod.id === p.productId);
            const rate = getRate(product?.currency || product?.market || "BR");
            const cpcInBRL = p.cpc * rate;
            return [product?.name.slice(0, 10) || "N/A", cpcInBRL];
          })
        ),
      },
    ];
  }, [comparison, products]);

  const radarData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!comparison) return [];

    return [
      {
        subject: "ROI",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p, i) => [
            `produto${i}`,
            Math.max(0, Math.min(100, p.roi)),
          ])
        ),
      },
      {
        subject: "CR",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p, i) => [`produto${i}`, p.cr * 10])
        ),
      },
      {
        subject: "Score",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p, i) => [`produto${i}`, p.overallScore])
        ),
      },
      {
        subject: "Volume",
        ...Object.fromEntries(
          comparison.products.map((p, i) => [
            `produto${i}`,
            Math.min(100, p.totalCampaigns * 10),
          ])
        ),
      },
    ];
  }, [comparison]);

  // ============================================================================
  // CUSTOM TOOLTIP
  // ============================================================================

  const CustomBarTooltip: React.FC<any> = ({ active, payload, label }) => {
    if (!active || !payload || !payload.length) return null;

    return (
      <div className="bg-white p-3 rounded-lg shadow-lg border border-gray-200">
        <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-2">{label}</p>
        {payload.map((entry: any, index: number) => {
          let displayValue = entry.value;
          let suffix = "";

          if (label === "ROI (%)" || label === "CR (%)") {
            suffix = "%";
          } else if (label === "CPC (R$)") {
            suffix = "";
          }

          return (
            <div key={index} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <div
                className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full"
                style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
              />
              <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">
                {entry.name}:{" "}
                <span className="font-bold">
                  {label === "CPC (R$)" ? "R$ " : ""}
                  {displayValue.toFixed(2)}
                  {suffix}
                </span>
              </span>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    );
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // RENDER
  // ============================================================================

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 text-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
          <Target size={28} />
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Comparador de Produtos</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="text-purple-100">
          Selecione de 2 a 3 produtos para comparar performance lado a lado
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Seleção de Produtos */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <CheckCircle size={18} className="text-green-600" />
          Selecione os Produtos ({selectedProducts.length}/3)
        </h3>

        {productsWithHistory.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-12">
            <AlertCircle size={48} className="mx-auto mb-4 text-gray-300" />
            <p className="text-gray-500 font-medium">
              Aguardando produtos com histórico de campanhas para comparação.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            {productsWithHistory.map((product) => {
              const isSelected = selectedProducts.includes(product.id);
              const symbol = getCurrencySymbol(
                product.currency || product.market || "BR"
              );

              return (
                <button
                  key={product.id}
                  onClick={() => toggleProduct(product.id)}
                  className={`px-4 py-3 rounded-lg font-medium transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? "bg-purple-600 text-white shadow-lg scale-105"
                      : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-800 hover:bg-gray-200 border border-gray-300"
                  }`}
                >
                  {isSelected && "✓ "}
                  {product.name}
                  <span className="ml-1.5 text-xs opacity-75">({symbol})</span>
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {selectedProducts.length === 0 && productsWithHistory.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-blue-50 border-l-4 border-blue-500 rounded">
            <p className="text-sm text-blue-800">
              💡 <strong>Dica:</strong> Selecione pelo menos 2 produtos para ver
              a comparação.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}

        {selectedProducts.length === 1 && (
          <div className="mt-4 p-4 bg-yellow-50 border-l-4 border-yellow-500 rounded">
            <p className="text-sm text-yellow-800">
              ⚠️ Selecione mais 1 produto para começar a comparação.
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Comparação */}
      {comparison && (
        <>
          {/* Vencedor */}
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-yellow-50 to-amber-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-yellow-300 shadow-lg">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
              <div className="p-3 bg-yellow-100 rounded-full">
                <Award className="text-yellow-600" size={28} />
              </div>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-black text-yellow-900">
                  🏆 Vencedor: {comparison.winner.productName}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-yellow-700">
                  {comparison.winner.reason}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Recomendações */}
          {comparison.recommendations.length > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertCircle size={18} className="text-blue-600" />
                Recomendações Estratégicas
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {comparison.recommendations.map((rec, idx) => (
                  <div
                    key={idx}
                    className="flex items-start gap-3 p-3 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg border-l-4 border-blue-500"
                  >
                    <div className="text-2xl">
                      {rec.includes("⚠️")
                        ? "⚠️"
                        : rec.includes("💰")
                        ? "💰"
                        : "🎯"}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm text-blue-900 flex-1">{rec}</p>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Insights */}
          {comparison.insights.length > 0 && (
            <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 p-6 rounded-xl border border-indigo-200 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-indigo-900 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <TrendingUp size={18} />
                Insights da Comparação
              </h3>
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {comparison.insights.map((insight, idx) => (
                  <p key={idx} className="text-sm text-indigo-800">
                    {insight}
                  </p>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Tabela Comparativa */}
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm overflow-x-auto">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
              📊 Comparação Detalhada
            </h3>
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-gray-50">
                <tr>
                  <th className="text-left p-3 font-bold text-gray-700">
                    Métrica
                  </th>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => {
                    const product = products.find(
                      (prod) => prod.id === p.productId
                    );
                    const symbol = getCurrencySymbol(
                      product?.currency || product?.market || "BR"
                    );
                    return (
                      <th
                        key={p.productId}
                        className="text-center p-3 font-bold text-gray-700"
                      >
                        {p.productName}
                        <span className="ml-1.5 text-xs font-normal text-gray-500">
                          ({symbol})
                        </span>
                        {p.productId === comparison.winner.productId && " 🏆"}
                      </th>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">Score Geral</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => (
                    <td
                      key={p.productId}
                      className="p-3 text-center font-bold text-purple-600"
                    >
                      {p.overallScore.toFixed(0)}/100
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">ROI</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => (
                    <td
                      key={p.productId}
                      className={`p-3 text-center font-bold ${
                        p.roi >= 0 ? "text-green-600" : "text-red-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">Lucro</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => {
                    const product = products.find(
                      (prod) => prod.id === p.productId
                    );
                    return (
                      <td key={p.productId} className="p-3 text-center">
                        {product && (
                          <MoneyCell
                            value={p.totalProfit}
                            product={product}
                            showOriginal={true}
                          />
                        )}
                      </td>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">CR (%)</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => (
                    <td
                      key={p.productId}
                      className="p-3 text-center font-bold text-blue-600"
                    >
                      {p.cr.toFixed(2)}%
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">CPC (BRL)</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => {
                    const product = products.find(
                      (prod) => prod.id === p.productId
                    );
                    return (
                      <td key={p.productId} className="p-3 text-center">
                        {product && (
                          <MoneyCell
                            value={p.cpc}
                            product={product}
                            showOriginal={false}
                          />
                        )}
                      </td>
                    );
                  })}
                </tr>
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">Conversões</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => (
                    <td
                      key={p.productId}
                      className="p-3 text-center text-gray-700"
                    >
                      {p.totalConversions}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
                <tr className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <td className="p-3 font-medium text-gray-700">Campanhas</td>
                  {comparison.products.map((p) => (
                    <td
                      key={p.productId}
                      className="p-3 text-center text-gray-700"
                    >
                      {p.totalCampaigns}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          {/* Gráficos */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {/* Gráfico de Barras */}
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
                📊 Métricas Principais
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-3">
                * Valores convertidos para BRL para comparação justa
              </p>
              <div className="h-[300px]">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={barData}>
                    <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="name" stroke="#6b7280" fontSize={12} />
                    <YAxis stroke="#6b7280" fontSize={12} />
                    <Tooltip content={<CustomBarTooltip />} />
                    <Legend />
                    {comparison.products.map((p, idx) => {
                      const product = products.find(
                        (prod) => prod.id === p.productId
                      );
                      return (
                        <Bar
                          key={p.productId}
                          dataKey={product?.name.slice(0, 10) || "N/A"}
                          fill={["#8b5cf6", "#3b82f6", "#10b981"][idx]}
                          radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                        />
                      );
                    })}
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Gráfico Radar */}
            <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4">
                🎯 Performance Geral
              </h3>
              <div className="h-[300px]">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <RadarChart data={radarData}>
                    <PolarGrid stroke="#e5e7eb" />
                    <PolarAngleAxis
                      dataKey="subject"
                      stroke="#6b7280"
                      fontSize={12}
                    />
                    <PolarRadiusAxis stroke="#9ca3af" fontSize={10} />
                    {comparison.products.map((p, idx) => (
                      <Radar
                        key={p.productId}
                        name={p.productName.slice(0, 15)}
                        dataKey={`produto${idx}`}
                        stroke={["#8b5cf6", "#3b82f6", "#10b981"][idx]}
                        fill={["#8b5cf6", "#3b82f6", "#10b981"][idx]}
                        fillOpacity={0.3}
                      />
                    ))}
                    <Legend />
                  </RadarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: ReportGenerator.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\ReportGenerator.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import {
  FileText,
  Download,
  Calendar,
  TrendingUp,
  TrendingDown,
  Award,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle,
  DollarSign,
  Target,
  Zap,
  Clock,
  Filter,
  Globe,
  PieChart as PieChartIcon,
  BarChart3,
  TrendingDown as Loss,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  PieChart,
  Pie,
  Cell,
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  Tooltip,
  ResponsiveContainer,
} from "recharts";
import { startOfDay, endOfDay, subDays } from "date-fns";
import jsPDF from "jspdf";
import autoTable from "jspdf-autotable";
import { generateReport } from "../services/logic";
import type { Product, Campaign } from "../types";

interface ReportGeneratorProps {
  products: Product[];
  campaigns: Campaign[];
  monthlyGoal?: number;
  rates?: Record<string, number>;
}

const getCurrencySymbol = (currency: string) => {
  const symbols: Record<string, string> = {
    USD: "$",
    EUR: "€",
    GBP: "£",
    BRL: "R$",
  };
  return symbols[currency] || currency;
};

const getCurrencyFlag = (currency: string) => {
  const flags: Record<string, string> = {
    USD: "🇺🇸",
    EUR: "🇪🇺",
    GBP: "🇬🇧",
    BRL: "🇧🇷",
  };
  return flags[currency] || "🌍";
};

export const ReportGenerator: React.FC<ReportGeneratorProps> = ({
  products,
  campaigns,
  monthlyGoal = 0,
  rates = {},
}) => {
  const [periodType, setPeriodType] = useState<"week" | "month" | "custom">(
    "week"
  );
  const [customStart, setCustomStart] = useState("");
  const [customEnd, setCustomEnd] = useState("");
  const [filterMarket, setFilterMarket] = useState<
    "all" | "nacional" | "internacional"
  >("all");
  const [filterNiche, setFilterNiche] = useState<string>("all");
  const [filterPlatform, setFilterPlatform] = useState<string>("all");
  const [pieChartMode, setPieChartMode] = useState<"niche" | "market">("niche");
  const [barChartMode, setBarChartMode] = useState<"roi" | "profit">("roi");

  const { startDate, endDate } = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();
    let start: Date;
    let end: Date = endOfDay(now);

    if (periodType === "week") {
      start = startOfDay(subDays(now, 7));
    } else if (periodType === "month") {
      start = startOfDay(subDays(now, 30));
    } else {
      start = customStart
        ? startOfDay(new Date(customStart))
        : startOfDay(subDays(now, 7));
      end = customEnd ? endOfDay(new Date(customEnd)) : endOfDay(now);
    }

    return { startDate: start, endDate: end };
  }, [periodType, customStart, customEnd]);

  const baseReport = useMemo(() => {
    return generateReport(
      products,
      campaigns,
      startDate,
      endDate,
      monthlyGoal,
      rates
    );
  }, [products, campaigns, startDate, endDate, monthlyGoal, rates]);

  const availableNiches = useMemo(() => {
    const niches = new Set<string>();
    baseReport.productPerformance.forEach((p: any) => {
      niches.add(p.nicho || "Sem Nicho");
    });
    return Array.from(niches).sort();
  }, [baseReport]);

  const availablePlatforms = useMemo(() => {
    const platforms = new Set<string>();
    products.forEach((p) => {
      if (p.platform) platforms.add(p.platform);
    });
    return Array.from(platforms).sort();
  }, [products]);

  const report = useMemo(() => {
    let filteredPerformance = [...baseReport.productPerformance];

    if (filterMarket === "nacional") {
      filteredPerformance = filteredPerformance.filter(
        (p: any) => p.market === "BR"
      );
    } else if (filterMarket === "internacional") {
      filteredPerformance = filteredPerformance.filter(
        (p: any) => p.market !== "BR"
      );
    }

    if (filterNiche !== "all") {
      filteredPerformance = filteredPerformance.filter(
        (p: any) => (p.nicho || "Sem Nicho") === filterNiche
      );
    }

    if (filterPlatform !== "all") {
      const filteredProductIds = products
        .filter((p) => p.platform === filterPlatform)
        .map((p) => p.id);
      filteredPerformance = filteredPerformance.filter((p: any) => {
        const prod = products.find((pr) => pr.name === p.name);
        return prod && filteredProductIds.includes(prod.id);
      });
    }

    const totalRevenue = filteredPerformance.reduce(
      (sum: number, p: any) => sum + p.revenue,
      0
    );
    const totalSpend = filteredPerformance.reduce(
      (sum: number, p: any) => sum + p.spend,
      0
    );
    const totalProfit = filteredPerformance.reduce(
      (sum: number, p: any) => sum + p.profitBRL,
      0
    );
    const roi = totalSpend > 0 ? (totalProfit / totalSpend) * 100 : 0;
    const margin = totalRevenue > 0 ? (totalProfit / totalRevenue) * 100 : 0;

    return {
      ...baseReport,
      productPerformance: filteredPerformance,
      summary: {
        ...baseReport.summary,
        totalRevenue,
        totalSpend,
        totalProfit,
        roi,
        margin,
        productsActive: filteredPerformance.length,
      },
    };
  }, [baseReport, filterMarket, filterNiche, filterPlatform, products]);

  const topPerformers = useMemo(() => {
    const withSales = report.productPerformance.filter(
      (p: any) => p.conversions >= 3
    );

    const bestProfit = [...report.productPerformance].sort(
      (a, b) => b.profitBRL - a.profitBRL
    )[0];
    const bestROI =
      withSales.length > 0
        ? [...withSales].sort((a, b) => b.roi - a.roi)[0]
        : null;
    const worstLoss = [...report.productPerformance].sort(
      (a, b) => a.profitBRL - b.profitBRL
    )[0];

    return {
      bestProfit,
      bestROI,
      worstLoss: worstLoss?.profitBRL < 0 ? worstLoss : null,
    };
  }, [report]);

  const goalProgress = useMemo(() => {
    if (!monthlyGoal || monthlyGoal <= 0) return null;

    const currentProfit = report.summary.totalProfit;
    const progressPercent = Math.min((currentProfit / monthlyGoal) * 100, 100);
    const remaining = Math.max(monthlyGoal - currentProfit, 0);

    let color = "bg-red-500";
    if (progressPercent >= 70) color = "bg-green-500";
    else if (progressPercent >= 30) color = "bg-yellow-500";

    return { currentProfit, monthlyGoal, progressPercent, remaining, color };
  }, [report, monthlyGoal]);

  const downloadReport = () => {
    const doc = new jsPDF();

    doc.setFontSize(18);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
    doc.text("Relatorio de Performance - Afiliados", 14, 22);

    doc.setFontSize(11);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
    doc.text(`Periodo: ${report.period.label}`, 14, 30);
    doc.text(
      `Gerado em: ${report.generatedAt.toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}`,
      14,
      36
    );

    if (
      filterMarket !== "all" ||
      filterNiche !== "all" ||
      filterPlatform !== "all"
    ) {
      doc.setFontSize(9);
      doc.setTextColor(100);
      const filters = [];
      if (filterMarket !== "all")
        filters.push(
          filterMarket === "nacional" ? "Nacional" : "Internacional"
        );
      if (filterNiche !== "all") filters.push(`Nicho: ${filterNiche}`);
      if (filterPlatform !== "all")
        filters.push(`Plataforma: ${filterPlatform}`);
      doc.text(`Filtros: ${filters.join(" | ")}`, 14, 42);
      doc.setTextColor(0);
    }

    const summaryY =
      filterMarket !== "all" ||
      filterNiche !== "all" ||
      filterPlatform !== "all"
        ? 52
        : 46;
    doc.setFontSize(12);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
    doc.text("Resumo Financeiro (Consolidado em BRL):", 14, summaryY);

    doc.setFontSize(10);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
    doc.text(
      `Receita Total: R$ ${report.summary.totalRevenue.toFixed(2)}`,
      14,
      summaryY + 7
    );
    doc.text(
      `Investimento: R$ ${report.summary.totalSpend.toFixed(2)}`,
      80,
      summaryY + 7
    );
    doc.text(
      `Lucro: R$ ${report.summary.totalProfit.toFixed(2)}`,
      14,
      summaryY + 13
    );
    doc.text(`ROI: ${report.summary.roi.toFixed(1)}%`, 80, summaryY + 13);

    if (goalProgress) {
      doc.setFontSize(10);
      doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
      doc.text(
        `Meta Mensal: R$ ${goalProgress.monthlyGoal.toFixed(
          2
        )} (${goalProgress.progressPercent.toFixed(0)}% atingido)`,
        14,
        summaryY + 20
      );
      doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
      doc.text(
        `Faltam: R$ ${goalProgress.remaining.toFixed(2)}`,
        14,
        summaryY + 26
      );
    }

    const tableData = report.productPerformance.map((p: any) => [
      p.name,
      p.nicho,
      `R$ ${p.profitBRL.toFixed(2)}`,
      `${p.roi.toFixed(1)}%`,
      p.conversions.toString(),
    ]);

    autoTable(doc, {
      startY: goalProgress ? summaryY + 32 : summaryY + 20,
      head: [["Produto", "Nicho", "Lucro (BRL)", "ROI", "Vendas"]],
      body: tableData,
      theme: "grid",
      styles: { fontSize: 8, cellPadding: 3 },
      headStyles: {
        fillColor: [22, 163, 74],
        textColor: 255,
        fontStyle: "bold",
      },
      alternateRowStyles: { fillColor: [245, 247, 250] },
    });

    const finalY = (doc as any).lastAutoTable.finalY || summaryY + 20;
    const currencyY = finalY + 10;
    doc.setFontSize(12);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "bold");
    doc.text("Fechamento de Cambio:", 14, currencyY);

    doc.setFontSize(9);
    doc.setFont("helvetica", "normal");
    let currencyTextY = currencyY + 7;
    Object.entries(baseReport.breakdowns.byCurrency).forEach(
      ([currency, data]: [string, any]) => {
        const rate = rates[currency] || 1;
        const profitBRL = data.profit * rate;
        doc.text(
          `${currency}: Lucro ${getCurrencySymbol(
            currency
          )} ${data.profit.toFixed(2)} (~ R$ ${profitBRL.toFixed(2)})`,
          14,
          currencyTextY
        );
        currencyTextY += 5;
      }
    );

    doc.save(`relatorio-${startDate.toISOString().split("T")[0]}.pdf`);
  };

  const getTrendIcon = (value: number) => {
    if (value > 0) return <TrendingUp className="text-green-600" size={20} />;
    if (value < 0) return <TrendingDown className="text-red-600" size={20} />;
    return <div className="w-5 h-5" />;
  };

  const pieChartData = useMemo(() => {
    if (pieChartMode === "market") {
      return [
        {
          name: "Nacional",
          value: baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.profitBRL,
        },
        {
          name: "Internacional",
          value: baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.profitBRL,
        },
      ].filter((item) => item.value > 0);
    }

    const sorted = [...baseReport.breakdowns.byNiche].sort(
      (a, b) => b.profitBRL - a.profitBRL
    );
    const top4 = sorted.slice(0, 4);
    const others = sorted
      .slice(4)
      .reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.profitBRL, 0);
    const data = top4.map((item) => ({
      name: item.nicho,
      value: item.profitBRL,
    }));
    if (others > 0) data.push({ name: "Outros", value: others });
    return data;
  }, [baseReport, pieChartMode]);

  const COLORS = ["#10b981", "#3b82f6", "#8b5cf6", "#f59e0b", "#6b7280"];

  const barChartData = useMemo(() => {
    if (barChartMode === "roi") {
      return [
        {
          name: "Nacional",
          value: baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.roiAvg,
          fill:
            baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.roiAvg > 50
              ? "#10b981"
              : baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.roiAvg > 0
              ? "#f59e0b"
              : "#ef4444",
        },
        {
          name: "Internacional",
          value: baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.roiAvg,
          fill:
            baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.roiAvg > 50
              ? "#10b981"
              : baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.roiAvg > 0
              ? "#f59e0b"
              : "#ef4444",
        },
      ];
    } else {
      return [
        {
          name: "Nacional",
          value: baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.profitBRL,
          fill:
            baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.profitBRL > 0
              ? "#10b981"
              : "#ef4444",
        },
        {
          name: "Internacional",
          value: baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.profitBRL,
          fill:
            baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.profitBRL > 0
              ? "#10b981"
              : "#ef4444",
        },
      ];
    }
  }, [baseReport, barChartMode]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-green-600 to-emerald-600 text-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
          <FileText size={28} />
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Dashboard BI - Relatórios</h2>
        </div>
        <p className="text-green-100">
          Inteligência de negócios multi-moeda com análise de plataformas e
          metas
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Calendar size={18} className="text-blue-600" />
              Período do Relatório
            </h3>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-3">
              <button
                onClick={() => setPeriodType("week")}
                className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-all ${
                  periodType === "week"
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md"
                    : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200"
                }`}
              >
                Últimos 7 dias
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setPeriodType("month")}
                className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-all ${
                  periodType === "month"
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md"
                    : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200"
                }`}
              >
                Últimos 30 dias
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setPeriodType("custom")}
                className={`px-3 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium transition-all ${
                  periodType === "custom"
                    ? "bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-md"
                    : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-200"
                }`}
              >
                Personalizado
              </button>
            </div>

            {periodType === "custom" && (
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={customStart}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomStart(e.target.value)}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                />
                <input
                  type="date"
                  value={customEnd}
                  onChange={(e) => setCustomEnd(e.target.value)}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none"
                />
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Filter size={18} className="text-purple-600" />
              Filtros de Análise
            </h3>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              <select
                value={filterMarket}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterMarket(e.target.value as any)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none"
              >
                <option value="all">🌍 Todos os Mercados</option>
                <option value="nacional">🇧🇷 Nacional</option>
                <option value="internacional">🌎 Internacional</option>
              </select>

              <select
                value={filterNiche}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterNiche(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none"
              >
                <option value="all">📂 Todos os Nichos</option>
                {availableNiches.map((nicho) => (
                  <option key={nicho} value={nicho}>
                    {nicho}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>

              <select
                value={filterPlatform}
                onChange={(e) => setFilterPlatform(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-purple-500 outline-none"
              >
                <option value="all">🏪 Todas as Plataformas</option>
                {availablePlatforms.map((platform) => (
                  <option key={platform} value={platform}>
                    {platform}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-4 p-3 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between">
          <p className="text-sm text-blue-800">
            <Clock size={14} className="inline mr-1" />
            <strong>{report.period.label}</strong>
            {(filterMarket !== "all" ||
              filterNiche !== "all" ||
              filterPlatform !== "all") && (
              <span className="ml-2 text-xs">• Filtros ativos</span>
            )}
          </p>
          <button
            onClick={downloadReport}
            className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 bg-green-600 text-white rounded-lg text-sm font-bold shadow hover:bg-green-700 transition-all"
          >
            <Download size={16} />
            Gerar PDF
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {goalProgress && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Target size={20} className="text-indigo-600" />
            Progresso da Meta Mensal
          </h3>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
              <span className="font-medium text-gray-700">
                Meta: R$ {goalProgress.monthlyGoal.toFixed(2)}
              </span>
              <span className="font-bold text-gray-900">
                {goalProgress.progressPercent.toFixed(1)}% atingido
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-6 overflow-hidden">
              <div
                className={`h-full ${goalProgress.color} transition-all duration-500 flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-bold`}
                style={{ width: `${goalProgress.progressPercent}%` }}
              >
                {goalProgress.progressPercent >= 10 &&
                  `${goalProgress.progressPercent.toFixed(0)}%`}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-sm">
              <span className="text-gray-600">
                Lucro Atual:{" "}
                <strong className="text-green-700">
                  R$ {goalProgress.currentProfit.toFixed(2)}
                </strong>
              </span>
              <span className="text-gray-600">
                Faltam:{" "}
                <strong className="text-red-700">
                  R$ {goalProgress.remaining.toFixed(2)}
                </strong>
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <DollarSign size={20} className="text-green-600" />
          Resumo Executivo (Consolidado em BRL)
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          <div className="p-4 bg-green-50 rounded-lg border border-green-200">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <div className="text-xs text-green-700 font-bold">Receita</div>
              {getTrendIcon(report.trends.revenueChange)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-900">
              R$ {report.summary.totalRevenue.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            {report.trends.revenueChange !== 0 && (
              <div
                className={`text-xs mt-1 ${
                  report.trends.revenueChange > 0
                    ? "text-green-700"
                    : "text-red-700"
                }`}
              >
                {report.trends.revenueChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                {report.trends.revenueChange.toFixed(1)}% vs anterior
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 rounded-lg border border-red-200">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <div className="text-xs text-red-700 font-bold">Investimento</div>
