              {getTrendIcon(report.trends.spendChange)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-red-900">
              R$ {report.summary.totalSpend.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            {report.trends.spendChange !== 0 && (
              <div
                className={`text-xs mt-1 ${
                  report.trends.spendChange > 0
                    ? "text-red-700"
                    : "text-green-700"
                }`}
              >
                {report.trends.spendChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                {report.trends.spendChange.toFixed(1)}% vs anterior
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div
            className={`p-4 rounded-lg border ${
              report.summary.totalProfit >= 0
                ? "bg-blue-50 border-blue-200"
                : "bg-red-50 border-red-200"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <div
                className={`text-xs font-bold ${
                  report.summary.totalProfit >= 0
                    ? "text-blue-700"
                    : "text-red-700"
                }`}
              >
                Lucro
              </div>
              {getTrendIcon(report.trends.profitChange)}
            </div>
            <div
              className={`text-2xl font-black ${
                report.summary.totalProfit >= 0
                  ? "text-blue-900"
                  : "text-red-900"
              }`}
            >
              R$ {report.summary.totalProfit.toFixed(2)}
            </div>
            {report.trends.profitChange !== 0 && (
              <div
                className={`text-xs mt-1 ${
                  report.trends.profitChange > 0
                    ? "text-green-700"
                    : "text-red-700"
                }`}
              >
                {report.trends.profitChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                {report.trends.profitChange.toFixed(1)}% vs anterior
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="p-4 bg-purple-50 rounded-lg border border-purple-200">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <div className="text-xs text-purple-700 font-bold">ROI</div>
              {getTrendIcon(report.trends.roiChange)}
            </div>
            <div className="text-2xl font-black text-purple-900">
              {report.summary.roi.toFixed(1)}%
            </div>
            {report.trends.roiChange !== 0 && (
              <div
                className={`text-xs mt-1 ${
                  report.trends.roiChange > 0
                    ? "text-green-700"
                    : "text-red-700"
                }`}
              >
                {report.trends.roiChange > 0 ? "+" : ""}
                {report.trends.roiChange.toFixed(1)}pp vs anterior
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {topPerformers.bestProfit && (
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Award size={20} className="text-yellow-600" />
            Destaques do Período
          </h3>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
            <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-yellow-50 to-amber-50 rounded-lg border-2 border-yellow-300">
              <div className="text-3xl mb-2">🥇</div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                Maior Lucro Absoluto
              </div>
              <div className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900">
                {topPerformers.bestProfit.name}
              </div>
              <div className="text-2xl font-black text-yellow-700 mt-2">
                R$ {topPerformers.bestProfit.profitBRL.toFixed(2)}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                {topPerformers.bestProfit.conversions} vendas
              </div>
            </div>

            {topPerformers.bestROI && (
              <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-green-50 to-emerald-50 rounded-lg border-2 border-green-300">
                <div className="text-3xl mb-2">🚀</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                  Maior ROI (mín. 3 vendas)
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900">
                  {topPerformers.bestROI.name}
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-700 mt-2">
                  {topPerformers.bestROI.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                  Lucro: R$ {topPerformers.bestROI.profitBRL.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {topPerformers.worstLoss && (
              <div className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-red-50 to-rose-50 rounded-lg border-2 border-red-300">
                <div className="text-3xl mb-2">📉</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                  Maior Prejuízo
                </div>
                <div className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900">
                  {topPerformers.worstLoss.name}
                </div>
                <div className="text-2xl font-black text-red-700 mt-2">
                  R$ {topPerformers.worstLoss.profitBRL.toFixed(2)}
                </div>
                <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-1">
                  {topPerformers.worstLoss.conversions} vendas
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Globe size={20} className="text-indigo-600" />
          Fechamento de Câmbio
        </h3>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
          {Object.entries(baseReport.breakdowns.byCurrency).map(
            ([currency, data]: [string, any]) => {
              const rate = rates[currency] || 1;
              const profitBRL = data.profit * rate;
              return (
                <div
                  key={currency}
                  className="p-4 bg-gradient-to-br from-indigo-50 to-purple-50 rounded-lg border border-indigo-200"
                >
                  <div className="text-2xl mb-2">
                    {getCurrencyFlag(currency)}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                    {currency}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xl font-black text-indigo-900 mb-1">
                    {getCurrencySymbol(currency)} {data.profit.toFixed(2)}
                  </div>
                  {currency !== "BRL" && (
                    <div className="text-sm text-gray-600 mb-2">
                      ≈ R$ {profitBRL.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 space-y-0.5 pt-2 border-t border-indigo-200">
                    <div>
                      Receita: {getCurrencySymbol(currency)}{" "}
                      {data.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      Gasto: {getCurrencySymbol(currency)}{" "}
                      {data.spend.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            }
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {baseReport.breakdowns.byPlatform &&
        baseReport.breakdowns.byPlatform.length > 0 && (
          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
              <BarChart3 size={20} className="text-blue-600" />
              Performance por Plataforma
            </h3>
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b-2 border-gray-200">
                    <th className="text-left py-3 px-4 font-bold text-gray-700">
                      Plataforma
                    </th>
                    <th className="text-right py-3 px-4 font-bold text-gray-700">
                      Receita (BRL)
                    </th>
                    <th className="text-right py-3 px-4 font-bold text-gray-700">
                      Gasto (BRL)
                    </th>
                    <th className="text-right py-3 px-4 font-bold text-gray-700">
                      Lucro (BRL)
                    </th>
                    <th className="text-right py-3 px-4 font-bold text-gray-700">
                      ROI
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {baseReport.breakdowns.byPlatform.map(
                    (platform: any, index: number) => (
                      <tr
                        key={index}
                        className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50"
                      >
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">
                          {platform.platform}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right text-green-700 font-bold">
                          R$ {platform.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right text-red-700 font-bold">
                          R$ {platform.spend.toFixed(2)}
                        </td>
                        <td
                          className={`py-3 px-4 text-right font-bold ${
                            platform.profitBRL >= 0
                              ? "text-blue-700"
                              : "text-red-700"
                          }`}
                        >
                          R$ {platform.profitBRL.toFixed(2)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                          <span
                            className={`font-bold ${
                              platform.roi > 100
                                ? "text-green-700"
                                : platform.roi > 50
                                ? "text-blue-700"
                                : platform.roi > 0
                                ? "text-yellow-700"
                                : "text-red-700"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {platform.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    )
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <PieChartIcon size={20} className="text-green-600" />
              Distribuição de Lucro
            </h3>
            <div className="flex bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
              <button
                onClick={() => setPieChartMode("niche")}
                className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-md transition-all ${
                  pieChartMode === "niche"
                    ? "bg-white shadow text-green-700 font-bold"
                    : "text-gray-500"
                }`}
              >
                Por Nicho
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setPieChartMode("market")}
                className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-md transition-all ${
                  pieChartMode === "market"
                    ? "bg-white shadow text-green-700 font-bold"
                    : "text-gray-500"
                }`}
              >
                Por Mercado
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
            <PieChart>
              <Pie
                data={pieChartData}
                cx="50%"
                cy="50%"
                labelLine={false}
                label={({ name, percent }) =>
                  `${name}: ${(percent * 100).toFixed(0)}%`
                }
                outerRadius={80}
                fill="#8884d8"
                dataKey="value"
              >
                {pieChartData.map((entry, index) => (
                  <Cell
                    key={`cell-${index}`}
                    fill={COLORS[index % COLORS.length]}
                  />
                ))}
              </Pie>
              <Tooltip formatter={(value: any) => `R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`} />
            </PieChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
            <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 flex items-center gap-2">
              <BarChart3 size={20} className="text-blue-600" />
              Comparativo por Mercado
            </h3>
            <div className="flex bg-gray-100 p-1 rounded-lg">
              <button
                onClick={() => setBarChartMode("roi")}
                className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-md transition-all ${
                  barChartMode === "roi"
                    ? "bg-white shadow text-blue-700 font-bold"
                    : "text-gray-500"
                }`}
              >
                ROI (%)
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={() => setBarChartMode("profit")}
                className={`px-3 py-1 text-xs rounded-md transition-all ${
                  barChartMode === "profit"
                    ? "bg-white shadow text-blue-700 font-bold"
                    : "text-gray-500"
                }`}
              >
                Lucro (R$)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={300}>
            <BarChart data={barChartData}>
              <XAxis dataKey="name" />
              <YAxis
                label={{
                  value: barChartMode === "roi" ? "ROI (%)" : "Lucro (R$)",
                  angle: -90,
                  position: "insideLeft",
                }}
              />
              <Tooltip
                formatter={(value: any) =>
                  barChartMode === "roi"
                    ? `${value.toFixed(1)}%`
                    : `R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`
                }
              />
              <Bar dataKey="value" fill="#8884d8">
                {barChartData.map((entry, index) => (
                  <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={entry.fill} />
                ))}
              </Bar>
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
          <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
            <div className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mb-1">Nacional</div>
              <div className="font-bold">
                {barChartMode === "roi"
                  ? `${baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.roiAvg.toFixed(
                      1
                    )}%`
                  : `R$ ${baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.profitBRL.toFixed(
                      2
                    )}`}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                {baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.nacional.productsCount}{" "}
                produto(s)
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded">
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 mb-1">Internacional</div>
              <div className="font-bold">
                {barChartMode === "roi"
                  ? `${baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.roiAvg.toFixed(
                      1
                    )}%`
                  : `R$ ${baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.profitBRL.toFixed(
                      2
                    )}`}
              </div>
              <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                {baseReport.breakdowns.byMarket.internacional.productsCount}{" "}
                produto(s)
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm overflow-x-auto">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-900 mb-4">
          📊 Performance Detalhada por Produto
        </h3>
        <table className="w-full text-sm">
          <thead>
            <tr className="border-b-2 border-gray-200">
              <th className="text-left py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                Produto
              </th>
              <th className="text-left py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                Nicho
              </th>
              <th className="text-right py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                Receita
              </th>
              <th className="text-right py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                Custo
              </th>
              <th className="text-right py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                Lucro
              </th>
              <th className="text-right py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                ROI
              </th>
              <th className="text-center py-3 px-2 font-bold text-gray-700">
                Vendas
              </th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {report.productPerformance.map((p: any, index: number) => {
              const currency =
                p.market === "US" || p.market === "OTHER"
                  ? "USD"
                  : p.market === "EU"
                  ? "EUR"
                  : p.market === "UK"
                  ? "GBP"
                  : "BRL";
              const symbol = getCurrencySymbol(currency);
              const showConversion = currency !== "BRL";
              const rate = rates[currency] || 1;

              return (
                <tr
                  key={index}
                  className="border-b border-gray-100 hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  <td className="py-3 px-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-xl">
                        {p.status === "excellent"
                          ? "🟢"
                          : p.status === "good"
                          ? "🟡"
                          : p.status === "warning"
                          ? "🟠"
                          : "🔴"}
                      </span>
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-medium">{p.name}</div>
                        {showConversion && (
                          <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                            {getCurrencyFlag(currency)} {currency}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-2 text-gray-700">{p.nicho}</td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right">
                    {showConversion && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                        {symbol} {p.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div className="font-bold text-green-700">
                      R$ {(p.revenue * rate).toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right">
                    {showConversion && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                        {symbol} {p.spend.toFixed(2)}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div className="font-bold text-red-700">
                      R$ {(p.spend * rate).toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right">
                    {showConversion && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                        {symbol} {p.profit.toFixed(2)}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    <div
                      className={`font-bold ${
                        p.profitBRL >= 0 ? "text-blue-700" : "text-red-700"
                      }`}
                    >
                      R$ {p.profitBRL.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-2 text-right">
                    <span
                      className={`font-bold ${
                        p.roi > 100
                          ? "text-green-700"
                          : p.roi > 50
                          ? "text-blue-700"
                          : p.roi > 0
                          ? "text-yellow-700"
                          : "text-red-700"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {p.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-3 px-2 text-center font-medium">
                    {p.conversions}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>

        {report.productPerformance.length === 0 && (
          <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-500">
            Nenhum produto encontrado com os filtros selecionados
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: ScaleSimulator.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\components\ScaleSimulator.tsx
==========================================

import React, { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useCurrency } from "../contexts/CurrencyContext";
import { simulateScale } from "../services/logic";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle,
  ArrowRight,
  Info,
  Calendar,
  Zap,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  Legend,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Line,
  ComposedChart,
} from "recharts";
import type { Product } from "../types";

// ============================================================================
// TIPOS LOCAIS
// ============================================================================

interface ScaleSimulatorProps {
  products: Product[];
  fixedCostsTotal?: number;
  activeProductsCount?: number;
}

// ============================================================================
// COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
// ============================================================================

export const ScaleSimulator: React.FC<ScaleSimulatorProps> = ({
  products,
  fixedCostsTotal = 0,
  activeProductsCount = 1,
}) => {
  // Estado
  const [selectedProductId, setSelectedProductId] = useState<string>("");
  const [currentBudgetBRL, setCurrentBudgetBRL] = useState(1000);
  const [currentCPCBRL, setCurrentCPCBRL] = useState(2.5);
  const [currentCR, setCurrentCR] = useState(2.0);
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<"daily" | "monthly">("daily");

  // Puxa as taxas reais da API (atualizadas a cada 5 min)
  const { rates } = useCurrency();

  const selectedProduct = products.find((p) => p.id === selectedProductId);

  // Dias do mês (automático)
  const now = new Date();
  const daysInMonth = new Date(
    now.getFullYear(),
    now.getMonth() + 1,
    0
  ).getDate();

  // ============================================================================
  // UTILITÁRIOS DE MOEDA
  // ============================================================================

  const getRate = (market: string): number => {
    if (market === "BR") return 1;
    if (market === "US" || market === "OTHER") return rates.USD || 6.0;
    if (market === "EU") return rates.EUR || 6.5;
    if (market === "UK") return rates.GBP || 7.5;
    return 1;
  };

  const getCurrencySymbol = (market: string): string => {
    if (market === "BR") return "R$";
    if (market === "US" || market === "OTHER") return "$";
    if (market === "EU") return "€";
    if (market === "UK") return "£";
    return "R$";
  };

  const rate = selectedProduct ? getRate(selectedProduct.market) : 1;
  const currencySymbol = selectedProduct
    ? getCurrencySymbol(selectedProduct.market)
    : "R$";
  const isInternational = selectedProduct?.market !== "BR";

  // ============================================================================
  // SMART DEFAULTS (Engenharia Reversa)
  // ============================================================================

  const applySmartDefaults = (product: Product) => {
    // 1. Pega a cotação correta
    const currency =
      product.market === "US" || product.market === "OTHER"
        ? "USD"
        : product.market === "EU"
        ? "EUR"
        : product.market === "UK"
        ? "GBP"
        : "BRL";
    const conversionRate = currency === "BRL" ? 1 : rates[currency] || 1;

    // 2. Converte a comissão para BRL
    const commissionBRL = (product.commissionLiquid || 0) * conversionRate;

    // 3. Define CPC (Prioridade: Benchmark manual, senão padrão)
    const benchmarkCPC = product.manualBenchmarkCPC || 2.5;
    setCurrentCPCBRL(benchmarkCPC);

    // 4. Calcula CR para ROI 30% (Engenharia Reversa)
    const targetCPA = commissionBRL / 1.3;
    const calculatedCR = targetCPA > 0 ? (benchmarkCPC / targetCPA) * 100 : 2.0;

    // Trava entre 0.5% e 15%
    const safeCR = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(15, calculatedCR));
    setCurrentCR(Number(safeCR.toFixed(2)));

    // 5. Budget Mínimo para 1 venda
    const clicksNeeded = 100 / safeCR;
    const minBudget = clicksNeeded * benchmarkCPC;
    setCurrentBudgetBRL(Math.ceil(minBudget / 100) * 100);
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // SIMULAÇÃO
  // ============================================================================

  const simulation = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selectedProduct) return null;

    return simulateScale(
      selectedProduct,
      currentBudgetBRL,
      currentCPCBRL,
      currentCR,
      fixedCostsTotal,
      activeProductsCount,
      rates,
      daysInMonth,
      viewMode
    );
  }, [
    selectedProduct,
    currentBudgetBRL,
    currentCPCBRL,
    currentCR,
    fixedCostsTotal,
    activeProductsCount,
    rates,
    daysInMonth,
    viewMode,
  ]);

  // ============================================================================
  // HELPER: MÉTRICAS COM VENDAS INTEIRAS (VIEW MODEL)
  // ============================================================================

  const getIntegerMetrics = (
    conversionsDecimal: number,
    budgetValue: number
  ) => {
    const unitCommissionBRL = selectedProduct
      ? (selectedProduct.commissionLiquid || 0) *
        getRate(selectedProduct.market)
      : 0;

    const sales = Math.floor(conversionsDecimal);
    const revenue = sales * unitCommissionBRL;

    const productSpecificCost = selectedProduct?.specificCosts?.total || 0;
    const globalShare =
      activeProductsCount > 0 ? fixedCostsTotal / activeProductsCount : 0;
    const totalProductMonthlyCost = globalShare + productSpecificCost;

    const fixedCost =
      viewMode === "daily"
        ? totalProductMonthlyCost / daysInMonth
        : totalProductMonthlyCost;

    // ✅ ROI CORRIGIDO: Considera investimento total (Ads + Custos Fixos)
    const profit = revenue - budgetValue - fixedCost;
    const totalInvested = budgetValue + fixedCost;
    const roi = totalInvested > 0 ? (profit / totalInvested) * 100 : 0;

    return { sales, revenue, profit, roi, salesDecimal: conversionsDecimal };
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // DADOS PARA GRÁFICO
  // ============================================================================

  const chartData = simulation
    ? [
        {
          name: "Atual",
          Lucro: getIntegerMetrics(
            simulation.current.conversions,
            simulation.current.budget
          ).profit,
          ROI: getIntegerMetrics(
            simulation.current.conversions,
            simulation.current.budget
          ).roi,
        },
        ...simulation.scenarios.map((s) => {
          const metrics = getIntegerMetrics(
            s.projectedConversions,
            s.newBudget
          );
          return {
            name: s.label,
            Lucro: metrics.profit,
            ROI: metrics.roi,
          };
        }),
      ]
    : [];

  // ============================================================================
  // HELPERS DE UI
  // ============================================================================

  const getRiskColor = (risk: string) => {
    switch (risk) {
      case "low":
        return "bg-green-50 border-green-300 text-green-800";
      case "medium":
        return "bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-300 text-yellow-800";
      case "high":
        return "bg-red-50 border-red-300 text-red-800";
      default:
        return "bg-gray-50 border-gray-300 text-gray-800";
    }
  };

  const getRiskIcon = (risk: string) => {
    switch (risk) {
      case "low":
        return <CheckCircle className="text-green-600" size={20} />;
      case "medium":
        return <AlertTriangle className="text-yellow-600" size={20} />;
      case "high":
        return <AlertTriangle className="text-red-600" size={20} />;
      default:
        return null;
    }
  };

  const getRiskLabel = (risk: string) => {
    switch (risk) {
      case "low":
        return "Baixo";
      case "medium":
        return "Médio";
      case "high":
        return "Alto";
      default:
        return "Desconhecido";
    }
  };

  // ============================================================================
  // RENDER
  // ============================================================================

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 max-w-7xl mx-auto p-4">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-orange-600 to-red-600 text-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow-lg">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-2">
              <span className="text-3xl">📈</span>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-black">Simulador de Escala</h2>
            </div>
            <p className="text-orange-100">
              Simule diferentes cenários de aumento de budget e veja o impacto
              no lucro
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Toggle Daily/Monthly */}
          <div className="bg-white bg-opacity-20 rounded-lg p-1 flex gap-1">
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode("daily")}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md font-bold text-sm transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${
                viewMode === "daily"
                  ? "bg-white text-orange-600 shadow-lg"
                  : "text-white hover:bg-white hover:bg-opacity-10"
              }`}
            >
              <Zap size={16} />
              Diário
            </button>
            <button
              onClick={() => setViewMode("monthly")}
              className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-md font-bold text-sm transition-all flex items-center gap-2 ${
                viewMode === "monthly"
                  ? "bg-white text-orange-600 shadow-lg"
                  : "text-white hover:bg-white hover:bg-opacity-10"
              }`}
            >
              <Calendar size={16} />
              Mensal
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Alerta de Modo */}
      <div
        className={`${
          viewMode === "daily"
            ? "bg-blue-50 border-blue-500"
            : "bg-purple-50 border-purple-500"
        } border-l-4 p-4 rounded-lg`}
      >
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          {viewMode === "daily" ? (
            <Zap className="text-blue-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={20} />
          ) : (
            <Calendar
              className="text-purple-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5"
              size={20}
            />
          )}
          <div className="text-sm">
            <p className="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-1">
              {viewMode === "daily"
                ? "Visão Diária Ativa"
                : "Visão Mensal Ativa"}
            </p>
            <p className="text-gray-700">
              {viewMode === "daily"
                ? `Analisando métricas por dia. Custos fixos rateados em ${daysInMonth} dias.`
                : `Projeção para ${daysInMonth} dias. Vendas acumuladas e custos fixos aplicados uma única vez.`}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Alerta Multi-Moeda */}
      {selectedProduct && isInternational && (
        <div className="bg-orange-50 border-l-4 border-orange-500 p-4 rounded-lg">
          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <Info className="text-orange-600 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={20} />
            <div className="text-sm">
              <p className="font-bold text-orange-900 mb-1">
                Modo Multi-Moeda Ativo
              </p>
              <p className="text-orange-800">
                Comissão em <strong>{currencySymbol}</strong> convertida para
                BRL (taxa: 1 {currencySymbol} = R$ {rate.toFixed(2)}). Custos de
                tráfego permanecem em Reais.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Configuração */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
          <span className="text-xl">🎯</span>
          Configuração da Simulação
        </h3>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Seleção de Produto */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">
              Produto
            </label>
            <select
              value={selectedProductId}
              onChange={(e) => {
                setSelectedProductId(e.target.value);
                const product = products.find((p) => p.id === e.target.value);
                if (product) applySmartDefaults(product);
              }}
              className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-lg font-medium"
            >
              <option value="">Selecione um produto...</option>
              {products.map((p) => {
                const prodRate = getRate(p.market);
                const symbol = getCurrencySymbol(p.market);
                const commBRL = (p.commissionLiquid || 0) * prodRate;

                return (
                  <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>
                    {p.name} - {symbol} {p.commissionLiquid?.toFixed(2)}
                    {p.market !== "BR" && ` (≈ R$ ${commBRL.toFixed(2)})`}
                  </option>
                );
              })}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Inputs de métricas atuais */}
          {selectedProduct && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                  {viewMode === "daily"
                    ? "Budget Diário (R$)"
                    : "Budget Diário Base (R$)"}
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={currentBudgetBRL}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrentBudgetBRL(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                  min="0"
                  step="100"
                />
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">
                  {viewMode === "daily"
                    ? "Gasto diário em tráfego (Google/Facebook Ads)"
                    : `Será multiplicado por ${daysInMonth} dias para projeção mensal`}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                  CPC Atual (R$)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={currentCPCBRL}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrentCPCBRL(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                  min="0"
                  step="0.1"
                />
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">
                  Custo médio por clique na campanha atual
                </p>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">
                  CR Atual (%)
                </label>
                <input
                  type="number"
                  value={currentCR}
                  onChange={(e) => setCurrentCR(Number(e.target.value))}
                  className="w-full px-4 py-3 border-2 border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none text-lg font-bold"
                  min="0"
                  max="100"
                  step="0.1"
                />
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2">
                  Taxa de conversão (vendas ÷ cliques × 100)
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Resultados */}
      {simulation &&
        selectedProduct &&
        (() => {
          const currentMetrics = getIntegerMetrics(
            simulation.current.conversions,
            simulation.current.budget
          );

          return (
            <>
              {/* Cenário Atual */}
              <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-50 to-indigo-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-blue-200 shadow-sm">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-blue-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xl">📊</span>
                  Cenário Atual{" "}
                  {viewMode === "monthly" && `(${daysInMonth} dias)`}
                </h3>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5 gap-4">
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-200">
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                      Budget {viewMode === "monthly" ? "Total" : "Diário"}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-blue-900">
                      R$ {simulation.current.budget.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-200">
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                      Vendas
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-blue-900">
                      {currentMetrics.sales}
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">
                      {simulation.current.conversions.toFixed(2)} estimadas
                    </div>
                    {/* Badge "Venda em Formação" - Ajustado para ≥10% */}
                    {(() => {
                      const fraction = currentMetrics.salesDecimal % 1;
                      if (fraction >= 0.1) {
                        const percentage = Math.round(fraction * 100);
                        return (
                          <div
                            className="mt-1 text-[9px] text-blue-600 font-bold bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 rounded inline-block"
                            title={`O investimento já cobriu ${percentage}% do custo da próxima venda. Em um cenário de vários dias, isso se converterá em lucro.`}
                          >
                            🔍 +{percentage}% da próxima venda
                          </div>
                        );
                      }
                      return null;
                    })()}
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-200">
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                      Receita
                    </div>
                    <div className="text-2xl font-black text-green-600">
                      R$ {currentMetrics.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-200">
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                      Lucro Líquido
                    </div>
                    <div
                      className={`text-2xl font-black ${
                        currentMetrics.profit >= 0
                          ? "text-green-600"
                          : "text-red-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      R$ {currentMetrics.profit.toFixed(2)}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-lg border border-blue-200">
                    <div className="text-xs text-gray-600 font-bold mb-1">
                      ROI
                    </div>
                    <div
                      className={`text-2xl font-black ${
                        currentMetrics.roi >= 0
                          ? "text-green-600"
                          : "text-red-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {currentMetrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Recomendação */}
              <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-50 to-pink-50 p-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-purple-200 shadow-sm">
                <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                  <span className="text-2xl flex-shrink-0">⚡</span>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-purple-900 text-lg mb-2">
                      Recomendação Estratégica
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-purple-800">
                      {simulation.recommendation}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Cenários de Escala */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xl">🚀</span>
                  Cenários de Escala
                </h3>

                <div className="space-y-4">
                  {simulation.scenarios.map((scenario, idx) => {
                    const scenarioMetrics = getIntegerMetrics(
                      scenario.projectedConversions,
                      scenario.newBudget
                    );

                    const isRecommended =
                      simulation.bestScenario?.budgetIncrease ===
                      scenario.budgetIncrease;

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={idx}
                        className={`p-5 rounded-xl border-2 ${getRiskColor(
                          scenario.riskLevel
                        )} transition-all hover:shadow-lg ${
                          isRecommended ? "ring-4 ring-purple-300" : ""
                        }`}
                      >
                        {/* Header */}
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                            {getRiskIcon(scenario.riskLevel)}
                            <div>
                              <h4 className="font-black text-lg">
                                {scenario.label}
                                {isRecommended && (
                                  <span className="ml-2 text-sm bg-purple-600 text-white px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
                                    ⭐ Recomendado
                                  </span>
                                )}
                              </h4>
                              <p className="text-xs font-bold opacity-75">
                                Risco: {getRiskLabel(scenario.riskLevel)}
                              </p>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <ArrowRight size={24} className="opacity-50" />
                        </div>

                        {/* Métricas */}
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-5 gap-3 mb-4">
                          <div className="bg-white bg-opacity-70 p-3 rounded-lg">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold opacity-70 mb-1">
                              Novo Budget
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-lg font-black">
                              R$ {scenario.newBudget.toFixed(2)}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="bg-white bg-opacity-70 p-3 rounded-lg">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold opacity-70 mb-1">
                              Vendas
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-lg font-black">
                              {scenarioMetrics.sales}
                            </div>
                            {/* ✅ NOVO: Mostra decimais estimadas */}
                            <div className="text-[10px] text-gray-500">
                              {scenario.projectedConversions.toFixed(2)}{" "}
                              estimadas
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="bg-white bg-opacity-70 p-3 rounded-lg">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold opacity-70 mb-1">
                              Receita
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-lg font-black text-green-600">
                              R$ {scenarioMetrics.revenue.toFixed(2)}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="bg-white bg-opacity-70 p-3 rounded-lg">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold opacity-70 mb-1">
                              Lucro
                            </div>
                            <div
                              className={`text-lg font-black ${
                                scenarioMetrics.profit >= 0
                                  ? "text-green-600"
                                  : "text-red-600"
                              }`}
                            >
                              R$ {scenarioMetrics.profit.toFixed(2)}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-[9px] opacity-60 mt-1">
                              {scenarioMetrics.profit - currentMetrics.profit >=
                              0
                                ? "+"
                                : ""}
                              R${" "}
                              {(
                                scenarioMetrics.profit - currentMetrics.profit
                              ).toFixed(0)}
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="bg-white bg-opacity-70 p-3 rounded-lg">
                            <div className="text-[10px] font-bold opacity-70 mb-1">
                              ROI
                            </div>
                            <div
                              className={`text-lg font-black ${
                                scenarioMetrics.roi >= 0
                                  ? "text-green-600"
                                  : "text-red-600"
                              }`}
                            >
                              {scenarioMetrics.roi.toFixed(1)}%
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Ajustes de Mercado */}
                        <div className="flex gap-4 mb-3 text-xs">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <span className="font-bold">CPC Estimado:</span>
                            <span className="text-red-600 font-bold">
                              +{scenario.estimatedCPCIncrease}%
                            </span>
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <span className="font-bold">CR Estimado:</span>
                            <span className="text-red-600 font-bold">
                              -{scenario.estimatedCRDecrease}%
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Warnings */}
                        {scenario.warnings.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="mb-3 space-y-1">
                            {scenario.warnings.map((warning, i) => (
                              <div
                                key={i}
                                className="text-xs font-medium flex items-start gap-1"
                              >
                                <AlertTriangle
                                  size={12}
                                  className="mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0"
                                />
                                <span>{warning}</span>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}

                        {/* Recomendações */}
                        {scenario.recommendations.length > 0 && (
                          <div className="space-y-1 pt-3 border-t border-current border-opacity-20">
                            {scenario.recommendations.map((rec, i) => (
                              <div
                                key={i}
                                className="text-xs font-medium flex items-start gap-1"
                              >
                                <CheckCircle
                                  size={12}
                                  className="mt-0.5 flex-shrink-0"
                                />
                                <span>{rec}</span>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Gráfico */}
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-sm">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xl">📊</span>
                  Comparação Visual: Lucro vs ROI
                </h3>
                <div className="h-[400px]">
                  <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    <ComposedChart data={chartData}>
                      <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#f0f0f0" />
                      <XAxis
                        dataKey="name"
                        stroke="#6b7280"
                        fontSize={12}
                        fontWeight="bold"
                      />
                      <YAxis
                        yAxisId="left"
                        stroke="#10b981"
                        fontSize={12}
                        label={{
                          value: "Lucro (R$)",
                          angle: -90,
                          position: "insideLeft",
                        }}
                      />
                      <YAxis
                        yAxisId="right"
                        orientation="right"
                        stroke="#8b5cf6"
                        fontSize={12}
                        label={{
                          value: "ROI (%)",
                          angle: 90,
                          position: "insideRight",
                        }}
                      />
                      <Tooltip
                        formatter={(value: number, name: string) => [
                          name === "Lucro"
                            ? `R$ ${value.toFixed(2)}`
                            : `${value.toFixed(1)}%`,
                          name,
                        ]}
                        contentStyle={{
                          borderRadius: "8px",
                          border: "none",
                          boxShadow: "0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.1)",
                        }}
                      />
                      <Legend />
                      <Bar
                        yAxisId="left"
                        dataKey="Lucro"
                        fill="#10b981"
                        radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]}
                      />
                      <Line
                        yAxisId="right"
                        type="monotone"
                        dataKey="ROI"
                        stroke="#8b5cf6"
                        strokeWidth={3}
                        dot={{ r: 5 }}
                      />
                    </ComposedChart>
                  </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
              </div>
            </>
          );
        })()}

      {/* Estado vazio */}
      {!selectedProduct && (
        <div className="bg-blue-50 p-8 rounded-xl border-2 border-blue-200 text-center">
          <span className="text-6xl block mb-4">🎯</span>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-blue-900 mb-2">
            Selecione um produto para começar
          </h3>
          <p className="text-blue-700">
            Escolha um produto acima e as métricas serão preenchidas
            automaticamente
          </p>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

import React from 'react';
import { Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-react';
import { useTheme } from '../hooks/ThemeContext';

export const ThemeToggle: React.FC = () => {
  const { isDark, toggleTheme } = useTheme();

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      // ✅ Alterei para p-2.5 para ficar quadrado e removi o gap
      className="relative flex items-center justify-center p-2.5 rounded-lg transition-all duration-300 hover:scale-105 bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-purple-600 text-white shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl"
      title={isDark ? 'Ativar modo claro' : 'Ativar modo escuro'}
    >
      {/* Ícone animado */}
      <div className="relative w-5 h-5">
        <Sun 
          size={20} 
          className={`absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ${
            isDark ? 'opacity-0 rotate-90 scale-0' : 'opacity-100 rotate-0 scale-100'
          }`}
        />
        <Moon 
          size={20} 
          className={`absolute inset-0 transition-all duration-300 ${
            isDark ? 'opacity-100 rotate-0 scale-100' : 'opacity-0 -rotate-90 scale-0'
          }`}
        />
      </div>
    </button>
  );
};

ARQUIVO: CurrencyContext.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\contexts\CurrencyContext.tsx
==========================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react';

// ========================================
// TIPOS E INTERFACES
// ========================================

export interface CurrencyRates {
  USD: number; // Dólar → Real (USDBRL)
  EUR: number; // Euro → Real (EURBRL)
  GBP: number; // Libra → Real (GBPBRL)
}

interface CurrencyContextData {
  rates: CurrencyRates;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  lastUpdate: Date | null;
  refetch: () => Promise<void>;
}

// ========================================
// CONTEXTO
// ========================================

const CurrencyContext = createContext<CurrencyContextData | undefined>(undefined);

// ========================================
// PROVIDER
// ========================================

interface CurrencyProviderProps {
  children: ReactNode;
}

const CACHE_KEY = 'currency_rates_cache';
const CACHE_DURATION = 5 * 60 * 1000; // 5 minutos

export const CurrencyProvider: React.FC<CurrencyProviderProps> = ({ children }) => {
  const [rates, setRates] = useState<CurrencyRates>({ USD: 5.0, EUR: 5.5, GBP: 6.5 }); // Fallback inicial
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [lastUpdate, setLastUpdate] = useState<Date | null>(null);

  // ===== FUNÇÃO: Carregar do Cache =====
  const loadFromCache = (): { rates: CurrencyRates; timestamp: number } | null => {
    try {
      const cached = localStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
      if (!cached) return null;

      const data = JSON.parse(cached);
      const now = Date.now();
      
      // Verificar se o cache ainda é válido
      if (now - data.timestamp < CACHE_DURATION) {
        return data;
      }
      
      return null;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('❌ Erro ao ler cache de moedas:', err);
      return null;
    }
  };

  // ===== FUNÇÃO: Salvar no Cache =====
  const saveToCache = (rates: CurrencyRates) => {
    try {
      const data = {
        rates,
        timestamp: Date.now()
      };
      localStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('❌ Erro ao salvar cache de moedas:', err);
    }
  };

  // ===== FUNÇÃO: Buscar Cotações da API =====
  const fetchRates = async () => {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      const response = await fetch(
        'https://economia.awesomeapi.com.br/last/USD-BRL,EUR-BRL,GBP-BRL'
      );

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error('Falha ao buscar cotações');
      }

      const data = await response.json();

      // Extrair valores
      const newRates: CurrencyRates = {
        USD: parseFloat(data?.USDBRL?.bid ?? '5.0'),
        EUR: parseFloat(data?.EURBRL?.bid ?? '5.5'),
        GBP: parseFloat(data?.GBPBRL?.bid ?? '6.5'),
      };

      // Validar valores (não aceitar zeros ou valores absurdos)
      if (newRates.USD > 0 && newRates.USD < 20 && 
          newRates.EUR > 0 && newRates.EUR < 20 &&
          newRates.GBP > 0 && newRates.GBP < 20) {
        
        setRates(newRates);
        setLastUpdate(new Date());
        saveToCache(newRates);
        
        console.log('✅ Cotações atualizadas:', newRates);
      } else {
        throw new Error('Valores de cotação inválidos');
      }

    } catch (err) {
      console.error('❌ Erro ao buscar cotações:', err);
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Erro desconhecido');
      
      // Tentar carregar do cache como fallback
      const cached = loadFromCache();
      if (cached) {
        setRates(cached.rates);
        setLastUpdate(new Date(cached.timestamp));
        console.log('⚠️ Usando cotações em cache:', cached.rates);
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // ===== EFEITO: Inicialização =====
  useEffect(() => {
    // Tentar carregar do cache primeiro (resposta instantânea)
    const cached = loadFromCache();
    if (cached) {
      setRates(cached.rates);
      setLastUpdate(new Date(cached.timestamp));
      setLoading(false);
      console.log('📦 Cotações carregadas do cache:', cached.rates);
    }

    // Buscar cotações frescas da API
    fetchRates();

    // Atualizar a cada 5 minutos
    const interval = setInterval(fetchRates, CACHE_DURATION);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  return (
    <CurrencyContext.Provider
      value={{
        rates,
        loading,
        error,
        lastUpdate,
        refetch: fetchRates,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </CurrencyContext.Provider>
  );
};

// ========================================
// HOOK CUSTOMIZADO
// ========================================

export const useCurrency = (): CurrencyContextData => {
  const context = useContext(CurrencyContext);
  
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useCurrency deve ser usado dentro de CurrencyProvider');
  }
  
  return context;
};

==========================================
ARQUIVO: ThemeContext.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\hooks\ThemeContext.tsx
==========================================

import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect, ReactNode } from 'react';

type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';

interface ThemeContextType {
  theme: Theme;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  isDark: boolean;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType | undefined>(undefined);

const THEME_STORAGE_KEY = 'app_theme_preference';

export const ThemeProvider: React.FC<{ children: ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  // Inicializar tema do localStorage ou usar 'light' como padrão
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY);
      return (saved as Theme) || 'light';
    }
    return 'light';
  });

  // Aplicar tema no documento
  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    
    if (theme === 'dark') {
      root.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      root.classList.remove('dark');
    }

    // Salvar preferência
    localStorage.setItem(THEME_STORAGE_KEY, theme);
  }, [theme]);

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme(prev => prev === 'light' ? 'dark' : 'light');
  };

  const isDark = theme === 'dark';

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggleTheme, isDark }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useTheme = () => {
  const context = useContext(ThemeContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useTheme must be used within a ThemeProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

ARQUIVO: useAppContext.ts
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\hooks\useAppContext.ts
==========================================

// ================================
// HOOK DE CONTEXTO GLOBAL DO APP
// Coleta informações de toda a aplicação
// ================================

import { useMemo } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";

export interface AppContext {
  // Informações da página atual
  paginaAtual: string;
  rotaAtual: string;

  // Estatísticas gerais (você vai preencher com seus dados reais)
  totalProdutos?: number;
  totalCampanhas?: number;
  totalVendas?: number;
  totalComissoes?: number;

  // Dados específicos da página (quando aplicável)
  produtoAtual?: {
    id: string;
    nome: string;
    categoria?: string;
  };

  campanhaAtual?: {
    id: string;
    nome: string;
    status?: string;
  };

  // Capacidades do assistente
  capacidades: string[];
}

/**
 * Hook que retorna o contexto completo do aplicativo
 * Use este hook para passar informações para o AIAssistant
 */
export const useAppContext = (
  // Parâmetros opcionais que você passa de cada página
  dadosPagina?: {
    produtos?: any[];
    campanhas?: any[];
    vendas?: any[];
    produtoSelecionado?: any;
    campanhaSelecionada?: any;
  }
): AppContext => {
  const location = useLocation();

  // 🗺️ Detecta qual página o usuário está
  const paginaAtual = useMemo(() => {
    const path = location.pathname;

    if (path === "/" || path === "/dashboard") return "Dashboard";
    if (path.includes("/produtos") && path.includes("/edit"))
      return "Edição de Produto";
    if (path.includes("/produtos") && path.includes("/new"))
      return "Novo Produto";
    if (path.includes("/produtos/")) return "Detalhes do Produto";
    if (path.includes("/produtos")) return "Lista de Produtos";
    if (path.includes("/campanhas") && path.includes("/edit"))
      return "Edição de Campanha";
    if (path.includes("/campanhas") && path.includes("/new"))
      return "Nova Campanha";
    if (path.includes("/campanhas/")) return "Detalhes da Campanha";
    if (path.includes("/campanhas")) return "Lista de Campanhas";
    if (path.includes("/vendas")) return "Relatório de Vendas";
    if (path.includes("/comissoes")) return "Gestão de Comissões";
    if (path.includes("/afiliados")) return "Gestão de Afiliados";
    if (path.includes("/configuracoes")) return "Configurações";
    if (path.includes("/relatorios")) return "Relatórios";

    return "Página Desconhecida";
  }, [location.pathname]);

  // 📊 Monta o contexto completo
  const contexto: AppContext = useMemo(() => {
    return {
      paginaAtual,
      rotaAtual: location.pathname,

      // Estatísticas gerais
      totalProdutos: dadosPagina?.produtos?.length,
      totalCampanhas: dadosPagina?.campanhas?.length,
      totalVendas: dadosPagina?.vendas?.length,

      // Produto atual (se estiver em página de produto)
      produtoAtual: dadosPagina?.produtoSelecionado
        ? {
            id: dadosPagina.produtoSelecionado.id,
            nome:
              dadosPagina.produtoSelecionado.nome ||
              dadosPagina.produtoSelecionado.name,
            categoria: dadosPagina.produtoSelecionado.categoria,
          }
        : undefined,

      // Campanha atual (se estiver em página de campanha)
      campanhaAtual: dadosPagina?.campanhaSelecionada
        ? {
            id: dadosPagina.campanhaSelecionada.id,
            nome:
              dadosPagina.campanhaSelecionada.nome ||
              dadosPagina.campanhaSelecionada.name,
            status: dadosPagina.campanhaSelecionada.status,
          }
        : undefined,

      // 🎯 Capacidades do assistente (o que ele pode fazer)
      capacidades: [
        "Explicar funcionalidades do sistema",
        "Ajudar com estratégias de marketing de afiliados",
        "Sugerir otimizações de campanhas",
        "Analisar dados e métricas",
        "Tirar dúvidas sobre produtos e comissões",
        "Dar dicas de gestão de afiliados",
        "Auxiliar na configuração do sistema",
      ],
    };
  }, [paginaAtual, location.pathname, dadosPagina]);

  return contexto;
};

ARQUIVO: useDashboardConfig.ts
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\hooks\useDashboardConfig.ts
==========================================

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { 
  DashboardConfig, 
  DashboardMode, 
  DashboardSections 
} from '../types/dashboardConfig';
import { 
  PRESET_RESUMIDA, 
  PRESET_COMPLETA, 
  PRESET_CUSTOM 
} from '../types/dashboardConfig';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'dashboard_config_v1';

// ==========================================
// CARREGAR CONFIGURAÇÃO DO LOCALSTORAGE
// ==========================================
const loadConfig = (): DashboardConfig => {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') {
    return PRESET_RESUMIDA; // Padrão: Modo Resumida
  }

  try {
    const stored = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (stored) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(stored) as DashboardConfig;
      
      // Validar que tem todas as propriedades necessárias
      if (parsed.mode && parsed.sections) {
        return parsed;
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Erro ao carregar config do dashboard:', error);
  }

  return PRESET_RESUMIDA; // Fallback
};

// ==========================================
// HOOK PRINCIPAL
// ==========================================
export const useDashboardConfig = () => {
  const [config, setConfig] = useState<DashboardConfig>(loadConfig);

  // Salvar no localStorage sempre que mudar
  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
    
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(config));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar config do dashboard:', error);
    }
  }, [config]);

  // ==========================================
  // APLICAR PRESET (Resumida, Completa, Custom)
  // ==========================================
  const applyPreset = useCallback((mode: DashboardMode) => {
    switch (mode) {
      case 'resumida':
        setConfig(PRESET_RESUMIDA);
        break;
      case 'completa':
        setConfig(PRESET_COMPLETA);
        break;
      case 'custom':
        setConfig(PRESET_CUSTOM);
        break;
    }
  }, []);

  // ==========================================
  // TOGGLE DE SEÇÃO INDIVIDUAL
  // ==========================================
  const toggleSection = useCallback((sectionId: keyof DashboardSections) => {
    setConfig(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      mode: 'custom', // Automaticamente vira custom ao alterar
      sections: {
        ...prev.sections,
        [sectionId]: !prev.sections[sectionId],
      }
    }));
  }, []);

  // ==========================================
  // ATUALIZAR SEÇÕES EM LOTE
  // ==========================================
  const updateSections = useCallback((newSections: Partial<DashboardSections>) => {
    setConfig(prev => ({
      ...prev,
      mode: 'custom',
      sections: {
        ...prev.sections,
        ...newSections,
      }
    }));
  }, []);

  // ==========================================
  // RESETAR PARA PADRÃO
  // ==========================================
  const resetToDefault = useCallback(() => {
    setConfig(PRESET_RESUMIDA);
  }, []);

  // ==========================================
  // EXPORTAR CONFIGURAÇÃO (JSON)
  // ==========================================
  const exportConfig = useCallback(() => {
    const dataStr = JSON.stringify(config, null, 2);
    const dataBlob = new Blob([dataStr], { type: 'application/json' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(dataBlob);
    
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.href = url;
    link.download = `dashboard-config-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
    link.click();
    
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }, [config]);

  // ==========================================
  // IMPORTAR CONFIGURAÇÃO (JSON)
  // ==========================================
  const importConfig = useCallback((file: File) => {
    const reader = new FileReader();
    
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      try {
        const text = e.target?.result as string;
        const imported = JSON.parse(text) as DashboardConfig;
        
        // Validar estrutura básica
        if (imported.mode && imported.sections) {
          setConfig(imported);
          alert('✅ Configuração importada com sucesso!');
        } else {
          alert('❌ Arquivo inválido');
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Erro ao importar config:', error);
        alert('❌ Erro ao ler arquivo');
      }
    };
    
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }, []);

  return {
    config,
    applyPreset,
    toggleSection,
    updateSections,
    resetToDefault,
    exportConfig,
    importConfig,
  };
};

ARQUIVO: useDebouncedLocalStorage.ts
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\hooks\useDebouncedLocalStorage.ts
==========================================

// Criar hook com debounce
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

function useDebouncedLocalStorage<T>(key: string, initialValue: T, delay = 1000) {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<T>(() => {
    try {
      const item = localStorage.getItem(key);
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : initialValue;
    } catch {
      return initialValue;
    }
  });

  const timeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout>();

  useEffect(() => {
    // Limpa timeout anterior
    if (timeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    
    // Salva após delay
    timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value));
      } catch (error) {
        console.error(`Erro ao salvar ${key}:`, error);
      }
    }, delay);

    return () => {
      if (timeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
    };
  }, [value, key, delay]);

  return [value, setValue] as const;
}

ARQUIVO: useKeyboardShortcuts.tsx
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\hooks\useKeyboardShortcuts.tsx
==========================================

import { useEffect } from 'react';

export type ShortcutKey = 
  | 'Ctrl+1' | 'Ctrl+2' | 'Ctrl+3' | 'Ctrl+4' | 'Ctrl+5'
  | 'Ctrl+6' | 'Ctrl+7' | 'Ctrl+8' | 'Ctrl+9'
  | 'Ctrl+D' | 'Ctrl+C' | 'Ctrl+R' | 'Ctrl+S'
  | 'Ctrl+N' | 'Ctrl+F' | 'Ctrl+H'
  | '?'; // Help

interface ShortcutConfig {
  key: ShortcutKey;
  description: string;
  action: () => void;
}

export const useKeyboardShortcuts = (shortcuts: ShortcutConfig[]) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      // Ignorar se estiver digitando em input/textarea
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement;
      if (target.tagName === 'INPUT' || target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA') {
        return;
      }

      let shortcutKey = '';

      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) {
        shortcutKey = `Ctrl+${e.key.toUpperCase()}`;
      } else {
        shortcutKey = e.key;
      }

      const matchingShortcut = shortcuts.find(s => s.key === shortcutKey);

      if (matchingShortcut) {
        e.preventDefault();
        matchingShortcut.action();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [shortcuts]);
};

// Componente de ajuda de atalhos
export const KeyboardShortcutsHelp: React.FC<{ shortcuts: ShortcutConfig[] }> = ({ shortcuts }) => {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 shadow-lg max-w-md">
      <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
        ⌨️ Atalhos de Teclado
      </h3>
      <div className="space-y-2">
        {shortcuts.map((shortcut, idx) => (
          <div key={idx} className="flex items-center justify-between p-2 hover:bg-gray-50 rounded">
            <span className="text-sm text-gray-700">{shortcut.description}</span>
            <kbd className="px-2 py-1 bg-gray-100 border border-gray-300 rounded text-xs font-mono font-bold text-gray-700">
              {shortcut.key}
            </kbd>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-200 text-xs text-gray-600">
        💡 Pressione <kbd className="px-1 py-0.5 bg-gray-100 border border-gray-300 rounded font-mono">?</kbd> para ver esta ajuda novamente
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

ARQUIVO: useLocalStorage.ts
CAMINHO: C:\Projetos\App gestao afiliados\src\hooks\useLocalStorage.ts
==========================================

import { useState } from 'react';

// Hook personalizado para acessar o localStorage
function useLocalStorage<T>(key: string, initialValue: T) {
  // Tentando pegar o valor do localStorage, caso contrário usa o valor inicial
  const [storedValue, setStoredValue] = useState<T>(() => {
    try {
      const item = localStorage.getItem(key);
      // Se o item existe e é um valor válido, faz o parse
      return item ? JSON.parse(item) : initialValue;
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Erro ao ler ${key} do localStorage`, error);
      return initialValue; // Retorna o valor inicial em caso de erro
    }
  });

  // Função para atualizar o valor no localStorage e no estado
  const setValue = (value: T) => {
    try {
      setStoredValue(value);
      localStorage.setItem(key, JSON.stringify(value)); // Atualiza o localStorage
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Erro ao salvar ${key} no localStorage`, error);
    }
  };

  // Função para remover o item do localStorage
  const removeValue = () => {
    try {
      setStoredValue(initialValue); // Reseta o valor no estado
      localStorage.removeItem(key); // Remove o item do localStorage
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(`Erro ao remover ${key} do localStorage`, error);
    }
  };

  return [storedValue, setValue, removeValue] as const; // Retorna um tuple [valor, função de set, função de remove]
}

export default useLocalStorage;
